// ============================================================================
// BODY TRANSFORMATION TRACKER (BTT) — 2.0.0 RC1
// ============================================================================
//
// PURPOSE
// BTT tracks canonical identity, anatomy, appearance, temporary transformations,
// Developable Risks, and Conditions for the Player and admitted NPCs.
//
// RUNTIME FLOW
// Story-card edits are imported during preflight. Narrative text is parsed once
// into shared observations, subsystem consumers update state.BTT.entities, and
// canonical cards are serialized once after processing completes.
//
// STATE OWNERSHIP
// During narrative processing, state.BTT.entities is authoritative. Story cards
// written during a transaction are not read back until a later preflight pass.
// ============================================================================

var BTT_VERSION = "2.0.0-rc1";

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   RC1 RELEASE HOTFIX — SHARED OWNER/SLOT EVIDENCE
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Explicit possessive owner→slot evidence is extracted once per narrative
   fragment during the shared parse build and stored in parse.shared. Later
   Anatomy, Conditions, reproductive routing, delivery routing, and Developable
   Risk consumers read that shared evidence instead of rescanning raw prose.

   BTT_VERSION intentionally remains 2.0.0-rc1 so installing this performance
   hotfix does not discard an existing adventure's canonical BTT state.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

/* ============================================================================
   1. CANONICAL CONSTANTS AND CONFIGURATION
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   These values define the public configuration surface and stable domain names.
   They are not evidence decisions by themselves. Evidence-specific thresholds
   remain separate so domains can consume the same parse graph without forcing
   one universal scalar acceptance score.
============================================================================ */

  dev_risk_ai_unrouted_margin: 0.10,
  dev_risk_ai_candidate_replacement_margin: 0.12,
  /* Authoring-readiness scoring is parent-level. Event evidence remains intact
     while a topic is active; bounded coherence/recurrence/pattern terms decide
     when BTT knows enough to define a possible risk profile. */
  dev_risk_ai_causal_coherence_bonus_max: 0.35,
  dev_risk_ai_recurrence_bonus_max: 0.38,
  dev_risk_ai_pattern_coherence_bonus_max: 0.20,
  dev_risk_ai_ambiguity_penalty_max: 0.35,
  /* Candidate memory is an inactivity horizon. Dormant parents retain one
     compressed historical seed and may later be revived by fresh evidence. */
  dev_risk_ai_dormant_max: 16,
  dev_risk_ai_dormant_compaction_factor: 0.50,
  /* Response-channel discovery admits modest direct effects into recurrence
     history without making them sufficient to qualify a risk by themselves. */
  dev_risk_ai_response_owner_min: 0.55,
  dev_risk_ai_response_channel_min: 0.30,
  dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_min: 0.52,
  dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_margin: 0.10,
  /* Existing-risk progression watches use the normal generation thresholds for
     higher SUCCESS authoring, but retain weaker evidence for diagnostics. */
  dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns: 18,
  dev_risk_ai_progression_track_match_min: 0.46,
  dev_risk_ai_progression_novel_effect_min: 0.38,

  debug: true,
  debug_keep_turns: 5,
  debug_max_lines: 180,
  anatomy_warning_chars: 900
};

/* ============================================================================
   2. GENERAL UTILITIES AND CLEAN RUNTIME STATE
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Runtime state is deliberately small and explicit. No function in this section
   reads story cards except the preflight import functions defined later.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_escapeRegExp(s){ return String(s||"").replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,"\\$&"); }
function BTT_lower(s){ return String(s||"").toLowerCase(); }
function BTT_cleanSpace(s){ return String(s||"").replace(/[\u200B-\u200D]+/g,"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim(); }
function BTT_clip(s,n){ s=String(s||""); n=Math.max(8,Number(n)||160); return s.length<=n?s:s.slice(0,n-1)+"…"; }
function BTT_clamp(n,a,b){ n=Number(n); if(!isFinite(n)) n=0; return Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n)); }
function BTT_round(n,p){ p=isFinite(Number(p))?Number(p):2; var m=Math.pow(10,p); return Math.round((Number(n)||0)*m)/m; }
function BTT_bool(v,f){ if(typeof v==="boolean")return v; var s=BTT_lower(v).trim(); if(/^(?:true|yes|y|on|1|enabled?)$/.test(s))return true; if(/^(?:false|no|n|off|0|disabled?)$/.test(s))return false; return f; }
function BTT_num(v,f){ var n=Number(v); return isFinite(n)?n:f; }
function BTT_clone(v){ try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));}catch(_e){return v;} }
function BTT_unique(xs){ var out=[],seen={}; xs=Array.isArray(xs)?xs:[]; for(var i=0;i<xs.length;i++){var x=BTT_cleanSpace(xs[i]);if(!x)continue;var k=BTT_lower(x);if(seen[k])continue;seen[k]=1;out.push(x);} return out; }
function BTT_splitList(v){ if(Array.isArray(v))return BTT_unique(v); return BTT_unique(String(v||"").split(/[,;|]/g)); }
function BTT_normId(s){ return BTT_lower(s).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,"-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g,""); }
function BTT_wordBoundaryHas(text,term){ if(!term)return false; return new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(term).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","i").test(String(text||"")); }
function BTT_hash(s){ var h=2166136261>>>0; s=String(s||""); for(var i=0;i<s.length;i++){h^=s.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)>>>0;} return h>>>0; }
function BTT_hashInt(seed,min,max){ min=Math.floor(Number(min)||0);max=Math.floor(Number(max)||0);if(max<min){var t=min;min=max;max=t;}return min+(BTT_hash(seed)%(max-min+1)); }
function BTT_hashRoll(seed){ return BTT_hashInt(seed,1,100); }
function BTT_nowAction(){ try{return Number(info&&info.actionCount)||0;}catch(_e){return 0;} }

function BTT_pronounSet(x){
  x=BTT_lower(x).replace(/\s+/g,"");
  if(x.indexOf("she/her")===0||x==="she")return "she/her/her";
  if(x.indexOf("he/him")===0||x==="he")return "he/him/his";
  if(x.indexOf("they/them")===0||x==="they")return "they/them/their";
  return "";
}
function BTT_defaultPronouns(g){ g=BTT_lower(g); if(g==="female")return "she/her/her"; if(g==="male")return "he/him/his"; return "they/them/their"; }
function BTT_pronounParts(s){ s=BTT_pronounSet(s)||"they/them/their"; var p=s.split("/"); return {subj:p[0],obj:p[1],pos:p[2]}; }

function BTT_newSlot(){ return {traits:[],origin:"",updatedTurn:0}; }
function BTT_inferSlotOrigin(meta,slot){
  var so=meta&&meta.slots&&meta.slots[slot];if(!so)return "";if(so.origin)return BTT_normId(so.origin);var first=so.traits&&so.traits.length?BTT_normId(so.traits[0]):"";if(!first)return "";if(first===BTT_normId(meta&&meta.heritage||"")||first==="human"||BTT_profile("heritage",first)){so.origin=first;return first;}return "";
}
function BTT_newAppearance(){
  return {
    summary_ai:"",
    fields:{skin:"",hair_color:"",hair_length:"",eye_color:"",build:"",scent:""},
    axes:{fem:0,height:20,fat:20,gut_size:20,breast_size:0,ass_size:20,cock_size:0,fertility:0,virility:0},
    evidence:[],debug:[],_delta_since_refresh:0,_last_refresh_turn:-1,
    _need_ai_refresh:false,_pendingRefresh:false,_did_ai_init:false,_refresh_flag_reason:"",
    _refresh_requested_turn:-1,_refresh_captured_turn:-1,_refresh_request_id:"",_refresh_signature:""
  };
}
function BTT_newMeta(entity,name){
  name=BTT_cleanSpace(name)||(entity==="Player"?"Player":String(entity||"").replace(/^NPC:/,""));
  return {
    entity:String(entity||"Player"),initialized:false,
    identity:{entityId:String(entity||"Player"),currentName:name,originalName:name,aliases:BTT_unique(entity==="Player"?["Player","you","your","yourself",name]:[name]),gender:"",pronouns:"they/them/their",previousPronouns:"",pronounGrace:0,heritage:""},
    gender:"",heritage:"",species:"",overallFem:0,
    slots:{},appearance:BTT_newAppearance(),conditions:[],
    instantchange:{effects:[],currentDay:0,sourceSnapshot:null,canonicalProfile:null,persistentRiskProfiles:[],transformationSeed:null,phenotypeSignature:"",committedPhenotype:null},
    developableRisks:{active:[],conditionedTriggers:[],acquisitionHistory:{}},
    evidenceHistory:{},sharedEvidenceHistory:{},conditionEvidenceHistory:{},genesis:{state:"unmaterialized",firstTurn:BTT_getTurn(),admissionTurn:null,sealedTurn:null,buffer:[],baselineSignature:"",sourceText:"",baselineSource:"",cardAuthority:{gender:false,heritage:false}},
    lastSerialized:{anatomy:"",appearance:"",appearanceDescription:"",instantchange:"",ai:"",anatomyParsed:null,appearanceParsed:null},
    debug:{identity:[],anatomy:[],appearance:[],conditions:[],instantchange:[],risks:[],devrisks:[],manual:[]}
  };
}

function BTT_newRunSeed(){
  /* Fresh adventures need a roll seed that is stable for the life of that
     adventure but not identical across every Player initialization. Profile
     choices remain deterministic after the seed is created, so repeated card
     writes cannot reroll appearance accidentally. */
  var r=0;try{r=Math.floor(Math.random()*4294967295)>>>0;}catch(_e){}
  if(!r)r=BTT_hash(String(Date.now?Date.now():0)+"|"+String(Math.random?Math.random():0)+"|btt-run");
  return String(r>>>0);
}
function BTT_ensureState(){
  if(typeof state==="undefined"||!state)state={};
  if(!state.BTT||state.BTT.cleanCoreVersion!==BTT_VERSION){
    state.BTT={
      cleanCoreVersion:BTT_VERSION,turnCounter:0,worldDay:0,entities:{},rollSeed:BTT_newRunSeed(),
      discourse:{recentEntity:"",recentNPC:"",recentSpeaker:"",productObjects:{},containers:{},nextObject:1,nextContainer:1,lastProductObject:"",lastContainerByEntity:{}},
      processedEvents:{},recentTransactions:[],profileCache:{signature:"",gender:{},heritage:{}},devRiskCache:{signature:"",profiles:{}},
      devRiskGeneration:{candidates:[],dormantCandidates:[],unroutedEvents:[],pending:null,generatedCount:0,lastGeneratedTurn:-9999,debug:[],seenEvents:{},nextCandidate:1,lastMaintenanceNotice:"",lastCompleted:null},
      currentTransaction:null
    };
  }
  if(!state.BTT.rollSeed)state.BTT.rollSeed=BTT_newRunSeed();
  if(!state.BTT.entities)state.BTT.entities={};
  if(!state.BTT.entities.Player)state.BTT.entities.Player=BTT_newMeta("Player","Player");
  return state.BTT;
}
function BTT_runSeed(){return String(BTT_ensureState().rollSeed||"0");}
function BTT_getTurn(){ try{return Number(BTT_ensureState().turnCounter)||0;}catch(_e){return 0;} }
function BTT_getDay(){ try{return Number(BTT_ensureState().worldDay)||0;}catch(_e){return 0;} }
function BTT_getEntity(entity){ var s=BTT_ensureState(); entity=String(entity||"Player"); if(!s.entities[entity])s.entities[entity]=BTT_newMeta(entity,entity.replace(/^NPC:/,"")); return s.entities[entity]; }
function BTT_findEntity(entity){ var s=BTT_ensureState(); return s.entities[String(entity||"")]||null; }
function BTT_touchDebug(meta,domain,line){
  if(!meta||!meta.debug||!meta.debug[domain])return;
  meta.debug[domain].push({turn:BTT_getTurn(),line:String(line||"")});
  /* Five-turn retention is the normal bound. This is only an emergency memory
     ceiling and is intentionally much larger than a story-card Entry limit. */
  if(meta.debug[domain].length>1600)meta.debug[domain]=meta.debug[domain].slice(-1600);
}
function BTT_recentDebug(meta,domain){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),out=[],a=meta&&meta.debug&&meta.debug[domain]||[],min=BTT_getTurn()-Math.max(1,cfg.debug_keep_turns)+1;
  for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(Number(a[i].turn)>=min)out.push(String(a[i].line||""));
  return out;
}

/**
 * Global AI-Developable-Risk generation diagnostics.
 *
 * Generation is world-level rather than entity-level: multiple independently
 * owner/source/mechanism-bound discovery candidates may coexist, while only one
 * batched maintenance-authoring job has exclusive metadata/output authority at a time.
 * All missing or invalid semantic fields are requested together whenever context
 * capacity permits; Continue is used only for incomplete or repair responses. Keep this debug separate
 * from per-entity Developable Risk acquisition/track logs so creators can distinguish
 * "a risk profile is being discovered/authored" from "an existing risk is firing."
 */
function BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(){
  var s=BTT_ensureState(),g=s.devRiskGeneration;
  if(!g||typeof g!=="object")g=s.devRiskGeneration={candidates:[],dormantCandidates:[],unroutedEvents:[],pending:null,generatedCount:0,lastGeneratedTurn:-9999,debug:[],seenEvents:{},nextCandidate:1,lastMaintenanceNotice:"",lastCompleted:null};
  if(!Array.isArray(g.candidates))g.candidates=[];
  if(!Array.isArray(g.dormantCandidates))g.dormantCandidates=[];
  if(!Array.isArray(g.unroutedEvents))g.unroutedEvents=[];
  /* Normalize generation state to one candidate/event structure. Obsolete singleton
     fields are discarded so discovery has one authoritative state shape. */
  if("candidate" in g)delete g.candidate;
  if(!Array.isArray(g.debug))g.debug=[];if(!g.seenEvents||typeof g.seenEvents!=="object")g.seenEvents={};if(!isFinite(Number(g.generatedCount)))g.generatedCount=0;if(!isFinite(Number(g.lastGeneratedTurn)))g.lastGeneratedTurn=-9999;if(!isFinite(Number(g.nextCandidate))||Number(g.nextCandidate)<1)g.nextCandidate=1;if(!("pending" in g))g.pending=null;if(typeof g.lastMaintenanceNotice!=="string")g.lastMaintenanceNotice="";if(!("lastCompleted" in g))g.lastCompleted=null;return g;
}
function BTT_devRiskGenerationDebugEnabled(){try{return !!BTT_getConfig().dev_risk_ai_debug_card_enabled;}catch(_e){return false;}}
function BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug(line){if(!BTT_devRiskGenerationDebugEnabled())return;var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),row={turn:BTT_getTurn(),line:String(line||"")},a=g.debug,last=a.length?a[a.length-1]:null;if(last&&Number(last.turn)===row.turn&&String(last.line||"")===row.line)return;a.push(row);if(a.length>2400)g.debug=a.slice(-2400);}
function BTT_recentDevRiskGenerationDebug(){var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),a=g.debug||[],out=[],min=BTT_getTurn()-Math.max(1,Number(cfg.debug_keep_turns)||1)+1,max=Math.max(1,Math.min(650,Math.round(Number(cfg.debug_max_lines)||180)));for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(Number(a[i].turn)>=min)out.push("TURN "+String(a[i].turn)+" | "+String(a[i].line||""));return out.slice(-max);}
function BTT_devRiskGenerationCooldownRemaining(cfg,g){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();g=g||BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState();var last=Number(g.lastGeneratedTurn);if(!isFinite(last)||last<-1000)return 0;return Math.max(0,Math.round(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_cooldown_turns)||0)-(BTT_getTurn()-last));}
function BTT_devCandidatePublic(c){
  if(!c)return null;
  return {
    id:String(c.id||""),fingerprint:String(c.fingerprint||""),label:String(c.label||""),receiver:String(c.receiver||""),
    sourceEntity:String(c.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(c.sourceDescriptor||""),mechanism:String(c.mechanism||""),
    responseFamily:String(c.responseFamily||""),responseChannels:BTT_clone(c.responseChannels||{}),mechanismTags:BTT_clone(c.mechanismTags||[]),
    trackHints:BTT_clone(c.trackHints||[]),blueprint:BTT_clone(c.blueprint||null),score:BTT_round(Number(c.score)||0,3),
    scoreComponents:BTT_clone(c.scoreComponents||{}),events:(c.events||[]).map(function(ev){return {
      id:String(ev.id||""),turn:Number(ev.turn)||0,action:Number(ev.action)||0,receiver:String(ev.receiver||""),
      sourceEntity:String(ev.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(ev.sourceDescriptor||""),sourceConfidence:BTT_round(Number(ev.sourceConfidence)||0,3),
      mechanism:String(ev.mechanism||""),responseFamily:String(ev.responseFamily||""),responseChannels:BTT_clone(ev.responseChannels||{}),
      mechanismTags:BTT_clone(ev.mechanismTags||[]),trackHints:BTT_clone(ev.trackHints||[]),strength:BTT_round(Number(ev.strength)||0,3),
      support:BTT_round(Number(ev.support)||0,3),contribution:BTT_round(Number(ev.contribution)||0,3),clauses:(ev.clauses||[]).map(function(o){return {
        phase:String(o.phase||""),fragmentIndex:Number(o.fragmentIndex)||0,raw:BTT_clip(o.raw,420),rawScore:BTT_round(Number(o.rawScore)||0,3),
        actor:String(o.actor||""),triggerActor:String(o.triggerActor||o.actor||""),triggerActorConfidence:BTT_round(Number(o.triggerActorConfidence)||0,3),receiver:String(o.receiver||""),sourceEntity:String(o.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(o.sourceDescriptor||""),
        causalSourceEntity:String(o.causalSourceEntity||o.sourceEntity||""),causalSourceDescriptor:String(o.causalSourceDescriptor||o.sourceDescriptor||""),ownerConfidence:BTT_round(Number(o.ownerConfidence)||0,3),sourceConfidence:BTT_round(Number(o.sourceConfidence)||0,3),slot:String(o.slot||""),
        mechanism:String(o.mechanism||""),responseFamily:String(o.responseFamily||""),responseChannels:BTT_clone(o.responseChannels||{}),
        evidenceComponents:BTT_clone(o.evidenceComponents||{}),semanticMatch:BTT_round(Number(o.semanticMatch)||0,3),novelty:BTT_round(Number(o.novelty)||0,3)
      };})
    };}),createdTurn:Number(c.createdTurn)||0,lastTurn:Number(c.lastTurn)||0,lastSupportingTurn:Number(c.lastSupportingTurn)||0,retryAfterTurn:Number(c.retryAfterTurn)||0,revivalCount:Number(c.revivalCount)||0
  };
}
function BTT_devDormantPublic(d){if(!d)return null;return {id:String(d.id||""),fingerprint:String(d.fingerprint||""),label:String(d.label||""),receiver:String(d.receiver||""),sourceEntity:String(d.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(d.sourceDescriptor||""),mechanism:String(d.mechanism||"unresolved"),responseFamily:String(d.responseFamily||"response"),responseChannels:BTT_clone(d.responseChannels||{}),blueprint:BTT_clone(d.blueprint||null),historicalConfidence:BTT_round(Number(d.historicalConfidence)||0,3),originalConfidence:BTT_round(Number(d.originalConfidence)||0,3),originalEventCount:Number(d.originalEventCount)||0,dormantTurn:Number(d.dormantTurn)||0,lastSupportingTurn:Number(d.lastSupportingTurn)||0,revivalCount:Number(d.revivalCount)||0};}
function BTT_devUnroutedPublic(row){if(!row)return null;return {turn:Number(row.turn)||0,action:Number(row.action)||0,phase:String(row.phase||""),fragmentIndex:Number(row.fragmentIndex)||0,raw:BTT_clip(row.raw,300),receiver:String(row.receiver||""),sourceEntity:String(row.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(row.sourceDescriptor||""),mechanism:String(row.mechanism||"unresolved"),responseFamily:String(row.responseFamily||"response"),responseChannels:BTT_clone(row.responseChannels||{}),rawScore:BTT_round(Number(row.rawScore)||0,3),reason:String(row.unroutedReason||"UNROUTED")};}
function BTT_devRiskGenerationPayload(){
  var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),p=g.pending,fields={},k;if(p&&p.fieldValues)for(k in p.fieldValues)if(p.fieldValues.hasOwnProperty(k))fields[k]=BTT_clip(p.fieldValues[k],900);
  return {version:BTT_VERSION,turn:BTT_getTurn(),generatedCount:Number(g.generatedCount)||0,lastGeneratedTurn:Number(g.lastGeneratedTurn)||-9999,candidates:(g.candidates||[]).map(BTT_devCandidatePublic),dormantCandidates:(g.dormantCandidates||[]).map(BTT_devDormantPublic),unroutedEvents:(g.unroutedEvents||[]).slice(0,24).map(BTT_devUnroutedPublic),pending:p?{mode:String(p.mode||"new"),candidateId:String(p.candidateId||""),fingerprint:String(p.fingerprint||""),receiver:String(p.receiver||""),sourceEntity:String(p.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(p.sourceDescriptor||""),mechanism:String(p.mechanism||""),blueprint:BTT_clone(p.blueprint||null),extension:BTT_clone(p.extension||null),createdTurn:Number(p.createdTurn)||0,retries:Number(p.retries)||0,authoringResponses:Number(p.authoringResponses)||0,maintenanceContinues:Number(p.maintenanceContinues)||0,lastError:String(p.lastError||""),fieldStatus:BTT_clone(p.fieldStatus||{}),fieldErrors:BTT_clone(p.fieldErrors||{}),requestedFields:BTT_devRequestedFields(p),derived:BTT_clone(p.derived||{}),outcomeSemantics:BTT_clone(p.outcomeSemantics||{}),evidence:BTT_clone(p.evidence||[]),events:BTT_clone(p.events||[]),fields:fields}:null,lastCompleted:BTT_clone(g.lastCompleted||null)};
}
function BTT_devRiskGenerationStatusEntry(){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),p=g.pending,remaining=BTT_devRiskGenerationCooldownRemaining(cfg,g),cands=(g.candidates||[]).slice().sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.score)-Number(a.score);}),dorm=(g.dormantCandidates||[]).slice().sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.historicalConfidence||0)-Number(a.historicalConfidence||0);}),status=!cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_enabled?"DISABLED":p?"AUTHORING MAINTENANCE":Number(g.generatedCount)>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_generated)?"GENERATED LIMIT":remaining>0?"COOLDOWN":cands.length?"DISCOVERY CANDIDATES":dorm.length?"DORMANT HYPOTHESES":"DISCOVERY IDLE";
  var lines=["BTT AI DEVELOPABLE RISK GENERATION","","Enabled: "+(cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_enabled?"YES":"NO"),"Debug Card: "+(cfg.dev_risk_ai_debug_card_enabled?"YES":"NO"),"Status: "+status,"Current Story Turn: "+BTT_getTurn(),"","Generated Risks: "+String(Number(g.generatedCount)||0)+" / "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_generated),"Generation Cooldown: "+(remaining>0?String(remaining)+" turn(s) remaining":"READY"),"Observation Admission: actual + affected-owner-bound response/change event; response channel may precede causal routing","Parent Candidate Key: affected owner + causal source/mechanism hypothesis","Authoring Readiness Threshold: "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_confidence),"Required Distinct Logical Events: "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_observations),"Candidate Memory: "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns)+" turns of topic inactivity before dormancy","Dormant Compaction: 1 historical event at "+String(Math.round(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dormant_compaction_factor||0.50)*100))+"% of current authoring confidence","Active Parent Limit: "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_candidates)+" (dynamic score-prioritized)","Dormant Parent Seeds: "+String(dorm.length)+" / "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dormant_max),"Unrouted Event Ledger: "+String((g.unroutedEvents||[]).length)+" / "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_max),"Response Channels: mental, behavioral, reward, aversive, sensory, physiological, morphological, chemical/dependency, biological-host, adaptive","Existing-Profile Progression Watch: ON (creator profiles diagnostic-only; AI profiles may extend higher SUCCESS or add qualified tracks)","Authoring Transport: confidence blueprint + semantic wrappers; incomplete quoted values re-prompt","","--- ACTIVE PARENT RISK HYPOTHESES ---"];
  if(!cands.length)lines.push("none");
  for(var i=0;i<Math.min(8,cands.length);i++){
    var c=cands[i],events=c.events||[],ch=c.responseChannels||{},sc=c.scoreComponents||{},inactive=Math.max(0,BTT_getTurn()-Number(c.lastSupportingTurn||c.lastTurn||BTT_getTurn()));lines.push("","PARENT RISK "+String(c.id||("#"+(i+1)))+" | "+String(c.label||"unresolved"));lines.push("Fingerprint: "+String(c.fingerprint||"unresolved"));lines.push("Receiver: "+String(c.receiver||"unresolved")+" | Source: "+String(c.sourceEntity||c.sourceDescriptor||"unresolved"));lines.push("Mechanism: "+String(c.mechanism||"unresolved"));lines.push("Response Evidence: "+(Object.keys(ch).length?Object.keys(ch).sort(function(a,b){return ch[b]-ch[a];}).slice(0,8).map(function(k){return k+"="+BTT_round(ch[k],2);}).join(", "):"none"));lines.push("Authoring Confidence: "+BTT_round(c.score,2)+" / "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_confidence)+" | Distinct Events: "+String(events.length)+" / "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_observations));lines.push("Score Components: event_evidence="+BTT_round(sc.eventEvidence||0,2)+" + causal_coherence="+BTT_round(sc.causalCoherence||0,2)+" + recurrence="+BTT_round(sc.recurrence||0,2)+" + pattern="+BTT_round(sc.patternCoherence||0,2)+" - ambiguity="+BTT_round(sc.ambiguityPenalty||0,2)+" = "+BTT_round(sc.total||c.score||0,2));lines.push("Event Evidence Detail: strongest="+BTT_round(sc.strongest||0,2)+" + corroboration="+BTT_round(sc.corroboration||0,2)+" + same-event-support="+BTT_round(sc.eventSupport||0,2));lines.push("Last Meaningful Support: T"+String(c.lastSupportingTurn||c.lastTurn||0)+" | Inactive: "+String(inactive)+" / "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns)+" turn(s)");if(c.blueprint){lines.push("Authoring Blueprint:");var bt=BTT_devBlueprintText(c.blueprint).split("\n");for(var bi=0;bi<bt.length;bi++)lines.push("  "+bt[bi]);}for(var e=Math.max(0,events.length-4);e<events.length;e++){var ev=events[e];lines.push("  EVENT "+String(ev.id||e+1)+" | T"+String(ev.turn)+" A"+String(ev.action)+" | strength="+BTT_round(ev.strength,2)+" | response="+String(ev.responseFamily||"response")+" | contribution="+BTT_round(ev.contribution,2)+(ev.historical?" | HISTORICAL":""));for(var j=0;j<Math.min(2,(ev.clauses||[]).length);j++)lines.push("    - "+String(ev.clauses[j].phase||"").toUpperCase()+" C"+String(ev.clauses[j].fragmentIndex)+" | "+BTT_clip(ev.clauses[j].raw,170));}
  }
  lines.push("","--- DORMANT PARENT HYPOTHESES ---");if(!dorm.length)lines.push("none");for(var d=0;d<Math.min(6,dorm.length);d++){var dr=dorm[d];lines.push("DORMANT "+String(dr.id||("#"+(d+1)))+" | "+String(dr.label||"unresolved")+" | receiver="+String(dr.receiver||"unresolved")+" | source="+String(dr.sourceEntity||dr.sourceDescriptor||"unresolved")+" | mechanism="+String(dr.mechanism||"unresolved"));lines.push("  Historical Confidence: "+BTT_round(dr.historicalConfidence||0,2)+" | Original: "+BTT_round(dr.originalConfidence||0,2)+" | Events: "+String(dr.originalEventCount||0)+" -> 1 | Dormant Since: T"+String(dr.dormantTurn||0));}
  lines.push("","--- UNROUTED EVENT LEDGER ---");var unr=(g.unroutedEvents||[]).slice().sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.rawScore||0)-Number(a.rawScore||0);});if(!unr.length)lines.push("none");for(var u=0;u<Math.min(6,unr.length);u++){var ur=unr[u];lines.push("T"+String(ur.turn)+" A"+String(ur.action)+" | receiver="+String(ur.receiver||"unresolved")+" | response="+String(ur.responseFamily||"response")+" | strength="+BTT_round(ur.rawScore||0,2)+" | reason="+String(ur.unroutedReason||"UNROUTED"));lines.push("  "+BTT_clip(ur.raw,160));}
  lines.push("","--- AUTHORING STATE ---");if(p){BTT_devRecomputeSectionStatus(p);var req=BTT_devRequestedFields(p),extMode=p.mode==="extension"&&p.extension&&p.extension.kind==="new_track"?"ADD NEW TRACK":p.mode==="extension"?"EXTEND HIGHER SUCCESS":"NEW RISK";lines.push("Pending: YES | Mode: "+extMode);if(p.mode==="extension"&&p.extension){if(p.extension.kind==="new_track")lines.push("Risk: "+p.extension.riskName+" | New Track: "+p.extension.trackNumber);else lines.push("Risk: "+p.extension.riskName+" | Track: "+p.extension.trackName+" | Next SUCCESS: "+p.extension.nextSuccess);lines.push("Novel Response Family: "+p.extension.responseFamily);}else{lines.push("Candidate: "+String(p.candidateId||"unknown")+" | "+String(p.fingerprint||"unresolved"));if(p.blueprint){lines.push("Blueprint:");var bp=BTT_devBlueprintText(p.blueprint).split("\n");for(var bpi=0;bpi<bp.length;bpi++)lines.push("  "+bp[bpi]);}}lines.push("Story Frozen: YES");lines.push("Continue Retries Used: "+String(p.maintenanceContinues||0)+" / "+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_authoring_continues));lines.push("Next Atomic Fields: "+(req.length?req.join(", "):"validation"));lines.push("Last Error: "+String(p.lastError||"none"));}else{lines.push("Pending: NO");if(g.lastCompleted)lines.push("Last Completion: "+String(g.lastCompleted.status||"none")+(g.lastCompleted.name?" | "+g.lastCompleted.name:"")+(g.lastCompleted.reason?" | "+g.lastCompleted.reason:""));}
  return lines.join("\n");
}
function BTT_devRiskGenerationDebugDescription(){
  var lines=["BTT global AI Developable Risk discovery/authoring diagnostics. Discovery consumes the shared canonical actor/trigger/source/receiver/affected-owner response graph from Input and Output. Response channels are confidence evidence inside a parent risk hypothesis, not separate top-level risks.","","AUTHORING READINESS is intentionally separate from downstream risk maturity. While a parent topic is active, admitted evidence does not decay. Parent readiness combines event evidence with causal coherence, bounded recurrence confidence, response-pattern coherence, and ambiguity penalties. The ordinary creator-card acquisition and track thresholds still decide whether an authored possible risk actually acquires or progresses.","","Candidate memory is an inactivity horizon. A parent with no meaningful supporting event for that window is compacted into one DORMANT historical seed at 50% of its current readiness confidence. Dormant seeds do not passively decay or repeatedly halve. A later compatible actual event may revive the seed through the same confidence routing; fresh events must then rebuild normal recurrence.","","Trigger actors and causal sources are separate. A character may issue an order or administer a source while a named pact, enchanted garment, chemical, curse, or biological-host mechanism remains the actual parent-risk source. Compatible trigger-actor/source duplicates are consolidated before authoring.","","The UNROUTED EVENT LEDGER is reserved for materially risk-relevant direct responses or changes. Low-information social/sensory observations remain in the shared canonical observation graph but do not occupy the Dev-Risk rerouting queue.","","Existing acquired Developable Risks remain under progression watch. Evidence already explained by a track is logged as existing-track support; independently corroborated novel higher effects remain visible in the normal Developable-Risk summary. Creator-authored profiles are diagnostic-only. AI-generated profiles may queue targeted maintenance to append a higher SUCCESS level or to add an independently qualified new track after recurrence/confidence thresholds are met.","","--- DEBUG HISTORY ---"],dbg=BTT_recentDevRiskGenerationDebug();if(dbg.length)lines=lines.concat(dbg);else lines.push("No retained AI-generation debug events.");lines=lines.concat(["","--- BTT AI DEV RISK GENERATION STATE ---","Payload JSON: "+JSON.stringify(BTT_devRiskGenerationPayload()),"--- END BTT AI DEV RISK GENERATION STATE ---"]);return lines.join("\n");
}
function BTT_writeDevRiskGenerationDebugCard(){var cfg=BTT_getConfig();if(!cfg.dev_risk_ai_debug_card_enabled)return null;if(!BTT_hasStoryCards())return null;var title="BTT AI Dev Risk Generation Debug",c=BTT_findCardByTitle(title);if(!c)c=BTT_createCard({title:title,type:"BTT Debug",keys:"",entry:"",description:""});if(!c)return null;c.title=title;c.type="BTT Debug";if("cardType" in c)c.cardType="BTT Debug";c.keys="";c.entry=BTT_devRiskGenerationStatusEntry();c.description=BTT_devRiskGenerationDebugDescription();return c;}
/**
 * Appearance-card debug has its own bounded store because the Appearance card
 * renders `meta.appearance.debug` rather than the general domain arrays.
 */
function BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,line){
  if(!meta||!meta.appearance)return;
  meta.appearance.debug=Array.isArray(meta.appearance.debug)?meta.appearance.debug:[];
  meta.appearance.debug.push({turn:BTT_getTurn(),line:String(line||"")});
  if(meta.appearance.debug.length>1600)meta.appearance.debug=meta.appearance.debug.slice(-1600);
}
function BTT_debugClauseLabel(fragment){return "TURN "+BTT_getTurn()+" — C"+String(fragment&&isFinite(Number(fragment.index))?Number(fragment.index):"?");}
function BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,domain,fragment,appearance){
  var label=BTT_debugClauseLabel(fragment),arr=appearance?(meta&&meta.appearance&&meta.appearance.debug):(meta&&meta.debug&&meta.debug[domain]);if(!arr)return;
  for(var i=arr.length-1;i>=0&&i>=arr.length-12;i--){var line=String(arr[i]&&arr[i].line||"");if(/^TURN \d+ — C/.test(line)){if(line===label)return;break;}}
  if(appearance)BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,label);else BTT_touchDebug(meta,domain,label);
}
function BTT_debugCueText(xs){var out=[];xs=Array.isArray(xs)?xs:[xs];for(var i=0;i<xs.length;i++){var x=BTT_cleanSpace(xs[i]);if(!x||out.indexOf(x)>=0)continue;out.push(x);}return BTT_clip(out.slice(0,4).join("; "),150);}
function BTT_additiveContextSupport(ownership,actuality,change){
  /* Independent confidence channels contribute additively. None of these values
     is multiplied by another confidence channel. */
  return Math.max(0,Number(ownership||0)-0.55)*0.20+Math.max(0,Number(actuality||0)-0.55)*0.12+Math.max(0,Number(change||0))*0.10;
}


/* ============================================================================
   3. STORY-CARD I/O AND CONTROL CARDS
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Story-card functions are divided into two categories:
   - preflight import: cards may update live state before parsing starts;
   - final serialization: live state overwrites BTT-owned cards once.
   No gameplay-domain function is permitted to read a story card mid-transaction.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_hasStoryCards(){ return typeof storyCards!=="undefined"&&Array.isArray(storyCards)&&typeof addStoryCard==="function"; }
function BTT_createCard(fields){
  fields=fields||{}; if(!BTT_hasStoryCards())return null; var card=null,before=storyCards.length;
  try{card=addStoryCard(String(fields.keys||""),String(fields.entry||""),String(fields.type||"class"),String(fields.title||""),String(fields.description||""),{returnCard:true});}catch(_e){
    try{addStoryCard(String(fields.keys||""),String(fields.entry||""),String(fields.type||"class"),String(fields.title||""),String(fields.description||""));}catch(_e2){}
  }
  if(!card&&storyCards.length>before)card=storyCards[storyCards.length-1];
  if(!card){for(var i=storyCards.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(storyCards[i]&&String(storyCards[i].title||"")===String(fields.title||"")){card=storyCards[i];break;}}
  if(!card)return null;
  card.title=String(fields.title||"");card.type=String(fields.type||"class");if("cardType" in card)card.cardType=card.type;card.keys=String(fields.keys||"");card.entry=String(fields.entry||"");card.description=String(fields.description||"");return card;
}
function BTT_removeCard(card){ if(!card||!Array.isArray(storyCards))return false; var i=storyCards.indexOf(card);if(i<0)return false;try{if(typeof removeStoryCard==="function")removeStoryCard(i);else storyCards.splice(i,1);}catch(_e){storyCards.splice(i,1);}return true; }
function BTT_findCardByTitle(title){ if(!Array.isArray(storyCards))return null;for(var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++)if(storyCards[i]&&String(storyCards[i].title||"").trim()===String(title||"").trim())return storyCards[i];return null; }

function BTT_configEntry(){
  return [
    "Configure BTT","",
    "BTT settings are stored in this card's NOTES / DESCRIPTION field.",
    "Edit the labeled values there; this Entry is intentionally kept short so it remains editable.",
    "Developable Risk AI generation is disabled by default until you explicitly enable it."
  ].join("\n");
}

  return [
    "BTT CONFIGURATION — EDIT VALUES BELOW","",
    "enabled: true","context_mode: excerpt","excerpt_always_include_genitals: true","",

    "# Developable Risk settings (separate from Instant-Change Risk Tracks)",
    "dev_risk_enabled: true",
    "# 0 = broad semantic assumptions; 1 = very narrow/close semantic match",

    "dev_risk_acquire_threshold: 2.25","dev_risk_acquire_store_min: 1.55","dev_risk_acquire_history_bonus_max: 0.55","dev_risk_acquire_history_turns: 5",

    "dev_risk_default_base_chance: 10","dev_risk_default_repeat_increase: 10","dev_risk_default_maximum_chance: 70","dev_risk_default_memory_turns: 10","",

    "dev_risk_ai_generation_enabled: false","dev_risk_ai_generation_cooldown_turns: 15","dev_risk_ai_min_confidence: 2.65","dev_risk_ai_min_observations: 3","dev_risk_ai_max_generated: 8","dev_risk_ai_debug_card_enabled: false","dev_risk_ai_max_authoring_continues: 4","dev_risk_ai_existing_coverage_min: 0.76",
    "dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns: 14","dev_risk_ai_candidate_merge_min: 0.58","dev_risk_ai_candidate_margin: 0.10","dev_risk_ai_dedupe_similarity: 0.86","dev_risk_ai_duplicate_support: 0.06","dev_risk_ai_max_candidates: 8","dev_risk_ai_unrouted_max: 24","dev_risk_ai_unrouted_attach_min: 0.58","dev_risk_ai_unrouted_margin: 0.10","dev_risk_ai_candidate_replacement_margin: 0.12",
    "# Shared response-channel / confidence-blueprint tuning",
    "dev_risk_ai_response_owner_min: 0.55","dev_risk_ai_response_channel_min: 0.30","dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_min: 0.52","dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_margin: 0.10",
    "# Parent authoring-readiness confidence and inactive-topic lifecycle",
    "dev_risk_ai_causal_coherence_bonus_max: 0.35","dev_risk_ai_recurrence_bonus_max: 0.38","dev_risk_ai_pattern_coherence_bonus_max: 0.20","dev_risk_ai_ambiguity_penalty_max: 0.35",
    "dev_risk_ai_dormant_max: 16","dev_risk_ai_dormant_compaction_factor: 0.50",
    "# Existing-track progression watch; creator-authored profiles are never auto-mutated",
    "dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns: 18","dev_risk_ai_progression_track_match_min: 0.46","dev_risk_ai_progression_novel_effect_min: 0.38","",
    "debug: true","debug_keep_turns: 5","debug_max_lines: 180","anatomy_warning_chars: 900","",
    "NPC target names belong in the BTT NPC Targets card, not here."
