function BTT_configTextHasSettings(text){return /(?:^|\n)\s*enabled\s*:|(?:^|\n)\s*dev_risk_enabled\s*:/i.test(String(text||""));}
function BTT_ensureConfigCard(){
  if(!BTT_hasStoryCards())return null;var c=BTT_findCardByTitle("Configure BTT");
  if(!c)c=BTT_createCard({title:"Configure BTT",type:"Config",keys:"",entry:BTT_configEntry(),description:BTT_configNotes()});
  if(c){
    c.title="Configure BTT";c.type="Config";if("cardType" in c)c.cardType="Config";c.keys="";
    var oldEntry=String(c.entry||""),oldDesc=String(c.description||"");
    /* Configuration cards may store settings in Entry. Preserve creator edits by
       migrating those lines into Notes once, then keep Entry deliberately short. */
    if(!BTT_configTextHasSettings(oldDesc)&&BTT_configTextHasSettings(oldEntry))oldDesc=oldEntry;
    c.entry=BTT_configEntry();
    if(!BTT_configTextHasSettings(oldDesc))c.description=BTT_configNotes();else c.description=oldDesc;
  }
  return c;
}
function BTT_getConfig(){
  var cfg={},k;for(k in BTT_DEFAULT_CONFIG)if(BTT_DEFAULT_CONFIG.hasOwnProperty(k))cfg[k]=BTT_DEFAULT_CONFIG[k];var c=BTT_ensureConfigCard();
  var configText=c?(BTT_configTextHasSettings(c.description)?String(c.description||""):String(c.entry||"")):"";
  if(configText){var lines=configText.split(/\n/g);for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++){var line=lines[i].replace(/#.*$/g,"");if(line.indexOf(":")<0)continue;var p=line.split(":"),key=BTT_cleanSpace(p.shift()),val=BTT_cleanSpace(p.join(":"));if(!cfg.hasOwnProperty(key))continue;if(typeof cfg[key]==="boolean")cfg[key]=BTT_bool(val,cfg[key]);else if(typeof cfg[key]==="number")cfg[key]=BTT_num(val,cfg[key]);else cfg[key]=val;}}
  cfg.context_mode=cfg.context_mode==="full"?"full":"excerpt";cfg.retained_turns=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.retained_turns),0,50);cfg.decay_base=BTT_clamp(cfg.decay_base,0,0.999);cfg.clause_window=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.clause_window),1,6);cfg.dosed_container_expiration_turns=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dosed_container_expiration_turns),1,100);cfg.dosed_container_revival_grace_turns=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dosed_container_revival_grace_turns),0,100);
  cfg.dev_risk_assumption_narrowness=BTT_clamp(cfg.dev_risk_assumption_narrowness,0,1);cfg.dev_risk_acquire_history_turns=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_acquire_history_turns),0,50);cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_cooldown_turns=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_cooldown_turns),1,200);cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_observations=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_observations),1,20);cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_generated=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_generated),0,100);cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_authoring_continues=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_authoring_continues),1,12);cfg.dev_risk_ai_existing_coverage_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_existing_coverage_min)||0.76,0.50,0.98);cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns),2,100);cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_merge_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_merge_min)||0.58,0,1);cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_margin=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_margin)||0.10,0,0.5);cfg.dev_risk_ai_dedupe_similarity=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dedupe_similarity)||0.86,0.5,1);cfg.dev_risk_ai_duplicate_support=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_duplicate_support)||0.06,0,0.25);cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_candidates=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_candidates),1,30);cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_max=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_max||24),4,120);cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_attach_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_attach_min)||0.58,0.25,0.95);cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_margin=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_margin)||0.10,0,0.5);cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_replacement_margin=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_replacement_margin)||0.12,0,1);cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_owner_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_owner_min)||0.55,0.40,0.95);cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_channel_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_channel_min)||0.30,0.10,0.80);cfg.dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_min)||0.52,0.30,0.90);cfg.dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_margin=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_blueprint_track_margin)||0.10,0,0.40);cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns=BTT_clamp(Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns||18),3,100);cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_track_match_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_track_match_min)||0.46,0.20,0.90);cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_novel_effect_min=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_novel_effect_min)||0.38,0.15,0.90);
  return cfg;
}

function BTT_targetConfigEntry(){return ["BTT NPC Targets","","Use this card to identify AI-introduced people that BTT is allowed to track as NPCs.","","Write target names in the NOTES/DESCRIPTION field as a plain comma-separated list.","","Example:","Mark, Anthony, Richard, Jessica","","Listed names are permissions only. BTT still requires accepted identity/body/event evidence before creating generated BTT cards.","","Character NPC story cards remain eligible automatically."].join("\n");}
function BTT_ensureTargetCard(){ if(!BTT_hasStoryCards())return null;var c=BTT_findCardByTitle("BTT NPC Targets");if(!c)c=BTT_createCard({title:"BTT NPC Targets",type:"Config",keys:"",entry:BTT_targetConfigEntry(),description:""});if(c){c.title="BTT NPC Targets";c.type="Config";if("cardType" in c)c.cardType="Config";c.keys="";c.entry=BTT_targetConfigEntry();if(typeof c.description!=="string")c.description="";}return c; }

function BTT_safeName(name){
  name=BTT_cleanSpace(name);if(name.length<2||name.length>60)return "";if(!/^[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\-]*(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\-]*){0,2}$/.test(name))return "";
  if(/^(?:You|Your|He|Him|His|She|Her|They|Them|Their|Team|Office|Coffee|Tea|Water|Drink|Glass|Cup|Mug|Bottle|Can|Pill|Capsule|Dose|Player|Configure|Anatomy|Appearance|Instant|Change|BTT)$/i.test(name))return "";
  return name;
}
function BTT_targetNames(){ var c=BTT_ensureTargetCard(),out=[];if(c){var p=String(c.description||"").slice(0,2000).split(",");for(var i=0;i<p.length;i++){var n=BTT_safeName(p[i]);if(n)out.push(n);}}return BTT_unique(out); }

function BTT_isBTTCard(card){ var d=String(card&&card.description||"");return /BTT (?:SOURCE ENTITY|AI-FACING ENTITY):/i.test(d)||/^BTT\b/i.test(String(card&&card.title||""))||/^BTT\b/i.test(String(card&&card.type||"")); }
/**
 * Returns all human-authored text fields that can carry a Character-card
 * baseline. AI Dungeon scenarios commonly store the visible prose in `value`,
 * while creator metadata lives in `description`; both are first-class inputs.
 */
function BTT_characterCardText(card){
  if(!card)return "";return [card.value,card.entry,card.description].map(function(x){return String(x||"");}).filter(Boolean).join("\n");
}
/**
 * Parses explicit BTT Identity metadata from a Character card. Baseline gender
 * and Heritage are high-authority initialization evidence, but Heritage still
 * must resolve to an available BTT Heritage profile before it becomes canon.
 */
function BTT_characterBaselineMetadata(card){
  var raw=BTT_characterCardText(card),out={gender:"",heritage:""},m;
  m=raw.match(/\bbaseline_gender\s*=\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z _-]*)/i);if(m)out.gender=BTT_normId(m[1]);
  m=raw.match(/\bbaseline_heritage\s*=\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9 _-]*)/i);if(m)out.heritage=BTT_normId(m[1]);
  return out;
}
/**
 * Imports only stable, representable physical details from a Character card.
 * This intentionally does not run the ordinary narrative transformation parser:
 * creator profile prose is baseline evidence, not an event, so verbs such as
 * "grow" or incidental short-term descriptions must never mutate axes here.
 */
function BTT_applyCharacterCardBaselineDetails(meta,card){
  if(!meta||!card||!meta.appearance)return;var raw=BTT_characterCardText(card),m;
  function field(key,value){value=BTT_cleanSpace(value);if(!value||meta.appearance.fields[key])return;meta.appearance.fields[key]=value;BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"CHARACTER BASELINE FIELD | "+key+"="+value+" | source=character_card");}
  m=raw.match(/\b(pale|olive|tan|tanned|brown|dark|fair|ebony|porcelain|warm brown|light brown|deep brown)\s+skin\b/i);if(m)field("skin",BTT_lower(m[1]));
  m=raw.match(/\b(blond|blonde|black|brown|red|auburn|white|silver|pink|blue|green|purple|gray|grey)\s+(?:hair|locks)\b/i);if(m)field("hair_color",BTT_lower(m[1]).replace("grey","gray"));
  m=raw.match(/\b(bald|shaved|cropped|close-cropped|short|shoulder-length|medium|long|very long|waist-length)\s+(?:[a-z-]+\s+)?(?:hair|locks)\b/i);if(m)field("hair_length",BTT_normalizeHairLength(BTT_lower(m[1])));
  m=raw.match(/\b(blue|green|brown|hazel|gray|grey|silver|gold|amber|red|pink|purple|black)\s+eyes?\b/i);if(m)field("eye_color",BTT_lower(m[1]).replace("grey","gray"));
  m=raw.match(/\b(broad|slender|narrow|lean|stocky|athletic|curvy|petite|muscular|fit)\s+(?:build|frame)\b/i);if(m)field("build",BTT_lower(m[1])+" build");
  /* Explicit creator-facing numeric/body-size descriptions are safe baseline
     values because they name the current state rather than a transformation. */
  m=raw.match(/\b([A-U]{1,2})\s*-?cup\b/i);if(m){var cup=BTT_cupToAxis(m[1]);if(cup!==null){var oldB=meta.appearance.axes.breast_size;meta.appearance.axes.breast_size=cup;BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"CHARACTER BASELINE AXIS | breast_size | "+oldB+"→"+cup+" | source=character_card");}}
  m=raw.match(/\b(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:-|\s*)inch(?:es)?\s+(?:penis|cock|shaft)\b/i);if(m){var inches=Number(m[1]);if(isFinite(inches)&&inches>=0){var oldC=meta.appearance.axes.cock_size,newC=inches*5;meta.appearance.axes.cock_size=newC;BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"CHARACTER BASELINE AXIS | cock_size | "+oldC+"→"+newC+" | source=character_card");}}
}
function BTT_characterCardSources(){
  var out=[];if(!Array.isArray(storyCards))return out;for(var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++){var c=storyCards[i];if(!c||BTT_isBTTCard(c))continue;var type=BTT_lower(c.type||c.cardType),title=BTT_cleanSpace(c.title||"");if(type.indexOf("character")===-1)continue;var n=BTT_safeName(title);if(!n)continue;var base=BTT_characterBaselineMetadata(c),keyAliases=[];
    /* Only proper-name-shaped key phrases become aliases. Lowercase role and
       occupation keywords remain trigger metadata and cannot become identities. */
    var keyParts=String(c.keys||"").split(/[,;|]/g);for(var k=0;k<keyParts.length;k++){var alias=BTT_safeName(BTT_cleanSpace(keyParts[k]));if(alias)keyAliases.push(alias);}
    out.push({name:n,aliases:BTT_unique([n,n.split(/\s+/)[0]].concat(keyAliases)),card:c,baselineGender:base.gender,baselineHeritage:base.heritage});}return out;
}
function BTT_playerIdentityNames(extraName){var p=BTT_findEntity("Player")||BTT_getEntity("Player"),a=["Player","you","your","yourself"];if(p&&p.identity)a=a.concat(p.identity.aliases||[],[p.identity.currentName,p.identity.originalName]);if(extraName)a.push(extraName);return BTT_unique(a).filter(function(x){return x&&!/^(?:Player|you|your|yourself)$/i.test(x);});}
function BTT_nameMatchesPlayerIdentity(name,extraName){var n=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(name));if(!n)return false;var a=BTT_playerIdentityNames(extraName);for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(a[i]))===n)return true;return false;}
function BTT_admittedNPCSources(playerHint){
  var out=[],seen={},cards=BTT_characterCardSources(),targets=BTT_targetNames(),i;
  for(i=0;i<cards.length;i++){if(BTT_nameMatchesPlayerIdentity(cards[i].name,playerHint))continue;var e="NPC:"+cards[i].name,k=BTT_lower(e);if(seen[k])continue;seen[k]=1;out.push({entity:e,name:cards[i].name,aliases:BTT_unique(cards[i].aliases),card:cards[i].card,carded:true,baselineGender:cards[i].baselineGender||"",baselineHeritage:cards[i].baselineHeritage||""});}
  for(i=0;i<targets.length;i++){if(BTT_nameMatchesPlayerIdentity(targets[i],playerHint))continue;var e2="NPC:"+targets[i],k2=BTT_lower(e2);if(seen[k2])continue;seen[k2]=1;out.push({entity:e2,name:targets[i],aliases:BTT_unique([targets[i],targets[i].split(/\s+/)[0]]),card:null,carded:false});}
  return out;
}

function BTT_roleMarker(entity,role){return "BTT SOURCE ENTITY: "+entity+"\nBTT SOURCE ROLE: "+role;}
function BTT_aiMarker(entity){return "BTT AI-FACING ENTITY: "+entity;}
function BTT_cardRole(card){var d=String(card&&card.description||"");var m=d.match(/BTT SOURCE ROLE:\s*(anatomy|appearance|instantchange)/i);if(m)return BTT_lower(m[1]);if(/BTT AI-FACING ENTITY:/i.test(d))return "ai";return "";}
function BTT_cardEntity(card){var d=String(card&&card.description||"");var m=d.match(/BTT (?:SOURCE|AI-FACING) ENTITY:\s*([^\n]+)/i);return m?BTT_cleanSpace(m[1]):"";}
/**
 * Exact Player-as-NPC collision cleanup. This only removes an NPC namespace
 * whose canonical suffix matches the resolved Player current/original name.
 * It is intentionally narrower than generic same-name handling.
 */
function BTT_cleanupPlayerDuplicateNPCs(player,reason){
  player=player||BTT_findEntity("Player");if(!player||!player.identity)return 0;var names=BTT_unique([player.identity.currentName,player.identity.originalName]),s=BTT_ensureState(),removed=0;
  for(var ni=0;ni<names.length;ni++){var name=BTT_cleanSpace(names[ni]);if(!name||/^Player$/i.test(name))continue;var entity="NPC:"+name,meta=s.entities&&s.entities[entity];if(!meta)continue;var exact=BTT_lower(BTT_entityName(entity,meta))===BTT_lower(name)||BTT_lower(entity.replace(/^NPC:/,""))===BTT_lower(name);if(!exact)continue;
    if(Array.isArray(storyCards)){for(var i=storyCards.length-1;i>=0;i--){var c=storyCards[i];if(c&&BTT_cardEntity(c)===entity){BTT_removeCard(c);removed++;}}}
    delete s.entities[entity];if(s.discourse){if(s.discourse.recentNPC===entity)s.discourse.recentNPC="";if(s.discourse.recentEntity===entity)s.discourse.recentEntity="";if(s.discourse.recentSpeaker===entity)s.discourse.recentSpeaker="";}BTT_touchDebug(player,"identity","ENTITY COLLISION SUPPRESSED | candidate="+entity+" | resolved=Player | reason="+String(reason||"character_card_matches_player_identity"));
  }return removed;
}
function BTT_roleTitle(role){if(role==="anatomy")return "Anatomy BTT";if(role==="appearance")return "Appearance BTT";if(role==="instantchange")return "Instant-Change BTT";return "AI-Facing BTT";}
function BTT_cardType(entity,meta){var name=BTT_entityName(entity,meta);return entity==="Player"?("BTTC "+name):("BTTC "+name);}
function BTT_findRoleCards(entity,role){var out=[];if(!Array.isArray(storyCards))return out;for(var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++)if(storyCards[i]&&BTT_cardEntity(storyCards[i])===entity&&BTT_cardRole(storyCards[i])===role)out.push(storyCards[i]);return out;}
function BTT_ensureEntityCardSet(entity,meta){
  if(!BTT_hasStoryCards())return null;var roles=["anatomy","appearance","instantchange","ai"],set={};
  for(var r=0;r<roles.length;r++){
    var role=roles[r],cards=BTT_findRoleCards(entity,role),c=cards.length?cards[0]:null,created=false;for(var x=1;x<cards.length;x++)BTT_removeCard(cards[x]);
    if(!c){c=BTT_createCard({title:BTT_roleTitle(role),type:BTT_cardType(entity,meta),keys:"",entry:"",description:role==="ai"?BTT_aiMarker(entity):BTT_roleMarker(entity,role)});created=true;}
    if(c){c.title=BTT_roleTitle(role);c.type=BTT_cardType(entity,meta);if("cardType" in c)c.cardType=c.type;
      /* Existing descriptions are preserved during preflight because Anatomy,
         Appearance and lifecycle Notes are import sources. Only final
         serialization is allowed to overwrite them. */
      if(created||!String(c.description||"").trim())c.description=role==="ai"?BTT_aiMarker(entity):BTT_roleMarker(entity,role);if(role!=="ai"&&created)c.keys="";
    }set[role]=c;
  }return set;
}

/* ============================================================================
   4. STORY-CARD PROFILE PARSING
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   BTT Gender and BTT Heritage cards define the scenario's available phenotype
   space and Instant-Change products. The parser accepts both JSON-oriented cards
   and the established human-readable field format. Profile data is reusable;
   profile cards themselves never become narrative context sources.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_profileKind(card){
  var blob=[card&&card.type,card&&card.cardType,card&&card.title,card&&card.keys].join(" ");
  if(/\bBTT\s+Heritage\b/i.test(blob))return "heritage";
  if(/\bBTT\s+Genders?\b/i.test(blob)||/\bBTT\s+Gender\b/i.test(blob))return "gender";
  return "";
}
function BTT_profileSourceText(card){return [card&&card.value,card&&card.entry,card&&card.description].map(function(x){return String(x||"");}).join("\n");}
function BTT_jsonObject(s){try{var o=JSON.parse(String(s||""));return o&&typeof o==="object"&&!Array.isArray(o)?o:null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
function BTT_lineValue(text,names){text=String(text||"");for(var i=0;i<names.length;i++){var re=new RegExp("^\\s*"+names[i]+"\\s*:\\s*(.+)$","im"),m=text.match(re);if(m)return BTT_cleanSpace(m[1]);}return "";}
function BTT_parseAxisSpec(spec){
  /* Creator axis grammar. The operation belongs outside a parenthesized range:
       19-21      absolute roll
       -10-10     absolute negative-to-positive roll
       +(14-24)   add rolled magnitude
       -(45-55)   subtract rolled magnitude
       =(35-55)   replace with rolled value
       +12 / -8   scalar add/subtract
       =0 / 20    scalar replacement
     Explicit negative literal ranges may also use "to" or a comma. */
  var raw=BTT_cleanSpace(spec);if(!raw)return null;
  var op="",body=raw,m;
  m=raw.match(/^([+=-])\s*\((.*)\)$/);if(m){op=m[1];body=BTT_cleanSpace(m[2]);}
  else if(/^=/.test(raw)){op="=";body=BTT_cleanSpace(raw.slice(1));}
  else if(/^\+/.test(raw)){op="+";body=BTT_cleanSpace(raw.slice(1));}
  else if(/^-(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/.test(raw)){op="-";body=raw;}
  else if(/^-(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*-\s*(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/.test(raw)){op="=";body=raw;}

  m=body.match(/^(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:,|\bto\b)\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/i);
  if(!m)m=body.match(/^(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*-\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/);
  if(m){var lo=Number(m[1]),hi=Number(m[2]);if(!isFinite(lo)||!isFinite(hi))return null;if(hi<lo){var t=lo;lo=hi;hi=t;}return {op:op||"=",min:lo,max:hi,raw:raw};}
  m=body.match(/^(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/);if(!m)return null;var n=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(n))return null;
  return {op:op||"=",min:n,max:n,raw:raw};
}
function BTT_parseDurationSpec(v){
  v=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(v));if(!v||/^(?:permanent|perm|none|false|no|0)$/.test(v))return null;var m=v.match(/(\d+)\s*-\s*(\d+)/);if(m)return {min:Math.max(1,Number(m[1])),max:Math.max(1,Number(m[2]))};m=v.match(/(\d+)/);if(m)return {min:Math.max(1,Number(m[1])),max:Math.max(1,Number(m[1]))};return null;
}
/**
 * Parses one creator-facing duration modifier value.
 * `permanent`, `none`, and zero mean no timed reversion. Positive numbers are
 * normalized to whole days. The modifier belongs to an already-resolved product
 * and never participates in product identity scoring.
 */
function BTT_parseDurationModifierValue(raw){
  var s=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(raw));if(!s)return null;
  if(/^(?:permanent|perm|permanently|none|no\s+reversion|no\s+return|0)(?:\s+days?)?$/.test(s))return {timedDays:null,permanent:true};
  var m=s.match(/(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/);if(!m)return null;var n=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(n)||n<=0)return null;
  return {timedDays:Math.max(1,Math.floor(n)),permanent:false};
}
/**
 * Normalizes creator-defined product duration modifiers. Plain-text cards use:
 *   Duration Modifiers: Basic = 1 day; X-tra Strength / Xtra Strength = 30 days
 * Object profiles may provide the same information as an array of records.
 */
function BTT_parseDurationModifiers(raw){
  if(Array.isArray(raw)){
    var normalized=[];for(var ai=0;ai<raw.length;ai++){var item=raw[ai]||{},aliases=[];
      if(Array.isArray(item.aliases))aliases=item.aliases.slice();else if(item.alias!==undefined)aliases=[item.alias];else if(item.name!==undefined)aliases=[item.name];
      aliases=BTT_unique(aliases);var value=item.permanent===true?{timedDays:null,permanent:true}:BTT_parseDurationModifierValue(item.timedDays!==undefined?item.timedDays:(item.duration!==undefined?item.duration:item.timed));
      if(!aliases.length||!value)continue;normalized.push({label:String(item.label||aliases[0]),aliases:aliases,timedDays:value.timedDays,permanent:value.permanent});
    }return normalized;
  }
  raw=BTT_cleanSpace(raw);if(!raw)return [];var parts=String(raw).split(/\s*;\s*/g),out=[];
  for(var i=0;i<parts.length;i++){var m=parts[i].match(/^(.+?)\s*(?:=|->|→|:)\s*(.+)$/);if(!m)continue;
    var aliases=String(m[1]).split(/\s*(?:\/|\|)\s*/g).map(BTT_cleanSpace).filter(Boolean),v=BTT_parseDurationModifierValue(m[2]);if(!aliases.length||!v)continue;
    out.push({label:aliases[0],aliases:BTT_unique(aliases),timedDays:v.timedDays,permanent:v.permanent});
  }return out;
}
/**
 * Returns a modifier only when its phrase is locally bound to the resolved
 * product phrase. This prevents unrelated words such as "plus" elsewhere in a
 * sentence from changing a product's duration.
 */
function BTT_resolveDurationModifier(profile,raw,trigger){
  if(!profile)return null;var mods=BTT_parseDurationModifiers(profile.duration_modifiers||profile.durationModifiers||[]);if(!mods.length)return null;
  raw=String(raw||"");var anchors=BTT_unique([trigger,profile.name].concat(profile.instant_change_detection||[])),best=null;
  function ranges(term){var out=[],re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(term).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig"),m;while((m=re.exec(raw))){out.push({start:m.index,end:m.index+m[0].length});if(!m[0].length)re.lastIndex++;}return out;}
  for(var mi=0;mi<mods.length;mi++)for(var aa=0;aa<mods[mi].aliases.length;aa++)for(var an=0;an<anchors.length;an++){
    if(!mods[mi].aliases[aa]||!anchors[an])continue;var ar=ranges(anchors[an]),br=ranges(mods[mi].aliases[aa]);
    for(var x=0;x<ar.length;x++)for(var y=0;y<br.length;y++){var left=ar[x].start<=br[y].start?ar[x]:br[y],right=ar[x].start<=br[y].start?br[y]:ar[x],gap=Math.max(0,right.start-left.end);
      if(gap>32)continue;var between=raw.slice(left.end,right.start);if(/[.!?;,\n:]/.test(between))continue;
      var glue=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(between)).replace(/\b(?:pill|capsule|tablet|dose|product|formula|formulation|variant|version|edition|strength|x-change|xchange|the|a|an|of)\b/g,"").replace(/\s+/g,"");
      if(glue)continue;var score=1.0-Math.min(0.35,gap/100)+(BTT_lower(anchors[an])===BTT_lower(trigger)?0.08:0);
      if(!best||score>best.score)best={label:mods[mi].label,alias:mods[mi].aliases[aa],timedDays:mods[mi].timedDays,permanent:mods[mi].permanent,score:score,bindingGap:gap};
    }
  }return best;
}

function BTT_parseDeltaTriggers(raw,defaultDelta){var out=[],parts=BTT_splitList(raw);for(var i=0;i<parts.length;i++){var p=parts[i],phrase=p,days=defaultDelta,m=p.match(/^(.+?)\s*[:=]\s*([+-]?\d+)\s*(?:days?)?$/i);if(m){phrase=BTT_cleanSpace(m[1]);days=Number(m[2]);}else{m=p.match(/^(.+?)\s+([+-]?\d+)\s*(?:days?)?$/i);if(m){phrase=BTT_cleanSpace(m[1]);days=Number(m[2]);}}if(phrase)out.push({trigger:phrase,days:isFinite(days)?Math.floor(days):defaultDelta});}return out;}
function BTT_normalizeRiskTrigger(t){
  /* `role` is optional creator metadata for causal triggers. Reproductive
     trigger families default to receiver-role validation when omitted; ordinary
     lexical triggers do not require participant-role validation. */
  if(typeof t==="string")return {terms:[BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(t))],threshold:0.76,context:[],requireContext:false,chanceBonus:0,role:""};
  t=t||{};var role=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(t.role||t.eventRole||t.participantRole||""));if(["receiver","source","participant","any"].indexOf(role)<0)role="";
  return {terms:BTT_splitList(t.terms||t.aliases||t.trigger||t.phrase).map(BTT_lower),threshold:BTT_clamp(t.threshold===undefined?0.76:t.threshold,0,1),context:BTT_splitList(t.context||t.contextTerms||t.boosts).map(BTT_lower),requireContext:!!t.requireContext,chanceBonus:Number(t.chanceBonus)||0,role:role};
}
function BTT_normalizeRiskOutcome(o){
  o=o||{};var type=BTT_lower(o.type||"traits"),target=BTT_lower(o.target||o.destination||"current");if(target==="snapshot")target="reversion_snapshot";if(["current","reversion_snapshot","both"].indexOf(target)<0)target="current";
  if(type==="buildshift"||type==="build-shift")return {type:"buildShift",steps:Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(o.steps)||1)),successAt:Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(o.successAt)||1)),target:target};
  return {type:"traits",slot:BTT_lower(o.slot||"head"),traits:BTT_splitList(o.traits||o.trait).map(function(x){return BTT_lower(x).replace(/\s+/g,"-");}),successAt:Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(o.successAt)||1)),target:target};
}
function BTT_parseRiskTracks(raw){
  var arr=Array.isArray(raw)?raw:(raw&&typeof raw==="object"?[raw]:[]),out=[];for(var i=0;i<arr.length;i++){var r=arr[i]||{},id=BTT_normId(r.id||r.name||("risk-"+i));if(!id)continue;var ts=Array.isArray(r.triggers)?r.triggers:(r.triggers?[r.triggers]:[]),tr=[];for(var t=0;t<ts.length;t++){var nt=BTT_normalizeRiskTrigger(ts[t]);if(nt.terms.length)tr.push(nt);}var os=Array.isArray(r.outcomes)?r.outcomes:(r.outcomes?[r.outcomes]:[]),outs=[];for(var o=0;o<os.length;o++)outs.push(BTT_normalizeRiskOutcome(os[o]));var ps=Array.isArray(r.progression)?r.progression:(r.progression?[r.progression]:[]),prog=[];for(var p=0;p<ps.length;p++)prog.push(BTT_normalizeRiskOutcome(ps[p]));out.push({id:id,triggers:tr,baseChance:BTT_clamp(r.baseChance===undefined?5:r.baseChance,0,100),repeatIncrease:BTT_clamp(r.repeatIncrease===undefined?5:r.repeatIncrease,0,100),maximumChance:BTT_clamp(r.maximumChance===undefined?60:r.maximumChance,0,100),memoryTurns:Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(r.memoryTurns)||12)),outcomes:outs,progression:prog,requiresRecentTrack:String(r.requiresRecentTrack||""),withinTurns:Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(r.withinTurns)||0)),passive:r.passive&&typeof r.passive==="object"?BTT_clone(r.passive):null,notes:String(r.notes||"")});}return out;
}


function BTT_profileBlank(kind,name){
  return {
    kind:kind,id:BTT_normId(name),name:BTT_cleanSpace(name),aliases:[],enabled:true,init_detection_enabled:true,
    manufacturer:"",knockoff:false,target_gender:"",fields:{},axes:{},slots:{},slot_markers:{},
    heritage_traits:[],gender_traits:[],pronouns:"",instant_change_detection:[],duration:null,duration_modifiers:[],
    extend_time_conditions:[],reduce_time_triggers:[],permanency_triggers:[],reversion_target:"",
    reversal:false,reversal_detection:[],risk_tracks:[]
  };
}
function BTT_profileSlotValue(raw){
  var parts=BTT_splitList(raw),marker=parts.length?BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(parts.shift())):"",traits=[];
  marker=marker.replace(/\s+/g,"");
  for(var i=0;i<parts.length;i++){var t=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(parts[i])).replace(/\s+/g,"-");if(t)traits.push(t);}
  return {marker:marker,traits:traits};
}
function BTT_profileFromPlain(text,kind,fallbackTitle){
  text=String(text||"");var lines=text.split(/\n/g),first="";for(var z=0;z<lines.length;z++){var q=BTT_cleanSpace(lines[z]);if(q&&!/^\s*[A-Za-z][A-Za-z _-]*\s*:/.test(q)){first=q;break;}}
  var name=first.replace(/^BTT\s+(?:Heritage|Genders?|Gender)\s*-?\s*/i,"")||String(fallbackTitle||"").replace(/^BTT\s+(?:Heritage|Genders?|Gender)\s*-?\s*/i,"");
  var p=BTT_profileBlank(kind,name);if(!p.id)return null;p._present={};
  function present(field,names){for(var ni=0;ni<names.length;ni++)if(new RegExp("^\\s*"+names[ni]+"\\s*:","im").test(text)){p._present[field]=true;return true;}return false;}
  p.aliases=BTT_unique([p.name,p.id].concat(BTT_splitList(BTT_lineValue(text,["Aliases?"]))));present("aliases",["Aliases?"]);
  var enabled=BTT_lineValue(text,["Enabled"]),disabled=BTT_lineValue(text,["Disabled"]);if(present("enabled",["Enabled"]))p.enabled=BTT_bool(enabled,true);if(present("disabled",["Disabled"])&&BTT_bool(disabled,false))p.enabled=false;
  var init=BTT_lineValue(text,["Init\\s+Detection\\s+Enabled","Initialization\\s+Detection\\s+Enabled","Use\\s+For\\s+Init","Init\\s+Enabled"]);if(present("init_detection_enabled",["Init\\s+Detection\\s+Enabled","Initialization\\s+Detection\\s+Enabled","Use\\s+For\\s+Init","Init\\s+Enabled"]))p.init_detection_enabled=BTT_bool(init,true);
  if(present("manufacturer",["Manufacturer","Maker","Brand"]))p.manufacturer=BTT_lineValue(text,["Manufacturer","Maker","Brand"]);
  if(present("knockoff",["Knockoff","Knock\\s+Off"]))p.knockoff=BTT_bool(BTT_lineValue(text,["Knockoff","Knock\\s+Off"]),false);
  if(present("pronouns",["Pronouns?"]))p.pronouns=BTT_pronounSet(BTT_lineValue(text,["Pronouns?"]));
  if(present("target_gender",["Target\\s+Gender","Gender\\s+Target"]))p.target_gender=BTT_normId(BTT_lineValue(text,["Target\\s+Gender","Gender\\s+Target"]));
  var slots=["head","arms","legs","breasts","gut","penis","vagina","ass","tail","wings","overall"];
  for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++){var m=lines[i].match(/^\s*([A-Za-z_ ]+)\s*:\s*(.*)$/);if(!m)continue;var key=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(m[1])),val=BTT_cleanSpace(m[2]);if(slots.indexOf(key)>=0){var sv=BTT_profileSlotValue(val);p.slot_markers[key]=sv.marker;if(key!=="overall")p.slots[key]={present:sv.marker,traits:sv.traits};p._present["slot:"+key]=true;}}
  if(present("heritage_traits",["Heritage\\s+Trait","Heritage\\s+Traits"]))p.heritage_traits=BTT_splitList(BTT_lineValue(text,["Heritage\\s+Trait","Heritage\\s+Traits"])).map(function(x){return BTT_lower(x).replace(/\s+/g,"-");});
  if(present("gender_traits",["Gender\\s+Trait","Gender\\s+Traits"]))p.gender_traits=BTT_splitList(BTT_lineValue(text,["Gender\\s+Trait","Gender\\s+Traits"])).map(function(x){return BTT_lower(x).replace(/\s+/g,"-");});
  var axesLine=BTT_lineValue(text,["Axes?"]);if(present("axes",["Axes?"])&&axesLine){var pairs=axesLine.split(/\s*,\s*(?=[a-z_ ]+\s*:)/i);for(var a=0;a<pairs.length;a++){var mm=pairs[a].match(/^([a-z_ ]+)\s*:\s*(.+)$/i);if(mm){var ak=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(mm[1])).replace(/\s+/g,"_");p.axes[ak]=BTT_cleanSpace(mm[2]);}}}
  var fieldDefs=[["skin_options","Skin"],["hair_color_options","Hair\\s+Color"],["hair_length_options","Hair\\s+Length"],["eye_color_options","Eye\\s+Color"],["build_options","Build"],["scent_options","Scent"]];
  for(var fd=0;fd<fieldDefs.length;fd++){if(present("field:"+fieldDefs[fd][0],[fieldDefs[fd][1]]))p.fields[fieldDefs[fd][0]]=BTT_lineValue(text,[fieldDefs[fd][1]]);}
  if(present("instant_change_detection",["Instant\\s+Change\\s+Detection","Immediate\\s+Change\\s+Detection","Immediate\\s+Swap\\s+Triggers?","Swap\\s+Triggers?"]))p.instant_change_detection=BTT_splitList(BTT_lineValue(text,["Instant\\s+Change\\s+Detection","Immediate\\s+Change\\s+Detection","Immediate\\s+Swap\\s+Triggers?","Swap\\s+Triggers?"]));
  if(present("swap_mode",["Swap\\s+Mode","Immediate\\s+Swap\\s+Mode"]))p.swap_mode=BTT_lower(BTT_lineValue(text,["Swap\\s+Mode","Immediate\\s+Swap\\s+Mode"]));
  if(present("swap_strength",["Swap\\s+Strength","Immediate\\s+Swap\\s+Strength"]))p.swap_strength=BTT_num(BTT_lineValue(text,["Swap\\s+Strength","Immediate\\s+Swap\\s+Strength"]),0);
  if(present("duration",["Timed","Duration","Duration\\s+Days","Time"]))p.duration=BTT_parseDurationSpec(BTT_lineValue(text,["Timed","Duration","Duration\\s+Days","Time"]));
  if(present("duration_modifiers",["Duration\\s+Modifiers?"]))p.duration_modifiers=BTT_parseDurationModifiers(BTT_lineValue(text,["Duration\\s+Modifiers?"]));
  if(present("extend_time_conditions",["Extend\\s+Time\\s+Conditions?","Extend\\s+Time\\s+Triggers?","Time\\s+Extension\\s+Triggers?"]))p.extend_time_conditions=BTT_parseDeltaTriggers(BTT_lineValue(text,["Extend\\s+Time\\s+Conditions?","Extend\\s+Time\\s+Triggers?","Time\\s+Extension\\s+Triggers?"]),1);
  if(present("reduce_time_triggers",["Reduce\\s+Time\\s+Triggers?","Reduce\\s+Time\\s+Conditions?","Time\\s+Reduction\\s+Triggers?"]))p.reduce_time_triggers=BTT_parseDeltaTriggers(BTT_lineValue(text,["Reduce\\s+Time\\s+Triggers?","Reduce\\s+Time\\s+Conditions?","Time\\s+Reduction\\s+Triggers?"]),-1);
  if(present("permanency_triggers",["Permanency\\s+Triggers?","Permanent\\s+Triggers?","Make\\s+Permanent\\s+Triggers?"]))p.permanency_triggers=BTT_splitList(BTT_lineValue(text,["Permanency\\s+Triggers?","Permanent\\s+Triggers?","Make\\s+Permanent\\s+Triggers?"]));
  if(present("reversion_target",["Reversion\\s+Target","Return\\s+To","Revert\\s+To"]))p.reversion_target=BTT_lineValue(text,["Reversion\\s+Target","Return\\s+To","Revert\\s+To"]);
  if(present("opposite_aliases",["Opposite\\s+Gender\\s+Aliases?","Opposite\\s+Aliases?"]))p.opposite_aliases=BTT_splitList(BTT_lineValue(text,["Opposite\\s+Gender\\s+Aliases?","Opposite\\s+Aliases?"]));
  if(present("reversal",["Reversal","Reversion\\s+Product","Reversal\\s+Product"]))p.reversal=BTT_bool(BTT_lineValue(text,["Reversal","Reversion\\s+Product","Reversal\\s+Product"]),false);
  if(present("reversal_detection",["Reversal\\s+Detection","Reversion\\s+Detection"]))p.reversal_detection=BTT_splitList(BTT_lineValue(text,["Reversal\\s+Detection","Reversion\\s+Detection"]));
  var riskJson=BTT_lineValue(text,["Risk\\s+Tracks\\s+JSON"]);if(present("risk_tracks",["Risk\\s+Tracks\\s+JSON"])){try{p.risk_tracks=BTT_parseRiskTracks(JSON.parse(riskJson));}catch(_r){p.risk_tracks=[];}}
  return p;
}
function BTT_profileFromObject(obj,kind,fallbackTitle){
  if(!obj||typeof obj!=="object")return null;var name=String(obj.name||obj.id||obj.key||fallbackTitle||""),p=BTT_profileBlank(kind,name);p.id=BTT_normId(obj.id||obj.key||name);if(!p.id)return null;
  p.aliases=BTT_unique([p.name,p.id].concat(Array.isArray(obj.aliases)?obj.aliases:BTT_splitList(obj.aliases)));
  p.enabled=obj.enabled===undefined?!BTT_bool(obj.disabled,false):BTT_bool(obj.enabled,true);
  p.init_detection_enabled=obj.init_detection_enabled===undefined?(obj.initDetectionEnabled===undefined?true:BTT_bool(obj.initDetectionEnabled,true)):BTT_bool(obj.init_detection_enabled,true);
  p.manufacturer=String(obj.manufacturer||obj.maker||obj.brand||"");p.knockoff=BTT_bool(obj.knockoff,false);p.pronouns=BTT_pronounSet(obj.pronouns||"");p.target_gender=BTT_normId(obj.target_gender||obj.targetGender||obj.gender_target||obj.genderTarget||"");
  p.fields=BTT_clone(obj.fields||{});p.axes=BTT_clone(obj.axis_operations||obj.axis_values||obj.axes||{});p.slots=BTT_clone(obj.slots||{});p.slot_markers=BTT_clone(obj.slot_markers||obj.slotMarkers||{});
  p.heritage_traits=BTT_splitList(obj.heritage_traits||obj.heritage_trait).map(function(x){return BTT_lower(x).replace(/\s+/g,"-");});p.gender_traits=BTT_splitList(obj.gender_traits||obj.gender_trait).map(function(x){return BTT_lower(x).replace(/\s+/g,"-");});
  p.instant_change_detection=BTT_unique(BTT_splitList(obj.instant_change_detection||obj.instantChangeDetection).concat(BTT_splitList(obj.immediate_swap_triggers||obj.immediateSwapTriggers||obj.swap_triggers||obj.swapTriggers)));
  p.swap_mode=BTT_lower(obj.swap_mode||obj.swapMode||"");p.swap_strength=BTT_num(obj.swap_strength||obj.swapStrength,0);
  p.duration=BTT_parseDurationSpec(obj.timed!==undefined?obj.timed:(obj.duration!==undefined?obj.duration:(obj.duration_days!==undefined?obj.duration_days:obj.durationDays)));
  p.duration_modifiers=BTT_parseDurationModifiers(obj.duration_modifiers||obj.durationModifiers||[]);
  p.extend_time_conditions=Array.isArray(obj.extend_time_conditions)?BTT_clone(obj.extend_time_conditions):BTT_parseDeltaTriggers(obj.extendTimeConditions||"",1);
  p.reduce_time_triggers=Array.isArray(obj.reduce_time_triggers)?BTT_clone(obj.reduce_time_triggers):BTT_parseDeltaTriggers(obj.reduceTimeTriggers||"",-1);
  p.permanency_triggers=BTT_splitList(obj.permanency_triggers||obj.permanencyTriggers);p.reversion_target=String(obj.reversion_target||obj.reversionTarget||obj.return_to||"");p.opposite_aliases=BTT_splitList(obj.opposite_aliases||obj.oppositeAliases||obj.opposite_gender_aliases||obj.oppositeGenderAliases);
  p.reversal=BTT_bool(obj.reversal||obj.reversal_product||obj.reversalProduct,false);p.reversal_detection=BTT_splitList(obj.reversal_detection||obj.reversalDetection);p.risk_tracks=BTT_parseRiskTracks(obj.risk_tracks||obj.riskTracks||[]);
  for(var slot in p.slots)if(p.slots.hasOwnProperty(slot)){var s0=p.slots[slot];if(typeof s0==="string"){var sv=BTT_profileSlotValue(s0);p.slots[slot]={present:sv.marker,traits:sv.traits};p.slot_markers[slot]=sv.marker;}else if(s0&&typeof s0==="object"){if(!p.slot_markers[slot]&&s0.present!==undefined)p.slot_markers[slot]=String(s0.present);if(!Array.isArray(s0.traits))s0.traits=BTT_splitList(s0.traits);}}
  return p;
}
/**
 * Normalizes JSON and human-readable profile cards into the same internal shape.
 * Plain-text fields overlay parsed JSON only when the field is explicitly present,
 * so absent creator-facing fields cannot replace values already read from JSON.
 */
function BTT_overlayPlainProfile(base,extra){
  if(!base)return extra;if(!extra)return base;var pr=extra._present||{};
  if(pr.aliases)base.aliases=BTT_unique(base.aliases.concat(extra.aliases||[]));if(pr.enabled||pr.disabled)base.enabled=extra.enabled;if(pr.init_detection_enabled)base.init_detection_enabled=extra.init_detection_enabled;if(pr.manufacturer)base.manufacturer=extra.manufacturer;if(pr.knockoff)base.knockoff=extra.knockoff;if(pr.pronouns)base.pronouns=extra.pronouns;if(pr.target_gender)base.target_gender=extra.target_gender;
  for(var k in pr)if(pr.hasOwnProperty(k)&&/^slot:/.test(k)){var slot=k.slice(5);base.slot_markers[slot]=extra.slot_markers[slot];if(slot!=="overall")base.slots[slot]=BTT_clone(extra.slots[slot]);}
  if(pr.heritage_traits)base.heritage_traits=BTT_clone(extra.heritage_traits);if(pr.gender_traits)base.gender_traits=BTT_clone(extra.gender_traits);if(pr.axes)base.axes=BTT_clone(extra.axes);
  for(var f in pr)if(pr.hasOwnProperty(f)&&/^field:/.test(f)){var fk=f.slice(6);base.fields[fk]=extra.fields[fk];}
  var simple=["instant_change_detection","duration_modifiers","extend_time_conditions","reduce_time_triggers","permanency_triggers","opposite_aliases","reversal_detection","risk_tracks"];
  for(var i=0;i<simple.length;i++)if(pr[simple[i]])base[simple[i]]=BTT_clone(extra[simple[i]]);
  var scalar=["swap_mode","swap_strength","duration","reversion_target","reversal"];for(var j=0;j<scalar.length;j++)if(pr[scalar[j]])base[scalar[j]]=BTT_clone(extra[scalar[j]]);
  return base;
}
function BTT_parseProfileCard(card,kind){
  if(!card)return null;kind=kind||BTT_profileKind(card);if(!kind)return null;var sources=[card.description,card.value,card.entry],obj=null,plain="";
  for(var i=0;i<sources.length;i++){var raw=String(sources[i]||"").trim();if(!raw)continue;var jo=BTT_jsonObject(raw);if(jo&&!obj)obj=jo;else if(!plain)plain=raw;}
  /* The creator-facing value field is the preferred plain representation. */
  var value=String(card.value||"").trim(),entry=String(card.entry||"").trim();if(value&&!BTT_jsonObject(value))plain=value;else if(!plain&&entry&&!BTT_jsonObject(entry))plain=entry;
  var base=obj?BTT_profileFromObject(obj,kind,card.title):null,extra=plain?BTT_profileFromPlain(plain,kind,base&&base.name||card.title):null,p=BTT_overlayPlainProfile(base,extra);if(!p)p=base||extra;if(!p||!p.id)return null;
  p.kind=kind;p.aliases=BTT_unique([p.name,p.id].concat(p.aliases||[]));p.instant_change_detection=BTT_unique(p.instant_change_detection||[]);p.reversal_detection=BTT_unique(p.reversal_detection||[]);delete p._present;return p;
}

function BTT_loadProfiles(force){
  var st=BTT_ensureState(),signature="";if(Array.isArray(storyCards)){for(var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++){var k=BTT_profileKind(storyCards[i]);if(k){var blob=[storyCards[i].title,storyCards[i].type,storyCards[i].value,storyCards[i].entry,storyCards[i].description].join("\u241e");signature+=k+"|"+BTT_hash(blob)+";";}}}
  if(!force&&st.profileCache&&st.profileCache.signature===signature)return st.profileCache;var out={signature:signature,gender:{},heritage:{}};
  if(Array.isArray(storyCards)){for(var j=0;j<storyCards.length;j++){var c=storyCards[j],kind=BTT_profileKind(c);if(!kind)continue;var p=BTT_parseProfileCard(c,kind);if(p&&p.id)out[kind][p.id]=p;}}
  st.profileCache=out;return out;
}
function BTT_profiles(kind){var p=BTT_loadProfiles(false);return p[String(kind||"")]||{};}
function BTT_profile(kind,id){return BTT_profiles(kind)[BTT_normId(id)]||null;}
function BTT_profileAliases(p){return BTT_unique([p&&p.id,p&&p.name].concat(p&&p.aliases||[]));}
function BTT_profileAliasOccurrences(text,alias){
  text=String(text||"");alias=BTT_cleanSpace(alias);if(!alias)return 0;
  var re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(alias).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig"),m,n=0;
  while((m=re.exec(text))){n++;if(!m[0].length)re.lastIndex++;}
  return n;
}
function BTT_profileIdentityHits(text,kind,initOnly){
  /* Profile aliases are vocabulary, not authority. Every available profile can
     generate a candidate; aliases contribute equally regardless of string
     length and are combined additively with ordinary assertion/change evidence. */
  var ps=BTT_profiles(kind),out=[],raw=String(text||""),assertion=BTT_stateAssertionConfidence(raw),change=(typeof BTT_changeEvidence==="function"?BTT_changeEvidence(raw).score:0);
  for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){
    var p=ps[id];if(!p||p.enabled===false||(initOnly&&p.init_detection_enabled===false))continue;
    var aliases=BTT_profileAliases(p),count=0,matched=[];
    for(var i=0;i<aliases.length;i++){var n=BTT_profileAliasOccurrences(raw,aliases[i]);if(n){count+=n;matched.push(aliases[i]);}}
    if(!count)continue;
    var score=Math.min(1.65,count*0.55)+assertion*0.30+Math.min(0.18,Math.max(0,change)*0.18);
    out.push({id:id,profile:p,score:score,matches:count,aliases:BTT_unique(matched)});
  }
  out.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});return out;
}
function BTT_findProfileByIdentity(text,kind,initOnly){
  var hits=BTT_profileIdentityHits(text,kind,initOnly);return hits.length?hits[0]:null;
}
function BTT_identityRelationScore(raw,entity,aliases){
  raw=String(raw||"");var lower=BTT_lower(raw),refs=BTT_entityAliases(entity),best=0;
  /* Direction matters: identity evidence links the owned entity reference to a
     later copular/change relation and then to a profile alias. Merely mentioning
     an object of that Heritage in the same owned sentence ("you stand beside a
     slime vat") produces no such relation and cannot accumulate into genesis. */
  for(var r=0;r<refs.length;r++){
    var ref=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(refs[r]));if(!ref)continue;var refRe=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(ref).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig"),rm;
    while((rm=refRe.exec(lower))){for(var a=0;a<(aliases||[]).length;a++){var al=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[a]));if(!al)continue;var tail=lower.slice(rm.index+rm[0].length,Math.min(lower.length,rm.index+rm[0].length+92)),am=tail.search(new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(al).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","i"));if(am<0)continue;var between=tail.slice(0,am),score=0;if(/\b(?:am|is|are|was|were|become|becomes|became|turned|turns|remain|remains)\b/i.test(between))score+=0.42;else if(/^\s*[,;:—-]?\s*(?:an?\s+)?(?:[a-z-]+\s+){0,3}$/i.test(between))score+=0.26;if(/\b(?:gender|heritage|species|race|identity|form)\b/i.test(between))score+=0.12;if(score>best)best=score;}}
  }
  /* Explicit field-style identity statements are another evidence channel. */
  if(/\b(?:gender|heritage|species|race)\s*[:=]/i.test(raw))best=Math.max(best,0.44);
  return BTT_clamp(best,0,0.54);
}

function BTT_fragmentProfileCandidates(fragment,kind,initOnly,ownership,baseline,entity){
  var ps=BTT_profiles(kind),out=[],raw=String(fragment&&fragment.raw||""),own=BTT_clamp(Number(ownership)||0,0,1),assertion=BTT_clamp(Number(fragment&&fragment.assertion)||BTT_stateAssertionConfidence(raw),0,1),actual=BTT_clamp(Number(fragment&&fragment.actuality)||BTT_actuality(raw,!!(fragment&&fragment.quoted)),0,1),change=(typeof BTT_changeEvidence==="function"?BTT_changeEvidence(raw).score:0);
  for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){
    var p=ps[id];if(!p||p.enabled===false||(initOnly&&p.init_detection_enabled===false))continue;
    var aliases=BTT_profileAliases(p),matches=0,seen=[];
    for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){var n=BTT_profileAliasOccurrences(raw,aliases[a]);if(n){matches+=n;seen.push(aliases[a]);}}
    if(!matches)continue;
    var relation=entity?BTT_identityRelationScore(raw,entity,seen):0;
    var score=0.20+own*0.22+assertion*0.15+actual*0.10+relation+Math.min(0.08,Math.max(0,matches-1)*0.04);
    /* Conflicts and baseline-change language are additive penalties, not
       multiplicative shrinkage of otherwise independent evidence channels. */
    if(fragment&&fragment.quoted&&fragment.speaker&&fragment.speaker!==entity)score-=0.28;
    if(baseline&&change>=0.45)score-=0.62;
    out.push({id:id,profile:p,score:BTT_clamp(score,0,2.5),aliases:BTT_unique(seen),matches:matches,change:change,relation:relation});
  }
  out.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});return out;
}

function BTT_baselineChangeBoundary(parse,entity){
  if(!parse||!parse.fragments)return null;var owned=BTT_ownedFragments(parse,entity),boundary=null;
  for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){
    var f=owned[i].fragment,shared=f.shared&&f.shared.change?f.shared.change:BTT_sharedChangeEvidence(f.raw),s=Number(shared.score)||0;
    /* A boundary is a change-state decision, not a vocabulary whitelist. The
       shared morphology score is strengthened by owned present physiology and
       by multiple axis/structure features in the same fragment. */
    var sf=f.shared||BTT_sharedLexicalFrame(f);s+=Math.min(0.30,(sf.axes||[]).length*0.08)+Math.min(0.20,Object.keys(sf.structure||{}).length*0.06);
    if(s>=0.34&&f.actuality>=0.55){boundary=f.index;break;}
  }
  return boundary;
}
function BTT_baselineProfileEvidence(parse,entity,kind,initOnly){
  if(!parse||!parse.fragments)return null;var owned=BTT_ownedFragments(parse,entity),boundary=BTT_baselineChangeBoundary(parse,entity),scores={},details={};
  function add(obs,fallbackScale){for(var j=0;j<obs.length;j++){var o=obs[j],v=o.score*(fallbackScale||1);scores[o.id]=(scores[o.id]||0)+v;if(!details[o.id])details[o.id]={profile:o.profile,observations:0};details[o.id].observations++;}}
  var used=0;
  for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){var f=owned[i].fragment;if(boundary!==null&&f.index>=boundary)continue;var c=BTT_fragmentProfileCandidates(f,kind,initOnly,owned[i].ownership,true,entity).filter(function(x){return Number(x.relation)>=0.18;});if(c.length){add(c,1);used++;}}
  /* If the opening gives no usable identity evidence, use the whole owned text
     as a low-authority fallback rather than defaulting because one exact grammar
     template was absent. Change-state fragments remain strongly discounted. */
  if(!used){for(var k=0;k<owned.length;k++){var fc=BTT_fragmentProfileCandidates(owned[k].fragment,kind,initOnly,owned[k].ownership,true,entity);if(fc.length)add(fc,0.42);}}
  var ranked=[];for(var id in scores)if(scores.hasOwnProperty(id))ranked.push({id:id,profile:details[id].profile,score:scores[id],observations:details[id].observations});ranked.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});
  if(!ranked.length)return null;var best=ranked[0],next=ranked.length>1?ranked[1].score:0;best.margin=best.score-next;best.boundary=boundary;best.ranked=ranked;return best;
}
function BTT_profileFieldValues(profile,key){
  var f=profile&&profile.fields||{},direct=f[key],options=f[key+"_options"],vals=[];
  if(Array.isArray(direct))vals=vals.concat(direct);else if(direct!==undefined&&direct!==null&&BTT_cleanSpace(direct))vals.push(BTT_cleanSpace(direct));
  if(Array.isArray(options))vals=vals.concat(options);else if(options){var m=String(options).match(/\[([^\]]+)\]/);vals=vals.concat(m?BTT_splitList(m[1]):BTT_splitList(options));}
  return BTT_unique(vals.map(BTT_cleanSpace).filter(Boolean));
}
function BTT_baselineFieldVocabulary(key){
  var vals=[],kinds=["heritage","gender"];
  for(var k=0;k<kinds.length;k++){var ps=BTT_profiles(kinds[k]);for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id))vals=vals.concat(BTT_profileFieldValues(ps[id],key));}
  var common={
    skin:["pale","fair","tan","tanned","olive","brown","dark brown","deep brown","ebony","ivory","porcelain","bronze","golden","gray","grey","green","red","crimson","violet","blue"],
    hair_color:["black","dark brown","brown","chestnut brown","chestnut","auburn","red","blond","blonde","ash blond","white","silver","gray","grey","gold","golden","green","blue","violet","purple","pink"],
    hair_length:["bald","shaved","cropped","close-cropped","short","moderate","medium","shoulder-length","long","very long","waist-length","flowing","shaggy","braided"],
    eye_color:["black","brown","dark brown","hazel","amber","gold","golden","green","emerald","blue","gray","grey","silver","violet","purple","red"],
    build:["lean","slim","slender","lithe","fit","athletic","broad","thick","stocky","muscular","heavy","soft","curvy","wiry"],
    scent:["sweet","musky","musk","floral","earthy","ozone","citrus","spicy"]
  };vals=vals.concat(common[key]||[]);return BTT_unique(vals.map(function(x){return BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(x));}).filter(function(x){return x&&x!=="na"&&x!=="none";}));
}
function BTT_nearestCueDistance(raw,term,cues){
  var lower=BTT_lower(raw),best=9999,reTerm=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(term).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig"),m,positions=[];while((m=reTerm.exec(lower))){positions.push(m.index);if(!m[0].length)reTerm.lastIndex++;}if(!positions.length)return 9999;
  for(var c=0;c<cues.length;c++){var reCue=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(cues[c]).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig"),q;while((q=reCue.exec(lower))){for(var p=0;p<positions.length;p++)best=Math.min(best,Math.abs(positions[p]-q.index));if(!q[0].length)reCue.lastIndex++;}}
  return best;
}
function BTT_baselineAppearanceEvidence(meta,parse,entity,boundary){
  if(!meta||!parse)return;var owned=BTT_ownedFragments(parse,entity),defs={skin:["skin","hide","complexion"],hair_color:["hair","locks","mane"],hair_length:["hair","locks","mane","length"],eye_color:["eye","eyes"],build:["build","frame","body","physique"]},best={};
  for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){
    var f=owned[i].fragment;if(boundary!==null&&f.index>=boundary)continue;var own=owned[i].ownership;
    for(var key in defs)if(defs.hasOwnProperty(key)){
      var vocab=BTT_baselineFieldVocabulary(key);for(var v=0;v<vocab.length;v++){
        var term=vocab[v],dist=BTT_nearestCueDistance(f.raw,term,defs[key]);if(dist>88)continue;
        var score=own*0.45+BTT_clamp(f.assertion,0,1)*0.18+BTT_clamp(f.actuality,0,1)*0.14+Math.max(0,0.36-dist/260);
        if(!best[key]||score>best[key].score)best[key]={value:term,score:score,fragment:f.raw};
      }
    }
  }
  for(var k in best)if(best.hasOwnProperty(k)&&best[k].score>=0.70){var val=best[k].value;if(k==="hair_length")val=BTT_normalizeHairLength(val);if(k==="hair_color"||k==="eye_color"||k==="skin")val=val.replace(/\bgrey\b/g,"gray");if(k==="build"&&!/\bbuild\b/.test(val))val=val+" build";var old=String(meta.appearance.fields[k]||"");meta.appearance.fields[k]=val;if(old!==val)BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"BASELINE FIELD | "+k+" | "+(old||"∅")+"→"+val+" | confidence="+BTT_round(best[k].score,2));}
}

function BTT_profileProductHits(text){
  var hits=[],kinds=["gender","heritage"],raw=String(text||""),lower=BTT_lower(raw);
  function ranges(term){var out=[],re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(term).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig"),m;while((m=re.exec(raw))){out.push({start:m.index,end:m.index+m[0].length});if(!m[0].length)re.lastIndex++;}return out;}
  function sameSurface(a,b){
    if(!a||!b||a.kind!==b.kind||a.id===b.id)return false;var left=a.start<=b.start?a:b,right=a.start<=b.start?b:a,gap=Math.max(0,right.start-left.end);if(gap>34)return false;var between=raw.slice(left.end,right.start);
    if(/[;\n!?]/.test(between)||/\b(?:and|or|versus|vs|another|second|third|different|separate|additional|extra|new)\b/i.test(between))return false;
    if(/\b(?:pill|capsule|tablet|tab|dose|sample|formula|product|serum|potion|vial|patch)\b/i.test(between))return false;return true;
  }
  for(var k=0;k<kinds.length;k++){var ps=BTT_profiles(kinds[k]);for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){var p=ps[id];if(!p||p.enabled===false)continue;
      var explicit=BTT_unique((p.instant_change_detection||[]).concat(p.reversal_detection||[]));
      var productLike=explicit.length||p.reversal||p.duration||(p.duration_modifiers||[]).length||(p.risk_tracks||[]).length||(p.permanency_triggers||[]).length||p.manufacturer||p.knockoff;
      var terms=explicit.slice();if(productLike)terms=BTT_unique(terms.concat([p.name]).concat(p.aliases||[]));
      for(var i=0;i<terms.length;i++){var term=BTT_cleanSpace(terms[i]);if(!term||!BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,term))continue;var rs=ranges(term),isExplicit=explicit.some(function(x){return BTT_lower(x)===BTT_lower(term);});
        for(var rr=0;rr<rs.length;rr++){var idx=rs[rr].start;if(!isExplicit&&!/\b(?:pill|capsule|tablet|dose|product|formula|formulation|variant|version|edition|sample|vial)\b/i.test(raw.slice(Math.max(0,idx-45),rs[rr].end+45)))continue;
          var specificity=(term.split(/\s+/).length*12)+term.length+(isExplicit?18:0);if(BTT_lower(p.name||"")===BTT_lower(term))specificity+=18;
          hits.push({kind:kinds[k],id:id,profile:p,trigger:term,start:idx,end:rs[rr].end,specificity:specificity,reversal:!!p.reversal,suppressedCandidates:[]});
        }
      }
    }}
  /* Specific profile identity wins anywhere inside one physical product surface,
     not only when trigger spans literally overlap. Generic color/family wording
     therefore remains a valid Basic candidate when it stands alone but loses to
     a stronger named variant in the same pill/capsule/product phrase. */
  var kept=[];for(var h=0;h<hits.length;h++){var x=hits[h],winner=null;for(var q=0;q<hits.length;q++){if(q===h)continue;var y=hits[q];if(!sameSurface(x,y))continue;if(y.specificity>=x.specificity+8&&(!winner||y.specificity>winner.specificity))winner=y;}if(winner){winner.suppressedCandidates=winner.suppressedCandidates||[];winner.suppressedCandidates.push({id:x.id,trigger:x.trigger,specificity:x.specificity,reason:"MORE_SPECIFIC_SAME_PRODUCT_PHRASE"});continue;}kept.push(x);}
  /* Collapse duplicate hits for the same profile occurrence while retaining all
     distinct physical surfaces for downstream product-object grouping. */
  kept.sort(function(a,b){if(a.start!==b.start)return a.start-b.start;return b.specificity-a.specificity;});var out=[];for(var z=0;z<kept.length;z++){var d=false;for(var u=0;u<out.length;u++)if(out[u].kind===kept[z].kind&&out[u].id===kept[z].id&&Math.abs(out[u].start-kept[z].start)<=2){d=true;if(kept[z].specificity>out[u].specificity)out[u]=kept[z];break;}if(!d)out.push(kept[z]);}return out;
}
function BTT_profileTraitRegistry(){var set={};for(var i=0;i<BTT_CORE_TRAITS.length;i++)set[BTT_lower(BTT_CORE_TRAITS[i])]=1;var kinds=["gender","heritage"];for(var k=0;k<kinds.length;k++){var ps=BTT_profiles(kinds[k]);for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){var p=ps[id],slots=p.slots||{};for(var sl in slots)if(slots.hasOwnProperty(sl)&&slots[sl]&&Array.isArray(slots[sl].traits))for(var t=0;t<slots[sl].traits.length;t++)set[BTT_lower(slots[sl].traits[t]).replace(/\s+/g,"-")]=1;var arr=(p.heritage_traits||[]).concat(p.gender_traits||[]);for(var a=0;a<arr.length;a++)set[BTT_lower(arr[a]).replace(/\s+/g,"-")]=1;}}var dr=BTT_devRiskOutcomeTraits();for(var dt in dr)if(dr.hasOwnProperty(dt))set[dt]=1;return set;}


/* ============================================================================
   4B. DEVELOPABLE RISK PROFILE LANGUAGE AND SEMANTIC DELIVERY LIBRARY
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Developable Risks are independent of Instant-Change Risk Tracks. They model
   durable/semi-durable ambient world mechanics that must be acquired through a
   qualifying event, then progress through later exposures. Creator cards stay
   compact and line-oriented; this runtime compiles those lines into semantic
   acquisition/track profiles over the shared confidence engine.
============================================================================ */

var BTT_DEV_STOPWORDS={"the":1,"a":1,"an":1,"to":1,"of":1,"and":1,"or":1,"in":1,"on":1,"at":1,"from":1,"with":1,"by":1,"for":1,"into":1,"through":1,"its":1,"their":1,"your":1,"his":1,"her":1,"this":1,"that":1,"same":1,"kind":1,"meaningful":1,"further":1};
var BTT_DEV_CONCEPTS={
  expose:"exposure",exposure:"exposure",contact:"contact",touch:"contact",rub:"contact",brush:"contact",
  fluid:"fluid",secretion:"fluid",saliva:"fluid",blood:"fluid",semen:"fluid",cum:"fluid",milk:"fluid",lubricant:"fluid",venom:"fluid",
  inhale:"inhalation",breathe:"inhalation",breath:"inhalation",smell:"scent",scent:"scent",odor:"scent",aroma:"scent",pheromone:"pheromone",musk:"pheromone",

  psychic:"psychic",telepathic:"psychic",mental:"psychic",mind:"psychic",cognitohazard:"cognitohazard",memetic:"cognitohazard",knowledge:"cognitohazard",

  orgasm:"orgasm",climax:"orgasm",birth:"birth",born:"birth",deliver:"birth",gestation:"gestation",pregnancy:"gestation",pregnant:"gestation",incubation:"gestation",incubating:"gestation",
  egg:"egg-laying",oviposition:"egg-laying",oviposit:"egg-laying",hatch:"lifecycle",ritual:"ritual",spell:"spell",magic:"spell",magical:"spell",
  transform:"transformation",transformation:"transformation",mutation:"transformation",corruption:"corruption",corruptive:"corruption",addiction:"addiction",addictive:"addiction",dependency:"addiction",conditioning:"conditioning",conditioned:"conditioning",
  behavior:"behavioral",behavioral:"behavioral",seek:"behavioral",seeking:"behavioral",pursue:"behavioral",compulsion:"compulsion",compelled:"compulsion",urge:"compulsion",craving:"compulsion",crave:"compulsion",
  pleasure:"pleasure",arousal:"pleasure",aroused:"pleasure",reward:"pleasure",stimulation:"pleasure",pain:"pain",ache:"pain",feeding:"feeding",feed:"feeding",sleep:"sleep",asleep:"sleep",bond:"bonding",bonding:"bonding",attachment:"bonding"
};
function BTT_devStem(tok){tok=BTT_lower(tok).replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g,"");if(tok.length>5)tok=tok.replace(/(?:ingly|edly|ation|ions|ing|ed|es|s)$/i,"");return tok;}
function BTT_devConceptTokens(text){var raw=BTT_lower(text).replace(/[^a-z0-9-]+/g," ").split(/\s+/),out=[],seen={};for(var i=0;i<raw.length;i++){var t=BTT_devStem(raw[i]);if(!t||BTT_DEV_STOPWORDS[t])continue;var c=BTT_DEV_CONCEPTS[t]||t;if(!seen[c]){seen[c]=1;out.push(c);}}return out;}
function BTT_devCueHit(raw,cue){
  /* Delivery/profile cues are token/stem matched rather than substring matched.
     This prevents short cues such as `eat` from firing inside unrelated words such
     as `repeats`, while still allowing creator stem cues like `penetrat`/`expos`. */
  var r=BTT_lower(String(raw||"")).replace(/[^a-z0-9-]+/g," ").trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean),q=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(cue)).replace(/[^a-z0-9-]+/g," ").trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);if(!r.length||!q.length)return false;
  var rs=r.map(BTT_devStem),qs=q.map(BTT_devStem);for(var i=0;i<=rs.length-qs.length;i++){var ok=true;for(var j=0;j<qs.length;j++)if(rs[i+j]!==qs[j]){ok=false;break;}if(ok)return true;}return false;
}
function BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(prototype,raw){prototype=BTT_cleanSpace(prototype);raw=String(raw||"");if(!prototype||!raw)return 0;if(BTT_devCueHit(raw,prototype))return 1;var a=BTT_devConceptTokens(prototype),b=BTT_devConceptTokens(raw),set={};for(var i=0;i<b.length;i++)set[b[i]]=1;if(!a.length)return 0;var hits=0;for(var j=0;j<a.length;j++)if(set[a[j]])hits++;var cover=hits/a.length;if(hits>=2)return BTT_clamp(0.36+cover*0.62,0,0.98);if(hits===1)return BTT_clamp(0.22+cover*0.45,0,0.62);return 0;}
function BTT_devDeliveryProfile(id){id=BTT_normId(id);return BTT_DEV_DELIVERY_PROFILES[id]||null;}
function BTT_devDeliveryScore(ids,f){ids=BTT_splitList(ids);if(!ids.length)ids=["exposure"];var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),best=0,why="";for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){var p=BTT_devDeliveryProfile(ids[i]);if(!p)continue;var score=0;for(var a=0;a<p.aliases.length;a++)score=Math.max(score,BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(p.aliases[a],raw));var cueHits=0;for(var c=0;c<p.cues.length;c++)if(BTT_devCueHit(raw,p.cues[c]))cueHits++;if(cueHits)score=Math.max(score,BTT_clamp(0.48+Math.min(0.42,cueHits*0.12),0,0.95));if(score>best){best=score;why=BTT_normId(ids[i]);}}return {score:BTT_round(best,2),profile:why};}
function BTT_isDevRiskCard(card){return !!card&&(/^(?:BTT\s+)?Developable\s+Risk$/i.test(String(card.type||""))||/^BTT\s+(?:DEV|DEVELOPABLE)\s+RISK\b/i.test(String(card.title||"")));}
function BTT_devRiskCardText(card){var value=String(card&&card.value||"").trim(),entry=String(card&&card.entry||"").trim();/* AI Dungeon/card helpers may expose the same Entry through both `value` and `entry`. Parsing both would duplicate ACQUIRE/TRACK blocks, so identical representations collapse to one text value. When the fields contain different text, both are joined before profile parsing. */if(value&&entry&&value===entry)return value;if(value&&entry)return value+"\n"+entry;return value||entry;}
function BTT_devOutcomeParts(raw,successAt){var out=[],parts=String(raw||"").split(/\s*;\s*/);for(var i=0;i<parts.length;i++){var p=BTT_cleanSpace(parts[i]),m;if(!p)continue;
  m=p.match(/^(HEAD|ARMS|LEGS|BREASTS|GUT|PENIS|VAGINA|ASS|TAIL|WINGS)\s+([+-])\s*(.+)$/i);if(m){out.push({type:"traits",slot:BTT_lower(m[1]),operation:m[2]==="+"?"add":"remove",traits:BTT_splitList(m[3]).map(function(x){return BTT_normId(x);}),successAt:successAt});continue;}
  m=p.match(/^AXIS\s+([a-z_ ]+)\s*([+\-=])\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/i);if(m){out.push({type:"axis",axis:BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(m[1])).replace(/\s+/g,"_"),operation:m[2],value:Number(m[3]),successAt:successAt});continue;}
  m=p.match(/^CONDITION\s+([+-])\s*([a-z][a-z _-]*)$/i);if(m){out.push({type:"condition",operation:m[1]==="+"?"add":"remove",condition:BTT_conditionType(m[2]),successAt:successAt});continue;}
  m=p.match(/^TRIGGER\s+\+\s*([^:=]+)(?:\s*[:=]>?\s*(.+))?$/i);if(m){out.push({type:"conditionedTrigger",id:BTT_normId(m[1]),description:BTT_cleanSpace(m[2]||m[1]),successAt:successAt});continue;}
 }return out;}
function BTT_parseDevAcquireAxes(raw){var out=[],parts=String(raw||"").split(/\s*[;,]\s*/);for(var i=0;i<parts.length;i++){var p=BTT_cleanSpace(parts[i]),m=p.match(/^([a-z_ ]+)\s*([+\-=])\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/i);if(!m)continue;out.push({axis:BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(m[1])).replace(/\s+/g,"_"),operation:m[2],value:Number(m[3])});}return out;}
function BTT_parseDevReceiverRouting(raw){var out={},parts=String(raw||"").split(/\s*[;,]\s*/);for(var i=0;i<parts.length;i++){var m=BTT_cleanSpace(parts[i]).match(/^(head|arms|legs|breasts|gut|penis|vagina|ass|tail|wings)\s*(?:=|->|→|:)\s*(.+)$/i);if(!m)continue;var slot=BTT_lower(m[1]),ids=String(m[2]||"").split(/\s*\+\s*/).map(BTT_normId).filter(Boolean);if(ids.length)out[slot]=ids;}return out;}
function BTT_parseDevRiskCard(card){var text=BTT_devRiskCardText(card),lines=text.split(/\n/g),name="";for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++){var q=BTT_cleanSpace(lines[i]);if(q&&!/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9 _-]*\s*:/i.test(q)){name=q.replace(/^BTT\s+(?:DEV|DEVELOPABLE)\s+RISK\s*-?\s*/i,"");break;}}if(!name)name=String(card.title||"").replace(/^BTT\s+(?:DEV|DEVELOPABLE)\s+RISK\s*-?\s*/i,"");var p={id:BTT_normId(name),name:BTT_cleanSpace(name),aliases:[],acquisitions:[],tracks:[],persistence:"acquired",removal:[],sourceCardTitle:String(card.title||""),generated:/AI-GENERATED\s*:\s*true/i.test(text)},acq=null,track=null,cfg=BTT_getConfig();if(!p.id)return null;
  function ensureAcq(proto){if(!acq){acq={id:"acquire-"+(p.acquisitions.length+1),prototype:BTT_cleanSpace(proto||"exposure"),delivery:["exposure"],qualifiers:[],sourceTraits:[],sourceSlotTraits:[],sourceHeritage:[],sourceProfiles:[],sourceConditions:[],receiverTraits:[],receiverConditions:[],excludeTraits:[],excludeHeritage:[],excludeRisks:[],acquireSlot:"",slotOrigin:"",naturalTraits:[],acquireAxes:[],acquireConditions:[],threshold:cfg.dev_risk_acquire_threshold,historyTurns:cfg.dev_risk_acquire_history_turns};p.acquisitions.push(acq);}return acq;}
  for(i=0;i<lines.length;i++){var m=lines[i].match(/^\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9 _-]*?)\s*:\s*(.*)$/);if(!m)continue;var key=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(m[1])),val=BTT_cleanSpace(m[2]);
    if(key==="aliases"){p.aliases=BTT_splitList(val);continue;}if(key==="ai-generated"||key==="ai generated")continue;if(/^acquire(?:\s+\d+)?$/.test(key)){acq=null;track=null;ensureAcq(val);continue;}if(key==="track"){track={id:BTT_normId(val)||("track-"+(p.tracks.length+1)),name:val||("Track "+(p.tracks.length+1)),triggers:[],delivery:[],qualifiers:[],sourceSlotTraits:[],receiverRouting:{},threshold:cfg.dev_risk_track_threshold,baseChance:cfg.dev_risk_default_base_chance,repeatIncrease:cfg.dev_risk_default_repeat_increase,maximumChance:cfg.dev_risk_default_maximum_chance,memoryTurns:cfg.dev_risk_default_memory_turns,outcomes:[]};p.tracks.push(track);continue;}
    if(!track){var a=ensureAcq("");if(key==="delivery")a.delivery=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="qualifiers")a.qualifiers=BTT_splitList(val);else if(key==="source traits")a.sourceTraits=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="source slot traits")a.sourceSlotTraits=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="source heritage")a.sourceHeritage=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="source profiles"||key==="source profile")a.sourceProfiles=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="source conditions")a.sourceConditions=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_conditionType);else if(key==="receiver traits")a.receiverTraits=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="receiver conditions")a.receiverConditions=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_conditionType);else if(key==="exclude traits")a.excludeTraits=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="exclude heritage")a.excludeHeritage=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="exclude risks")a.excludeRisks=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="acquire slot")a.acquireSlot=BTT_lower(val).replace(/\s+/g,"_");else if(key==="slot origin")a.slotOrigin=BTT_normId(val);else if(key==="natural traits")a.naturalTraits=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="acquire axes")a.acquireAxes=BTT_parseDevAcquireAxes(val);else if(key==="acquire conditions")a.acquireConditions=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_conditionType);else if(key==="acquire threshold")a.threshold=BTT_num(val,a.threshold);else if(key==="acquire history")a.historyTurns=Math.max(0,Math.round(BTT_num(val,a.historyTurns)));else if(key==="persistence")p.persistence=BTT_lower(val)||"acquired";else if(key==="removal")p.removal=BTT_splitList(val);continue;}
    if(key==="triggers")track.triggers=BTT_splitList(val);else if(key==="delivery"||key==="track delivery")track.delivery=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="qualifiers"||key==="track qualifiers")track.qualifiers=BTT_splitList(val);else if(key==="source slot traits")track.sourceSlotTraits=BTT_splitList(val).map(BTT_normId);else if(key==="receiver routing"||key==="receiver routes")track.receiverRouting=BTT_parseDevReceiverRouting(val);else if(key==="threshold"||key==="track threshold")track.threshold=BTT_num(val,track.threshold);else if(key==="chance")track.baseChance=BTT_clamp(BTT_num(val,track.baseChance),0,100);else if(key==="repeat")track.repeatIncrease=BTT_clamp(BTT_num(val,track.repeatIncrease),0,100);else if(key==="max")track.maximumChance=BTT_clamp(BTT_num(val,track.maximumChance),0,100);else if(key==="memory")track.memoryTurns=Math.max(1,Math.round(BTT_num(val,track.memoryTurns)));else if(/^success\s+\d+$/.test(key)){var n=Number(key.match(/\d+/)[0]);track.outcomes=track.outcomes.concat(BTT_devOutcomeParts(val,n));}else if(key==="persistence")p.persistence=BTT_lower(val)||"acquired";else if(key==="removal")p.removal=BTT_splitList(val);
  }
  if(!p.acquisitions.length)ensureAcq("exposure");p.aliases=BTT_unique([p.name,p.id].concat(p.aliases));return p;
}
function BTT_loadDevRiskProfiles(force){if(!Array.isArray(storyCards))return {};var cards=[],sigParts=[];for(var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++)if(BTT_isDevRiskCard(storyCards[i])){cards.push(storyCards[i]);sigParts.push(String(storyCards[i].title||"")+"|"+BTT_devRiskCardText(storyCards[i]));}var s=BTT_ensureState(),sig=String(BTT_hash(sigParts.join("||")));if(!force&&s.devRiskCache&&s.devRiskCache.signature===sig)return s.devRiskCache.profiles||{};var profiles={};for(i=0;i<cards.length;i++){var p=BTT_parseDevRiskCard(cards[i]);if(p)profiles[p.id]=p;}s.devRiskCache={signature:sig,profiles:profiles};return profiles;}
function BTT_devRiskProfiles(){return BTT_loadDevRiskProfiles(false);}
function BTT_devRiskProfile(id){return BTT_devRiskProfiles()[BTT_normId(id)]||null;}
function BTT_devRiskOutcomeTraits(){var set={},ps=BTT_devRiskProfiles();for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){for(var a=0;a<ps[id].acquisitions.length;a++){var aq=ps[id].acquisitions[a];for(var n=0;n<(aq.naturalTraits||[]).length;n++)set[aq.naturalTraits[n]]=1;if(aq.slotOrigin)set[aq.slotOrigin]=1;}for(var t=0;t<ps[id].tracks.length;t++)for(var o=0;o<ps[id].tracks[t].outcomes.length;o++){var x=ps[id].tracks[t].outcomes[o];if(x.type==="traits")for(var k=0;k<x.traits.length;k++)set[x.traits[k]]=1;}}return set;}
function BTT_devHasCondition(meta,type){type=BTT_conditionType(type);for(var i=0;i<(meta.conditions||[]).length;i++)if(meta.conditions[i].active!==false&&BTT_conditionType(meta.conditions[i].type)===type)return true;return false;}
function BTT_devActive(meta,id){id=BTT_normId(id);var a=meta&&meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.active||[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(a[i].id===id)return a[i];return null;}
function BTT_devExclusion(meta,p,a){if(a.excludeHeritage.indexOf(BTT_normId(meta.heritage))>=0)return "EXCLUDED_HERITAGE";if(a.excludeTraits.length&&BTT_hasAnyTrait(meta,a.excludeTraits))return "EXCLUDED_TRAIT";for(var r=0;r<a.excludeRisks.length;r++)if(BTT_devActive(meta,a.excludeRisks[r]))return "EXCLUDED_RISK";return "";}
function BTT_devSourceForFragment(f,parse,receiver,work){if(f&&f._bttReplaySource){var rs=f._bttReplaySource,rm=rs.entity?BTT_findEntity(rs.entity):null;return {entity:String(rs.entity||""),confidence:Number(rs.confidence)||0.90,meta:rm,descriptor:String(rs.descriptor||"")};}var src=BTT_conditionSourceCandidates(f,parse,receiver,work);if(src&&src.entity)return {entity:src.entity,confidence:src.confidence,meta:BTT_findEntity(src.entity),descriptor:src.descriptor||""};var loose=BTT_conditionSource(f,parse,receiver);return {entity:loose.entity||"",confidence:Number(loose.confidence)||0,meta:BTT_eventLocalSourceMeta(f,loose),descriptor:loose.descriptor||""};}
function BTT_devSourceQualifies(source,a,f,parse,requiredSlotTraits){
  requiredSlotTraits=BTT_unique((requiredSlotTraits||[]).map(BTT_normId));var hasEntityConstraint=a.sourceTraits.length||a.sourceHeritage.length||a.sourceProfiles.length||a.sourceConditions.length,hasSlotConstraint=requiredSlotTraits.length,m=source&&source.meta;if((hasEntityConstraint||hasSlotConstraint)&&!m)return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"source_unresolved",sourceSlot:{slot:"",confidence:0}};
  if(!m)return {pass:true,score:0.55,reason:"no_source_constraint",sourceSlot:{slot:"",confidence:0}};
  if(a.sourceTraits.length&&!BTT_hasAnyTrait(m,a.sourceTraits))return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"source_trait",sourceSlot:{slot:"",confidence:0}};
  if(a.sourceHeritage.length&&a.sourceHeritage.indexOf(BTT_normId(m.heritage))<0)return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"source_heritage",sourceSlot:{slot:"",confidence:0}};
  if(a.sourceConditions.length){for(var c=0;c<a.sourceConditions.length;c++)if(!BTT_devHasCondition(m,a.sourceConditions[c]))return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"source_condition",sourceSlot:{slot:"",confidence:0}};}
  if(a.sourceProfiles.length){var refs=[];if(m.heritage)refs.push(BTT_normId(m.heritage));if(m.gender)refs.push(BTT_normId(m.gender));var ok=false;for(var p=0;p<a.sourceProfiles.length;p++)if(refs.indexOf(a.sourceProfiles[p])>=0)ok=true;if(!ok)return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"source_profile",sourceSlot:{slot:"",confidence:0}};}
  var sourceSlot={slot:"",confidence:0,reason:"source_slot_unresolved"};if(f&&source&&source.entity)sourceSlot=BTT_reproductiveSourceSlot(f,{entity:source.entity,confidence:source.confidence},"",m,parse);if(hasSlotConstraint&&!sourceSlot.slot){var inferred=BTT_uniqueSlotWithTraits(m,requiredSlotTraits);if(inferred.slot)sourceSlot=inferred;}
  if(hasSlotConstraint){if(!sourceSlot.slot||Number(sourceSlot.confidence)<0.56)return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"source_slot_unresolved",sourceSlot:sourceSlot};for(var st=0;st<requiredSlotTraits.length;st++)if(!BTT_slotHas(m,sourceSlot.slot,requiredSlotTraits[st]))return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"source_slot_trait:"+requiredSlotTraits[st],sourceSlot:sourceSlot};}
  return {pass:true,score:BTT_clamp(Math.max(hasEntityConstraint||hasSlotConstraint?0.72:0.55,Number(source.confidence)||0),0,1),reason:(hasEntityConstraint||hasSlotConstraint)?"source_qualified":"no_source_constraint",sourceSlot:sourceSlot};
}
function BTT_devReceiverQualifies(meta,a){if(a.receiverTraits.length&&!BTT_hasAnyTrait(meta,a.receiverTraits))return false;for(var c=0;c<a.receiverConditions.length;c++)if(!BTT_devHasCondition(meta,a.receiverConditions[c]))return false;return true;}
function BTT_devEventWindow(parse,entity,index){
  /* Dev Risk prototypes consume the same bounded clause adjacency as the shared
     confidence engine. This allows one clause to identify the vector/source and
     the next to establish delivery without requiring creators to enumerate a
     combined sentence form. Competing entities stop borrowing. */
  if(!parse||!parse.fragments||index<0||index>=parse.fragments.length)return String(parse&&parse.fragments&&parse.fragments[index]&&parse.fragments[index].raw||"");
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),parts=[parse.fragments[index].raw];for(var d=1;d<=cfg.clause_window;d++){for(var side=-1;side<=1;side+=2){var ni=index+side*d;if(ni<0||ni>=parse.fragments.length)continue;var nf=parse.fragments[ni];if(BTT_fragmentHasCompetingEntity(nf,entity))continue;var fr=BTT_sharedEntityFrame(parse,entity,ni);if(!fr||fr.ownership<0.40)continue;if(nf.eventStatus&&nf.eventStatus.actual===false)continue;parts.push(nf.raw);}}return parts.join(" " );
}
function BTT_devSemanticContext(raw,source,receiverMeta,sourceSlot){
  /* Event semantics may use canonical source context only after the source body
     anchor is resolved. Traits from unrelated source slots are never flattened
     into acquisition similarity. */
  var parts=[String(raw||"")],m=source&&source.meta;if(m){if(m.heritage)parts.push(String(m.heritage));if(m.gender)parts.push(String(m.gender));var sl=sourceSlot&&sourceSlot.slot||"",so=sl&&m.slots&&m.slots[sl];if(so)for(var t=0;t<(so.traits||[]).length;t++)parts.push(so.traits[t]);for(var i=0;i<(m.conditions||[]).length;i++)if(m.conditions[i].active!==false)parts.push(BTT_conditionType(m.conditions[i].type));}
  if(receiverMeta)for(var j=0;j<(receiverMeta.conditions||[]).length;j++)if(receiverMeta.conditions[j].active!==false)parts.push(BTT_conditionType(receiverMeta.conditions[j].type));return parts.join(" " );
}
function BTT_devSexualTransferEvent(raw){raw=String(raw||"");return /\b(?:sexual|intercourse|penetrat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|genital|ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|semen|cum|reproductive fluid|body fluid|intimate contact)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:into|inside|within|transfer|transmit|ejaculat|release|deposit|penetrat|contact|fluid)\b/i.test(raw);}
function BTT_devPropagationVectorRequirements(a,raw){var out=(a.sourceSlotTraits||[]).slice(),phen=(a.naturalTraits||[]).map(BTT_normId);if(phen.indexOf("parasite-propagating")>=0&&BTT_devSexualTransferEvent(raw)&&out.indexOf("parasite-propagating")<0)out.push("parasite-propagating");return BTT_unique(out);}

function BTT_devQualifierScore(list,raw){if(!list||!list.length)return 0.50;var best=0;for(var i=0;i<list.length;i++)best=Math.max(best,BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(list[i],raw));return best;}
function BTT_devAcquireHistory(meta,key,turns){var h=meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory&&meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory[key];if(!h)return 0;var age=BTT_getTurn()-Number(h.turn||0);if(age>turns)return 0;return Number(h.score||0)*Math.pow(BTT_getConfig().decay_base,Math.max(0,age));}
function BTT_devStoreAcquireHistory(meta,key,score,raw){meta.developableRisks=meta.developableRisks||{active:[],conditionedTriggers:[],acquisitionHistory:{}};meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory=meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory||{};meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory[key]={score:BTT_clamp(score,0,2),turn:BTT_getTurn(),raw:BTT_clip(raw,140)};}
function BTT_devAcquisitionEvidence(meta,entity,p,a,f,parse,work){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),status=f.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(f.raw,f.quoted),own=BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity),ex=BTT_devExclusion(meta,p,a);
  if(ex)return {pass:false,score:0,reason:ex};if(!BTT_devReceiverQualifies(meta,a))return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"RECEIVER_REQUIREMENT"};
  if(!status.actual||f.actuality<0.50||own<0.48)return {pass:false,score:0,reason:"EVENT_ROLE"};
  var windowRaw=BTT_devEventWindow(parse,entity,f.index),source=BTT_devSourceForFragment(f,parse,entity,work),slotRequirements=BTT_devPropagationVectorRequirements(a,windowRaw),sq=BTT_devSourceQualifies(source,a,f,parse,slotRequirements),semanticText=BTT_devSemanticContext(windowRaw,source,meta,sq.sourceSlot),semantic=BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(a.prototype,semanticText),delivery=BTT_devDeliveryScore(a.delivery,{raw:windowRaw}),qual=BTT_devQualifierScore(a.qualifiers,windowRaw),narrow=cfg.dev_risk_assumption_narrowness,
      semanticMin=0.24+narrow*0.34,deliveryMin=0.32+narrow*0.34,qualifierMin=0.52+narrow*0.22;
  if(semantic<semanticMin)return {pass:false,score:semantic,reason:"SEMANTIC_ADJACENCY",semantic:semantic,delivery:delivery.score,qualifier:qual};
  if(delivery.score<deliveryMin)return {pass:false,score:semantic+delivery.score,reason:"DELIVERY",semantic:semantic,delivery:delivery.score,qualifier:qual};
  if(a.qualifiers.length&&qual<qualifierMin)return {pass:false,score:semantic+delivery.score,reason:"QUALIFIER",semantic:semantic,delivery:delivery.score,qualifier:qual};
  if(!sq.pass){if(/^source_slot/.test(String(sq.reason||"")))BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ACQUIRE BLOCK | "+p.name+" | reason="+sq.reason+" | source="+(source.entity||"unresolved")+" | source_slot="+(sq.sourceSlot&&sq.sourceSlot.slot||"unresolved")+" | route=OWNER_BOUND_SOURCE_SLOT");return {pass:false,score:semantic+delivery.score,reason:sq.reason,semantic:semantic,delivery:delivery.score,qualifier:qual,source:source,sourceSlot:sq.sourceSlot};}
  /* Actuality is a gate and also a modest additive support channel. A clearly
     asserted/observed acquisition event should be able to clear a deliberately
     narrow creator threshold without needing a second redundant paraphrase, while
     epistemic/anticipated events still fail the gate before scoring. */
  var base=semantic+delivery.score+Math.min(1,Math.max(0.45,own))+Math.max(0,qual-0.50)*0.45+Math.max(0,sq.score-0.55)*0.45+Math.max(0,Number(f.actuality)-0.50)*0.40+(sq.sourceSlot&&sq.sourceSlot.slot?0.10:0);
  return {pass:true,score:BTT_round(base,2),semantic:semantic,delivery:delivery.score,deliveryProfile:delivery.profile,source:source,sourceSlot:sq.sourceSlot,sourceScore:sq.score,sourceSlotRequirements:slotRequirements,ownership:own,qualifier:qual,reason:"candidate"};
}
function BTT_setSlotOriginCanonical(meta,slot,origin){
