  slot=BTT_lower(slot);origin=BTT_normId(origin);if(!slot||BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.indexOf(slot)<0||!origin)return false;var existed=!!meta.slots[slot],so=meta.slots[slot]||BTT_newSlot(),traits=Array.isArray(so.traits)?so.traits.slice():[],oldOrigin=BTT_inferSlotOrigin(meta,slot),heritageProfiles=BTT_profiles("heritage");
  /* A slot has one canonical Heritage/origin marker. Any other currently valid
     Heritage profile noun is removed from the flat trait rendering when origin
     changes; ordinary non-Heritage descriptors remain untouched. */
  traits=traits.filter(function(x){var id=BTT_normId(x);return !(id==="human"||heritageProfiles[id]);});traits.unshift(origin);so.traits=BTT_unique(traits);so.origin=origin;so.updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();meta.slots[slot]=so;return !existed||oldOrigin!==origin;
}
function BTT_devSetSlotOrigin(meta,slot,origin){return BTT_setSlotOriginCanonical(meta,slot,origin);}

function BTT_devApplyAcquireAxes(meta,p,a){for(var i=0;i<(a.acquireAxes||[]).length;i++){var x=a.acquireAxes[i],axis=x.axis,op=x.operation,val=Number(x.value)||0;if(!axis)continue;var before=axis==="fem"?Number(meta.overallFem)||0:Number(meta.appearance.axes[axis])||0;if(op==="="){var nv=BTT_round(axis==="fem"?BTT_clamp(val,-100,100):BTT_clamp(val,0,100),2);if(axis==="fem"){meta.overallFem=nv;meta.appearance.axes.fem=nv;}else meta.appearance.axes[axis]=nv;}else BTT_addAxis(meta,axis,(op==="-"?-1:1)*Math.abs(val),"dev risk acquisition "+p.id,"DEV_RISK_ACQUIRE");var after=axis==="fem"?Number(meta.overallFem)||0:Number(meta.appearance.axes[axis])||0;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ACQUIRE AXIS | "+p.name+" | "+axis+" "+BTT_round(before,2)+"→"+BTT_round(after,2));}}
function BTT_devApplyAcquisitionPhenotype(meta,entity,p,a){var slot=BTT_lower(a.acquireSlot||"");if(slot&&BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.indexOf(slot)>=0){var created=!meta.slots[slot];if(a.slotOrigin)BTT_devSetSlotOrigin(meta,slot,a.slotOrigin);else if(!meta.slots[slot]){meta.slots[slot]=BTT_newSlot();meta.slots[slot].updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();}for(var i=0;i<(a.naturalTraits||[]).length;i++){var tr=BTT_normId(a.naturalTraits[i]);if(!tr)continue;var so=meta.slots[slot]||BTT_newSlot();if(!meta.slots[slot])meta.slots[slot]=so;if(so.traits.indexOf(tr)<0){so.traits.push(tr);so.updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();}}BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ACQUIRE PHENOTYPE | "+p.name+" | slot="+slot+" | origin="+(a.slotOrigin||"unchanged")+" | traits="+((a.naturalTraits||[]).join(",")||"none")+" | result="+(created?"CREATE":"UPDATE"));}BTT_devApplyAcquireAxes(meta,p,a);for(var c=0;c<(a.acquireConditions||[]).length;c++){var type=BTT_conditionType(a.acquireConditions[c]);if(!type||BTT_devHasCondition(meta,type))continue;BTT_addCondition(meta,{type:type,slot:slot||"system",count:1,source:"Dev Risk: "+p.name,sourceEntityId:"",confidence:1,startedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),trigger:"acquisition",active:true});BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ACQUIRE CONDITION | "+p.name+" | "+type+" | slot="+(slot||"system"));}}
function BTT_devAcquire(meta,entity,p,a,ev){var active={id:p.id,name:p.name,aliases:BTT_clone(p.aliases),sourceCardTitle:p.sourceCardTitle,acquiredTurn:BTT_getTurn(),acquisitionId:a.id,acquisitionPrototype:a.prototype,acquisitionPhenotype:{slot:a.acquireSlot||"",slotOrigin:a.slotOrigin||"",naturalTraits:BTT_clone(a.naturalTraits||[]),axes:BTT_clone(a.acquireAxes||[]),conditions:BTT_clone(a.acquireConditions||[])},sourceEntityId:ev.source&&ev.source.entity||"",sourceDescriptor:ev.source&&ev.source.descriptor||"",persistence:p.persistence,removal:BTT_clone(p.removal),tracks:BTT_clone(p.tracks),riskState:{},progressionWatch:{clusters:{},lastTurn:0},generated:!!p.generated};meta.developableRisks.active.push(active);meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory={};BTT_devApplyAcquisitionPhenotype(meta,entity,p,a);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ACQUIRED | "+p.name+" | via="+a.prototype+" | delivery="+(ev.deliveryProfile||"semantic")+" | confidence="+BTT_round(ev.total||ev.score,2));return active;}function BTT_devTrackState(active,id){active.riskState=active.riskState||{};if(!active.riskState[id])active.riskState[id]={seen:{},exposureCount:0,successCount:0,lastExposureTurn:null,lastRoll:null,complete:false};return active.riskState[id];}
function BTT_devProgressionWatch(active){active.progressionWatch=active.progressionWatch&&typeof active.progressionWatch==="object"?active.progressionWatch:{clusters:{},lastTurn:0};active.progressionWatch.clusters=active.progressionWatch.clusters||{};return active.progressionWatch;}
function BTT_devOutcomeSemanticChannels(track){var ch={};for(var i=0;i<(track&&track.outcomes||[]).length;i++){var o=track.outcomes[i];if(o.type==="traits"&&o.slot==="head"){ch.behavioral=Math.max(ch.behavioral||0,0.72);ch.mental=Math.max(ch.mental||0,0.60);}if(o.type==="axis"||o.type==="traits"&&o.slot!=="head")ch.morphological=Math.max(ch.morphological||0,0.70);if(o.type==="condition")ch.physiological=Math.max(ch.physiological||0,0.42);if(o.type==="conditionedTrigger"){ch.behavioral=Math.max(ch.behavioral||0,0.62);ch.adaptive=Math.max(ch.adaptive||0,0.48);}}return ch;}
function BTT_devTrackResponseCoverage(track,row){var triggerText=(track.triggers||[]).join(" ")+" "+(track.qualifiers||[]).join(" ")+" "+String(track.name||track.id||""),semantic=BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(triggerText,row.raw),channel=BTT_responseChannelSimilarity(BTT_devOutcomeSemanticChannels(track),row.responseChannels||{}),delivery=BTT_devDeliveryScore(track.delivery||[],{raw:row.raw}).score;return BTT_clamp(0.48*semantic+0.32*channel+0.20*delivery,0,1);}
function BTT_devProgressionClusterScore(cluster,cfg){var ev=cluster.events||[],strong=0,corr=0;for(var i=0;i<ev.length;i++){strong=Math.max(strong,Number(ev[i].strength)||0);if(i>0)corr+=0.22+0.18*Math.min(1,Number(ev[i].trackCoverage)||0)+0.12*Math.min(1,Number(ev[i].novelty)||0);}cluster.score=BTT_round(BTT_clamp(strong+corr,0,5),3);cluster.lastTurn=ev.length?ev[ev.length-1].turn:BTT_getTurn();return cluster.score;}
function BTT_devProgressionNovelty(track,row){var existing=BTT_devOutcomeSemanticChannels(track),ch=row.responseChannels||{},novel=0,k;for(k in ch)if(ch.hasOwnProperty(k))novel=Math.max(novel,Math.max(0,(Number(ch[k])||0)-(Number(existing[k])||0)));if(Number(ch.adaptive)>=0.30)novel=Math.max(novel,Number(ch.adaptive));if(Number(ch.dependency)>=0.30)novel=Math.max(novel,Number(ch.dependency));return BTT_clamp(novel,0,1);}
function BTT_devQueueProgressionExtension(entity,meta,active,track,trackIndex,cluster,cfg){
  var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState();
  if(g.pending||!cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_enabled||!active.generated||cluster.authored||BTT_getTurn()<Number(cluster.retryAfterTurn||0)||BTT_devRiskGenerationCooldownRemaining(cfg,g)>0)return false;
  var st=BTT_devTrackState(active,track.id),max=0;
  for(var i=0;i<(track.outcomes||[]).length;i++)max=Math.max(max,Number(track.outcomes[i].successAt)||1);
  var next=Math.max(max+1,Number(st.successCount||0)+1),field="track_"+(trackIndex+1)+"_success_"+next+"_effect",evidence=[];
  for(i=0;i<(cluster.events||[]).length;i++)evidence.push("[T"+cluster.events[i].turn+" A"+cluster.events[i].action+" | track="+track.id+" | response="+cluster.events[i].responseFamily+" | strength="+BTT_round(cluster.events[i].strength,2)+"] "+BTT_clip(cluster.events[i].raw,300));
  var pending={mode:"extension",candidateId:"extend:"+active.id+":"+track.id+":"+next,fingerprint:active.id+"+"+track.id+"+higher-success",label:"higher progression",receiver:entity,sourceEntity:active.sourceEntityId||"",sourceDescriptor:active.sourceDescriptor||"",mechanism:String(cluster.mechanism||"developable-risk"),trackHints:BTT_clone(cluster.trackHints||[]),createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),createdAction:BTT_nowAction(),retries:0,authoringResponses:0,maintenanceContinues:0,lastError:"",fieldValues:{},fieldStatus:{},fieldErrors:{},sectionStatus:{},sectionErrors:{},derived:{track_count:trackIndex+1},evidence:evidence,events:BTT_clone(cluster.events||[]),kickoffNoticeEmitted:false,lastRequestedFields:[],lastPromptMode:0,extension:{kind:"higher_success",entity:entity,riskId:active.id,riskName:active.name,cardTitle:active.sourceCardTitle,trackIndex:trackIndex,trackId:track.id,trackName:track.name||track.id,nextSuccess:next,clusterKey:cluster.key,responseFamily:cluster.responseFamily,responseChannels:BTT_clone(cluster.responseChannels||{})}};
  pending.fieldStatus[field]="missing";BTT_devInitializeAuthoringPlan(pending,cfg);g.pending=pending;cluster.status="AUTHORING_QUEUED";cluster.queuedTurn=BTT_getTurn();
  BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","PROGRESSION EXTENSION QUEUED | "+active.name+" / "+track.id+" | SUCCESS "+next+" | score="+BTT_round(cluster.score,2)+" | events="+cluster.events.length);
  BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("EXISTING TRACK EXTENSION QUALIFIED | risk="+active.name+" | track="+track.id+" | next_success="+next+" | score="+BTT_round(cluster.score,2)+" | response_family="+cluster.responseFamily);return true;
}
function BTT_devQueueNewTrackExtension(entity,meta,active,cluster,cfg){
  var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState();
  if(g.pending||!cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_enabled||!active.generated||cluster.authored||BTT_getTurn()<Number(cluster.retryAfterTurn||0)||BTT_devRiskGenerationCooldownRemaining(cfg,g)>0)return false;
  var trackIndex=(active.tracks||[]).length,trackN=trackIndex+1,evidence=[];
  for(var i=0;i<(cluster.events||[]).length;i++)evidence.push("[T"+cluster.events[i].turn+" A"+cluster.events[i].action+" | new-track | response="+cluster.events[i].responseFamily+" | strength="+BTT_round(cluster.events[i].strength,2)+"] "+BTT_clip(cluster.events[i].raw,300));
  var pending={mode:"extension",candidateId:"extend:"+active.id+":track:"+trackN,fingerprint:active.id+"+new-track+"+String(cluster.responseFamily||"response"),label:"new progression track",receiver:entity,sourceEntity:active.sourceEntityId||"",sourceDescriptor:active.sourceDescriptor||"",mechanism:String(cluster.mechanism||"developable-risk"),trackHints:BTT_clone(cluster.trackHints||[]),createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),createdAction:BTT_nowAction(),retries:0,authoringResponses:0,maintenanceContinues:0,lastError:"",fieldValues:{},fieldStatus:{},fieldErrors:{},sectionStatus:{},sectionErrors:{},derived:{track_count:trackN},evidence:evidence,events:BTT_clone(cluster.events||[]),kickoffNoticeEmitted:false,lastRequestedFields:[],lastPromptMode:0,extension:{kind:"new_track",entity:entity,riskId:active.id,riskName:active.name,cardTitle:active.sourceCardTitle,trackIndex:trackIndex,trackNumber:trackN,clusterKey:cluster.key,responseFamily:cluster.responseFamily,responseChannels:BTT_clone(cluster.responseChannels||{}),existingTracks:(active.tracks||[]).map(function(t){return {id:t.id,name:t.name||t.id};})}};
  var inf=BTT_devInferGeneratedDeliveries(pending),delivery=inf.track.join(", ");if(delivery)pending.derived["track_"+trackN+"_delivery"]=delivery;pending.derived.deliveryScores=inf.scores;
  pending.fieldStatus["track_"+trackN+"_name"]="missing";pending.fieldStatus["track_"+trackN+"_triggers"]="missing";pending.fieldStatus["track_"+trackN+"_success_1_effect"]="missing";
  BTT_devInitializeAuthoringPlan(pending,cfg);g.pending=pending;cluster.status="AUTHORING_QUEUED";cluster.queuedTurn=BTT_getTurn();
  BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","NEW TRACK EXTENSION QUEUED | "+active.name+" | TRACK "+trackN+" | response="+cluster.responseFamily+" | score="+BTT_round(cluster.score,2)+" | events="+cluster.events.length);
  BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("NEW TRACK EXTENSION QUALIFIED | risk="+active.name+" | track_number="+trackN+" | score="+BTT_round(cluster.score,2)+" | response_family="+cluster.responseFamily);return true;
}
function BTT_devUpdateExistingRiskProgression(rows,parse){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),entities=BTT_ensureState().entities||{},turn=BTT_getTurn();
  for(var r=0;r<(rows||[]).length;r++){
    var row=rows[r],meta=entities[row.receiver];if(!meta||!meta.developableRisks)continue;var activeList=meta.developableRisks.active||[];
    for(var ai=0;ai<activeList.length;ai++){
      var active=activeList[ai],best=null;
      for(var ti=0;ti<(active.tracks||[]).length;ti++){var track=active.tracks[ti],coverage=BTT_devTrackResponseCoverage(track,row);if(!best||coverage>best.coverage)best={track:track,index:ti,coverage:coverage};}
      if(!best||best.coverage<Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_track_match_min||0.46)){
        var riskText=[active.name,active.acquisitionPrototype,active.sourceDescriptor].join(" "),riskCoverage=Math.max(BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(riskText,row.raw),(active.sourceDescriptor&&row.sourceDescriptor===active.sourceDescriptor)?0.62:0,(active.sourceEntityId&&row.sourceEntity===active.sourceEntityId)?0.66:0);
        if(riskCoverage>=0.42){
          var nw=BTT_devProgressionWatch(active),nk="__new__|"+String(row.responseFamily||"response"),nc=nw.clusters[nk];
          if(!nc)nc=nw.clusters[nk]={key:nk,trackId:"[possible new track]",trackIndex:-1,responseFamily:String(row.responseFamily||"response"),responseChannels:{},events:[],score:0,status:"POSSIBLE_NEW_TRACK",createdTurn:turn,lastTurn:turn,retryAfterTurn:0,authored:false,mechanism:row.mechanism,trackHints:BTT_clone(row.trackHints||[])};
          var ndupe=false;for(var ne=0;ne<nc.events.length;ne++)if(nc.events[ne].action===row.action&&BTT_observationTextSimilarity(nc.events[ne].raw,row.raw)>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dedupe_similarity||0.86)){ndupe=true;break;}
          if(!ndupe)nc.events.push({turn:turn,action:row.action,raw:BTT_clip(row.raw,420),strength:row.rawScore,trackCoverage:BTT_round(riskCoverage,3),novelty:1,responseFamily:row.responseFamily,responseChannels:BTT_clone(row.responseChannels||{})});
          nc.events=nc.events.filter(function(ev){return turn-Number(ev.turn||0)<=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns||18);});for(var nch in (row.responseChannels||{}))if(row.responseChannels.hasOwnProperty(nch))nc.responseChannels[nch]=Math.max(Number(nc.responseChannels[nch])||0,Number(row.responseChannels[nch])||0);BTT_devProgressionClusterScore(nc,cfg);nc.lastTurn=turn;
          var substantive=String(nc.responseFamily||"response")!=="response"||Object.keys(nc.responseChannels||{}).length>0,qualified=substantive&&nc.events.length>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_observations)&&nc.score>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_confidence);
          if(qualified){nc.status=active.generated?"POSSIBLE_NEW_TRACK":"CREATOR_PROFILE_HOLD";BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","PROGRESSION WATCH | "+active.name+" | "+nc.status+" | response="+nc.responseFamily+" | score="+BTT_round(nc.score,2)+" | events="+nc.events.length);if(active.generated)BTT_devQueueNewTrackExtension(row.receiver,meta,active,nc,cfg);}else{nc.status="POSSIBLE_NEW_TRACK";BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","PROGRESSION WATCH | "+active.name+" | POSSIBLE_NEW_TRACK | response="+nc.responseFamily+" | score="+BTT_round(nc.score,2)+" | events="+nc.events.length);}
          nw.lastTurn=turn;
        }
        continue;
      }
      var novelty=BTT_devProgressionNovelty(best.track,row),watch=BTT_devProgressionWatch(active),key=best.track.id+"|"+String(row.responseFamily||"response"),cluster=watch.clusters[key];
      if(!cluster)cluster=watch.clusters[key]={key:key,trackId:best.track.id,trackIndex:best.index,responseFamily:String(row.responseFamily||"response"),responseChannels:{},events:[],score:0,status:"EXISTING_TRACK",createdTurn:turn,lastTurn:turn,retryAfterTurn:0,authored:false,mechanism:row.mechanism,trackHints:BTT_clone(row.trackHints||[])};
      var duplicate=false;for(var ei=0;ei<cluster.events.length;ei++)if(cluster.events[ei].action===row.action&&BTT_observationTextSimilarity(cluster.events[ei].raw,row.raw)>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dedupe_similarity||0.86)){duplicate=true;break;}
      if(!duplicate)cluster.events.push({turn:turn,action:row.action,raw:BTT_clip(row.raw,420),strength:row.rawScore,trackCoverage:BTT_round(best.coverage,3),novelty:BTT_round(novelty,3),responseFamily:row.responseFamily,responseChannels:BTT_clone(row.responseChannels||{})});
      cluster.events=cluster.events.filter(function(ev){return turn-Number(ev.turn||0)<=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns||18);});for(var ch in (row.responseChannels||{}))if(row.responseChannels.hasOwnProperty(ch))cluster.responseChannels[ch]=Math.max(Number(cluster.responseChannels[ch])||0,Number(row.responseChannels[ch])||0);BTT_devProgressionClusterScore(cluster,cfg);cluster.lastTurn=turn;
      var higher=novelty>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_novel_effect_min||0.38)&&cluster.events.length>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_observations)&&cluster.score>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_confidence);
      if(higher){cluster.status=active.generated?"POSSIBLE_HIGHER_EFFECT":"CREATOR_PROFILE_HOLD";BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","PROGRESSION WATCH | "+active.name+" / "+best.track.id+" | "+cluster.status+" | response="+cluster.responseFamily+" | score="+BTT_round(cluster.score,2)+" | events="+cluster.events.length+" | novelty="+BTT_round(novelty,2));if(active.generated)BTT_devQueueProgressionExtension(row.receiver,meta,active,best.track,best.index,cluster,cfg);}else cluster.status="EXISTING_TRACK";watch.lastTurn=turn;
    }
  }
}
function BTT_devOutcomeSatisfied(meta,o,active){if(o.type==="traits"){var sl=meta.slots[o.slot],yes=!!sl;for(var i=0;i<o.traits.length;i++)if(!(sl&&sl.traits&&sl.traits.indexOf(o.traits[i])>=0))yes=false;return o.operation==="remove"?!yes:yes;}if(o.type==="axis")return false;if(o.type==="condition")return o.operation==="remove"?!BTT_devHasCondition(meta,o.condition):BTT_devHasCondition(meta,o.condition);if(o.type==="conditionedTrigger"){var tr=meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers||[];return tr.some(function(x){return x.id===o.id;});}return false;}
function BTT_applyDevOutcome(meta,active,track,o){if(o.type==="traits"){for(var i=0;i<o.traits.length;i++){if(o.operation==="remove")BTT_removeTraitDirect(meta,o.slot,o.traits[i]);else BTT_addTraitDirect(meta,o.slot,o.traits[i]);}return;}if(o.type==="axis"){var before=o.axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:Number(meta.appearance.axes[o.axis])||0;if(o.operation==="="){var val=BTT_round(o.axis==="fem"?BTT_clamp(o.value,-100,100):BTT_clamp(o.value,0,100),2);if(o.axis==="fem"){meta.overallFem=val;meta.appearance.axes.fem=val;}else meta.appearance.axes[o.axis]=val;}else BTT_addAxis(meta,o.axis,(o.operation==="-"?-1:1)*Math.abs(o.value),"dev risk "+active.id,"DEV_RISK");var after=o.axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:Number(meta.appearance.axes[o.axis])||0;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK AXIS | "+active.name+" / "+track.id+" | "+o.axis+" "+BTT_round(before,2)+"→"+BTT_round(after,2));return;}if(o.type==="condition"){if(o.operation==="remove"){for(var c=meta.conditions.length-1;c>=0;c--)if(BTT_conditionType(meta.conditions[c].type)===o.condition)meta.conditions.splice(c,1);}else BTT_addCondition(meta,{type:o.condition,slot:"system",count:1,source:"Dev Risk: "+active.name,sourceEntityId:active.sourceEntityId||"",confidence:1,startedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),trigger:track.id,active:true});return;}if(o.type==="conditionedTrigger"){var arr=meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers=meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers||[];if(!arr.some(function(x){return x.id===o.id;}))arr.push({id:o.id,description:o.description,sourceRisk:active.id,acquiredTurn:BTT_getTurn()});}}
function BTT_devTrackComplete(meta,active,track,st){var max=0;for(var i=0;i<track.outcomes.length;i++)max=Math.max(max,track.outcomes[i].successAt||1);st.complete=max>0&&st.successCount>=max&&track.outcomes.every(function(o){return o.type==="axis"?st.successCount>=o.successAt:BTT_devOutcomeSatisfied(meta,o,active);});return st.complete;}
function BTT_devApplySuccess(meta,active,track,st){st.successCount++;for(var i=0;i<track.outcomes.length;i++){var o=track.outcomes[i];if(st.successCount<o.successAt)continue;if(o.type==="axis"||!BTT_devOutcomeSatisfied(meta,o,active)){BTT_applyDevOutcome(meta,active,track,o);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK OUTCOME | "+active.name+" / "+track.id+" | success="+st.successCount+" | outcome="+o.type+(o.slot?":"+o.slot:"")+(o.condition?":"+o.condition:""));}}BTT_devTrackComplete(meta,active,track,st);}
function BTT_devTrackEvidence(meta,entity,active,track,f,parse,work){
  var status=f.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(f.raw,f.quoted),own=BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity);if(!status.actual||f.actuality<0.50||own<0.48)return {score:0};
  var windowRaw=BTT_devEventWindow(parse,entity,f.index),sem=0;for(var i=0;i<track.triggers.length;i++)sem=Math.max(sem,BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(track.triggers[i],windowRaw));if(!track.triggers.length)sem=BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(active.acquisitionPrototype||active.name,windowRaw);
  var delivery=BTT_devDeliveryScore(track.delivery.length?track.delivery:["exposure"],{raw:windowRaw}),qual=BTT_devQualifierScore(track.qualifiers,windowRaw),cfg=BTT_getConfig(),min=0.22+cfg.dev_risk_assumption_narrowness*0.32,qualifierMin=0.50+cfg.dev_risk_assumption_narrowness*0.20;
  if(sem<min||delivery.score<0.30+cfg.dev_risk_assumption_narrowness*0.30)return {score:0};if(track.qualifiers.length&&qual<qualifierMin)return {score:0};
  var sourceSlot={slot:"",confidence:0},route=null;if(track.sourceSlotTraits&&track.sourceSlotTraits.length){sourceSlot=BTT_reproductiveSourceSlot(f,{entity:entity,confidence:own},"",meta,parse);if(!sourceSlot.slot){var inferred=BTT_uniqueSlotWithTraits(meta,track.sourceSlotTraits);if(inferred.slot&&sem>=0.55&&delivery.score>=0.55)sourceSlot=inferred;}if(!sourceSlot.slot||sourceSlot.confidence<0.56)return {score:0};for(var st=0;st<track.sourceSlotTraits.length;st++)if(!BTT_slotHas(meta,sourceSlot.slot,track.sourceSlotTraits[st]))return {score:0};}
  var routed=track.receiverRouting&&Object.keys(track.receiverRouting).length;if(routed){route=BTT_deliveryDestination(f,entity,parse,work||{});if(!route.receiver||route.receiver===entity||route.confidence<0.72||route.margin<0.10)return {score:0};var routeSlot=route.finalSlot||route.entrySlot;if(!routeSlot||!track.receiverRouting[routeSlot])return {score:0};}
  return {score:BTT_round(sem+delivery.score+Math.min(1,Math.max(0.45,own))+Math.max(0,qual-0.50)*0.35+(sourceSlot.slot?0.16:0)+(route?Math.max(0,route.confidence-0.65)*0.35:0),2),semantic:sem,delivery:delivery.score,deliveryProfile:delivery.profile,qualifier:qual,sourceSlot:sourceSlot,route:route};
}
function BTT_devResolutionEvidence(f){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),score=0,sem=BTT_conditionSemantics(f||{raw:raw});for(var i=0;i<sem.length;i++)if(sem[i].mode==="resolution")score=Math.max(score,Number(sem[i].semantic)||0);
  var cues=["remove","removed","removal","cleanse","cleansed","cure","cured","purge","purged","extract","extracted","dispel","dispelled","break curse","lift curse","treat","treated","recover","recovered","detox","exorcise","banish","sever","heal"];
  for(i=0;i<cues.length;i++)score=Math.max(score,BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(cues[i],raw));return BTT_clamp(score,0,1);
}
function BTT_devRemovalHit(meta,entity,active,f){
  if(!active.removal||!active.removal.length)return false;if((f.eventStatus&&!f.eventStatus.actual)||f.actuality<0.55||BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity)<0.55)return false;
  /* Similarity to the configured cure is necessary but not sufficient: the
     current event must itself carry resolution/removal semantics. This keeps
     acquisition text from accidentally matching e.g. "tail parasite removal". */
  if(BTT_devResolutionEvidence(f)<0.52)return false;var best=0;for(var i=0;i<active.removal.length;i++)best=Math.max(best,BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(active.removal[i],f.raw));return best>=0.62;
}
function BTT_devRoutedAcquisitionCompatibility(sourceEntity,targetMeta,p,a,ev,receiverSlot){
  var ex=BTT_devExclusion(targetMeta,p,a);if(ex)return {pass:false,reason:ex};if(!BTT_devReceiverQualifies(targetMeta,a))return {pass:false,reason:"RECEIVER_REQUIREMENT"};if(a.acquireSlot&&a.acquireSlot!==receiverSlot)return {pass:false,reason:"RECEIVER_SLOT_MISMATCH"};var sourceMeta=BTT_findEntity(sourceEntity);if((a.sourceTraits.length||a.sourceHeritage.length||a.sourceProfiles.length||a.sourceConditions.length||a.sourceSlotTraits.length)&&!sourceMeta)return {pass:false,reason:"SOURCE_UNRESOLVED"};
  if(sourceMeta&&a.sourceTraits.length&&!BTT_hasAnyTrait(sourceMeta,a.sourceTraits))return {pass:false,reason:"SOURCE_TRAIT"};if(sourceMeta&&a.sourceHeritage.length&&a.sourceHeritage.indexOf(BTT_normId(sourceMeta.heritage))<0)return {pass:false,reason:"SOURCE_HERITAGE"};if(sourceMeta&&a.sourceConditions.length){for(var c=0;c<a.sourceConditions.length;c++)if(!BTT_devHasCondition(sourceMeta,a.sourceConditions[c]))return {pass:false,reason:"SOURCE_CONDITION"};}
  if(sourceMeta&&a.sourceProfiles.length){var refs=[];if(sourceMeta.heritage)refs.push(BTT_normId(sourceMeta.heritage));if(sourceMeta.gender)refs.push(BTT_normId(sourceMeta.gender));var ok=false;for(var q=0;q<a.sourceProfiles.length;q++)if(refs.indexOf(a.sourceProfiles[q])>=0)ok=true;if(!ok)return {pass:false,reason:"SOURCE_PROFILE"};}
  var sourceSlot=ev&&ev.sourceSlot||{slot:"",confidence:0};if(a.sourceSlotTraits.length){if(!sourceMeta||!sourceSlot.slot||Number(sourceSlot.confidence)<0.56)return {pass:false,reason:"SOURCE_SLOT_UNRESOLVED"};for(var st=0;st<a.sourceSlotTraits.length;st++)if(!BTT_slotHas(sourceMeta,sourceSlot.slot,a.sourceSlotTraits[st]))return {pass:false,reason:"SOURCE_SLOT_TRAIT:"+a.sourceSlotTraits[st]};}
  return {pass:true,sourceSlot:sourceSlot};
}
function BTT_devRouteTargetAcquisition(sourceEntity,active,track,ev,work){
  var route=ev&&ev.route;if(!route||!route.receiver)return false;var slot=route.finalSlot||route.entrySlot,ids=track.receiverRouting&&track.receiverRouting[slot]||[],targetMeta=work[route.receiver]||BTT_findEntity(route.receiver);if(!targetMeta||!ids.length)return false;var changed=false;
  for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){var p=BTT_devRiskProfile(ids[i]);if(!p||BTT_devActive(targetMeta,p.id))continue;var candidates=[];for(var j=0;j<p.acquisitions.length;j++){var a=p.acquisitions[j];if(a.acquireSlot&&a.acquireSlot!==slot)continue;var compat=BTT_devRoutedAcquisitionCompatibility(sourceEntity,targetMeta,p,a,ev,slot);if(compat.pass)candidates.push({a:a,compat:compat});else BTT_touchDebug(targetMeta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ROUTE BLOCK | source="+sourceEntity+" | source_risk="+active.name+" | receiver_slot="+slot+" | target="+p.name+" | acquisition="+a.id+" | reason="+compat.reason);}
    if(!candidates.length)continue;var chosen=candidates[0].a;for(j=0;j<candidates.length;j++)if(candidates[j].a.acquireSlot===slot){chosen=candidates[j].a;break;}BTT_devAcquire(targetMeta,route.receiver,p,chosen,{source:{entity:sourceEntity,descriptor:"routed from "+active.name},sourceSlot:ev.sourceSlot||{slot:"",confidence:0},deliveryProfile:ev.deliveryProfile||"routed",total:Math.max(Number(ev.score)||0,Number(route.confidence)||0)});BTT_touchDebug(targetMeta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ROUTED ACQUISITION | source="+sourceEntity+" | source_risk="+active.name+" | receiver_slot="+slot+" | target="+p.name+" | acquisition="+chosen.id+" | entry_slot="+(route.entrySlot||slot)+(route.finalSlot&&route.finalSlot!==route.entrySlot?" | final_slot="+route.finalSlot:"")+" | confidence="+BTT_round(route.confidence,2));changed=true;
  }return changed;
}
function BTT_processDevelopableRisks(work,parse){var cfg=BTT_getConfig();if(!cfg.dev_risk_enabled)return;var profiles=BTT_devRiskProfiles();for(var entity in work)if(work.hasOwnProperty(entity)){var meta=work[entity];meta.developableRisks=meta.developableRisks||{active:[],conditionedTriggers:[],acquisitionHistory:{}};
    for(var rid in profiles)if(profiles.hasOwnProperty(rid)&&!BTT_devActive(meta,rid)){var p=profiles[rid],best=null;for(var ai=0;ai<p.acquisitions.length;ai++){var a=p.acquisitions[ai],histKey=p.id+"|"+a.id;for(var fi=0;fi<parse.fragments.length;fi++){var f=parse.fragments[fi],ev=BTT_devAcquisitionEvidence(meta,entity,p,a,f,parse,work);if(!ev.pass)continue;var hist=Math.min(cfg.dev_risk_acquire_history_bonus_max,BTT_devAcquireHistory(meta,histKey,a.historyTurns)),total=BTT_round(ev.score+hist,2);if(!best||total>best.total)best={p:p,a:a,f:f,ev:ev,total:total,history:hist,histKey:histKey};} }
      if(best){if(best.total>=best.a.threshold){best.ev.total=best.total;BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,best.f,{kind:"devrisk_acquisition",domain:"devrisk",name:best.p.id,owner:entity,ownerConfidence:best.ev.ownership||BTT_fragmentMention(best.f,entity),sourceEntity:best.ev.source&&best.ev.source.entity||"",sourceDescriptor:best.ev.source&&best.ev.source.descriptor||"",sourceConfidence:best.ev.source&&best.ev.source.confidence||0,slot:best.a.acquireSlot||"",slotConfidence:best.a.acquireSlot?0.90:0,mechanism:best.ev.deliveryProfile||"developable-risk",mechanismTags:BTT_unique(["developable-risk","persistent",best.ev.deliveryProfile||"exposure"].concat(best.a.sourceSlotTraits&&best.a.sourceSlotTraits.length?["source-slot-gated"]:[]))});BTT_devAcquire(meta,entity,best.p,best.a,best.ev);delete meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory[best.histKey];}else if(best.total>=cfg.dev_risk_acquire_store_min){BTT_devStoreAcquireHistory(meta,best.histKey,Math.min(1.2,best.ev.score*0.45),best.f.raw);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ACQUIRE HOLD | "+best.p.name+" | current="+BTT_round(best.ev.score,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(best.history,2)+" | total="+best.total+" < "+BTT_round(best.a.threshold,2));}}
    }
    for(var ar=meta.developableRisks.active.length-1;ar>=0;ar--){var active=meta.developableRisks.active[ar],removed=false;for(var rf=0;rf<parse.fragments.length&&!removed;rf++)if(BTT_devRemovalHit(meta,entity,active,parse.fragments[rf])){meta.developableRisks.active.splice(ar,1);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK REMOVED | "+active.name+" | trigger="+BTT_clip(parse.fragments[rf].raw,80));removed=true;}if(removed)continue;
      for(var ti=0;ti<active.tracks.length;ti++){var track=active.tracks[ti],st=BTT_devTrackState(active,track.id);if(st.complete)continue;var bestE=null;for(var fi2=0;fi2<parse.fragments.length;fi2++){var fe=parse.fragments[fi2],e=BTT_devTrackEvidence(meta,entity,active,track,fe,parse,work);if(e.score&&(!bestE||e.score>bestE.score))bestE={f:fe,score:e.score,e:e};}if(!bestE||bestE.score<track.threshold)continue;var sig=BTT_eventSignature("devrisk:"+active.id+":"+track.id,bestE.f,entity+"|"+BTT_nowAction());st.seen=st.seen||{};if(st.seen[sig])continue;BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,bestE.f,{kind:"devrisk_exposure",domain:"devrisk",name:active.id+"/"+track.id,owner:entity,ownerConfidence:Math.max(0.55,BTT_fragmentMention(bestE.f,entity)),sourceEntity:bestE.e&&bestE.e.route?entity:"",sourceConfidence:bestE.e&&bestE.e.route?1:0,slot:bestE.e&&bestE.e.sourceSlot&&bestE.e.sourceSlot.slot||"",slotConfidence:bestE.e&&bestE.e.sourceSlot&&bestE.e.sourceSlot.confidence||0,mechanism:bestE.e&&bestE.e.deliveryProfile||"developable-risk",mechanismTags:BTT_unique(["developable-risk","conditioning","repeated",bestE.e&&bestE.e.deliveryProfile||"exposure"])});for(var sk in st.seen)if(st.seen.hasOwnProperty(sk)&&BTT_getTurn()-Number(st.seen[sk])>track.memoryTurns)delete st.seen[sk];st.seen[sig]=BTT_getTurn();st.exposureCount++;st.lastExposureTurn=BTT_getTurn();var chance=Math.min(track.maximumChance,track.baseChance+Math.max(0,st.exposureCount-1)*track.repeatIncrease),roll=BTT_hashRoll(entity+"|devrisk|"+active.id+"|"+track.id+"|"+st.exposureCount+"|"+BTT_nowAction()),ok=roll<=chance;st.lastRoll={turn:BTT_getTurn(),chance:chance,roll:roll,success:ok};BTT_touchDebug(meta,"devrisks","DEV RISK ROLL | "+active.name+" / "+track.id+" | exposure="+st.exposureCount+" | confidence="+BTT_round(bestE.score,2)+" | chance="+BTT_round(chance,2)+" | roll="+roll+" | "+(ok?"SUCCESS":"MISS"));if(ok){BTT_devApplySuccess(meta,active,track,st);if(track.receiverRouting&&Object.keys(track.receiverRouting).length)BTT_devRouteTargetAcquisition(entity,active,track,bestE.e,work);}}
    }
  }}
function BTT_devRiskSummary(meta){var lines=["--- DEVELOPABLE RISKS ---"],a=meta&&meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.active||[];if(!a.length)lines.push("No acquired Developable Risks.");for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){lines.push(a[i].name+" | acquired turn "+a[i].acquiredTurn+" | persistence="+(a[i].persistence||"acquired")+(a[i].generated?" | AI-GENERATED":""));for(var t=0;t<a[i].tracks.length;t++){var st=BTT_devTrackState(a[i],a[i].tracks[t].id);lines.push("  - "+a[i].tracks[t].id+": "+(st.complete?"COMPLETE":"ACTIVE")+" | exposures="+st.exposureCount+" | successes="+st.successCount+(st.lastRoll?" | last="+st.lastRoll.roll+"/"+st.lastRoll.chance:""));}var watch=BTT_devProgressionWatch(a[i]),clusters=[];for(var ck in watch.clusters)if(watch.clusters.hasOwnProperty(ck)){var c=watch.clusters[ck];if(BTT_getTurn()-Number(c.lastTurn||0)<=Number(BTT_getConfig().dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns||18))clusters.push(c);}clusters.sort(function(x,y){return Number(y.score)-Number(x.score);});if(clusters.length){lines.push("  Progression Watch:");for(var wi=0;wi<Math.min(5,clusters.length);wi++){var w=clusters[wi];lines.push("    - "+w.trackId+" / "+w.responseFamily+" | "+w.status+" | events="+(w.events||[]).length+" | score="+BTT_round(w.score,2)+(w.authored?" | PROFILE_EXTENDED":""));}}}var tr=meta&&meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers||[];if(tr.length){lines.push("Persistent Triggers:");for(var j=0;j<tr.length;j++)lines.push("  - "+tr[j].id+": "+tr[j].description);}return lines;}function BTT_devDiscoveryRelationSupport(raw){
  raw=String(raw||"");var score=0;
  /* Generic spatial "under" is intentionally not causal. Only an explicitly
     controlling/effect-bearing construction may contribute relation support. */
  if(/\b(?:causes?|caused|makes?|made|forces?|forced|leads? to|led to|results? in|resulted in|because|through|whenever|each time|after|following|under\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:influence|effect|control|curse|spell|pact|contract))\b/i.test(raw))score+=0.14;
  if(/\b(?:pulse|pulses|pulsed|flare|flares|flared|sting|stings|burn|burns|pressure|presses|tighten|tightens|cinch|cinches|fit|fits|refit|resize|reshape|adjust|correct|reward|relief|sooth|comfort|compel|urge|encourage|adapt|attun|learn|persist|remain|automatic|easier)\w*\b/i.test(raw))score+=0.12;
  return BTT_clamp(score,0,0.24);
}
function BTT_devMechanismLocalTags(raw,mechanism,sourceDescriptor,tags){
  /* A Developable-Risk family owns only semantic tags that are causally local to
     its resolved mechanism/source. Merely mentioning clothing beside compliance,
     for example, is not evidence that the clothing caused compliance. */
  raw=String(raw||"");mechanism=String(mechanism||"narrative");sourceDescriptor=BTT_lower(String(sourceDescriptor||""));tags=BTT_unique(tags||[]);
  var out=[],core={contract:1,clothing:1,curse:1,product:1,parasite:1},generic={conditioning:1,transformation:1,persistent:1,repeated:1,reward:1,aversive:1,obedience:1,social:1,behavioral:1};
  if(core[mechanism])out.push(mechanism);
  var sourceNamed=(mechanism==="contract"&&/(?:pact|contract|binding|wager)/i.test(raw+" "+sourceDescriptor))||(mechanism==="clothing"&&/(?:cloth|garment|fabric|silk|dress|skirt|wardrobe|chemise|weave)/i.test(raw+" "+sourceDescriptor))||(mechanism==="curse"&&/(?:curse|hex|spell|magic|enchant)/i.test(raw+" "+sourceDescriptor))||(mechanism==="product"&&/(?:pill|potion|tonic|drug|dose|serum|product)/i.test(raw+" "+sourceDescriptor))||(mechanism==="parasite"&&/(?:parasite|infest|implant|host)/i.test(raw+" "+sourceDescriptor));
  function explicitBehaviorLink(){
    if(mechanism==="contract")return sourceNamed;
    var mechNoun=mechanism==="clothing"?"(?:cloth(?:ing)?|garment|fabric|silk|dress|skirt|chemise|weave)":mechanism==="curse"?"(?:curse|hex|spell|magic|enchantment)":mechanism==="product"?"(?:pill|potion|tonic|drug|dose|serum)":mechanism==="parasite"?"(?:parasite|infestation|implant|symbiote)":"(?:source)";
    var behavior="(?:obey|obedien|comply|compliance|submit|submission|behavior|behaviour|habit|urge|compel|reward|punish|relief|pleasure|pressure)";
    return new RegExp("\\b"+mechNoun+"\\b[^.!?]{0,72}\\b(?:cause|make|force|compel|urge|reward|punish|condition|reinforce|teach|train)\\w*[^.!?]{0,52}\\b"+behavior,"i").test(raw)||new RegExp("\\b"+behavior+"\\w*[^.!?]{0,52}\\b(?:because of|from|through|due to|by)\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b"+mechNoun+"\\b","i").test(raw);
  }
  var behaviorLinked=explicitBehaviorLink();
  for(var i=0;i<tags.length;i++){
    var tag=tags[i];
    if(core[tag]){if(tag===mechanism&&!out.includes(tag))out.push(tag);continue;}
    if(!generic[tag])continue;
    if(tag==="obedience"||tag==="behavioral"||tag==="social"){if(behaviorLinked||mechanism==="contract"&&sourceNamed)out.push(tag);continue;}
    if(tag==="reward"||tag==="aversive"){if(mechanism==="contract"&&sourceNamed||behaviorLinked||sourceNamed&&/\b(?:reward|relief|warmth|rightness|sting|burn|pressure|pain|sooth|comfort|pleasure)\w*\b/i.test(raw))out.push(tag);continue;}
    /* Conditioning/transformation/persistence/repetition describe the mechanism's
       own progression when the source family is resolved. */
    if(sourceNamed||mechanism!=="narrative")out.push(tag);
  }
  return BTT_unique(out);
}
function BTT_devDiscoveryEvidenceComponents(f,row){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),status=f.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(f.raw,f.quoted),channels=BTT_clone(row&&row.responseChannels||BTT_responseChannelMap(f.raw)),keys=Object.keys(channels),vals=[],k;
  for(k in channels)if(channels.hasOwnProperty(k))vals.push(Number(channels[k])||0);vals.sort(function(a,b){return b-a;});
  var top=vals[0]||0,second=vals[1]||0,third=vals[2]||0,direct=BTT_responseDirectCue(f.raw),change=BTT_clamp(Math.max(0,Number(f.shared&&f.shared.change&&f.shared.change.score)||Number(row&&row.changeScore)||0),0,1),ownerPass=Number(row&&row.ownerConfidence||0)>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_owner_min||0.55),actualPass=!!status.actual&&Number(f.actuality)>=0.45;
  /* Admission and confidence are deliberately separate. A small but direct owner
     response may count as a recurring logical event while contributing too little
     confidence to author a risk until stronger progression evidence corroborates it. */
  var channelPass=top>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_channel_min||0.30)||change>=0.24;
  var resolvedMechanism=String(row&&row.mechanism||"")&&!/^(?:unresolved|narrative|response)$/.test(String(row&&row.mechanism||""));
  var mechanismScore=resolvedMechanism?0.18:(row&&(row.sourceEntity||row.sourceDescriptor)?0.10:0);
  var progressive=(Number(channels.adaptive)||0),dependency=(Number(channels.dependency)||0),morph=(Number(channels.morphological)||0),host=(Number(channels.host)||0),chemical=(Number(channels.chemical)||0),reward=(Number(channels.reward)||0),aversive=(Number(channels.aversive)||0),mental=(Number(channels.mental)||0),behavioral=(Number(channels.behavioral)||0),sensory=(Number(channels.sensory)||0),phys=Number(channels.physiological)||0;
  var strength=0.22+0.50*top+0.22*second+0.12*third+0.42*progressive+0.36*dependency+0.28*morph+0.22*host+0.12*chemical+mechanismScore+0.18*change+(direct?0.08:0);
  strength=BTT_clamp(strength,0,2.35);
  var reason=!actualPass?"EVENT_NOT_ACTUAL":!ownerPass?"RECEIVER_UNRESOLVED":!channelPass?"NO_RESPONSE_OR_CHANGE_CHANNEL":"ADMIT";
  return {admitted:reason==="ADMIT",reason:reason,strength:BTT_round(strength,3),components:{mechanism:BTT_round(mechanismScore,3),effect:BTT_round(Math.max(mental,behavioral,reward,aversive,sensory,phys,morph,host,dependency),3),progression:BTT_round(progressive,3),relation:0,change:BTT_round(change*0.18,3),durable:BTT_round(Math.max(progressive,dependency)*0.18,3)},mechanism:String(row&&row.mechanism||"unresolved"),mechanismTags:BTT_clone(row&&row.mechanismTags||[]),responseChannels:channels,responseFamily:BTT_responseDominantFamily(channels)};
}function BTT_devTagSimilarity(a,b){a=BTT_unique(a||[]);b=BTT_unique(b||[]);if(!a.length||!b.length)return 0;var set={},hit=0;for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)set[a[i]]=1;for(i=0;i<b.length;i++)if(set[b[i]])hit++;return hit/Math.max(a.length,b.length);}
function BTT_devRiskFingerprint(row){var mechanism=String(row&&row.mechanism||"unresolved"),src=BTT_normId(row&&row.sourceEntity||row&&row.sourceDescriptor||"");return mechanism+(src?"@"+src:"");}function BTT_devCandidateLabel(row){var mechanism=String(row&&row.mechanism||"unresolved").replace(/-/g," ");return mechanism==="unresolved"?"unresolved causal risk":mechanism+" risk";}function BTT_devEffectVerb(raw){return /\b(?:affect|alter|adapt|attun|change|condition|constrict|correct|encourage|force|learn|mold|mould|reshape|resize|reward|punish|reinforce|tighten|transform|urge|compel|make|cause|persist|remain|become|grow|soften|narrow|widen|shift|adjust)\w*\b/i.test(String(raw||""));}
function BTT_devResolveAffectedOwner(parse,f){
  /* Canonical direct-response observations are the primary ownership source. */
  var obs=parse&&parse.observations||[],rows=[];for(var oi=0;oi<obs.length;oi++){var o=obs[oi];if(Number(o.fragmentIndex)!==Number(f.index)||o.kind!=="response"||!o.owner)continue;rows.push({entity:o.affectedOwner||o.receiver||o.owner,score:Math.max(Number(o.ownerConfidence)||0,0.60),reason:"canonical_response"});}
  if(rows.length)return BTT_pickResolvedObservation(rows,0.55,0.08);
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),effect=BTT_devEffectVerb(raw),entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse),phase=String(parse&&parse.phase||"");
  if((effect||BTT_responseDirectCue(raw))&&phase==="input"&&!f.quoted&&/\b(?:I|me|my|mine|myself)\b/i.test(raw))rows.push({entity:"Player",score:1.02,reason:"input_effect_target"});
  if((effect||BTT_responseDirectCue(raw))&&!f.quoted&&/\b(?:you|your|yourself)\b/i.test(raw))rows.push({entity:"Player",score:0.99,reason:"second_person_effect_target"});
  var effectKinds={change:1,trait:1,axis:1,structure:1,condition_event:1,condition_state:1,condition_resolution:1,devrisk_acquisition:1,devrisk_exposure:1,profile_risk_exposure:1};
  for(oi=0;oi<obs.length;oi++){o=obs[oi];if(Number(o.fragmentIndex)!==Number(f.index)||!o.owner)continue;if(effectKinds[o.kind]||Number(o.changeScore)>0.25)rows.push({entity:o.affectedOwner||o.receiver||o.owner,score:Math.max(0.70,Number(o.ownerConfidence)||0),reason:"atomic_effect:"+String(o.kind||"event")});}
  for(var ei=0;ei<entities.length;ei++){var entity=entities[ei],aliases=BTT_entityAliases(entity),explicit=0;for(var ai=0;ai<aliases.length;ai++){var a=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[ai]);if(!a||/^(?:you|your|yourself|he|she|they|him|her|them|his|their)$/i.test(a))continue;var esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(a).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");if(new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"(?:['’]s)?\\b[^.!?]{0,45}\\b(?:body|skin|shape|form|posture|voice|mind|behavior|behaviour|chest|waist|hips?|hair|eyes?)\\b","i").test(raw))explicit=Math.max(explicit,0.96);if((effect||BTT_responseDirectCue(raw))&&new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"\\b[^.!?]{0,42}\\b(?:feel|sense|experience|react|respond|become|change|adapt|obey|resist|crave)\\w*","i").test(raw))explicit=Math.max(explicit,0.95);}if(explicit)rows.push({entity:entity,score:explicit,reason:"named_effect_target"});}
  if(!rows.length&&(effect||BTT_responseDirectCue(raw))){var generic=BTT_resolveGenericObservationOwner(parse,f);if(generic.entity)rows.push({entity:generic.entity,score:Number(generic.confidence)||0,reason:"generic_effect_owner"});}
  var by={},fold=[];for(var ri=0;ri<rows.length;ri++){var rr=rows[ri];if(!rr.entity)continue;if(!by[rr.entity]||Number(rr.score)>Number(by[rr.entity].score))by[rr.entity]=rr;}for(var re in by)if(by.hasOwnProperty(re))fold.push(by[re]);return BTT_pickResolvedObservation(fold,0.55,0.08);
}function BTT_devTrackHints(tags){var allow=["conditioning","transformation","obedience","social","behavioral","persistent","reward","aversive","repeated","dependency","chemical","host","adaptive","sensory","physiological","morphological","mental"];return BTT_unique((tags||[]).filter(function(x){return allow.indexOf(x)>=0;}));}function BTT_devFamilyMechanism(row){var d=BTT_lower(row&&row.sourceDescriptor||""),tags=row&&row.mechanismTags||[],m=BTT_lower(row&&row.mechanism||"");if(/cloth|garment|wardrobe|fabric|silk|chemise|robe|slip|slipper|shoe|heel|stocking|hosiery|lingerie|underwear|corset|bodice|gown/.test(d))return "clothing";if(/pact|contract|binding/.test(d))return "contract";if(/curse|hex|spell|cursecraft|glamour/.test(d))return "curse";if(/chemical|agent|product|pill|potion|tonic|drug|serum|tincture|draught|nectar/.test(d))return "chemical";if(/biological|parasite|infest|host|slime|organism|implant|tentacle/.test(d))return "biological-host";var core=["biological-host","parasite","chemical","product","contract","clothing","curse"];for(var i=0;i<core.length;i++)if(tags.indexOf(core[i])>=0)return core[i]==="parasite"?"biological-host":core[i]==="product"?"chemical":core[i];if(["contract","clothing","curse","chemical","biological-host"].indexOf(m)>=0)return m;return "unresolved";}
function BTT_devExplicitCausalSource(raw){raw=String(raw||"");if(/\b(?:binding\s+)?pact\b|\bmagical\s+contract\b|\bcontract(?:'s|’s)?\s+(?:magic|pressure|compulsion|reward|pulse|effect)\b/i.test(raw))return {entity:"",descriptor:"binding-pact",confidence:0.94,mechanism:"contract",reason:"explicit_named_mechanism"};if(/\b(?:potion|tincture|draught|elixir|serum|chemical|drug|dose|nectar)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:drink|swallow|ingest|dose|effect|warm|tingl|pleasur|crav|intoxic|sensit|relax|stimulat|sedat|change|adapt)\w*/i.test(raw))return {entity:"",descriptor:"chemical-agent",confidence:0.90,mechanism:"chemical",reason:"explicit_named_mechanism"};if(/\b(?:parasite|parasitic|slime|symbiote|organism|tentacle|infestation|implant|colony)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:host|attach|infest|implant|coloniz|adapt|change|urge|reward|pain|pleasur|sensit|integrat|breed|seed)\w*/i.test(raw))return {entity:"",descriptor:"biological-host-source",confidence:0.91,mechanism:"biological-host",reason:"explicit_named_mechanism"};var apparel=/\b(?:robe|slip|chemise|slipper|shoe|heel|stocking|hosiery|garment|fabric|silk|clothing|dress|skirt|lingerie|underwear|corset|bodice|gown|weave|embroidery)\b/i;/* Explicit curse/hex/spell wording outranks an apparel carrier so a genuinely cursed garment may form a distinct curse hypothesis. Generic enchanted/magical wording does not. */if(/\b(?:curse|cursed|hex|hexed|spell|glamour|cursecraft)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:flare|pulse|compel|force|change|transform|reward|punish|pain|pleasur|adapt|persist|respond|tighten|attun|reshape)\w*/i.test(raw))return {entity:"",descriptor:"curse-magic",confidence:0.91,mechanism:"curse",reason:"explicit_named_mechanism"};if(apparel.test(raw)&&/\b(?:tighten|mold|mould|adjust|move|shift|pulse|warm|caress|learn|attun|adapt|reshape|guide|press|cling|sensat|feel|tweak|brush|soften)\w*/i.test(raw))return {entity:"",descriptor:"enchanted-clothing",confidence:0.90,mechanism:"clothing",reason:"explicit_named_mechanism"};if(!apparel.test(raw)&&/\b(?:enchantment|enchanted|magic|magical)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:flare|pulse|compel|force|change|transform|reward|punish|pain|pleasur|adapt|persist|respond)\w*/i.test(raw))return {entity:"",descriptor:"curse-magic",confidence:0.78,mechanism:"curse",reason:"generic_magic_mechanism"};return null;}
function BTT_devTriggerActorForFragment(parse,f,receiver,source){
  /* Trigger actor and causal source are separate roles. A person can issue an
     order or administer a source without becoming the Developable-Risk source. */
  var rows=[],raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse),known=BTT_ensureState().entities||{},seen={};for(var ei=0;ei<entities.length;ei++)seen[entities[ei]]=1;for(var ke in known)if(known.hasOwnProperty(ke)&&!seen[ke]){seen[ke]=1;entities.push(ke);}
  if(f&&f.subject&&f.subject!==receiver&&Number(f.subjectConfidence)>=0.60)rows.push({entity:f.subject,score:Number(f.subjectConfidence),reason:"fragment_subject"});
  for(var i=0;i<entities.length;i++){var entity=entities[i];if(entity===receiver)continue;var aliases=BTT_entityAliases(entity);for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){var name=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[a]);if(!name||/^(?:he|she|they|him|her|them|his|their|you|your)$/i.test(name))continue;var esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(name).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");if(new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"(?:['’]s)?\\b[^.!?]{0,28}\\b(?:order|command|instruction|touch|administer|give|offer|feed|dress|put|force|cast|enchant|apply|inject|trigger)\\w*","i").test(raw))rows.push({entity:entity,score:0.97,reason:"named_trigger_actor"});if(new RegExp("\\b(?:order|command|instruction)\\w*[^.!?]{0,26}\\b(?:from|by)\\s+"+esc+"\\b","i").test(raw))rows.push({entity:entity,score:0.95,reason:"named_trigger_actor"});}}
  if(source&&source.entity&&source.entity!==receiver)rows.push({entity:source.entity,score:Math.min(0.90,Number(source.confidence)||0.70),reason:"source_actor_fallback"});
  var by={},out=[];for(i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(rows[i].entity&&(!by[rows[i].entity]||rows[i].score>by[rows[i].entity].score))by[rows[i].entity]=rows[i];for(var k in by)if(by.hasOwnProperty(k))out.push(by[k]);return BTT_pickResolvedObservation(out,0.55,0.08);
}
function BTT_devDiscoveryRows(parse,audit){
  audit=Array.isArray(audit)?audit:[];var out=[],obs=parse&&parse.observations||[];
  for(var fi=0;fi<(parse&&parse.fragments||[]).length;fi++){
    var f=parse.fragments[fi],responseObs=[],oi;for(oi=0;oi<obs.length;oi++)if(Number(obs[oi].fragmentIndex)===fi&&obs[oi].kind==="response")responseObs.push(obs[oi]);
    var receiver=BTT_devResolveAffectedOwner(parse,f);if(!receiver.entity){audit.push({fragmentIndex:fi,raw:f.raw,reason:"RECEIVER_UNRESOLVED",strength:0,components:{}});continue;}
    var channels={},responseFamily="response",responseStrength=0;for(oi=0;oi<responseObs.length;oi++){var ro=responseObs[oi];if((ro.affectedOwner||ro.receiver||ro.owner)!==receiver.entity)continue;var rc=ro.responseChannels||ro.payload&&ro.payload.responseChannels||{};for(var ck in rc)if(rc.hasOwnProperty(ck))channels[ck]=Math.max(Number(channels[ck])||0,Number(rc[ck])||0);responseStrength=Math.max(responseStrength,Number(ro.responseStrength)||0);}
    if(!Object.keys(channels).length)channels=BTT_responseChannelMap(f.raw);responseFamily=BTT_responseDominantFamily(channels);
    var source=BTT_resolveObservationSource(parse,f,receiver.entity),rawTags=BTT_observationMechanismTags(f.raw),preMechanism=BTT_devFamilyMechanism({mechanism:BTT_observationMechanism(f.raw,rawTags),mechanismTags:rawTags,sourceDescriptor:String(source.descriptor||"")}),tags=BTT_devMechanismLocalTags(f.raw,preMechanism,source.descriptor,rawTags),mechanism=preMechanism,slot="",slotConf=0;
    for(oi=0;oi<obs.length;oi++){var o=obs[oi];if(Number(o.fragmentIndex)!==fi||(o.affectedOwner||o.receiver||o.owner)!==receiver.entity)continue;if(o.slot&&Number(o.slotConfidence)>slotConf){slot=o.slot;slotConf=Number(o.slotConfidence);}if((o.sourceEntity||o.sourceDescriptor)&&Number(o.sourceConfidence)>Number(source.confidence||0))source={entity:o.sourceEntity||"",descriptor:o.sourceDescriptor||"",confidence:Number(o.sourceConfidence)||0,reason:String(o.sourceReason||"atomic_source")};}
    var triggerActor=BTT_devTriggerActorForFragment(parse,f,receiver.entity,source),explicitSource=BTT_devExplicitCausalSource(f.raw);
    if(explicitSource&&(Number(explicitSource.confidence)>=Number(source.confidence||0)-0.12||!!source.entity)){source={entity:String(explicitSource.entity||""),descriptor:String(explicitSource.descriptor||""),confidence:Number(explicitSource.confidence)||0,reason:String(explicitSource.reason||"explicit_named_mechanism")};mechanism=String(explicitSource.mechanism||mechanism);}
    mechanism=BTT_devFamilyMechanism({mechanism:mechanism,mechanismTags:tags,sourceDescriptor:String(source.descriptor||"")});tags=BTT_devMechanismLocalTags(f.raw,mechanism,source.descriptor,rawTags);
    /* Response families enrich tags only as local evidence dimensions; they never
       replace causal ownership or leak unrelated neighboring mechanism tags. */
    var channelTags=[];for(var ch in channels)if(channels.hasOwnProperty(ch)&&Number(channels[ch])>=0.30)channelTags.push(ch);tags=BTT_unique(tags.concat(channelTags));
    var row={turn:BTT_getTurn(),action:BTT_nowAction()||BTT_getTurn(),phase:String(parse.phase||""),fragmentIndex:fi,raw:BTT_clip(f.raw,500),receiver:receiver.entity,affectedOwner:receiver.entity,ownerConfidence:Number(receiver.confidence)||0,ownerMargin:Number(receiver.margin)||0,actor:String(triggerActor.entity||((f.subject&&f.subject!==receiver.entity)?f.subject:receiver.entity)||""),triggerActor:String(triggerActor.entity||""),triggerActorConfidence:Number(triggerActor.confidence)||0,sourceEntity:String(source.entity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(source.descriptor||""),causalSourceEntity:String(source.entity||""),causalSourceDescriptor:String(source.descriptor||""),sourceConfidence:Number(source.confidence)||0,sourceReason:String(source.reason||""),slot:slot,slotConfidence:slotConf,changeScore:Number(f.shared&&f.shared.change&&f.shared.change.score)||0,mechanism:mechanism,mechanismTags:tags,responseChannels:BTT_clone(channels),responseFamily:responseFamily,responseStrength:responseStrength};
    var assessment=BTT_devDiscoveryEvidenceComponents(f,row);row.effectMechanism=assessment.mechanism;row.mechanismTags=assessment.mechanismTags;row.mechanism=BTT_devFamilyMechanism({mechanism:assessment.mechanism,mechanismTags:row.mechanismTags,sourceDescriptor:row.sourceDescriptor});row.rawScore=assessment.strength;row.evidenceComponents=assessment.components;row.responseChannels=assessment.responseChannels||row.responseChannels;row.responseFamily=assessment.responseFamily||row.responseFamily;row.admissionReason=assessment.reason;
    if(!assessment.admitted){audit.push({fragmentIndex:fi,raw:f.raw,reason:assessment.reason,strength:assessment.strength,components:assessment.components,receiver:row.receiver,sourceEntity:row.sourceEntity,sourceDescriptor:row.sourceDescriptor,mechanism:row.mechanism,responseFamily:row.responseFamily,responseChannels:BTT_clone(row.responseChannels)});continue;}
    row.fingerprint=BTT_devRiskFingerprint(row);row.label=BTT_devCandidateLabel(row);row.trackHints=BTT_devTrackHints(BTT_unique((row.mechanismTags||[]).concat([row.responseFamily])));row.eventKey=[row.action,row.receiver,row.sourceEntity||row.sourceDescriptor||"",row.mechanism||"unresolved"].join("|");row.signature="devdisc|"+BTT_hash([row.eventKey,row.raw].join("|"));out.push(row);
  }
  return out;
}function BTT_devCandidateAllRows(c){var out=[],events=c&&c.events||[];for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++)for(var j=0;j<(events[i].clauses||[]).length;j++)out.push(events[i].clauses[j]);return out;}
function BTT_devEventText(ev){var seen={},parts=[];for(var i=0;i<(ev&&ev.clauses||[]).length;i++){var q=BTT_cleanSpace(ev.clauses[i].raw);if(!q)continue;var k=BTT_hash(BTT_lower(q));if(seen[k])continue;seen[k]=1;parts.push(q);}if(!parts.length&&ev&&ev.raw)parts.push(BTT_cleanSpace(ev.raw));return parts.join(" ");}
function BTT_devParentSourceKey(x){return BTT_normId(x&&x.causalSourceEntity||x&&x.causalSourceDescriptor||x&&x.sourceEntity||x&&x.sourceDescriptor||"");}
function BTT_devParentSeedable(row){return !!row&&!!row.receiver&&String(row.mechanism||"unresolved")!=="unresolved";}
function BTT_devCandidateMatch(c,row){
  /* Parent-risk routing is causal first. Response channels are evidence inside a
     parent and only weak tie-breakers here; they must not split one mechanism
     into sensory/physiological/morphological top-level candidates. */
  if(!c||!row||!c.receiver||!row.receiver||c.receiver!==row.receiver)return 0;
  var cm=String(c.mechanism||"unresolved"),rm=String(row.mechanism||"unresolved"),cs=BTT_devParentSourceKey(c),rs=BTT_devParentSourceKey(row),score=0;
  var sourceExact=!!cs&&!!rs&&cs===rs,sourceConflict=!!cs&&!!rs&&cs!==rs;
  if(sourceExact)score+=0.60;
  if(cm===rm&&cm!=="unresolved")score+=0.46;
  else if(cm==="unresolved"||rm==="unresolved")score+=0.08;
  else if(!sourceExact)return 0; /* two resolved mechanisms need a shared source */
  if(!sourceConflict&&(!cs||!rs)&&cm===rm&&cm!=="unresolved")score+=0.14;
  if(sourceConflict&&!sourceExact)return BTT_clamp(score*0.55,0,1);
  score+=0.06*BTT_responseChannelSimilarity(c.responseChannels||{},row.responseChannels||{});
  score+=0.04*BTT_devTagSimilarity(c.mechanismTags||[],row.mechanismTags||[]);
  return BTT_clamp(score,0,1);
}
function BTT_devCandidateNovelty(c,row){var max=0,rows=BTT_devCandidateAllRows(c);for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++)max=Math.max(max,BTT_observationTextSimilarity(rows[i].raw,row.raw));return BTT_clamp(1-max,0.12,1);}
function BTT_devSameLogicalEvent(ev,row,cfg){if(!ev||!row)return false;if(Number(ev.action)!==Number(row.action)||ev.receiver!==row.receiver)return false;var es=BTT_devParentSourceKey(ev),rs=BTT_devParentSourceKey(row);if(es&&rs&&es!==rs)return false;if(es&&rs&&es===rs)return true;if(ev.mechanism===row.mechanism&&ev.mechanism!=="unresolved")return true;if(ev.mechanism==="unresolved"||row.mechanism==="unresolved")return true;return BTT_devTagSimilarity(ev.mechanismTags||[],row.mechanismTags||[])>=0.45;}
function BTT_devSameEventCausalContinuity(ev,row){if(!ev||!row||Number(ev.action)!==Number(row.action)||ev.receiver!==row.receiver)return 0;var es=BTT_devParentSourceKey(ev),rs=BTT_devParentSourceKey(row),mechanism=String(ev.mechanism||row.mechanism||"unresolved"),raw=String(row.raw||""),score=0,borrowed=/continuity|atomic_source/i.test(String(row.sourceReason||"")),explicit=BTT_devExplicitCausalSource(raw),lex=BTT_atomicMechanismLexicalCue(raw,mechanism),channel=BTT_responseChannelSimilarity(ev.responseChannels||{},row.responseChannels||{}),minDist=99;for(var i=0;i<(ev.clauses||[]).length;i++)minDist=Math.min(minDist,Math.abs(Number(ev.clauses[i].fragmentIndex)-Number(row.fragmentIndex)));if(explicit){var exKey=BTT_normId(explicit.entity||explicit.descriptor||"");if(es&&exKey&&es!==exKey)return 0;if(String(explicit.mechanism||mechanism)!=="unresolved"&&mechanism!=="unresolved"&&String(explicit.mechanism)!==mechanism)return 0;score+=0.52;}if(es&&rs&&es===rs)score+=borrowed?0.30:0.62;if(mechanism!=="unresolved"&&String(row.mechanism||"unresolved")===mechanism)score+=0.16;if(lex)score+=0.20;score+=0.12*channel;if(BTT_responseDirectCue(raw))score+=0.08;if(minDist<=1)score+=0.12;else if(minDist<=2)score+=0.07;if(/\b(?:cool air|warm air|room air|her breath|his breath|their breath|perfume|scent|her tail|his tail|their tail|her eyes|his eyes|their eyes|her gaze|his gaze|their gaze|her voice|his voice|their voice)\b/i.test(raw)&&!lex)score-=0.30;if(borrowed&&!lex&&!BTT_responseDirectCue(raw)&&channel<0.30)score=Math.min(score,0.38);return BTT_clamp(score,0,1);}
function BTT_devNewLogicalEvent(row){return {id:"E"+String(row.action)+"-"+String(row.fragmentIndex),turn:Number(row.turn)||BTT_getTurn(),action:Number(row.action)||BTT_getTurn(),receiver:row.receiver,triggerActor:String(row.triggerActor||row.actor||""),triggerActorConfidence:Number(row.triggerActorConfidence)||0,sourceEntity:row.sourceEntity,sourceDescriptor:row.sourceDescriptor,causalSourceEntity:String(row.causalSourceEntity||row.sourceEntity||""),causalSourceDescriptor:String(row.causalSourceDescriptor||row.sourceDescriptor||""),sourceConfidence:Number(row.sourceConfidence)||0,mechanism:row.mechanism,responseFamily:String(row.responseFamily||"response"),responseChannels:BTT_clone(row.responseChannels||{}),mechanismTags:BTT_clone(row.mechanismTags||[]),trackHints:BTT_clone(row.trackHints||[]),strength:Number(row.rawScore)||0,support:0,semanticMatch:Number(row.semanticMatch)||1,novelty:Number(row.novelty)||1,contribution:Number(row.contribution)||0,historical:false,clauses:[row]};}
function BTT_devAggregateResponseChannels(events){var vals={},events0=events||[];for(var i=0;i<events0.length;i++)for(var j=0;j<(events0[i].clauses||[]).length;j++){var ch=events0[i].clauses[j].responseChannels||{};for(var k in ch)if(ch.hasOwnProperty(k)){vals[k]=vals[k]||[];vals[k].push(Number(ch[k])||0);}}var out={};for(var id in vals)if(vals.hasOwnProperty(id)){vals[id].sort(function(a,b){return b-a;});var score=(vals[id][0]||0)+0.20*(vals[id][1]||0)+0.10*(vals[id][2]||0);out[id]=BTT_round(BTT_clamp(score,0,1),3);}return out;}
function BTT_devBlueprintTrackRows(ch){ch=ch||{};var rows=[];function push(id,score,channels){score=BTT_clamp(Number(score)||0,0,1);if(score>0)rows.push({id:id,score:BTT_round(score,3),channels:channels||[]});}
  push("behavioral-conditioning",Math.max(Number(ch.behavioral)||0,Number(ch.mental)||0)*0.68+Math.max(Number(ch.reward)||0,Number(ch.aversive)||0)*0.22+(Number(ch.adaptive)||0)*0.22,["mental","behavioral","reward","aversive","adaptive"]);
  push("dependency",(Number(ch.dependency)||0)*0.82+(Number(ch.reward)||0)*0.16+(Number(ch.adaptive)||0)*0.20+(Number(ch.chemical)||0)*0.10,["dependency","reward","adaptive","chemical"]);
  push("morphological-adaptation",(Number(ch.morphological)||0)*0.86+(Number(ch.adaptive)||0)*0.22,["morphological","adaptive"]);
  push("biological-host-adaptation",(Number(ch.host)||0)*0.78+Math.max(Number(ch.physiological)||0,Number(ch.morphological)||0)*0.18+(Number(ch.adaptive)||0)*0.18,["host","physiological","morphological","adaptive"]);
  push("sensory-sensitization",(Number(ch.sensory)||0)*0.74+(Number(ch.adaptive)||0)*0.28+(Number(ch.reward)||0)*0.10+(Number(ch.aversive)||0)*0.10,["sensory","adaptive","reward","aversive"]);
  push("physiological-adaptation",(Number(ch.physiological)||0)*0.74+(Number(ch.adaptive)||0)*0.26,["physiological","adaptive"]);
  push("reinforcement",Math.max(Number(ch.reward)||0,Number(ch.aversive)||0)*0.72+(Number(ch.behavioral)||0)*0.20+(Number(ch.adaptive)||0)*0.14,["reward","aversive","behavioral","adaptive"]);
  rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});return rows;
}
function BTT_devBuildAuthoringBlueprint(c,cfg){
  cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var ch=BTT_devAggregateResponseChannels(c&&c.events||[]),rows=BTT_devBlueprintTrackRows(ch),selected=[];
  /* The parent hypothesis qualifies the basic risk. Initial authoring chooses one
     strongest coherent track even when that track's own confidence is modest;
     lower hypotheses remain progression-watch material instead of blocking the
     profile or forcing speculative extra tracks. */
  if(rows.length)selected.push(rows[0]);
  var mechanism=String(c&&c.mechanism||"unresolved"),family=selected.length?selected[0].id:String(BTT_responseDominantFamily(ch)||"response");
  return {receiver:String(c&&c.receiver||""),sourceEntity:String(c&&c.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(c&&c.sourceDescriptor||""),mechanism:mechanism,responseChannels:ch,riskFamily:family,acquisitionCount:1,trackCandidates:rows,selectedTracks:selected,trackCount:1,mechanismResolved:mechanism!=="unresolved"};
}
function BTT_devBlueprintText(bp){bp=bp||{};var ch=bp.responseChannels||{},cks=Object.keys(ch).sort(function(a,b){return Number(ch[b])-Number(ch[a]);}),lines=["Receiver: "+(bp.receiver||"unresolved"),"Source: "+(bp.sourceEntity||bp.sourceDescriptor||"unresolved"),"Mechanism: "+(bp.mechanism||"unresolved"),"Risk family: "+(bp.riskFamily||"response")];if(cks.length)lines.push("Response confidence: "+cks.slice(0,8).map(function(k){return k+"="+BTT_round(ch[k],2);}).join(", "));var rows=bp.trackCandidates||[];if(rows.length)lines.push("Track hypotheses: "+rows.slice(0,5).map(function(x,idx){var state=idx===0?"PRIMARY":x.score>=0.25?"WATCH":"HOLD";return x.id+"="+BTT_round(x.score,2)+" ["+state+"]";}).join("; "));return lines.join("\n");}
function BTT_devCandidateMechanismVote(events){var votes={},events0=events||[];for(var i=0;i<events0.length;i++){var ev=events0[i],m=String(ev.mechanism||"unresolved");if(m==="unresolved")continue;votes[m]=(Number(votes[m])||0)+Math.max(0.25,Number(ev.strength)||0)*(0.75+0.25*Math.max(0,Number(ev.sourceConfidence)||0));}var rows=[];for(var k in votes)if(votes.hasOwnProperty(k))rows.push({id:k,score:votes[k]});rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});return rows.length?rows[0].id:"unresolved";}
function BTT_devCandidateSourceVote(events){var votes={},meta={},events0=events||[];for(var i=0;i<events0.length;i++){var ev=events0[i],key=BTT_devParentSourceKey(ev);if(!key)continue;votes[key]=(Number(votes[key])||0)+Math.max(0.25,Number(ev.strength)||0)*(0.55+0.45*Math.max(0,Number(ev.sourceConfidence)||0));if(!meta[key])meta[key]={entity:String(ev.causalSourceEntity||ev.sourceEntity||""),descriptor:String(ev.causalSourceDescriptor||ev.sourceDescriptor||"")};}var rows=[];for(var k in votes)if(votes.hasOwnProperty(k))rows.push({id:k,score:votes[k]});rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});return rows.length?meta[rows[0].id]:{entity:"",descriptor:""};}
function BTT_devWeightedDistribution(events,keyFn){var totals={},total=0,resolved=0,events0=events||[];for(var i=0;i<events0.length;i++){var ev=events0[i],key=String(keyFn(ev)||"");if(!key||key==="unresolved")continue;var w=Math.max(0.25,Number(ev.strength)||0)*(0.75+0.25*Math.max(0,Number(ev.sourceConfidence)||0));totals[key]=(Number(totals[key])||0)+w;total+=w;resolved++;}var rows=[];for(var k in totals)if(totals.hasOwnProperty(k))rows.push({id:k,score:totals[k]});rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});var top=rows[0]||{id:"",score:0},next=rows[1]||{id:"",score:0};return {rows:rows,total:total,resolved:resolved,winner:top.id,ratio:total?top.score/total:0,margin:total?(top.score-next.score)/total:0,runner:next.id};}
function BTT_devCausalCoherence(events,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var max=Math.max(0,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_causal_coherence_bonus_max)||0.35),mech=BTT_devWeightedDistribution(events,function(ev){return String(ev.mechanism||"unresolved");}),src=BTT_devWeightedDistribution(events,function(ev){return BTT_devParentSourceKey(ev);}),count=Math.max(1,(events||[]).length),mechCoverage=BTT_clamp(mech.resolved/count,0,1),srcCoverage=BTT_clamp(src.resolved/count,0,1),mechTerm=mech.resolved?0.52*mech.ratio*(0.55+0.45*mechCoverage):0,srcTerm=src.resolved?0.48*src.ratio*(0.45+0.55*srcCoverage):0;return BTT_round(BTT_clamp(max*(mechTerm+srcTerm),0,max),3);}
function BTT_devRecurrenceConfidence(count,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var max=Math.max(0,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_recurrence_bonus_max)||0.38),n=Math.max(0,Number(count)||0),v=n<3?0:n===3?0.15:n===4?0.25:n===5?0.32:Math.min(max,0.32+0.03*(n-5));return BTT_round(Math.min(max,v),3);}
function BTT_devPatternCoherence(c,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var max=Math.max(0,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_pattern_coherence_bonus_max)||0.20),bp=BTT_devBuildAuthoringBlueprint(c,cfg),rows=bp.trackCandidates||[],top=rows[0]||{score:0},next=rows[1]||{score:0},strength=BTT_clamp(Number(top.score)||0,0,1),separation=BTT_clamp((Number(top.score||0)-Number(next.score||0))/0.25,0,1),v=max*(0.72*strength+0.28*separation);return {score:BTT_round(BTT_clamp(v,0,max),3),blueprint:bp};}
function BTT_devAmbiguityPenalty(events,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var max=Math.max(0,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_ambiguity_penalty_max)||0.35),mech=BTT_devWeightedDistribution(events,function(ev){return String(ev.mechanism||"unresolved");}),src=BTT_devWeightedDistribution(events,function(ev){return BTT_devParentSourceKey(ev);}),p=0;if(mech.rows.length>1)p+=0.52*(1-mech.margin);if(src.rows.length>1)p+=0.48*(1-src.margin);return BTT_round(BTT_clamp(max*p,0,max),3);}
function BTT_devEventMeaningfulSupport(ev){if(!ev)return false;if(ev.historical)return true;if(Number(ev.strength)>=0.45)return true;var ch=ev.responseChannels||{};return Math.max(Number(ch.mental)||0,Number(ch.behavioral)||0,Number(ch.reward)||0,Number(ch.aversive)||0,Number(ch.morphological)||0,Number(ch.chemical)||0,Number(ch.dependency)||0,Number(ch.host)||0,Number(ch.adaptive)||0)>=0.30;}
function BTT_devCandidateLastMeaningfulTurn(c){var best=Number(c&&c.createdTurn)||0,events=c&&c.events||[];for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++)if(BTT_devEventMeaningfulSupport(events[i]))best=Math.max(best,Number(events[i].turn)||0);return best;}
function BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(c,cfg){
  cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var turn=BTT_getTurn(),events=(c.events||[]).slice();c.events=events;
  if(!events.length){c.score=0;c.scoreComponents={eventEvidence:0,strongest:0,corroboration:0,eventSupport:0,causalCoherence:0,recurrence:0,patternCoherence:0,ambiguityPenalty:0,total:0};c.responseChannels={};c.mechanism="unresolved";c.blueprint=BTT_devBuildAuthoringBlueprint(c,cfg);return c;}
  /* Active parent evidence uses no per-event age decay while the topic
     is active. Candidate memory is handled by active->dormant compaction. */
  var strongest=0,sum=0,support=0;for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++){var raw=Number(events[i].strength)||0;strongest=Math.max(strongest,raw);if(i>0)sum+=Number(events[i].contribution)||0;support+=Math.min(0.12,Number(events[i].support)||0);}support=Math.min(0.24,support);var eventEvidence=BTT_clamp(strongest+sum+support,0,5);
  c.responseChannels=BTT_devAggregateResponseChannels(events);c.responseFamily=BTT_responseDominantFamily(c.responseChannels);c.mechanism=BTT_devCandidateMechanismVote(events);var src=BTT_devCandidateSourceVote(events);c.sourceEntity=String(src.entity||"");c.sourceDescriptor=String(src.descriptor||"");c.fingerprint=BTT_devRiskFingerprint(c);c.label=BTT_devCandidateLabel(c);
  var causal=BTT_devCausalCoherence(events,cfg),recurrence=BTT_devRecurrenceConfidence(events.length,cfg),patternInfo=BTT_devPatternCoherence(c,cfg),ambiguity=BTT_devAmbiguityPenalty(events,cfg),total=BTT_clamp(eventEvidence+causal+recurrence+patternInfo.score-ambiguity,0,5);
  c.blueprint=patternInfo.blueprint;c.score=BTT_round(total,3);c.lastTurn=events.reduce(function(m,ev){return Math.max(m,Number(ev.turn)||0);},Number(c.lastTurn)||0);c.lastSupportingTurn=BTT_devCandidateLastMeaningfulTurn(c);c.scoreComponents={eventEvidence:BTT_round(eventEvidence,3),strongest:BTT_round(strongest,3),corroboration:BTT_round(sum,3),eventSupport:BTT_round(support,3),causalCoherence:BTT_round(causal,3),recurrence:BTT_round(recurrence,3),patternCoherence:BTT_round(patternInfo.score,3),ambiguityPenalty:BTT_round(ambiguity,3),total:BTT_round(total,3)};return c;
}
function BTT_devUnroutedRiskRelevant(row,reason){if(!row)return false;if(/^PARENT_DISPLACED/.test(String(reason||"")))return true;var ch=row.responseChannels||{},core=Math.max(Number(ch.mental)||0,Number(ch.behavioral)||0,Number(ch.reward)||0,Number(ch.aversive)||0,Number(ch.morphological)||0,Number(ch.chemical)||0,Number(ch.dependency)||0,Number(ch.host)||0,Number(ch.adaptive)||0),sens=Math.max(Number(ch.sensory)||0,Number(ch.physiological)||0),resolved=String(row.mechanism||"unresolved")!=="unresolved"||!!BTT_devParentSourceKey(row),change=Number(row.changeScore)||0;if(core>=0.30)return true;if(change>=0.12)return true;if(resolved&&sens>=0.30)return true;if(sens>=0.30&&Number(row.rawScore)>=0.58&&BTT_responseDirectCue(row.raw))return true;return false;}
function BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,row,reason,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();if(!row)return;if(!BTT_devUnroutedRiskRelevant(row,reason)){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("UNROUTED EVENT DROP | reason=LOW_RISK_RELEVANCE | original_reason="+String(reason||"UNRESOLVED_PARENT")+" | receiver="+String(row.receiver||"unresolved")+" | response="+String(row.responseFamily||"response")+" | strength="+BTT_round(row.rawScore||0,2)+" | evidence="+BTT_clip(row.raw,110));return;}g.unroutedEvents=Array.isArray(g.unroutedEvents)?g.unroutedEvents:[];var sig=String(row.signature||BTT_hash([row.action,row.receiver,row.raw].join("|")));for(var i=0;i<g.unroutedEvents.length;i++)if(String(g.unroutedEvents[i].signature||"")===sig)return;var copy=BTT_clone(row);copy.unroutedReason=String(reason||"UNRESOLVED_PARENT");copy.retainedTurn=BTT_getTurn();g.unroutedEvents.push(copy);var mem=Math.max(2,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns)||14);g.unroutedEvents=g.unroutedEvents.filter(function(x){return BTT_getTurn()-Number(x.turn||x.retainedTurn||0)<=mem;});g.unroutedEvents.sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.rawScore||0)-Number(a.rawScore||0)||Number(b.turn||0)-Number(a.turn||0);});if(g.unroutedEvents.length>Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_max||24))g.unroutedEvents=g.unroutedEvents.slice(0,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_max||24));BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("UNROUTED EVENT RETAIN | reason="+copy.unroutedReason+" | receiver="+String(copy.receiver||"unresolved")+" | mechanism="+String(copy.mechanism||"unresolved")+" | response="+String(copy.responseFamily||"response")+" | strength="+BTT_round(copy.rawScore||0,2)+" | evidence="+BTT_clip(copy.raw,120));}
function BTT_devUnroutedMatch(c,row,g,cfg){if(!c||!row||c.receiver!==row.receiver)return 0;var base=BTT_devCandidateMatch(c,row);if(base>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_merge_min||0.58))return base;var receiverCandidates=(g.candidates||[]).filter(function(x){return x.receiver===row.receiver;}),unique=receiverCandidates.length===1?0.22:0,channel=0.36*BTT_responseChannelSimilarity(c.responseChannels||{},row.responseChannels||{}),age=Math.abs(BTT_getTurn()-Number(row.turn||BTT_getTurn())),temporal=0.14*BTT_clamp(1-age/Math.max(3,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns)||14),0,1),semantic=0.10*BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(BTT_devCandidateEvidenceText(c),String(row.raw||"")),tags=0.12*BTT_devTagSimilarity(c.mechanismTags||[],row.mechanismTags||[]);return BTT_clamp(base+unique+channel+temporal+semantic+tags,0,1);}
function BTT_devTryRerouteUnrouted(g,cfg){
  cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();if(!Array.isArray(g.unroutedEvents)||!g.unroutedEvents.length||!(g.candidates||[]).length)return;
  var keep=[];
  for(var i=0;i<g.unroutedEvents.length;i++){
    var row=g.unroutedEvents[i],matches=[];
    for(var c=0;c<g.candidates.length;c++){
      var cand=g.candidates[c],score=BTT_devUnroutedMatch(cand,row,g,cfg);
      matches.push({candidate:cand,score:score,channel:BTT_responseChannelSimilarity(cand.responseChannels||{},row.responseChannels||{}),tags:BTT_devTagSimilarity(cand.mechanismTags||[],row.mechanismTags||[])});
    }
    matches.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});
    var top=matches[0]||{score:0,channel:0,tags:0},next=matches[1]||{score:0},margin=Number(top.score)-Number(next.score),normalMin=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_attach_min||0.58),normalMargin=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_unrouted_margin||0.10),routeMode="";
    if(top.candidate&&top.score>=normalMin&&margin>=normalMargin)routeMode="CONFIDENT_CAUSAL";
    else{
      /* A direct character response can be obvious before its cause is named.  Do
         not strand that evidence forever: once a resolved parent later emerges,
         permit a lower-score contextual migration only when the response-channel
         pattern and local mechanism tags both support the same parent and it wins
         by a wider margin.  This is intentionally narrower than lowering the main
         parent-merge threshold. */
      var unresolved=!row.mechanism||String(row.mechanism)==="unresolved",contextMin=Math.max(0.38,normalMin-0.20),contextMargin=Math.max(0.14,normalMargin+0.04),channelPass=Number(top.channel)>=0.25,tagPass=Number(top.tags)>=0.20,resolvedParent=top.candidate&&String(top.candidate.mechanism||"unresolved")!=="unresolved";
      if(unresolved&&resolvedParent&&top.score>=contextMin&&margin>=contextMargin&&channelPass&&tagPass)routeMode="CONTEXTUAL_WINNER";
    }
    if(routeMode){
      var merged=BTT_devMergeObservation(top.candidate,row,top.score,cfg);
      if(merged&&merged.held){keep.push(row);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("UNROUTED EVENT HOLD | candidate="+top.candidate.id+" | reason="+String(merged.reason||"LOCAL_CAUSAL_CONTINUITY")+" | score="+BTT_round(top.score,2));continue;}
      BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("UNROUTED EVENT ROUTED | candidate="+top.candidate.id+" | mode="+routeMode+" | score="+BTT_round(top.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(margin,2)+" | channel="+BTT_round(top.channel,2)+" | tags="+BTT_round(top.tags,2)+" | original_reason="+String(row.unroutedReason||"unknown"));
    }else keep.push(row);
  }
  g.unroutedEvents=keep;
}
function BTT_devDormantHistoricalEvent(c,historicalConfidence){var ch=BTT_clone(c.responseChannels||{}),raw="Historical summary of "+String((c.events||[]).length)+" prior "+String(c.label||"Developable Risk")+" events.";var row={turn:BTT_getTurn(),action:-Math.max(1,Number(String(c.id||"").replace(/\D/g,""))||1),phase:"history",fragmentIndex:0,raw:raw,receiver:c.receiver,affectedOwner:c.receiver,ownerConfidence:1,ownerMargin:1,actor:"",triggerActor:"",triggerActorConfidence:0,sourceEntity:String(c.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(c.sourceDescriptor||""),causalSourceEntity:String(c.sourceEntity||""),causalSourceDescriptor:String(c.sourceDescriptor||""),sourceConfidence:0.90,slot:"",slotConfidence:0,changeScore:0,mechanism:String(c.mechanism||"unresolved"),mechanismTags:BTT_clone(c.mechanismTags||[]),responseChannels:ch,responseFamily:String(c.responseFamily||BTT_responseDominantFamily(ch)||"response"),responseStrength:0,rawScore:Number(historicalConfidence)||0,evidenceComponents:{historical:1},trackHints:BTT_clone(c.trackHints||[]),semanticMatch:1,novelty:1,contribution:0,signature:"history|"+String(c.id||"")+"|"+String(BTT_getTurn())};var ev=BTT_devNewLogicalEvent(row);ev.id="H"+String(c.id||"risk")+"-"+String(BTT_getTurn());ev.historical=true;ev.strength=Number(historicalConfidence)||0;ev.contribution=0;return ev;}
function BTT_devCompactCandidateToDormant(g,c,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();g.dormantCandidates=Array.isArray(g.dormantCandidates)?g.dormantCandidates:[];BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(c,cfg);var factor=BTT_clamp(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dormant_compaction_factor)||0.50,0.05,1),historical=BTT_round(Number(c.score||0)*factor,3),d={id:String(c.id||""),fingerprint:String(c.fingerprint||""),label:String(c.label||""),receiver:String(c.receiver||""),sourceEntity:String(c.sourceEntity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(c.sourceDescriptor||""),mechanism:String(c.mechanism||"unresolved"),responseFamily:String(c.responseFamily||"response"),responseChannels:BTT_clone(c.responseChannels||{}),mechanismTags:BTT_clone(c.mechanismTags||[]),trackHints:BTT_clone(c.trackHints||[]),blueprint:BTT_clone(c.blueprint||null),historicalConfidence:historical,originalConfidence:BTT_round(Number(c.score)||0,3),originalEventCount:(c.events||[]).length,dormantTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastSupportingTurn:Number(c.lastSupportingTurn||c.lastTurn)||BTT_getTurn(),createdTurn:Number(c.createdTurn)||BTT_getTurn(),retryAfterTurn:Number(c.retryAfterTurn)||0,revivalCount:Number(c.revivalCount)||0,historicalEvent:null};d.historicalEvent=BTT_devDormantHistoricalEvent(c,historical);for(var i=g.dormantCandidates.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(String(g.dormantCandidates[i].id||"")===d.id)g.dormantCandidates.splice(i,1);g.dormantCandidates.push(d);g.dormantCandidates.sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.historicalConfidence||0)-Number(a.historicalConfidence||0)||Number(b.dormantTurn||0)-Number(a.dormantTurn||0);});var max=Math.max(1,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dormant_max)||16);if(g.dormantCandidates.length>max){var evicted=g.dormantCandidates.splice(max);for(var e=0;e<evicted.length;e++)BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("DORMANT PARENT EVICT | id="+String(evicted[e].id||"?")+" | historical_confidence="+BTT_round(evicted[e].historicalConfidence||0,2)+" | reason=DORMANT_CAPACITY");}BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT DORMANT | candidate="+d.id+" | reason=NO_SUPPORTING_EVENT_WITHIN_MEMORY | inactive_turns="+(BTT_getTurn()-d.lastSupportingTurn)+" | events_compacted="+d.originalEventCount+"->1 | confidence_compacted="+BTT_round(d.originalConfidence,2)+"->"+BTT_round(d.historicalConfidence,2));return d;}
function BTT_devDormantMatch(d,row){return BTT_devCandidateMatch(d,row);}
function BTT_devReviveDormant(g,d,row,match,cfg){
  cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();
  /* Revival is evaluated before mutating either pool.  A dormant seed therefore
     cannot evict an active hypothesis merely by being mentioned: the historical
     seed plus the fresh event must first earn enough authoring confidence to win
