     the same dynamic-capacity comparison used by ordinary new parents. */
  var c={id:d.id,fingerprint:d.fingerprint,label:d.label,receiver:d.receiver,sourceEntity:d.sourceEntity,sourceDescriptor:d.sourceDescriptor,mechanism:d.mechanism,responseFamily:d.responseFamily,responseChannels:BTT_clone(d.responseChannels||{}),mechanismTags:BTT_clone(d.mechanismTags||[]),trackHints:BTT_clone(d.trackHints||[]),slot:"",score:Number(d.historicalConfidence)||0,events:[BTT_clone(d.historicalEvent)],createdTurn:Number(d.createdTurn)||BTT_getTurn(),lastTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastSupportingTurn:BTT_getTurn(),retryAfterTurn:Number(d.retryAfterTurn)||0,revivalCount:(Number(d.revivalCount)||0)+1};
  var novelty=BTT_devCandidateNovelty(c,row),strengthFactor=0.58+0.42*BTT_clamp((Number(row.rawScore)-0.45)/1.45,0,1),contrib=(0.18+0.22*match+0.18*novelty)*strengthFactor;row.semanticMatch=BTT_round(match,3);row.novelty=BTT_round(novelty,3);row.contribution=BTT_round(contrib,3);var ev=BTT_devNewLogicalEvent(row);ev.semanticMatch=row.semanticMatch;ev.novelty=row.novelty;ev.contribution=row.contribution;c.events.push(ev);c.mechanismTags=BTT_unique((c.mechanismTags||[]).concat(row.mechanismTags||[]));c.trackHints=BTT_unique((c.trackHints||[]).concat(row.trackHints||[]));BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(c,cfg);
  var max=Math.max(1,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_candidates)||8),weakest=null,stale=false,margin=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_replacement_margin||0.12);
  if((g.candidates||[]).length>=max){for(var i=0;i<g.candidates.length;i++)BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(g.candidates[i],cfg);weakest=g.candidates.slice().sort(function(a,b){return Number(a.score)-Number(b.score)||Number(a.lastSupportingTurn||a.lastTurn)-Number(b.lastSupportingTurn||b.lastTurn);})[0];stale=weakest&&BTT_getTurn()-Number(weakest.lastSupportingTurn||weakest.lastTurn||0)>Math.max(2,Math.floor(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns||14)/2));if(weakest&&!stale&&Number(c.score)<Number(weakest.score||0)+margin){BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,row,"DORMANT_REVIVAL_CAPACITY_HOLD",cfg);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("DORMANT REVIVAL HOLD | candidate="+d.id+" | reason=ACTIVE_CAPACITY | revived_confidence="+BTT_round(c.score,2)+" | weakest_active="+weakest.id+":"+BTT_round(weakest.score,2)+" | required_margin="+BTT_round(margin,2));return null;}}
  if(weakest){var wi=g.candidates.indexOf(weakest);if(wi>=0)g.candidates.splice(wi,1);for(var e=0;e<(weakest.events||[]).length;e++)for(var q=0;q<(weakest.events[e].clauses||[]).length;q++)BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,weakest.events[e].clauses[q],"PARENT_DISPLACED_BY_REVIVED_HYPOTHESIS",cfg);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT RISK DISPLACE | removed="+weakest.id+":"+BTT_round(weakest.score,2)+" | revived="+d.id+":"+BTT_round(c.score,2)+" | inactive="+(stale?"YES":"NO"));}
  var idx=(g.dormantCandidates||[]).indexOf(d);if(idx>=0)g.dormantCandidates.splice(idx,1);g.candidates.push(c);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT REVIVED | candidate="+c.id+" | historical_confidence="+BTT_round(d.historicalConfidence||0,2)+" | new_event_strength="+BTT_round(row.rawScore||0,2)+" | match="+BTT_round(match||0,2)+" | authoring_confidence="+BTT_round(c.score,2)+" | distinct_events="+(c.events||[]).length);return c;
}
function BTT_devPruneCandidates(g,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var keep=[],mem=Math.max(2,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns)||14),turn=BTT_getTurn();for(var i=0;i<(g.candidates||[]).length;i++){var c=BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(g.candidates[i],cfg),last=BTT_devCandidateLastMeaningfulTurn(c);if(!c.events||!c.events.length){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("CANDIDATE EXPIRED | id="+String(c.id||"?")+" | fingerprint="+String(c.fingerprint||""));continue;}if(turn-last>=mem){BTT_devCompactCandidateToDormant(g,c,cfg);continue;}keep.push(c);}g.candidates=keep;g.unroutedEvents=(g.unroutedEvents||[]).filter(function(x){return turn-Number(x.turn||x.retainedTurn||0)<=mem;});var min=turn-mem-2;for(var k in g.seenEvents)if(g.seenEvents.hasOwnProperty(k)&&Number(g.seenEvents[k].turn||0)<min)delete g.seenEvents[k];}
function BTT_devCreateCandidate(g,row){var id="risk-"+String(Number(g.nextCandidate)||1);g.nextCandidate=(Number(g.nextCandidate)||1)+1;row.semanticMatch=1;row.novelty=1;row.contribution=0;var c={id:id,fingerprint:row.fingerprint,label:row.label,receiver:row.receiver,sourceEntity:row.sourceEntity,sourceDescriptor:row.sourceDescriptor,mechanism:row.mechanism,responseFamily:row.responseFamily,responseChannels:BTT_clone(row.responseChannels||{}),mechanismTags:BTT_clone(row.mechanismTags||[]),trackHints:BTT_clone(row.trackHints||[]),slot:row.slot||"",score:row.rawScore,events:[BTT_devNewLogicalEvent(row)],createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastSupportingTurn:BTT_getTurn(),retryAfterTurn:0,revivalCount:0};BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(c,BTT_getConfig());g.candidates.push(c);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT RISK NEW | id="+id+" | fingerprint="+c.fingerprint+" | receiver="+c.receiver+" | source="+(c.sourceEntity||c.sourceDescriptor||"unresolved")+" | mechanism="+c.mechanism+" | event=1 | authoring_confidence="+BTT_round(c.score,2));return c;}
function BTT_devAddCandidatePrioritized(g,row,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();if(!BTT_devParentSeedable(row)){BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,row,"PARENT_CAUSE_UNRESOLVED",cfg);return null;}if((g.candidates||[]).length<Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_candidates||8))return BTT_devCreateCandidate(g,row);for(var i=0;i<g.candidates.length;i++)BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(g.candidates[i],cfg);var weakest=g.candidates.slice().sort(function(a,b){return Number(a.score)-Number(b.score)||Number(a.lastSupportingTurn||a.lastTurn)-Number(b.lastSupportingTurn||b.lastTurn);})[0],newScore=Number(row.rawScore)||0,stale=BTT_getTurn()-Number(weakest.lastSupportingTurn||weakest.lastTurn||0)>Math.max(2,Math.floor(Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_memory_turns||14)/2));if(stale||newScore>=Number(weakest.score||0)+Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_replacement_margin||0.12)){var idx=g.candidates.indexOf(weakest);if(idx>=0)g.candidates.splice(idx,1);for(var e=0;e<(weakest.events||[]).length;e++)for(var q=0;q<(weakest.events[e].clauses||[]).length;q++)BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,weakest.events[e].clauses[q],"PARENT_DISPLACED_BY_STRONGER_HYPOTHESIS",cfg);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT RISK DISPLACE | removed="+weakest.id+":"+BTT_round(weakest.score,2)+" | incoming_strength="+BTT_round(newScore,2)+" | inactive="+(stale?"YES":"NO"));return BTT_devCreateCandidate(g,row);}BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,row,"PARENT_CAPACITY_LOWER_PRIORITY",cfg);return null;}
function BTT_devMergeObservation(c,row,match,cfg){var same=null,sameAction=null;for(var i=0;i<(c.events||[]).length;i++){if(Number(c.events[i].action)===Number(row.action)&&c.events[i].receiver===row.receiver)sameAction=c.events[i];if(BTT_devSameLogicalEvent(c.events[i],row,cfg)){same=c.events[i];break;}}if(sameAction&&!same){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("EVENT ENRICH HOLD | candidate="+c.id+" | event="+sameAction.id+" | reason=LOCAL_CAUSAL_SOURCE_CONFLICT | response_family="+row.responseFamily+" | distinct_event=NO");return {held:true,reason:"LOCAL_CAUSAL_SOURCE_CONFLICT",sameEvent:true};}if(same){var continuity=BTT_devSameEventCausalContinuity(same,row);if(continuity<0.52){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("EVENT ENRICH HOLD | candidate="+c.id+" | event="+same.id+" | reason=LOCAL_CAUSAL_CONTINUITY | continuity="+BTT_round(continuity,2)+" | response_family="+row.responseFamily+" | distinct_event=NO");return {held:true,reason:"LOCAL_CAUSAL_CONTINUITY",sameEvent:true,continuity:continuity};}var maxSim=0,duplicate=false;for(var q=0;q<(same.clauses||[]).length;q++){var sim=BTT_observationTextSimilarity(same.clauses[q].raw,row.raw);maxSim=Math.max(maxSim,sim);if(sim>=cfg.dev_risk_ai_dedupe_similarity)duplicate=true;}if(duplicate){same.support=BTT_clamp((Number(same.support)||0)+Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_duplicate_support||0.06),0,0.12);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("EVENT DEDUPE | candidate="+c.id+" | event="+same.id+" | phase="+String(row.phase).toUpperCase()+" | action="+row.action+" | similarity="+BTT_round(maxSim,2)+" | causal_continuity="+BTT_round(continuity,2)+" | distinct_event=NO");}else{var novelty=BTT_devCandidateNovelty(c,row);same.clauses.push(row);same.strength=Math.max(Number(same.strength)||0,Number(row.rawScore)||0);same.support=BTT_clamp((Number(same.support)||0)+0.03+0.03*novelty,0,0.12);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("EVENT ENRICH | candidate="+c.id+" | event="+same.id+" | response_family="+row.responseFamily+" | causal_continuity="+BTT_round(continuity,2)+" | distinct_event=NO | clauses="+same.clauses.length);}if(!same.sourceEntity&&row.sourceEntity)same.sourceEntity=row.sourceEntity;if(!same.sourceDescriptor&&row.sourceDescriptor)same.sourceDescriptor=row.sourceDescriptor;c.mechanismTags=BTT_unique((c.mechanismTags||[]).concat(row.mechanismTags||[]));c.trackHints=BTT_unique((c.trackHints||[]).concat(row.trackHints||[]));BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(c,cfg);return {duplicate:duplicate,sameEvent:true,continuity:continuity};}var novelty2=BTT_devCandidateNovelty(c,row),strengthFactor=0.58+0.42*BTT_clamp((Number(row.rawScore)-0.45)/1.45,0,1),contrib=(0.18+0.22*match+0.18*novelty2)*strengthFactor;row.semanticMatch=BTT_round(match,3);row.novelty=BTT_round(novelty2,3);row.contribution=BTT_round(contrib,3);var ev=BTT_devNewLogicalEvent(row);ev.semanticMatch=row.semanticMatch;ev.novelty=row.novelty;ev.contribution=row.contribution;c.events.push(ev);c.mechanismTags=BTT_unique((c.mechanismTags||[]).concat(row.mechanismTags||[]));c.trackHints=BTT_unique((c.trackHints||[]).concat(row.trackHints||[]));BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(c,cfg);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT EVENT MERGE | id="+c.id+" | event="+ev.id+" | response_family="+row.responseFamily+" | strength="+BTT_round(row.rawScore,2)+" | match="+BTT_round(match,2)+" | contribution=+"+BTT_round(contrib,2)+" | score="+BTT_round(c.score,2)+" | distinct_events="+c.events.length);return {duplicate:false,sameEvent:false};}
function BTT_devNormalizeEventCausalSource(ev,preferred){if(!ev||!preferred)return ev;var rows=ev.clauses||[];for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++){var ex=BTT_devExplicitCausalSource(rows[i].raw);if(!ex)continue;if(BTT_normId(ex.descriptor||"")!==BTT_normId(preferred.descriptor||""))continue;rows[i].sourceEntity=String(preferred.entity||"");rows[i].sourceDescriptor=String(preferred.descriptor||"");rows[i].causalSourceEntity=String(preferred.entity||"");rows[i].causalSourceDescriptor=String(preferred.descriptor||"");rows[i].sourceConfidence=Math.max(Number(rows[i].sourceConfidence)||0,Number(ex.confidence)||0.85);rows[i].mechanism=String(ex.mechanism||rows[i].mechanism||"unresolved");}var ex0=BTT_devExplicitCausalSource(BTT_devEventText(ev));if(ex0&&BTT_normId(ex0.descriptor||"")===BTT_normId(preferred.descriptor||"")){ev.sourceEntity=String(preferred.entity||"");ev.sourceDescriptor=String(preferred.descriptor||"");ev.causalSourceEntity=String(preferred.entity||"");ev.causalSourceDescriptor=String(preferred.descriptor||"");ev.sourceConfidence=Math.max(Number(ev.sourceConfidence)||0,Number(ex0.confidence)||0.85);ev.mechanism=String(ex0.mechanism||ev.mechanism||"unresolved");}return ev;}
function BTT_devCandidateNamedDescriptor(c){var rows=BTT_devCandidateAllRows(c);for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++){var ex=BTT_devExplicitCausalSource(rows[i].raw);if(ex&&ex.descriptor)return {entity:String(ex.entity||""),descriptor:String(ex.descriptor||""),mechanism:String(ex.mechanism||"")};}return null;}
function BTT_devAbsorbCandidate(target,other,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var preferred=target.sourceDescriptor?{entity:target.sourceEntity||"",descriptor:target.sourceDescriptor}:other.sourceDescriptor?{entity:other.sourceEntity||"",descriptor:other.sourceDescriptor}:null,events=other.events||[];for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++){var ev=BTT_clone(events[i]);if(preferred)BTT_devNormalizeEventCausalSource(ev,preferred);var same=null;for(var j=0;j<(target.events||[]).length;j++)if(Number(target.events[j].action)===Number(ev.action)&&target.events[j].receiver===ev.receiver){same=target.events[j];break;}if(same){same.strength=Math.max(Number(same.strength)||0,Number(ev.strength)||0);same.support=BTT_clamp((Number(same.support)||0)+(Number(ev.support)||0),0,0.12);var mergedClauses=(same.clauses||[]).concat(ev.clauses||[]),seenClauses={},dedupClauses=[];for(var mc=0;mc<mergedClauses.length;mc++){var mk=String(mergedClauses[mc].signature||BTT_hash([mergedClauses[mc].phase,mergedClauses[mc].fragmentIndex,mergedClauses[mc].raw].join("|")));if(seenClauses[mk])continue;seenClauses[mk]=1;dedupClauses.push(mergedClauses[mc]);}same.clauses=dedupClauses;}else target.events.push(ev);}target.mechanismTags=BTT_unique((target.mechanismTags||[]).concat(other.mechanismTags||[]));target.trackHints=BTT_unique((target.trackHints||[]).concat(other.trackHints||[]));target.events.sort(function(a,b){return Number(a.turn)-Number(b.turn)||Number(a.action)-Number(b.action);});BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(target,cfg);return target;}
function BTT_devConsolidateDuplicateParents(g,cfg){cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();var changed=true;while(changed){changed=false;outer:for(var i=0;i<(g.candidates||[]).length;i++)for(var j=i+1;j<g.candidates.length;j++){var a=g.candidates[i],b=g.candidates[j];if(a.receiver!==b.receiver||String(a.mechanism||"unresolved")!==String(b.mechanism||"unresolved"))continue;var ak=BTT_devParentSourceKey(a),bk=BTT_devParentSourceKey(b);if(ak&&bk&&ak===bk){var winner=Number(a.score)>=Number(b.score)?a:b,loser=winner===a?b:a;BTT_devAbsorbCandidate(winner,loser,cfg);g.candidates.splice(g.candidates.indexOf(loser),1);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT CONSOLIDATE | kept="+winner.id+" | merged="+loser.id+" | reason=SAME_CAUSAL_SOURCE | events="+(winner.events||[]).length);changed=true;break outer;}var ad=BTT_devCandidateNamedDescriptor(a),bd=BTT_devCandidateNamedDescriptor(b),descriptor=null,descriptorOwner=null,other=null;if(ad&&BTT_normId(ad.descriptor)===bk){descriptor=ad;descriptorOwner=b;other=a;}else if(bd&&BTT_normId(bd.descriptor)===ak){descriptor=bd;descriptorOwner=a;other=b;}else if(a.sourceDescriptor&&bd&&BTT_normId(a.sourceDescriptor)===BTT_normId(bd.descriptor)){descriptor=bd;descriptorOwner=a;other=b;}else if(b.sourceDescriptor&&ad&&BTT_normId(b.sourceDescriptor)===BTT_normId(ad.descriptor)){descriptor=ad;descriptorOwner=b;other=a;}if(descriptor&&descriptorOwner&&other){var winner2=descriptorOwner,loser2=other;BTT_devAbsorbCandidate(winner2,loser2,cfg);g.candidates.splice(g.candidates.indexOf(loser2),1);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PARENT CONSOLIDATE | kept="+winner2.id+" | merged="+loser2.id+" | reason=TRIGGER_ACTOR_CAUSAL_SOURCE_COLLAPSE | source="+(winner2.sourceEntity||winner2.sourceDescriptor||"unresolved")+" | events="+(winner2.events||[]).length);changed=true;break outer;}}}return g;}
function BTT_devCandidateEvidenceText(c){var parts=[c.label||"",c.fingerprint||"",(c.mechanismTags||[]).join(" ")],events=c.events||[];for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++)parts.push(BTT_devEventText(events[i]));return parts.join(" ");}
function BTT_devProfileCoverage(c,p){if(!c||!p)return 0;var phrases=[p.name].concat(p.aliases||[]);for(var a=0;a<(p.acquisitions||[]).length;a++){var aq=p.acquisitions[a];phrases.push(aq.prototype);phrases=phrases.concat(aq.qualifiers||[]).concat(aq.delivery||[]);}for(var t=0;t<(p.tracks||[]).length;t++){var tr=p.tracks[t];phrases.push(tr.name);phrases=phrases.concat(tr.triggers||[]).concat(tr.qualifiers||[]).concat(tr.delivery||[]);}var evidence=BTT_devCandidateEvidenceText(c),best=0;for(var i=0;i<phrases.length;i++)if(phrases[i])best=Math.max(best,BTT_devSemanticSimilarity(phrases[i],evidence));var pTags=BTT_observationMechanismTags(phrases.join(" ")),tag=BTT_devTagSimilarity(c.mechanismTags||[],pTags),mechanismMatch=pTags.indexOf(c.mechanism)>=0?1:tag;return BTT_clamp(best*0.64+tag*0.22+mechanismMatch*0.14,0,1);}
function BTT_devCandidateCoverage(c,cfg){var profiles=BTT_devRiskProfiles(),best={profile:null,score:0};for(var id in profiles)if(profiles.hasOwnProperty(id)){var p=profiles[id],score=BTT_devProfileCoverage(c,p);if(score>best.score)best={profile:p,score:score};}return best;}
function BTT_devQueueEligibleCandidate(g,cfg){
  if(g.pending)return false;if(Number(g.generatedCount)>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_generated)){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING QUEUE BLOCK | reason=GENERATED_LIMIT | generated="+g.generatedCount+"/"+cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_generated);return false;}var remaining=BTT_devRiskGenerationCooldownRemaining(cfg,g);if(remaining>0){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING QUEUE HOLD | reason=COOLDOWN | remaining="+remaining);return false;}
  var eligible=(g.candidates||[]).filter(function(c){return Number(c.score)>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_confidence)&&(c.events||[]).length>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_min_observations)&&BTT_getTurn()>=Number(c.retryAfterTurn||0);}).sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.score)-Number(a.score);});if(!eligible.length)return false;
  for(var ei=0;ei<eligible.length;ei++){var c=eligible[ei];BTT_devCandidateRecalculate(c,cfg);if(!c.blueprint||!c.blueprint.mechanismResolved){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("CANDIDATE QUALIFIED HOLD | candidate="+c.id+" | reason=MECHANISM_UNRESOLVED | score="+BTT_round(c.score,2)+" | events="+(c.events||[]).length+" | response_family="+c.responseFamily);continue;}var coverage=BTT_devCandidateCoverage(c,cfg);if(coverage.profile&&coverage.score>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_existing_coverage_min)){var coveredIndex=g.candidates.indexOf(c);if(coveredIndex>=0)g.candidates.splice(coveredIndex,1);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("CANDIDATE COVERED | candidate="+c.id+" | existing="+coverage.profile.name+" | coverage="+BTT_round(coverage.score,2)+" >= "+BTT_round(cfg.dev_risk_ai_existing_coverage_min,2)+" | authoring=NO");continue;}
    var idx=g.candidates.indexOf(c);if(idx>=0)g.candidates.splice(idx,1);var evidence=[],events=BTT_clone(c.events||[]);for(var ev=0;ev<events.length;ev++)evidence.push("[EVENT "+(ev+1)+" | T"+events[ev].turn+" A"+events[ev].action+" | receiver="+events[ev].receiver+" | source="+(events[ev].sourceEntity||events[ev].sourceDescriptor||"unresolved")+" | mechanism="+events[ev].mechanism+" | response="+(events[ev].responseFamily||c.responseFamily)+" | strength="+BTT_round(events[ev].strength,2)+"] "+BTT_devEventText(events[ev]));var pending={mode:"new",candidateId:c.id,fingerprint:c.fingerprint,label:c.label,receiver:c.receiver,sourceEntity:c.sourceEntity,sourceDescriptor:c.sourceDescriptor,mechanism:c.mechanism,mechanismTags:BTT_clone(c.mechanismTags||[]),trackHints:BTT_clone(c.trackHints||[]),blueprint:BTT_clone(c.blueprint),createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),createdAction:BTT_nowAction(),retries:0,authoringResponses:0,maintenanceContinues:0,lastError:"",fieldValues:{},fieldStatus:{},fieldErrors:{},sectionStatus:{},sectionErrors:{},derived:{track_count:c.blueprint.trackCount||1,acquisition_count:c.blueprint.acquisitionCount||1},evidence:evidence,events:events,originalCandidate:BTT_clone(c),kickoffNoticeEmitted:false,lastRequestedFields:[],lastPromptMode:0};BTT_devInitializeAuthoringPlan(pending,cfg);g.pending=pending;BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("CANDIDATE QUALIFIED | candidate="+c.id+" | family="+c.blueprint.riskFamily+" | receiver="+c.receiver+" | source="+(c.sourceEntity||c.sourceDescriptor||"unresolved")+" | score="+BTT_round(c.score,2)+" | distinct_events="+String((c.events||[]).length)+" | tracks="+pending.derived.track_count+" | coverage="+BTT_round(coverage.score,2)+" | state=AWAITING_AUTHORING_MAINTENANCE");return true;
  }return false;
}function BTT_devAuditSummary(a){var ec=a&&a.components||{};return "reason="+String(a&&a.reason||"UNKNOWN")+" | strength="+BTT_round(a&&a.strength||0,2)+" | mechanism="+BTT_round(ec.mechanism||0,2)+" | effect="+BTT_round(ec.effect||0,2)+" | progression="+BTT_round(ec.progression||0,2)+" | relation="+BTT_round(ec.relation||0,2)+" | change="+BTT_round(ec.change||0,2)+" | durable="+BTT_round(ec.durable||0,2);}
function BTT_considerDevRiskDiscovery(parse){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),audit=[],rows=BTT_devDiscoveryRows(parse,audit);
  /* Existing acquired risks keep listening even when new-card generation is off.
     This lets the normal Dev Risk debug show current-track corroboration and novel
     possible higher effects throughout the life of the profile. */
  BTT_devUpdateExistingRiskProgression(rows,parse);
  if(!cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_enabled){if(rows.length)BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("DISCOVERY NEW-RISK BLOCK | reason=GENERATION_DISABLED | retained_existing_progression=YES | phase="+String(parse&&parse.phase||"").toUpperCase());return;}
  if(g.pending){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("DISCOVERY BLOCK | reason=AUTHORING_MAINTENANCE_PENDING | phase="+String(parse&&parse.phase||"").toUpperCase());return;}
  BTT_devPruneCandidates(g,cfg);BTT_devConsolidateDuplicateParents(g,cfg);
  /* Give retained risk-relevant unresolved events a chance to join any coherent
     active parent before evaluating the new transaction. Dormant parents require
     fresh current evidence before revival; old ledger rows do not revive them. */
  BTT_devTryRerouteUnrouted(g,cfg);
  if(!rows.length){audit.sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.strength||0)-Number(a.strength||0);});for(var aa=0;aa<Math.min(String(parse&&parse.phase||"")==="input"?3:1,audit.length);aa++){var topAudit=audit[aa];BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("DISCOVERY IDLE | phase="+String(parse&&parse.phase||"").toUpperCase()+" | "+BTT_devAuditSummary(topAudit)+" | clause=C"+topAudit.fragmentIndex+" | evidence="+BTT_clip(topAudit.raw,150));}BTT_devQueueEligibleCandidate(g,cfg);return;}
  for(var r=0;r<rows.length;r++){
    var row=rows[r],ch=row.responseChannels||{};
    BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("DISCOVERY EVENT EVIDENCE | phase="+String(row.phase).toUpperCase()+" | clause=C"+row.fragmentIndex+" | strength="+BTT_round(row.rawScore,2)+" | receiver="+row.receiver+" | source="+(row.sourceEntity||row.sourceDescriptor||"unresolved")+" | mechanism="+row.mechanism+" | response_family="+row.responseFamily+" | channels="+Object.keys(ch).sort(function(a,b){return ch[b]-ch[a];}).slice(0,5).map(function(k){return k+":"+BTT_round(ch[k],2);}).join(",")+" | evidence="+BTT_clip(row.raw,150));
    /* A response event can be real before its cause is known. Such events belong
       in the ledger, not in their own top-level candidate. */
    if(!BTT_devParentSeedable(row)){BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,row,"PARENT_CAUSE_UNRESOLVED",cfg);continue;}
    var matches=[];for(var c=0;c<g.candidates.length;c++)matches.push({kind:"active",candidate:g.candidates[c],score:BTT_devCandidateMatch(g.candidates[c],row)});for(var dc=0;dc<(g.dormantCandidates||[]).length;dc++)matches.push({kind:"dormant",candidate:g.dormantCandidates[dc],score:BTT_devDormantMatch(g.dormantCandidates[dc],row)});matches.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});var top=matches[0]||{score:0},next=matches[1]||{score:0},margin=Number(top.score)-Number(next.score);
    if(top.score>=cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_merge_min&&margin<cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_margin){BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,row,"PARENT_AMBIGUOUS",cfg);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("EVENT HOLD | reason=PARENT_AMBIGUOUS | top="+String(top.candidate&&top.candidate.id||"none")+":"+BTT_round(top.score,2)+"["+String(top.kind||"none")+"] | runner="+String(next.candidate&&next.candidate.id||"none")+":"+BTT_round(next.score,2)+"["+String(next.kind||"none")+"]");continue;}
    if(top.score>=cfg.dev_risk_ai_candidate_merge_min){if(top.kind==="dormant")BTT_devReviveDormant(g,top.candidate,row,top.score,cfg);else{var mergeResult=BTT_devMergeObservation(top.candidate,row,top.score,cfg);if(mergeResult&&mergeResult.held)BTT_devStoreUnrouted(g,row,mergeResult.reason||"LOCAL_CAUSAL_CONTINUITY",cfg);}}else BTT_devAddCandidatePrioritized(g,row,cfg);
    BTT_devConsolidateDuplicateParents(g,cfg);
  }
  /* New source/mechanism evidence can make earlier owner-bound events routable.
     Consolidate trigger-actor/source duplicates before and after rerouting so the
     ledger always compares against the cleanest available causal parent set. */
  BTT_devConsolidateDuplicateParents(g,cfg);BTT_devTryRerouteUnrouted(g,cfg);BTT_devConsolidateDuplicateParents(g,cfg);
  audit.sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.strength||0)-Number(a.strength||0);});for(var ai=0;ai<Math.min(3,audit.length);ai++){var a=audit[ai];BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("DISCOVERY EVENT REJECT | phase="+String(parse&&parse.phase||"").toUpperCase()+" | "+BTT_devAuditSummary(a)+" | clause=C"+a.fragmentIndex+" | evidence="+BTT_clip(a.raw,130));}
  BTT_devPruneCandidates(g,cfg);BTT_devQueueEligibleCandidate(g,cfg);
}
function BTT_devGenerationSections(){return ["name","acquisition","tracks","lifecycle"];}
function BTT_devFieldGroup(field){field=String(field||"");if(field==="name")return "name";if(/^acquire_\d+(?:_|$)/.test(field))return "acquisition";if(/^track_\d+(?:_|$)/.test(field))return "tracks";if(field==="removal")return "lifecycle";return "unknown";}
function BTT_devOwnedMechanicalField(field){return /(?:^|_)(?:threshold|history|chance|repeat|max|memory|persistence)$/.test(String(field||""));}
function BTT_devCanonicalFieldKey(rawKey){
  var s=BTT_lower(String(rawKey||"")).replace(/[\s-]+/g,"_").replace(/_+/g,"_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g,"");
  s=s.replace(/^btt_dev_risk_/,"");
  if(s==="name"||s==="title"||s==="risk_name")return "name";
  if(s==="removal"||s==="remove"||s==="lifecycle_removal"||s==="removal_conditions")return "removal";
  if(s==="persistence"||s==="lifecycle_persistence")return "persistence";
  var m=s.match(/^(?:acquire|acquisition)_?(\d+)?(?:_(.*))?$/);if(m){var n=Number(m[1]||1),tail=String(m[2]||"");if(!tail||/^(?:event|text|prototype|description)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n;if(/^(?:qualifier|qualifiers)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_qualifiers";if(tail==="delivery")return "acquire_"+n+"_delivery";if(/^(?:source_trait|source_traits)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_source_traits";if(/^(?:source_slot_trait|source_slot_traits)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_source_slot_traits";if(/^(?:source_heritage|source_heritages)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_source_heritage";if(/^(?:source_profile|source_profiles)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_source_profiles";if(/^(?:source_condition|source_conditions)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_source_conditions";if(/^(?:receiver_trait|receiver_traits)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_receiver_traits";if(/^(?:receiver_condition|receiver_conditions)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_receiver_conditions";if(/^(?:exclude_trait|exclude_traits)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_exclude_traits";if(/^(?:exclude_heritage|exclude_heritages)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_exclude_heritage";if(/^(?:exclude_risk|exclude_risks)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_exclude_risks";if(/^(?:slot|acquire_slot)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_slot";if(tail==="slot_origin"||tail==="origin")return "acquire_"+n+"_slot_origin";if(/^(?:natural_trait|natural_traits|traits)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_natural_traits";if(/^(?:axis|axes|acquire_axes)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_axes";if(/^(?:condition|conditions|acquire_conditions)$/.test(tail))return "acquire_"+n+"_conditions";if(tail==="threshold")return "acquire_"+n+"_threshold";if(tail==="history")return "acquire_"+n+"_history";return "";}
  m=s.match(/^track_?(\d+)(?:_(.*))?$/);if(m){var tn=Number(m[1]||1),tt=String(m[2]||"");if(!tt||tt==="name"||tt==="title")return "track_"+tn+"_name";if(/^(?:trigger|triggers)$/.test(tt))return "track_"+tn+"_triggers";if(tt==="delivery"||tt==="track_delivery")return "track_"+tn+"_delivery";if(/^(?:qualifier|qualifiers|track_qualifiers)$/.test(tt))return "track_"+tn+"_qualifiers";if(/^(?:source_slot_trait|source_slot_traits)$/.test(tt))return "track_"+tn+"_source_slot_traits";if(/^(?:receiver_route|receiver_routes|receiver_routing)$/.test(tt))return "track_"+tn+"_receiver_routing";var sm=tt.match(/^(?:success|outcome)_?(\d+)(?:_(effect|result|consequence|target|where|body_area|domain))?$/);if(sm){var suffix=String(sm[2]||"effect");if(/^(?:result|consequence)$/.test(suffix))suffix="effect";if(/^(?:where|body_area|domain)$/.test(suffix))suffix="target";return "track_"+tn+"_success_"+Number(sm[1])+"_"+suffix;}if(tt==="threshold")return "track_"+tn+"_threshold";if(tt==="chance")return "track_"+tn+"_chance";if(tt==="repeat")return "track_"+tn+"_repeat";if(tt==="max"||tt==="maximum")return "track_"+tn+"_max";if(tt==="memory")return "track_"+tn+"_memory";return "";}
  return s;
}
function BTT_devKnownAtomicField(field){if(field==="name"||field==="removal")return true;if(BTT_devOwnedMechanicalField(field))return true;if(/^acquire_\d+(?:_(?:qualifiers|delivery|source_traits|source_slot_traits|source_heritage|source_profiles|source_conditions|receiver_traits|receiver_conditions|exclude_traits|exclude_heritage|exclude_risks|slot|slot_origin|natural_traits|axes|conditions|threshold|history))?$/.test(field))return true;if(/^track_\d+_(?:name|triggers|delivery|qualifiers|source_slot_traits|receiver_routing|success_\d+_(?:effect|target)|threshold|chance|repeat|max|memory)$/.test(field))return true;return false;}
function BTT_devAtomicList(payload,normalizer){var vals=BTT_splitList(String(payload||"")),out=[];for(var i=0;i<vals.length;i++){var q=normalizer?normalizer(vals[i]):BTT_cleanSpace(vals[i]);if(q)out.push(q);}return BTT_unique(out);}
function BTT_devValidateAtomicField(field,payload){
  var raw=String(payload||"").replace(/\r\n?/g,"\n").trim(),vals,parts,parsed,i;if(!raw)return {valid:false,error:"EMPTY_VALUE",payload:""};
  if(field==="name"){var name=BTT_cleanSpace(raw).replace(/\n/g," ");if(name.length<3||name.length>90)return {valid:false,error:"NAME_LENGTH",payload:""};if(/\b(?:ACQUIRE|TRACK|SUCCESS|PERSISTENCE|REMOVAL)\s*:/i.test(name))return {valid:false,error:"NAME_CONTAINS_CARD_GRAMMAR",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:name};}
  if(field==="removal"){vals=BTT_devAtomicList(raw);if(!vals.length)return {valid:false,error:"NO_REMOVAL_PHRASE",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:vals.join(", ")};}
  if(BTT_devOwnedMechanicalField(field))return {valid:false,error:"SCRIPT_OWNED_FIELD",payload:""};
  if(/^acquire_\d+$/.test(field)){var ev=BTT_cleanSpace(raw).replace(/\n/g," ");if(ev.length<8||ev.length>500)return {valid:false,error:"ACQUIRE_EVENT_LENGTH",payload:""};if(/^ACQUIRE(?:\s+\d+)?\s*:/i.test(ev))return {valid:false,error:"VALUE_CONTAINS_ACQUIRE_LABEL",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:ev};}
  if(/_qualifiers$/.test(field)){vals=BTT_devAtomicList(raw);if(!vals.length)return {valid:false,error:"NO_QUALIFIERS",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:vals.join(", ")};}
  if(/_delivery$/.test(field)){vals=BTT_devAtomicList(raw,BTT_normId);if(!vals.length)return {valid:false,error:"NO_DELIVERY",payload:""};var bad=[];for(i=0;i<vals.length;i++)if(!BTT_devDeliveryProfile(vals[i]))bad.push(vals[i]);if(bad.length)return {valid:false,error:"UNSUPPORTED_DELIVERY:"+bad.join(","),payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:vals.join(", ")};}
  if(/_(?:source_traits|source_slot_traits|source_heritage|source_profiles|receiver_traits|exclude_traits|exclude_heritage|exclude_risks|natural_traits)$/.test(field)){vals=BTT_devAtomicList(raw,BTT_normId);if(!vals.length)return {valid:false,error:"EMPTY_ID_LIST",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:vals.join(", ")};}
  if(/_(?:source_conditions|receiver_conditions|conditions)$/.test(field)){vals=BTT_devAtomicList(raw,BTT_conditionType);if(!vals.length)return {valid:false,error:"EMPTY_CONDITION_LIST",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:vals.join(", ")};}
  if(/_slot$/.test(field)){var slot=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(raw)).replace(/\s+/g,"_");if(BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.indexOf(slot)<0)return {valid:false,error:"UNSUPPORTED_SLOT:"+slot,payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:slot};}
  if(/_slot_origin$/.test(field)){var origin=BTT_normId(raw);if(!origin)return {valid:false,error:"EMPTY_SLOT_ORIGIN",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:origin};}
  if(/_axes$/.test(field)){parsed=BTT_parseDevAcquireAxes(raw);parts=String(raw).split(/\s*[;,]\s*/).filter(function(x){return BTT_cleanSpace(x)!=="";});if(!parsed.length||parsed.length!==parts.length)return {valid:false,error:"INVALID_ACQUIRE_AXES",payload:""};var allowed={fem:1,height:1,fat:1,gut_size:1,breast_size:1,ass_size:1,cock_size:1,fertility:1,virility:1};for(i=0;i<parsed.length;i++)if(!allowed[parsed[i].axis])return {valid:false,error:"UNSUPPORTED_AXIS:"+parsed[i].axis,payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:parsed.map(function(x){return x.axis+" "+x.operation+" "+x.value;}).join(", ")};}
  if(/_receiver_routing$/.test(field)){var routes=BTT_parseDevReceiverRouting(raw),ks=Object.keys(routes);if(!ks.length)return {valid:false,error:"INVALID_RECEIVER_ROUTING",payload:""};var rr=[];for(i=0;i<ks.length;i++)rr.push(ks[i]+"="+routes[ks[i]].join("+"));return {valid:true,payload:rr.join("; ")};}
  if(/^track_\d+_name$/.test(field)){var tn=BTT_cleanSpace(raw).replace(/\n/g," ");if(tn.length<3||tn.length>90)return {valid:false,error:"TRACK_NAME_LENGTH",payload:""};if(/^TRACK\s*:/i.test(tn))return {valid:false,error:"VALUE_CONTAINS_TRACK_LABEL",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:tn};}
  if(/^track_\d+_triggers$/.test(field)){vals=BTT_devAtomicList(raw);if(!vals.length)return {valid:false,error:"NO_TRIGGERS",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:vals.join(", ")};}
  if(/^track_\d+_success_\d+_effect$/.test(field)){var effect=BTT_cleanSpace(raw).replace(/\n/g," ");if(effect.length<2||effect.length>420)return {valid:false,error:"OUTCOME_EFFECT_LENGTH",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:effect};}
  if(/^track_\d+_success_\d+_target$/.test(field)){var target=BTT_cleanSpace(raw).replace(/\n/g," ");if(target.length<2||target.length>100)return {valid:false,error:"OUTCOME_TARGET_LENGTH",payload:""};return {valid:true,payload:target};}
  return {valid:false,error:"UNKNOWN_FIELD",payload:""};
}

/* ----------------------- SEMANTIC SUCCESS COMPILER ------------------------
   The AI supplies consequence meaning. BTT resolves known invariants and only
   requests a missing semantic dimension when the engine cannot separate it. */
function BTT_devSuccessBase(field){var m=String(field||"").match(/^(track_\d+_success_\d+)(?:_(?:effect|target))?$/);return m?m[1]:"";}
function BTT_devSuccessIndices(p,trackN){var set={1:1},objs=[p&&p.fieldValues||{},p&&p.fieldStatus||{},p&&p.outcomeSemantics||{}];for(var o=0;o<objs.length;o++)for(var k in objs[o])if(objs[o].hasOwnProperty(k)){var m=String(k).match(new RegExp("^track_"+trackN+"_success_(\\d+)"));if(m)set[Number(m[1])]=1;}return Object.keys(set).map(Number).filter(function(n){return n>=1&&n<=6;}).sort(function(a,b){return a-b;});}
function BTT_devScriptAxisStep(axis){/* AI never balances numeric magnitude. */return 1;}
function BTT_devOutcomeTargetSlot(raw){var t=BTT_lower(String(raw||""));if(/\b(?:mind|mental|behavior|behaviour|personality|thoughts?|head|brain)\b/.test(t))return "head";if(/\b(?:breasts?|chest|bust|mammary)\b/.test(t))return "breasts";if(/\b(?:penis|cock|dick|shaft)\b/.test(t))return "penis";if(/\b(?:vagina|pussy|slit)\b/.test(t))return "vagina";if(/\b(?:ass|butt|buttocks?|rear|hips?|pelvis)\b/.test(t))return "ass";if(/\b(?:belly|gut|abdomen|stomach)\b/.test(t))return "gut";if(/\b(?:tail)\b/.test(t))return "tail";if(/\b(?:wings?)\b/.test(t))return "wings";if(/\b(?:arms?|hands?|fingers?)\b/.test(t))return "arms";if(/\b(?:legs?|feet|foot|thighs?|calves?)\b/.test(t))return "legs";if(/\b(?:face|hair|eyes?|ears?|horns?|mouth|lips?|voice)\b/.test(t))return "head";return "";}
function BTT_devGenericMorphologyTrait(raw){
  /* Non-axis slots still need a deterministic representation for simple growth
     or reduction. BTT owns this vocabulary so an AI clarification such as
     target=tail can complete an already-known effect=growth without re-authoring. */
  var t=BTT_lower(String(raw||""));
  if(/\b(?:grow|grows|grew|growing|growth|enlarge|enlarged|larger|bigger|expand|expanded|swell|swollen|increase(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size)\b/.test(t))return "enlarged";
  if(/\b(?:shrink|shrinks|shrunk|shrinking|reduce(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size|smaller|contract|contracted|decrease(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size)\b/.test(t))return "reduced";
  return "";
}
function BTT_devOutcomeAxis(raw,target){
  /* Axis compilation is intentionally semantic, not keyword-only. A body-area
     noun does not make every generic "increase" a size change: e.g. "penis
     sensitivity increases" must become a PENIS trait, not cock_size growth. */
  var effect=BTT_lower(String(raw||"")),targetText=BTT_lower(String(target||"")),t=effect+" "+targetText,axis="",op="+";
  var sizeUp=/\b(?:grow|grows|grew|growing|growth|enlarge|enlarges|enlarged|enlarging|larger|bigger|fuller|rounder|widen|widens|widened|widening|expand|expands|expanded|expanding|swell|swells|swollen|swelling|increase(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size|size\s+increase\w*)\b/.test(effect);
  var sizeDown=/\b(?:shrink|shrinks|shrunk|shrinking|reduce(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size|smaller|flatten|flattens|flattened|flattening|narrow|narrows|narrowed|narrowing|contract|contracts|contracted|contracting|decrease(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size|size\s+decrease\w*)\b/.test(effect);
  var genericDown=/\b(?:decrease|decreases|decreased|decreasing|less|lower|lowered|reduce|reduces|reduced|weaken|weakens|weakened|diminish|diminishes|diminished)\b/.test(effect);

  if(/\b(?:feminin\w*|femin(?:ine|ize|ise|ized|ised|ization|isation)|womanly|girlish)\b/.test(t)){
    axis="fem";op=/\b(?:less\s+feminin|masculin\w*|defeminiz\w*)\b/.test(effect)?"-":"+";
  }else if(/\b(?:fertility|fertile|fecund)\b/.test(t)){
    axis="fertility";op=genericDown||/\b(?:infertile|sterile)\b/.test(effect)?"-":"+";
  }else if(/\b(?:virility|virile)\b/.test(t)){
    axis="virility";op=genericDown?"-":"+";
  }else if(/\b(?:height|stature|taller|shorter)\b/.test(t)){
    if(/\b(?:shorter|height\s+(?:decrease|reduction)|reduced\s+height)\b/.test(effect))axis="height",op="-";
    else if(/\b(?:taller|height\s+(?:increase|growth)|increased\s+height)\b/.test(effect))axis="height",op="+";
  }else if(/\b(?:body\s*fat|fatness|weight|fatter|leaner|slimmer)\b/.test(t)){
    if(/\b(?:weight\s+loss|loses?\s+weight|leaner|slimmer|less\s+(?:body\s*)?fat|fat\s+(?:decrease|reduction))\b/.test(effect))axis="fat",op="-";
    else if(/\b(?:weight\s+gain|gains?\s+weight|fatter|more\s+(?:body\s*)?fat|fat\s+(?:increase|growth))\b/.test(effect))axis="fat",op="+";
  }else{
    /* Structural size axes require an explicit size/morphology direction. */
    if(/\b(?:breasts?|chest|bust|mammary)\b/.test(t))axis="breast_size";
    else if(/\b(?:ass|butt|buttocks?|rear|hips?|pelvis)\b/.test(t))axis="ass_size";
    else if(/\b(?:penis|cock|dick|shaft)\b/.test(t))axis="cock_size";
    else if(/\b(?:belly|gut|abdomen|stomach|waist)\b/.test(t))axis="gut_size";
    if(axis){if(sizeDown)op="-";else if(sizeUp)op="+";else axis="";}
  }
  if(!axis)return null;
  return {axis:axis,operation:op,value:BTT_devScriptAxisStep(axis),confidence:0.90,reason:"semantic_axis"};
}
function BTT_devOutcomeMentalTraits(raw){var t=BTT_lower(String(raw||"")),out=[];for(var tr in BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS)if(BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS.hasOwnProperty(tr)){var re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(tr).replace(/\\-/g,"[- ]")+"\\b","i");if(re.test(t))out.push(tr);}var map=[[/\b(?:obedien\w*|obey\w*)\b/i,"obedient"],[/\b(?:complian\w*|comply\w*)\b/i,"compliant"],[/\b(?:submiss\w*|submit\w*)\b/i,"submissive"],[/\bdocil\w*\b/i,"docile"],[/\b(?:pleasure[- ]?condition\w*|reward[- ]?condition\w*)\b/i,"pleasure-conditioned"],[/\b(?:impulse[- ]?driven|compulsive impulses?)\b/i,"impulse-driven"],[/\b(?:libido[- ]?heighten\w*|heightened libido)\b/i,"libido-heightened"]];for(var i=0;i<map.length;i++)if(map[i][0].test(t))out.push(map[i][1]);return BTT_unique(out);}
function BTT_devOutcomeKnownCondition(raw){var t=BTT_lower(String(raw||"")),pairs=[["pregnancy",/\b(?:pregnan\w*|fertiliz\w*)\b/],["incubation",/\b(?:incubat\w*|egg-bearing|gravid|brooding)\b/],["seeded",/\b(?:seeded|seeding|seed-bearing)\b/],["infestation",/\b(?:infest\w*|parasite infestation)\b/],["possession",/\b(?:possess\w*|spiritual possession)\b/],["infection",/\binfect\w*\b/],["corruption",/\bcorrupt\w*\b/],["pollination",/\bpollinat\w*\b/]];for(var i=0;i<pairs.length;i++)if(pairs[i][1].test(t))return pairs[i][0];return "";}
function BTT_devTraitCandidateSlots(trait){trait=BTT_normId(trait);if(!trait)return [];if(BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS[trait])return ["head"];var set={},kinds=["gender","heritage"];for(var ki=0;ki<kinds.length;ki++){var ps=BTT_profiles(kinds[ki]);for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){var slots=ps[id].slots||{};for(var sl in slots)if(slots.hasOwnProperty(sl)){var a=slots[sl]&&slots[sl].traits||[];for(var x=0;x<a.length;x++)if(BTT_normId(a[x])===trait)set[sl]=1;}}}return Object.keys(set);}
function BTT_devOutcomeTraitCandidate(raw){var t=BTT_lower(String(raw||"")),reg=BTT_profileTraitRegistry(),hits=[];for(var tr in reg)if(reg.hasOwnProperty(tr)){var re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(tr).replace(/\\-/g,"[- ]")+"\\b","i");if(re.test(t))hits.push(tr);}hits.sort(function(a,b){return b.length-a.length;});if(hits.length)return hits[0];var stripped=t.replace(/\b(?:becomes?|became|gets?|gains?|develops?|turns?|more|increasingly|less|trait|condition|axis|success|outcome|effect)\b/g," ").replace(/[^a-z0-9-]+/g," ").trim().replace(/\s+/g,"-");if(stripped&&stripped.split("-").length<=4&&!/^(?:growth|change|increase|decrease|transformation|effect|stronger|weaker)$/.test(stripped))return stripped;return "";}
function BTT_devOutcomeEvidenceSlot(p){var scores={},hits={},events=p&&p.events||[];for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++)for(var j=0;j<(events[i].clauses||[]).length;j++){var c=events[i].clauses[j],sl=String(c.slot||"");if(!sl||BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.indexOf(sl)<0)continue;var conf=Number(c.slotConfidence)||0;if(conf<0.40)continue;scores[sl]=(scores[sl]||0)+conf*Math.max(0.55,Math.min(1.2,Number(events[i].strength)||0.8));hits[sl]=(hits[sl]||0)+1;}var rows=Object.keys(scores).map(function(sl){return {slot:sl,score:scores[sl],hits:hits[sl]};}).sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;}),top=rows[0],next=rows[1]||{score:0};if(top&&((top.hits>=2&&top.score>=0.90&&top.score-next.score>=0.35)||(top.hits===1&&top.score>=0.92&&top.score-next.score>=0.45)))return {slot:top.slot,confidence:BTT_round(Math.min(0.90,0.56+top.score*0.18),2),reason:"retained_event_anchor"};return {slot:"",confidence:0,reason:rows.length?"ambiguous_event_anchors":"no_event_anchor"};}
function BTT_devOutcomeTargetContext(p,base){var m=String(base||"").match(/^track_(\d+)_success_(\d+)$/),v=p&&p.fieldValues||{},parts=[];if(m){var pre="track_"+m[1];if(v[pre+"_name"])parts.push(v[pre+"_name"]);if(v[pre+"_triggers"])parts.push(v[pre+"_triggers"]);}return parts.join(" ");}
function BTT_devResolveOutcomeSemantic(p,base){
  var v=p&&p.fieldValues||{},effect=BTT_cleanSpace(v[base+"_effect"]||""),target=BTT_cleanSpace(v[base+"_target"]||""),context=BTT_devOutcomeTargetContext(p,base),compiled=[],reason=[],partial={};
  if(!effect)return {base:base,status:"missing_effect",effect:"",target:target,compiled:[],partial:{},reason:"EFFECT_MISSING"};

  /* 1. Engine invariants win before any target question. Mental/behavioral
     traits are HEAD-only across BTT, so the model is never asked to rediscover
     their slot. Emit one creator-card outcome per trait. */
  var mental=BTT_devOutcomeMentalTraits(effect);
  if(mental.length){for(var mi=0;mi<mental.length;mi++)compiled.push("HEAD +"+mental[mi]);partial.type="traits";partial.slot="head";partial.traits=mental.slice();reason.push("mental_trait_invariant");}

  /* 2. Known scalar anatomy/appearance concepts compile to script-owned axis
     steps. The axis resolver deliberately requires size-specific language for
     body-size axes, preventing e.g. sensitivity increases -> cock_size growth. */
  var axis=BTT_devOutcomeAxis(effect,target||context);
  if(axis){compiled.push("AXIS "+axis.axis+" "+axis.operation+" "+axis.value);if(!partial.type)partial.type="axis";partial.axis=axis.axis;partial.direction=axis.operation;partial.magnitude=axis.value;reason.push(axis.reason);}

  /* 3. Known Conditions are canonical engine domains, not arbitrary traits. */
  var condition=BTT_devOutcomeKnownCondition(effect);
  if(condition&&!mental.length){compiled.push("CONDITION +"+condition);if(!partial.type)partial.type="condition";partial.condition=condition;reason.push("known_condition");}

  var genericMorph=/\b(?:grow|growth|growing|enlarge|increase(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size|bigger|larger|shrink|shrinking|reduce(?:s|d|ing)?\s+(?:in\s+)?size|smaller|sensitivity|sensitive)\w*\b/i.test(effect),targetSlot=BTT_devOutcomeTargetSlot(target||effect),trait=BTT_devOutcomeTraitCandidate(effect);
  if(!targetSlot&&genericMorph){var evSlot=BTT_devOutcomeEvidenceSlot(p);if(evSlot.slot)targetSlot=evSlot.slot;}

  /* 4. Physical morphology not represented by a numeric axis is still resolved
     deterministically once its target is known. This lets a repair answer of
     merely `tail` complete an already-captured `growth` consequence. */
  if(!axis&&genericMorph&&targetSlot){
    if(/\bsensitiv\w*\b/i.test(effect)){
      compiled.push(targetSlot.toUpperCase()+" +sensitive");if(!partial.type)partial.type="traits";partial.slot=targetSlot;partial.traits=BTT_unique((partial.traits||[]).concat(["sensitive"]));reason.push("targeted_sensitivity_trait");
    }else{
      var morphTrait=BTT_devGenericMorphologyTrait(effect);
      if(morphTrait){compiled.push(targetSlot.toUpperCase()+" +"+morphTrait);if(!partial.type)partial.type="traits";partial.slot=targetSlot;partial.traits=BTT_unique((partial.traits||[]).concat([morphTrait]));reason.push("script_owned_nonaxis_morphology");}
    }
  }

  /* 5. Canonical/known traits use a unique legal slot when possible, otherwise
     the retained evidence anchor or a separately captured target supplies the
     missing semantic dimension. */
  if(!mental.length&&!axis&&!condition&&trait){
    var already=compiled.some(function(x){return /\s\+/.test(x)&&x.indexOf("AXIS ")!==0&&x.indexOf("CONDITION ")!==0;}),slots=BTT_devTraitCandidateSlots(trait);
    if(!targetSlot&&slots.length===1)targetSlot=slots[0];
    if(!targetSlot){var ev=BTT_devOutcomeEvidenceSlot(p);if(ev.slot)targetSlot=ev.slot;}
    if(targetSlot&&!already&&trait!=="enlarged"&&trait!=="reduced"){compiled.push(targetSlot.toUpperCase()+" +"+trait);if(!partial.type)partial.type="traits";partial.slot=targetSlot;partial.traits=BTT_unique((partial.traits||[]).concat([trait]));reason.push(slots.length===1?"unique_trait_slot":"resolved_target_trait");}
  }

  compiled=BTT_unique(compiled);
  var unresolvedMorph=genericMorph&&!compiled.length,status=compiled.length&&!unresolvedMorph?"resolved":"needs_target";
  return {base:base,status:status,effect:effect,target:target,compiled:compiled,partial:partial,reason:reason.join("+")||(status==="resolved"?"semantic_resolved":"TARGET_REQUIRED")};
}
function BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p){p.outcomeSemantics=p.outcomeSemantics&&typeof p.outcomeSemantics==="object"?p.outcomeSemantics:{};var tracks=BTT_devActiveIndexedGroups?BTT_devActiveIndexedGroups(p,"track"):[1];for(var ti=0;ti<tracks.length;ti++){var sis=BTT_devSuccessIndices(p,tracks[ti]);for(var si=0;si<sis.length;si++){var base="track_"+tracks[ti]+"_success_"+sis[si],plan=BTT_devResolveOutcomeSemantic(p,base),old=p.outcomeSemantics[base]||{};p.outcomeSemantics[base]=plan;if(plan.status==="needs_target"&&p.fieldValues&&p.fieldValues[base+"_target"]){p.fieldStatus[base+"_target"]="invalid";p.fieldErrors[base+"_target"]="TARGET_DID_NOT_RESOLVE_EFFECT";}else if(plan.status==="resolved"&&p.fieldValues&&p.fieldValues[base+"_target"]){p.fieldStatus[base+"_target"]="valid";p.fieldErrors[base+"_target"]="";}if(old.status!==plan.status||old.reason!==plan.reason)BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("OUTCOME RESOLUTION | "+base+" | status="+plan.status+" | effect="+BTT_clip(plan.effect,120)+" | target="+(plan.target||"unresolved")+" | compiled="+(plan.compiled.join("; ")||"none")+" | reason="+plan.reason);}}return p.outcomeSemantics;}
function BTT_devOutcomePlansText(p){BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);var plans=p.outcomeSemantics||{},ks=Object.keys(plans).sort(),lines=[];for(var i=0;i<ks.length;i++){var q=plans[ks[i]];if(!q.effect)continue;lines.push("- "+ks[i]+": effect="+BTT_clip(q.effect,150)+"; target="+(q.target||"unresolved")+"; status="+q.status+"; compiled="+(q.compiled&&q.compiled.length?q.compiled.join("; "):"pending"));}return lines.length?lines.join("\n"):"[no outcome effects captured yet]";}

function BTT_devTransportOperations(raw){var t=String(raw||""),re=/(^|[\r\n]|[\(\[\{]\s*)(btt[\s_-]*dev[\s_-]*risk[\s_-]*[a-z0-9][a-z0-9 _-]*?)\s*(=|:)\s*/ig,m,found=[];while((m=re.exec(t))){var prefix=String(m[1]||""),container="",cm=prefix.match(/[\(\[\{]\s*$/);if(cm)container=cm[0].replace(/\s/g,"");found.push({matchIndex:m.index,key:String(m[2]||""),separator:String(m[3]||"="),payloadStart:re.lastIndex,container:container});}var ops=[];for(var i=0;i<found.length;i++){var f=found[i],end=i+1<found.length?found[i+1].matchIndex:t.length,seg=t.slice(f.payloadStart,end).trim(),quote="",closer="",repaired=false,payload="",incomplete=false,transportError="";if(seg){var ch=seg.charAt(0),pairs={"`":"`","'":"'","\"":"\"","‘":"’","“":"”"};if(pairs[ch]){quote=ch;closer=pairs[ch];var qi=seg.lastIndexOf(closer);if(qi>0){var tail=seg.slice(qi+1);if(/^\s*[\)\]\}]?\s*$/.test(tail))payload=seg.slice(1,qi);else{payload=seg.slice(1,qi);incomplete=true;transportError="TRAILING_OR_UNCLOSED_QUOTED_VALUE";}}else{payload=seg.slice(1);repaired=true;incomplete=true;transportError="UNCLOSED_QUOTED_VALUE";}}else{payload=seg;var nl=payload.search(/[\r\n]/);if(nl>=0)payload=payload.slice(0,nl);repaired=true;}payload=payload.replace(/^\s*```(?:text)?\s*/i,"").replace(/\s*```\s*$/i,"").replace(/\s*[\)\]\}]\s*$/," ").trim();}var canonical=BTT_devCanonicalFieldKey(f.key),containerName=f.container==="("?"()":f.container==="["?"[]":f.container==="{"?"{}":"none",quoteName=quote==="`"?"backtick":quote==="\""?"double":quote==="'"?"single":quote?"smart":"none";if(f.separator!=="="||containerName!=="()"||quoteName!=="backtick")repaired=true;ops.push({rawKey:f.key,canonical:canonical,payload:payload,container:containerName,quoting:quoteName,repaired:repaired,incomplete:incomplete,transportError:transportError});}return ops;}
function BTT_devAllEventText(p){var parts=[],events=p&&p.events||[];for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++)parts.push(BTT_devEventText(events[i]));return parts.join(" ");}
function BTT_devInferGeneratedDeliveries(p){
  /* Delivery inference is intentionally conservative. BTT only owns DELIVERY when
     the resolved mechanism or explicit lexical cues make the route clear enough;
     generic semantic similarity alone is never allowed to invent a route. */
  var text=BTT_devAllEventText(p),lower=BTT_lower(text),ids=Object.keys(BTT_DEV_DELIVERY_PROFILES),scores={},explicit={},i;
  for(i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
    var id=ids[i],prof=BTT_DEV_DELIVERY_PROFILES[id],best=0;
    for(var a=0;a<(prof.aliases||[]).length;a++)if(BTT_devCueHit(text,prof.aliases[a]))best=Math.max(best,0.86);
    var hits=0;for(var c=0;c<(prof.cues||[]).length;c++)if(BTT_devCueHit(text,prof.cues[c]))hits++;
    if(hits)best=Math.max(best,Math.min(0.94,0.74+Math.min(0.20,hits*0.06)));
    scores[id]=best;explicit[id]=best;
  }
  function prior(id,val){scores[id]=Math.max(Number(scores[id])||0,val);}
  var mech=String(p&&p.mechanism||""),tags=BTT_unique((p&&p.mechanismTags||[]).concat(p&&p.trackHints||[])),acq=[],track=[];
  if(mech==="contract"){
    if(tags.indexOf("reward")>=0||tags.indexOf("aversive")>=0||tags.indexOf("obedience")>=0||tags.indexOf("behavioral")>=0){prior("compulsion",0.88);prior("behavioral",0.82);acq=["compulsion","behavioral"];track=["compulsion","behavioral"];}
  }else if(mech==="clothing"){
    prior("direct-contact",0.90);acq=["direct-contact"];
    track=["direct-contact"];if(tags.indexOf("persistent")>=0||/again|repeated|continued|prolonged|hours|days|ongoing/i.test(text)){prior("repeated-exposure",0.82);track.push("repeated-exposure");}
  }else if(mech==="curse"){
    prior("curse",0.94);prior("spell",0.84);acq=["curse","spell"];track=["curse","spell"];
  }else if(mech==="parasite"){
    prior("infestation",0.94);acq=["infestation"];track=["infestation"];if(/implant|burrow|inside|host/i.test(text)){prior("implantation",0.84);acq.push("implantation");track.push("implantation");}
  }else if(mech==="product"){
    if(["drink","swallow","ingest","consume","eat"].some(function(x){return BTT_devCueHit(text,x);})){prior("ingestion",0.92);acq=["ingestion"];track=["ingestion"];}
    else if(["inject","needle","syringe"].some(function(x){return BTT_devCueHit(text,x);})){prior("injection",0.92);acq=["injection"];track=["injection"];}
    else if(["absorb","through skin","seep","soak","pores","permeat"].some(function(x){return BTT_devCueHit(text,x);})){prior("absorption",0.88);acq=["absorption"];track=["absorption"];}
  }
  /* If mechanism priors could not resolve the route, accept only explicit lexical
     delivery evidence. This is where the AI delivery wrapper becomes necessary. */
  function explicitChoices(){var ranked=ids.map(function(id){return {id:id,score:Number(explicit[id])||0};}).sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;}),out=[];for(var r=0;r<ranked.length&&out.length<2;r++){if(ranked[r].score<0.80)break;if(out.length&&ranked[r].score<ranked[0].score-0.12)break;out.push(ranked[r].id);}return out;}
  if(!acq.length)acq=explicitChoices();if(!track.length)track=acq.slice();
  var ranked=ids.map(function(id){return {id:id,score:Number(scores[id])||0,explicit:Number(explicit[id])||0};}).sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});
  return {acquisition:BTT_unique(acq).slice(0,2),track:BTT_unique(track).slice(0,2),scores:ranked.slice(0,8)};
}
function BTT_devInitializeAuthoringPlan(p,cfg){
  p.fieldValues=p.fieldValues&&typeof p.fieldValues==="object"?p.fieldValues:{};p.fieldStatus=p.fieldStatus&&typeof p.fieldStatus==="object"?p.fieldStatus:{};p.fieldErrors=p.fieldErrors&&typeof p.fieldErrors==="object"?p.fieldErrors:{};p.derived=p.derived&&typeof p.derived==="object"?p.derived:{};p.outcomeSemantics=p.outcomeSemantics&&typeof p.outcomeSemantics==="object"?p.outcomeSemantics:{};
  if(p.mode!=="extension"){var inf=BTT_devInferGeneratedDeliveries(p);p.derived.acquire_1_delivery=inf.acquisition.join(", ");p.derived.track_1_delivery=inf.track.join(", ");p.derived.deliveryScores=inf.scores;p.derived.track_count=Math.max(1,Number(p.derived.track_count||p.blueprint&&p.blueprint.trackCount||1));p.derived.acquisition_count=Math.max(1,Number(p.derived.acquisition_count||p.blueprint&&p.blueprint.acquisitionCount||1));}
  p.derived.acquire_threshold=Number(cfg.dev_risk_acquire_threshold);p.derived.acquire_history=Math.round(Number(cfg.dev_risk_acquire_history_turns));p.derived.track_threshold=Number(cfg.dev_risk_track_threshold);p.derived.chance=Number(cfg.dev_risk_default_base_chance);p.derived.repeat=Number(cfg.dev_risk_default_repeat_increase);p.derived.max=Number(cfg.dev_risk_default_maximum_chance);p.derived.memory=Math.round(Number(cfg.dev_risk_default_memory_turns));p.derived.persistence="acquired";p.kickoffNoticeEmitted=!!p.kickoffNoticeEmitted;p.lastRequestedFields=Array.isArray(p.lastRequestedFields)?p.lastRequestedFields:[];p.lastPromptMode=isFinite(Number(p.lastPromptMode))?Number(p.lastPromptMode):0;BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);BTT_devRecomputeSectionStatus(p);return p;
}function BTT_devActiveIndexedGroups(p,prefix){var set={},count=prefix==="track"?Number(p&&p.derived&&p.derived.track_count||0):Number(p&&p.derived&&p.derived.acquisition_count||0);if(count<1)count=1;for(var z=1;z<=Math.min(6,count);z++)set[z]=1;var objs=[p&&p.fieldValues||{},p&&p.fieldStatus||{},p&&p.outcomeSemantics||{}];for(var o=0;o<objs.length;o++)for(var k in objs[o])if(objs[o].hasOwnProperty(k)){var m=k.match(new RegExp("^"+prefix+"_(\\d+)(?:_|$)"));if(m)set[Number(m[1])]=1;}return Object.keys(set).map(Number).filter(function(n){return n>=1&&n<=6;}).sort(function(a,b){return a-b;});}function BTT_devFieldResolved(p,field){if(/^track_\d+_success_\d+_target$/.test(field)){var base=BTT_devSuccessBase(field),plan=p&&p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[base];return !!(plan&&plan.status==="resolved");}if(p&&p.fieldStatus&&p.fieldStatus[field]==="valid")return true;if(field==="acquire_1_delivery"&&p&&p.derived&&p.derived.acquire_1_delivery)return true;if(/^track_\d+_delivery$/.test(field)&&p&&p.derived&&p.derived[field])return true;return false;}
function BTT_devRequiredFields(p){
  BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);
  if(p&&p.mode==="extension"&&p.extension){
    var kind=String(p.extension.kind||"higher_success"),tn=Number(p.extension.trackIndex)+1;
    if(kind==="new_track"){
      var baseNew="track_"+tn+"_success_1",planNew=p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[baseNew],newFields=["track_"+tn+"_name","track_"+tn+"_triggers",baseNew+"_effect"];
      if(planNew&&planNew.effect&&planNew.status==="needs_target")newFields.push(baseNew+"_target");if(!(p.derived&&p.derived["track_"+tn+"_delivery"]))newFields.push("track_"+tn+"_delivery");return newFields.filter(function(f){return !BTT_devFieldResolved(p,f);});
    }
    var base="track_"+tn+"_success_"+Number(p.extension.nextSuccess),plan=p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[base],out=[base+"_effect"];if(plan&&plan.effect&&plan.status==="needs_target")out.push(base+"_target");return out.filter(function(f){return !BTT_devFieldResolved(p,f);});
  }
  var out=["name","removal"],acqs=BTT_devActiveIndexedGroups(p,"acquire"),tracks=BTT_devActiveIndexedGroups(p,"track"),i,n;for(i=0;i<acqs.length;i++){n=acqs[i];out.push("acquire_"+n,"acquire_"+n+"_qualifiers");if(n!==1||!(p.derived&&p.derived.acquire_1_delivery))out.push("acquire_"+n+"_delivery");}for(i=0;i<tracks.length;i++){n=tracks[i];out.push("track_"+n+"_name","track_"+n+"_triggers");var sis=BTT_devSuccessIndices(p,n);for(var si=0;si<sis.length;si++){var b="track_"+n+"_success_"+sis[si],ef=b+"_effect",pl=p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[b];out.push(ef);if(pl&&pl.effect&&pl.status==="needs_target")out.push(b+"_target");}if(!(p.derived&&p.derived["track_"+n+"_delivery"]))out.push("track_"+n+"_delivery");}return BTT_unique(out).filter(function(f){return !BTT_devFieldResolved(p,f);});
}function BTT_devRequestedFields(p){var req=BTT_devRequiredFields(p),status=p&&p.fieldStatus||{},out=[],k;for(var i=0;i<req.length;i++)if(!BTT_devFieldResolved(p,req[i]))out.push(req[i]);for(k in status)if(status.hasOwnProperty(k)&&status[k]==="invalid"&&out.indexOf(k)<0&&!BTT_devOwnedMechanicalField(k)){if(/^track_\d+_success_\d+_target$/.test(k)){var b=BTT_devSuccessBase(k),pl=p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[b];if(pl&&pl.status==="resolved")continue;}out.push(k);}return out;}
function BTT_devRecomputeSectionStatus(p){if(p&&p.mode==="extension"){var req=BTT_devRequiredFields(p),status={name:"valid",acquisition:"valid",tracks:req.length?"missing":"valid",lifecycle:"valid"},errors={name:"",acquisition:"",tracks:req.length?(p.fieldErrors&&p.fieldErrors[req[0]]||("MISSING:"+req[0])):"",lifecycle:""};for(var i=0;i<req.length;i++)if(p.fieldStatus&&p.fieldStatus[req[i]]==="invalid"){status.tracks="invalid";errors.tracks=p.fieldErrors[req[i]]||("INVALID:"+req[i]);break;}p.sectionStatus=status;p.sectionErrors=errors;return status;}var sections=BTT_devGenerationSections(),status={},errors={},required=BTT_devRequiredFields(p),fieldStatus=p.fieldStatus||{},fieldErrors=p.fieldErrors||{},i,s,k;for(i=0;i<sections.length;i++){s=sections[i];status[s]="valid";errors[s]="";}for(i=0;i<required.length;i++){var f=required[i],g=BTT_devFieldGroup(f);if(!BTT_devFieldResolved(p,f)){status[g]=fieldStatus[f]==="invalid"?"invalid":"missing";if(!errors[g])errors[g]=fieldErrors[f]||("MISSING:"+f);}}for(k in fieldStatus)if(fieldStatus.hasOwnProperty(k)&&fieldStatus[k]==="invalid"){g=BTT_devFieldGroup(k);if(g!=="unknown"){if(/^track_\d+_success_\d+_target$/.test(k)){var b=BTT_devSuccessBase(k),pl=p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[b];if(pl&&pl.status==="resolved")continue;}status[g]="invalid";if(!errors[g])errors[g]=fieldErrors[k]||("INVALID:"+k);}}p.sectionStatus=status;p.sectionErrors=errors;return status;}function BTT_devRequestedSections(p){BTT_devRecomputeSectionStatus(p);var out=[],sections=BTT_devGenerationSections();for(var i=0;i<sections.length;i++)if(p.sectionStatus[sections[i]]!=="valid")out.push(sections[i]);return out;}
function BTT_devCompileAcquisition(p,n,cfg){var v=p.fieldValues||{},d=p.derived||{},pre="acquire_"+n,delivery=n===1&&d.acquire_1_delivery?d.acquire_1_delivery:v[pre+"_delivery"],lines=["ACQUIRE "+n+": "+String(v[pre]||""),"DELIVERY: "+String(delivery||"exposure"),"QUALIFIERS: "+String(v[pre+"_qualifiers"]||"")],map=[["source_traits","SOURCE TRAITS"],["source_slot_traits","SOURCE SLOT TRAITS"],["source_heritage","SOURCE HERITAGE"],["source_profiles","SOURCE PROFILES"],["source_conditions","SOURCE CONDITIONS"],["receiver_traits","RECEIVER TRAITS"],["receiver_conditions","RECEIVER CONDITIONS"],["exclude_traits","EXCLUDE TRAITS"],["exclude_heritage","EXCLUDE HERITAGE"],["exclude_risks","EXCLUDE RISKS"],["slot","ACQUIRE SLOT"],["slot_origin","SLOT ORIGIN"],["natural_traits","NATURAL TRAITS"],["axes","ACQUIRE AXES"],["conditions","ACQUIRE CONDITIONS"]];for(var i=0;i<map.length;i++)if(v[pre+"_"+map[i][0]])lines.push(map[i][1]+": "+v[pre+"_"+map[i][0]]);lines.push("ACQUIRE THRESHOLD: "+BTT_round(cfg.dev_risk_acquire_threshold,2));lines.push("ACQUIRE HISTORY: "+Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_acquire_history_turns));return lines.join("\n");}
function BTT_devCompileTrack(p,n,cfg){BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);var v=p.fieldValues||{},d=p.derived||{},pre="track_"+n,delivery=v[pre+"_delivery"]||d[pre+"_delivery"]||d.track_1_delivery,lines=["TRACK: "+String(v[pre+"_name"]||"Track "+n),"TRIGGERS: "+String(v[pre+"_triggers"]||""),"DELIVERY: "+String(delivery||"exposure")];if(v[pre+"_qualifiers"])lines.push("QUALIFIERS: "+v[pre+"_qualifiers"]);if(v[pre+"_source_slot_traits"])lines.push("SOURCE SLOT TRAITS: "+v[pre+"_source_slot_traits"]);if(v[pre+"_receiver_routing"])lines.push("RECEIVER ROUTING: "+v[pre+"_receiver_routing"]);lines.push("TRACK THRESHOLD: "+BTT_round(cfg.dev_risk_track_threshold,2));lines.push("CHANCE: "+BTT_round(cfg.dev_risk_default_base_chance,2));lines.push("REPEAT: "+BTT_round(cfg.dev_risk_default_repeat_increase,2));lines.push("MAX: "+BTT_round(cfg.dev_risk_default_maximum_chance,2));lines.push("MEMORY: "+Math.round(cfg.dev_risk_default_memory_turns));var plans=p.outcomeSemantics||{},rows=[];for(var k in plans)if(plans.hasOwnProperty(k)){var m=k.match(new RegExp("^"+pre+"_success_(\\d+)$"));if(m&&plans[k].status==="resolved"&&plans[k].compiled&&plans[k].compiled.length)rows.push({n:Number(m[1]),value:plans[k].compiled.join("; ")});}rows.sort(function(a,b){return a.n-b.n;});for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++)lines.push("SUCCESS "+rows[i].n+": "+rows[i].value);return lines.join("\n");}
function BTT_devAssembleGeneratedCard(p){var cfg=BTT_getConfig();BTT_devRecomputeSectionStatus(p);var name=BTT_cleanSpace(p.fieldValues&&p.fieldValues.name||"Generated Developable Risk"),acqs=BTT_devActiveIndexedGroups(p,"acquire"),tracks=BTT_devActiveIndexedGroups(p,"track"),blocks=[];for(var i=0;i<acqs.length;i++)blocks.push(BTT_devCompileAcquisition(p,acqs[i],cfg));for(i=0;i<tracks.length;i++)blocks.push(BTT_devCompileTrack(p,tracks[i],cfg));blocks.push("PERSISTENCE: acquired\nREMOVAL: "+String(p.fieldValues&&p.fieldValues.removal||""));var value=[name,"","AI-GENERATED: true","",blocks.join("\n\n")].join("\n");return {name:name,title:"BTT Dev Risk - "+name,value:value,acquireCount:acqs.length,trackCount:tracks.length};}
function BTT_devValidateWholeCard(p){var req=BTT_devRequestedFields(p);if(req.length)return {valid:false,error:"FIELDS_NOT_VALID:"+req.join(","),sections:BTT_devRequestedSections(p)};var assembled=BTT_devAssembleGeneratedCard(p),fake={title:assembled.title,type:"BTT Developable Risk",value:assembled.value,entry:""},parsed=BTT_parseDevRiskCard(fake);if(!parsed)return {valid:false,error:"CARD_PARSE_FAILED",sections:["acquisition","tracks"]};if(parsed.acquisitions.length!==assembled.acquireCount)return {valid:false,error:"ACQUISITION_ROUNDTRIP_MISMATCH",sections:["acquisition"]};if(parsed.tracks.length!==assembled.trackCount)return {valid:false,error:"TRACK_ROUNDTRIP_MISMATCH",sections:["tracks"]};for(var i=0;i<parsed.tracks.length;i++)if(!(parsed.tracks[i].outcomes||[]).length)return {valid:false,error:"TRACK_"+(i+1)+"_NO_OUTCOMES_AFTER_ROUNDTRIP",sections:["tracks"]};return {valid:true,assembled:assembled,parsed:parsed};}
function BTT_devCapturedValuesText(p){var v=p&&p.fieldValues||{},ks=Object.keys(v).filter(function(k){return p.fieldStatus&&p.fieldStatus[k]==="valid";}).sort(),lines=[];for(var i=0;i<ks.length;i++)lines.push("- "+ks[i]+" = "+BTT_clip(v[ks[i]],220));var plans=BTT_devOutcomePlansText(p);if(plans&&plans.indexOf("[no outcome")!==0)lines.push("Outcome plans:\n"+plans);return lines.length?lines.join("\n"):"[none yet]";}
function BTT_devAllowedDeliveryIds(){return Object.keys(BTT_DEV_DELIVERY_PROFILES).join(", ");}
function BTT_devSuggestedDeliveryIds(p){var out=[],rank=p&&p.derived&&p.derived.deliveryScores||[];for(var i=0;i<rank.length&&out.length<8;i++)if(Number(rank[i].score)>=0.30)out.push(rank[i].id);var fallback=["exposure","repeated-exposure","direct-contact","behavioral","compulsion","spell","curse","transformation","ingestion","absorption","infestation","implantation"];for(i=0;i<fallback.length&&out.length<12;i++)if(out.indexOf(fallback[i])<0)out.push(fallback[i]);return out.join(", ");}
function BTT_devFieldInstruction(field,p){
  var bp=p&&p.blueprint||{},trackMatch=String(field||"").match(/^track_(\d+)_/),trackN=trackMatch?Number(trackMatch[1]):0,trackBlueprint=trackMatch&&bp.selectedTracks&&bp.selectedTracks[trackN-1]||null,ext=p&&p.mode==="extension"&&p.extension||null,newTrack=ext&&ext.kind==="new_track";
  if(field==="name")return "(btt_dev_risk_name = `<concise generalized name for "+String(bp.riskFamily||"the resolved risk family")+">`)";
  if(/^acquire_\d+$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<generalized establishing event for source/mechanism "+String(bp.mechanism||p.mechanism||"resolved mechanism")+"; summarize the event FAMILY, not one retained sentence; value only>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/acquire_\d+_qualifiers$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<comma-separated generalized recognition phrases that identify the same establishing event family>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/_delivery$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<comma-separated built-in delivery ids>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/_(?:source_traits|source_slot_traits|source_heritage|source_profiles|receiver_traits|exclude_traits|exclude_heritage|exclude_risks|natural_traits)$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<comma-separated canonical ids/traits only>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/_(?:source_conditions|receiver_conditions|conditions)$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<comma-separated condition ids only>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/_slot$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<one BTT anatomy slot: head/arms/legs/breasts/gut/penis/vagina/ass/tail/wings>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/_slot_origin$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<one canonical slot-origin/heritage id>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/_axes$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<semantic axis changes only when acquisition itself changes the body>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/^track_\d+_name$/.test(field)){var family=newTrack&&trackN===Number(ext.trackNumber)?ext.responseFamily:trackBlueprint&&trackBlueprint.id||"the resolved track family";return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<concise progression-track name for "+String(family)+">`)";}
  if(/^track_\d+_triggers$/.test(field)){var family2=newTrack&&trackN===Number(ext.trackNumber)?ext.responseFamily:trackBlueprint&&trackBlueprint.id||"this track";return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<comma-separated ACTUAL mechanism-response events that progress "+String(family2)+"; do not use mere dialogue, instructions, presence, or task completion unless the mechanism actually actuates/responds>` )".replace("` )","`)");}
  if(/^track_\d+_qualifiers$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<comma-separated track-recognition phrases>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/^track_\d+_receiver_routing$/.test(field))return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<slot=route+route; slot=route mapping using existing risk/track ids>` )".replace("` )","`)");
  if(/^track_\d+_success_\d+_effect$/.test(field)){var scope;if(ext&&ext.kind==="higher_success")scope="a NEW, HIGHER consequence beyond existing SUCCESS levels on track "+ext.trackName+"; evidence family="+ext.responseFamily;else if(newTrack)scope="the FIRST scoped consequence of the new "+ext.responseFamily+" track; it must be distinct from the existing tracks";else scope=trackBlueprint?("a scoped consequence of "+trackBlueprint.id):"a scoped consequence of this track";return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<semantic consequence only: "+scope+". Do NOT supply BTT grammar, slot labels, SUCCESS numbering, or numeric magnitude>` )".replace("` )","`)");}
  if(/^track_\d+_success_\d+_target$/.test(field)){var base=BTT_devSuccessBase(field),plan=p&&p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[base],known=plan&&plan.effect?BTT_clip(plan.effect,120):"the already-captured effect";return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<ONLY the unresolved affected target/body area/domain for: "+known+">`)";}
  if(field==="removal")return "(btt_dev_risk_removal = `<comma-separated lore-consistent removal/cure/end events; do not invent unsupported formal requirements>` )".replace("` )","`)");return "(btt_dev_risk_"+field+" = `<semantic value only>` )".replace("` )","`)");
}function BTT_devOptionalFieldGuide(p,compact){if(compact>=2)return "Optional only when directly evidenced: acquire_1 natural_traits/slot/slot_origin/axes/conditions/source_* or receiver_* constraints; track_1 qualifiers/source_slot_traits/receiver_routing; success_2/3; distinct acquire_2/track_2 pathways. Never author numeric mechanics or persistence.";var lines=["Optional wrappers — emit ONLY when directly supported by the discovery evidence; omission is valid:","- acquire_1_source_traits / source_slot_traits / source_heritage / source_profiles / source_conditions","- acquire_1_receiver_traits / receiver_conditions / exclude_traits / exclude_heritage / exclude_risks","- acquire_1_slot / slot_origin / natural_traits / axes / conditions","- track_1_qualifiers / source_slot_traits / receiver_routing","- track_1_success_2_effect and track_1_success_3_effect for genuinely later semantic milestones","- A second ACQUIRE or TRACK pathway may use acquire_2... / track_2... only when clearly distinct; never more than 3 of either.","Do NOT emit ACQUIRE THRESHOLD/HISTORY, TRACK THRESHOLD, CHANCE, REPEAT, MAX, MEMORY, numbering labels, or PERSISTENCE; BTT owns those mechanics."];return lines.join("\n");}
function BTT_devGenerationPrompt(p,compact,requestedOverride){
  compact=Number(compact)||0;BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),requested=Array.isArray(requestedOverride)?requestedOverride.slice():BTT_devRequestedFields(p),evCount=compact>=4?1:compact>=3?2:compact>=2?3:compact?4:6,evClip=compact>=4?90:compact>=3?120:compact>=2?150:compact?230:340,evidence=(p.evidence||[]).slice(-evCount).map(function(x){return "- "+BTT_clip(x,evClip);}).join("\n"),ops=[];for(var i=0;i<requested.length;i++)ops.push(BTT_devFieldInstruction(requested[i],p));var retry=p.lastError?("\nREPAIR NOTE: Previous error: "+BTT_clip(p.lastError,compact>=3?140:260)+". Preserve every semantic value already retained. Return only unresolved dimensions; never re-author known information."):"",deliveryNote=requested.some(function(x){return /_delivery$/.test(x);})?("\n\nALLOWED DELIVERY IDS\n"+BTT_devSuggestedDeliveryIds(p)):"",outcomeState=BTT_devOutcomePlansText(p),ext=p.mode==="extension"&&p.extension||null,mode="NEW RISK PROFILE",blueprint="";
  if(ext&&ext.kind==="new_track"){mode="EXISTING RISK NEW-TRACK EXTENSION";blueprint="Existing risk: "+ext.riskName+"\nExisting tracks: "+(ext.existingTracks||[]).map(function(t){return t.name||t.id;}).join(", ")+"\nNew track number: "+ext.trackNumber+"\nNovel response family: "+ext.responseFamily+"\nResponse confidence: "+Object.keys(ext.responseChannels||{}).map(function(k){return k+"="+BTT_round(ext.responseChannels[k],2);}).join(", ");}
  else if(ext){mode="EXISTING TRACK HIGHER-SUCCESS EXTENSION";blueprint="Existing risk: "+ext.riskName+"\nExisting track: "+ext.trackName+"\nNext SUCCESS: "+ext.nextSuccess+"\nNovel response family: "+ext.responseFamily+"\nResponse confidence: "+Object.keys(ext.responseChannels||{}).map(function(k){return k+"="+BTT_round(ext.responseChannels[k],2);}).join(", ");}
  else blueprint=BTT_devBlueprintText(p.blueprint||{});
  var captured=BTT_devCapturedValuesText(p);return "<SYSTEM>\n# BTT DEVELOPABLE RISK MAINTENANCE AUTHORING\nMode: "+mode+"\nMaintenance only; do NOT narrate. The story is frozen until BTT validates the requested semantic fields.\n\nPRINCIPLE\nBTT has already resolved structure with its confidence engine. Return semantic VALUES only in destination-specific wrappers. BTT writes creator-card grammar, numbering, thresholds, chance/repeat/max/memory, persistence, known slots/axes/directions/magnitudes, and any confidently resolved delivery. Never repeat a fact BTT already retained.\n\nAUTHORING BLUEPRINT\n"+BTT_clip(blueprint,compact>=2?700:1600)+"\n\nRETAINED EVENTS\n"+(evidence||"[retained by BTT]")+"\n\nPARTIAL OUTCOME RESOLUTION\n"+BTT_clip(outcomeState,compact>=2?500:1200)+"\n\nVALID VALUES RETAINED\n"+BTT_clip(captured,compact>=2?500:1200)+"\n\nREQUIRED WRAPPERS — output ALL requested values in this response\n"+(ops.length?ops.join("\n"):"[none; validation only]")+retry+deliveryNote+"\n\nOUTPUT CONTRACT\nUse canonical (btt_dev_risk_<field> = `value`) wrappers. Wrapper punctuation/quotes may vary harmlessly, but semantic values must satisfy the requested destination. Output wrappers only: no story, dialogue, explanation, creator-card labels, or script-owned mechanics.\n</SYSTEM>";
}function BTT_devAuthoringFieldPriority(fields){
  function rank(f){if(f==="name")return 1;if(/^acquire_1$/.test(f))return 2;if(/^acquire_1_qualifiers$/.test(f))return 3;if(/^acquire_1_delivery$/.test(f))return 4;if(/^track_1_name$/.test(f))return 5;if(/^track_1_triggers$/.test(f))return 6;if(/^track_1_delivery$/.test(f))return 7;if(/^track_1_success_1_effect$/.test(f))return 8;if(/^track_1_success_1_target$/.test(f))return 9;if(f==="removal")return 10;if(/^acquire_/.test(f))return 20;if(/^track_.*_success_\d+_effect$/.test(f))return 31;if(/^track_.*_success_\d+_target$/.test(f))return 32;if(/^track_/.test(f))return 30;return 40;}
  return fields.slice().sort(function(a,b){var d=rank(a)-rank(b);return d||String(a).localeCompare(String(b));});
}
function BTT_injectDevRiskAuthoringTask(ctx,p){
  var base=String(ctx||""),limit=0;try{limit=Math.max(0,Number(info&&info.maxChars)||0);}catch(_e){}
  var all=BTT_devAuthoringFieldPriority(BTT_devRequestedFields(p)),batch=all.slice(),mode=0,task=BTT_devGenerationPrompt(p,mode,batch),absoluteBudget=limit>0?Math.max(320,limit-24):0;
  if(limit>0&&task.length>Math.floor(limit*0.72)){mode=1;task=BTT_devGenerationPrompt(p,mode,batch);}
  if(limit>0&&task.length>Math.floor(limit*0.82)){mode=2;task=BTT_devGenerationPrompt(p,mode,batch);}
  if(limit>0&&task.length>Math.floor(limit*0.90)){mode=3;task=BTT_devGenerationPrompt(p,mode,batch);}
  /* If all missing fields plus their proper instructions cannot fit, reduce only
     the current maintenance batch. This preserves complete per-field guidance and
     lets later Continue calls request the remaining fields without moving story. */
  if(limit>0&&task.length>absoluteBudget){
    mode=3;
    while(batch.length>1&&task.length>absoluteBudget){batch.pop();task=BTT_devGenerationPrompt(p,mode,batch);}
    if(task.length>absoluteBudget){mode=4;task=BTT_devGenerationPrompt(p,mode,batch.slice(0,1));batch=batch.slice(0,1);}
  }
  p.lastRequestedFields=batch.slice();p.lastPromptMode=mode;
  var joined=base.trimEnd()+"\n\n"+task,trimmed=0;
  if(limit>0&&joined.length>limit){
    /* First trim oldest Recent Story, matching the proven Appearance/Auto-Cards
       strategy; if more space is still required, keep the whole maintenance task
       and only the newest tail of pre-existing context. Never cut the task itself. */
    var marker="Recent Story:",idx=base.indexOf(marker),overflow=joined.length-limit;
    if(idx>=0&&overflow>0){var storyStart=idx+marker.length,keep=Math.min(900,Math.max(180,Math.floor(limit*0.18))),story=base.slice(storyStart),cut=Math.min(Math.max(0,story.length-keep),overflow+96);if(cut>0){base=base.slice(0,storyStart)+"\n[older recent story trimmed for BTT Dev-Risk authoring]\n"+story.slice(cut);trimmed+=cut;joined=base.trimEnd()+"\n\n"+task;}}
    if(joined.length>limit&&task.length<=limit){var baseBudget=Math.max(0,limit-task.length-2),before=base.length;base=baseBudget?base.slice(Math.max(0,base.length-baseBudget)):"";trimmed+=Math.max(0,before-base.length);joined=(base?base.trimEnd()+"\n\n":"")+task;}
  }
  if(limit>0&&task.length>limit)BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING CONTEXT WARNING | reason=MINIMUM_TASK_EXCEEDS_MAX_CHARS | task_chars="+task.length+" | max_chars="+limit+" | field="+(batch[0]||"none"));
  BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING CONTEXT INJECT | prompt_mode="+mode+" | requested_batch="+(batch.join(",")||"validation")+" | unresolved_total="+all.length+" | task_chars="+task.length+" | max_chars="+(limit||"unknown")+" | trimmed_chars="+trimmed+" | final_chars="+joined.length);
  return joined;
}
function BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,reason){
  var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState();
  if(p&&p.mode==="extension"&&p.extension){var ext=p.extension,meta=BTT_getEntity(ext.entity),active=BTT_devActive(meta,ext.riskId),watch=active&&BTT_devProgressionWatch(active),cluster=watch&&watch.clusters&&watch.clusters[ext.clusterKey];if(cluster){cluster.retryAfterTurn=BTT_getTurn()+3;cluster.status=ext.kind==="new_track"?"POSSIBLE_NEW_TRACK":"POSSIBLE_HIGHER_EFFECT";}g.pending=null;g.lastCompleted={status:"AUTHORING_ABORTED",candidateId:String(p.candidateId||""),turn:BTT_getTurn(),reason:String(reason||"UNKNOWN")};BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING ABORT | mode="+(ext.kind==="new_track"?"NEW_TRACK":"HIGHER_SUCCESS")+" | risk="+ext.riskName+" | reason="+String(reason||"UNKNOWN")+" | retry_after_turn="+(cluster&&cluster.retryAfterTurn||"n/a"));return;}
  var c=BTT_clone(p&&p.originalCandidate||{});if(c&&c.id){c.retryAfterTurn=BTT_getTurn()+3;g.candidates.push(c);}g.pending=null;g.lastCompleted={status:"AUTHORING_ABORTED",candidateId:String(p&&p.candidateId||""),turn:BTT_getTurn(),reason:String(reason||"UNKNOWN")};BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING ABORT | candidate="+String(p&&p.candidateId||"unknown")+" | reason="+String(reason||"UNKNOWN")+" | candidate_retained="+(c&&c.id?"YES":"NO")+" | retry_after_turn="+(c&&c.id?c.retryAfterTurn:"n/a"));
