}function BTT_devReplayFragment(ev,receiver){var raw=BTT_devEventText(ev),src={entity:String(ev.sourceEntity||""),descriptor:String(ev.sourceDescriptor||""),confidence:Number(ev.sourceConfidence)||0.90},f={index:0,start:0,end:raw.length,raw:raw,lower:BTT_lower(raw),phase:"replay",quoted:false,speaker:"",speakerConfidence:0,addressee:"",mentions:[{entity:receiver,alias:"replay-receiver",score:1,reasons:["retained_event_replay"]}],subject:src.entity||receiver,subjectConfidence:src.entity?0.92:1,eventStatus:{actual:true,mode:"actual"},actuality:1,temporal:"CURRENT",assertion:1,completion:1,products:[],shared:{change:{score:0}},_bttReplaySource:src};return f;}
function BTT_replayGeneratedDevRiskEvidence(p,profile){
  if(!p||!profile||!p.receiver)return;var meta=BTT_findEntity(p.receiver)||BTT_getEntity(p.receiver),events=(p.events||[]).slice().sort(function(a,b){var d=Number(a.turn)-Number(b.turn);return d||Number(a.action)-Number(b.action);}),work={};work[p.receiver]=meta;if(p.sourceEntity){var sm=BTT_findEntity(p.sourceEntity);if(sm)work[p.sourceEntity]=sm;}var acquired=BTT_devActive(meta,profile.id),acquiredEvent=-1;
  for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++){var ev=events[i],f=BTT_devReplayFragment(ev,p.receiver),parse={text:f.raw,phase:"replay",fragments:[f],sources:[],admitted:[],baselineBoundaries:{},observations:[],shared:{entityCache:{}}};if(!acquired){var best=null;for(var ai=0;ai<profile.acquisitions.length;ai++){var a=profile.acquisitions[ai],e=BTT_devAcquisitionEvidence(meta,p.receiver,profile,a,f,parse,work);if(!e.pass)continue;var histKey=profile.id+"|"+a.id,hist=Math.min(BTT_getConfig().dev_risk_acquire_history_bonus_max,BTT_devAcquireHistory(meta,histKey,a.historyTurns)),total=BTT_round(e.score+hist,2);if(!best||total>best.total)best={a:a,e:e,total:total,hist:hist,histKey:histKey};}if(best){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("REPLAY ACQUIRE EVAL | event="+ev.id+" | risk="+profile.name+" | current="+BTT_round(best.e.score,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(best.hist,2)+" | total="+best.total+" | threshold="+BTT_round(best.a.threshold,2));if(best.total>=best.a.threshold){best.e.total=best.total;acquired=BTT_devAcquire(meta,p.receiver,profile,best.a,best.e);delete meta.developableRisks.acquisitionHistory[best.histKey];acquiredEvent=i;BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("REPLAY RISK ACQUIRED | event="+ev.id+" | risk="+profile.name+" | receiver="+p.receiver);}else if(best.total>=BTT_getConfig().dev_risk_acquire_store_min)BTT_devStoreAcquireHistory(meta,best.histKey,Math.min(1.2,best.e.score*0.45),f.raw);}continue;}
    if(i<=acquiredEvent)continue;for(var ti=0;ti<acquired.tracks.length;ti++){var tr=acquired.tracks[ti],st=BTT_devTrackState(acquired,tr.id);if(st.complete)continue;var te=BTT_devTrackEvidence(meta,p.receiver,acquired,tr,f,parse,work);if(!te.score||te.score<tr.threshold)continue;var sig="replay|"+profile.id+"|"+tr.id+"|"+String(ev.id);st.seen=st.seen||{};if(st.seen[sig])continue;st.seen[sig]=BTT_getTurn();st.exposureCount++;st.lastExposureTurn=BTT_getTurn();var chance=Math.min(tr.maximumChance,tr.baseChance+Math.max(0,st.exposureCount-1)*tr.repeatIncrease),roll=BTT_hashRoll(p.receiver+"|devrisk-replay|"+profile.id+"|"+tr.id+"|"+ev.id),ok=roll<=chance;st.lastRoll={turn:BTT_getTurn(),chance:chance,roll:roll,success:ok,replay:true};BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("REPLAY TRACK | event="+ev.id+" | risk="+profile.name+" | track="+tr.id+" | confidence="+BTT_round(te.score,2)+" | chance="+chance+" | roll="+roll+" | "+(ok?"SUCCESS":"MISS"));if(ok)BTT_devApplySuccess(meta,acquired,tr,st);}}
  if(acquired)BTT_writeEntity(p.receiver,meta);
}
function BTT_devGeneratedProfileParentAffinity(c,p,profile){if(!c||!p||c.receiver!==p.receiver)return 0;var score=0,cs=BTT_devParentSourceKey(c),ps=BTT_normId(p.sourceEntity||p.sourceDescriptor||""),cm=String(c.mechanism||"unresolved"),pm=String(p.mechanism||"unresolved"),events=c.events||[],rawParts=[String(c.sourceDescriptor||"")];for(var ei=0;ei<events.length;ei++)rawParts.push(BTT_devEventText(events[ei]));var rawEvidence=rawParts.join(" "),explicitCurse=/\b(?:curse|cursed|hex|hexed|spell|cursecraft|glamour)\b/i.test(rawEvidence),apparel=/\b(?:cloth(?:ing)?|garment|fabric|silk|chemise|robe|slip|slipper|shoe|heel|stocking|hosiery|lingerie|underwear|corset|bodice|gown|weave|embroidery)\b/i.test(rawEvidence),magicGarment=(pm==="clothing"&&cm==="curse"&&apparel&&!explicitCurse);/* Inferred mechanism tags are not proof of an explicit curse; source/event text must establish that mechanism. */if(cs&&ps&&cs===ps)score+=0.48;else if(cs&&ps&&cs!==ps&&!magicGarment)return 0;if(cm===pm&&pm!=="unresolved")score+=0.28;else if(magicGarment)score+=0.52;else if(cm!=="unresolved"&&pm!=="unresolved")score-=0.18;score+=0.14*BTT_responseChannelSimilarity(c.responseChannels||{},p.blueprint&&p.blueprint.responseChannels||{});score+=0.22*BTT_devProfileCoverage(c,profile);if(!cs&&pm===cm&&pm!=="unresolved")score+=0.10;return BTT_clamp(score,0,1);}
function BTT_devSeedProgressionWatchFromParent(entity,active,c,cfg){if(!active||!c)return null;var watch=BTT_devProgressionWatch(active),best=null,allRows=BTT_devCandidateAllRows(c),ti,ri;for(ti=0;ti<(active.tracks||[]).length;ti++){var tr=active.tracks[ti],max=0;for(ri=0;ri<allRows.length;ri++)max=Math.max(max,BTT_devTrackResponseCoverage(tr,allRows[ri]));if(!best||max>best.coverage)best={track:tr,index:ti,coverage:max};}var isExisting=best&&best.coverage>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_track_match_min||0.46),family=String(c.responseFamily||BTT_responseDominantFamily(c.responseChannels||{})||"response"),key=isExisting?best.track.id+"|"+family:"__new__|"+family,cluster=watch.clusters[key];if(!cluster)cluster=watch.clusters[key]={key:key,trackId:isExisting?best.track.id:"[possible new track]",trackIndex:isExisting?best.index:-1,responseFamily:family,responseChannels:{},events:[],score:0,status:isExisting?"EXISTING_TRACK":"POSSIBLE_NEW_TRACK",createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastTurn:0,retryAfterTurn:0,authored:false,mechanism:c.mechanism,trackHints:BTT_clone(c.trackHints||[]),reconciledFromParent:String(c.id||"")};var events=c.events||[];for(var e=0;e<events.length;e++){var ev=events[e],raw=BTT_devEventText(ev),dupe=false;for(var x=0;x<cluster.events.length;x++)if(Number(cluster.events[x].action)===Number(ev.action)&&BTT_observationTextSimilarity(cluster.events[x].raw,raw)>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_dedupe_similarity||0.86)){dupe=true;break;}if(dupe)continue;var rep=ev.clauses&&ev.clauses[0]||{},coverage=isExisting?BTT_devTrackResponseCoverage(best.track,rep):0.5,novelty=isExisting?BTT_devProgressionNovelty(best.track,rep):1;cluster.events.push({turn:Number(ev.turn)||BTT_getTurn(),action:Number(ev.action)||0,raw:BTT_clip(raw,420),strength:Number(ev.strength)||Number(rep.rawScore)||0,trackCoverage:BTT_round(coverage,3),novelty:BTT_round(novelty,3),responseFamily:family,responseChannels:BTT_clone(ev.responseChannels||rep.responseChannels||{})});}var now=BTT_getTurn(),window=Math.max(1,Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_progression_watch_turns||18));cluster.events=cluster.events.filter(function(ev){return now-Number(ev.turn||0)<=window;});for(var ch in (c.responseChannels||{}))if(c.responseChannels.hasOwnProperty(ch))cluster.responseChannels[ch]=Math.max(Number(cluster.responseChannels[ch])||0,Number(c.responseChannels[ch])||0);BTT_devProgressionClusterScore(cluster,cfg);cluster.status=isExisting?"EXISTING_TRACK":"POSSIBLE_NEW_TRACK";cluster.lastTurn=cluster.events.length?cluster.events.reduce(function(m,ev){return Math.max(m,Number(ev.turn)||0);},0):now;cluster.reconciledTurn=now;watch.lastTurn=Math.max(Number(watch.lastTurn)||0,cluster.lastTurn);return cluster;}
function BTT_devReconcileParentsIntoGeneratedProfile(g,p,profile){if(!g||!p||!profile)return 0;var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),meta=BTT_getEntity(p.receiver),active=BTT_devActive(meta,profile.id),removed=0,keep=[];for(var i=0;i<(g.candidates||[]).length;i++){var c=g.candidates[i],aff=BTT_devGeneratedProfileParentAffinity(c,p,profile);if(aff<0.62){keep.push(c);continue;}if(active){var cluster=BTT_devSeedProgressionWatchFromParent(p.receiver,active,c,cfg);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("POST-AUTHORING PARENT RECONCILE | profile="+profile.name+" | parent="+c.id+" | affinity="+BTT_round(aff,2)+" | destination="+(cluster&&cluster.status||"PROGRESSION_WATCH")+" | events="+(c.events||[]).length);removed++;}else{var cov=BTT_devProfileCoverage(c,profile);if(cov>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_existing_coverage_min||0.76)){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("POST-AUTHORING PARENT SUPPRESS | profile="+profile.name+" | parent="+c.id+" | affinity="+BTT_round(aff,2)+" | coverage="+BTT_round(cov,2)+" | active_profile=NO");removed++;}else keep.push(c);}}g.candidates=keep;return removed;}
function BTT_devAppendSuccessToTrackText(text,trackIndex,successN,value){var lines=String(text||"").split(/\n/g),starts=[];for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++)if(/^\s*TRACK\s*:/i.test(lines[i]))starts.push(i);if(trackIndex<0||trackIndex>=starts.length)return {ok:false,error:"TRACK_INDEX_NOT_FOUND"};var start=starts[trackIndex],end=lines.length;for(i=start+1;i<lines.length;i++)if(/^\s*(?:TRACK|PERSISTENCE|REMOVAL)\s*:/i.test(lines[i])){end=i;break;}var re=new RegExp("^\\s*SUCCESS\\s+"+successN+"\\s*:","i");for(i=start+1;i<end;i++)if(re.test(lines[i]))return {ok:false,error:"SUCCESS_ALREADY_EXISTS"};lines.splice(end,0,"SUCCESS "+successN+": "+value);return {ok:true,text:lines.join("\n")};}
function BTT_devAppendTrackToCardText(text,trackBlock){
  var lines=String(text||"").split(/\n/g),insert=lines.length;for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++)if(/^\s*(?:PERSISTENCE|REMOVAL)\s*:/i.test(lines[i])){insert=i;break;}
  var block=String(trackBlock||"").trim();if(!block||!/^TRACK\s*:/im.test(block))return {ok:false,error:"INVALID_TRACK_BLOCK"};var add=block.split(/\n/g);if(insert>0&&BTT_cleanSpace(lines[insert-1]))add.unshift("");if(insert<lines.length&&BTT_cleanSpace(lines[insert]))add.push("");lines.splice.apply(lines,[insert,0].concat(add));return {ok:true,text:lines.join("\n").replace(/\n{3,}/g,"\n\n")};
}
function BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRiskExtension(p){
  var ext=p.extension||{};if(ext.kind==="new_track")return BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRiskNewTrackExtension(p);return BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRiskHigherSuccessExtension(p);
}
function BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRiskHigherSuccessExtension(p){
  var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),ext=p.extension||{},base="track_"+(Number(ext.trackIndex)+1)+"_success_"+Number(ext.nextSuccess);BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);var plan=p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[base];if(!plan||plan.status!=="resolved"||!plan.compiled||!plan.compiled.length){p.lastError="EXTENSION_OUTCOME_UNRESOLVED";return false;}var card=BTT_findCardByTitle(ext.cardTitle);if(!card){p.lastError="EXTENSION_SOURCE_CARD_NOT_FOUND";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}var oldText=BTT_devRiskCardText(card),added=BTT_devAppendSuccessToTrackText(oldText,Number(ext.trackIndex),Number(ext.nextSuccess),plan.compiled.join("; "));if(!added.ok){p.lastError=added.error;BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}var fake={title:card.title,type:card.type||"BTT Developable Risk",value:added.text,entry:""},parsed=BTT_parseDevRiskCard(fake);if(!parsed||parsed.tracks.length<=Number(ext.trackIndex)){p.lastError="EXTENSION_ROUNDTRIP_FAILED";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}var found=false;for(var oi=0;oi<(parsed.tracks[Number(ext.trackIndex)].outcomes||[]).length;oi++)if(Number(parsed.tracks[Number(ext.trackIndex)].outcomes[oi].successAt)===Number(ext.nextSuccess)){found=true;break;}if(!found){p.lastError="EXTENSION_SUCCESS_NOT_PARSED";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}card.entry=added.text;if("value" in card)card.value=added.text;BTT_loadDevRiskProfiles(true);var meta=BTT_getEntity(ext.entity),active=BTT_devActive(meta,ext.riskId),profile=BTT_devRiskProfile(ext.riskId);if(active&&profile){active.tracks=BTT_clone(profile.tracks);var tr=active.tracks[Number(ext.trackIndex)],st=tr?BTT_devTrackState(active,tr.id):null;if(st)st.complete=false;var watch=BTT_devProgressionWatch(active),cluster=watch.clusters&&watch.clusters[ext.clusterKey];if(cluster){cluster.authored=true;cluster.status="PROFILE_EXTENDED";cluster.authoredTurn=BTT_getTurn();}}g.lastGeneratedTurn=BTT_getTurn();g.lastCompleted={status:"PROFILE_EXTENDED",candidateId:p.candidateId,name:ext.riskName,track:ext.trackId,successAt:Number(ext.nextSuccess),turn:BTT_getTurn()};BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PROFILE EXTENDED | risk="+ext.riskName+" | track="+ext.trackId+" | SUCCESS "+ext.nextSuccess+" | outcome="+plan.compiled.join("; ")+" | retroactive_apply=NO");g.pending=null;return true;
}
function BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRiskNewTrackExtension(p){
  var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),ext=p.extension||{},cfg=BTT_getConfig(),trackN=Number(ext.trackNumber||Number(ext.trackIndex)+1),base="track_"+trackN+"_success_1";BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);var plan=p.outcomeSemantics&&p.outcomeSemantics[base];if(!plan||plan.status!=="resolved"||!plan.compiled||!plan.compiled.length){p.lastError="NEW_TRACK_OUTCOME_UNRESOLVED";return false;}var req=BTT_devRequiredFields(p);if(req.length){p.lastError="NEW_TRACK_FIELDS_NOT_VALID:"+req.join(",");return false;}var card=BTT_findCardByTitle(ext.cardTitle);if(!card){p.lastError="EXTENSION_SOURCE_CARD_NOT_FOUND";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}var oldText=BTT_devRiskCardText(card),oldParsed=BTT_parseDevRiskCard({title:card.title,type:card.type||"BTT Developable Risk",value:oldText,entry:""});if(!oldParsed){p.lastError="NEW_TRACK_SOURCE_PARSE_FAILED";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}var block=BTT_devCompileTrack(p,trackN,cfg),added=BTT_devAppendTrackToCardText(oldText,block);if(!added.ok){p.lastError=added.error;BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}var parsed=BTT_parseDevRiskCard({title:card.title,type:card.type||"BTT Developable Risk",value:added.text,entry:""});if(!parsed||parsed.tracks.length!==oldParsed.tracks.length+1){p.lastError="NEW_TRACK_ROUNDTRIP_COUNT_FAILED";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}var newTrack=parsed.tracks[parsed.tracks.length-1];if(!newTrack||(newTrack.outcomes||[]).length<1){p.lastError="NEW_TRACK_OUTCOME_NOT_PARSED";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}card.entry=added.text;if("value" in card)card.value=added.text;BTT_loadDevRiskProfiles(true);var meta=BTT_getEntity(ext.entity),active=BTT_devActive(meta,ext.riskId),profile=BTT_devRiskProfile(ext.riskId);if(active&&profile){active.tracks=BTT_clone(profile.tracks);var watch=BTT_devProgressionWatch(active),cluster=watch.clusters&&watch.clusters[ext.clusterKey];if(cluster){cluster.authored=true;cluster.status="TRACK_ADDED";cluster.authoredTurn=BTT_getTurn();cluster.trackId=newTrack.id;cluster.trackIndex=active.tracks.length-1;}}g.lastGeneratedTurn=BTT_getTurn();g.lastCompleted={status:"PROFILE_TRACK_ADDED",candidateId:p.candidateId,name:ext.riskName,track:newTrack.id,turn:BTT_getTurn()};BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PROFILE TRACK ADDED | risk="+ext.riskName+" | track="+newTrack.id+" | outcome="+plan.compiled.join("; ")+" | retroactive_apply=NO");g.pending=null;return true;
}
function BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRisk(p){if(p&&p.mode==="extension")return BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRiskExtension(p);var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),check=BTT_devValidateWholeCard(p);if(!check.valid){p.retries=Number(p.retries||0)+1;p.lastError=check.error;var req=BTT_devRequestedFields(p);for(var i=0;i<req.length;i++){if(!p.fieldStatus[req[i]])p.fieldStatus[req[i]]="missing";if(!p.fieldErrors[req[i]])p.fieldErrors[req[i]]=check.error;}BTT_devRecomputeSectionStatus(p);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("WHOLE CARD VALIDATION FAILED | candidate="+p.candidateId+" | reason="+check.error+" | repair_fields="+req.join(","));return false;}var assembled=check.assembled,created=BTT_createCard({title:assembled.title,type:"BTT Developable Risk",keys:"",entry:assembled.value,description:"AI-generated Developable Risk. BTT compiled creator-card grammar/mechanics from confidence-resolved structure and validated semantic wrappers, then round-trip validated the profile before story resumption; edit Entry directly for creator overrides."});if(!created){p.lastError="CARD_CREATE_FAILED";BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,p.lastError);return false;}g.generatedCount=Number(g.generatedCount||0)+1;g.lastGeneratedTurn=BTT_getTurn();BTT_loadDevRiskProfiles(true);var profile=BTT_devRiskProfile(check.parsed.id)||check.parsed;g.lastCompleted={status:"PROFILE_CREATED",candidateId:p.candidateId,name:assembled.name,cardTitle:assembled.title,turn:BTT_getTurn(),events:(p.events||[]).length};BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("PROFILE CREATED | card="+assembled.title+" | acquisitions="+check.parsed.acquisitions.length+" | tracks="+check.parsed.tracks.length+" | retained_events="+(p.events||[]).length+" | generated="+g.generatedCount+" | blueprint_family="+String(p.blueprint&&p.blueprint.riskFamily||"unresolved"));BTT_replayGeneratedDevRiskEvidence(p,profile);var reconciled=BTT_devReconcileParentsIntoGeneratedProfile(g,p,profile);if(reconciled)BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("POST-AUTHORING RECONCILIATION COMPLETE | profile="+profile.name+" | parents_moved="+reconciled);g.pending=null;return true;}function BTT_devKickoffNotice(p){if(p&&p.mode==="extension"){if(p.extension&&p.extension.kind==="new_track")return '>>> BTT detected a qualified new progression track for an existing AI-generated Developable Risk. Please select "Continue" to extend the profile. <<<\n\n';return '>>> BTT detected a possible higher progression for an existing AI-generated Developable Risk. Please select "Continue" to extend the track. <<<\n\n';}return '>>> BTT qualified a Developable Risk. Please select "Continue" to author it. <<<\n\n';}function BTT_devAuthoringNotice(p,completedName,aborted){var extension=p&&p.mode==="extension",newTrack=extension&&p.extension&&p.extension.kind==="new_track";if(completedName){if(newTrack)return '>>> BTT added a new progression track to Developable Risk "'+completedName+'". Please select "Continue" to resume the story. <<<\n\n';return extension?'>>> BTT extended Developable Risk "'+completedName+'" with a higher progression state. Please select "Continue" to resume the story. <<<\n\n':'>>> BTT finished authoring Developable Risk "'+completedName+'". Please select "Continue" to resume the story. <<<\n\n';}if(aborted){if(newTrack)return '>>> BTT paused new-track authoring after repeated validation failures. Please select "Continue" to resume the story. <<<\n\n';return extension?'>>> BTT paused higher-progression authoring after repeated validation failures. Please select "Continue" to resume the story. <<<\n\n':'>>> BTT paused Developable Risk authoring after repeated validation failures. Please select "Continue" to resume the story. <<<\n\n';}var reqFields=BTT_devRequestedFields(p);if(extension)return '>>> BTT is extending an existing Developable Risk. Please select "Continue" (next fields: '+(reqFields.join(", ")||"validation")+') <<<\n\n';var reqSections=BTT_devRequestedSections(p),valid=4-reqSections.length;return '>>> BTT is authoring a Developable Risk. Please select "Continue" ('+valid+'/4 sections valid; next fields: '+(reqFields.slice(0,8).join(", ")||(reqSections.join(", ")||"validation"))+(reqFields.length>8?", …":"")+') <<<\n\n';}function BTT_captureDevRiskGenerationBatch(raw){
  var g=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState(),p=g&&g.pending;if(!p)return {maintenance:false,text:String(raw||"")};var cfg=BTT_getConfig();BTT_devInitializeAuthoringPlan(p,cfg);var allRequested=BTT_devRequestedFields(p),requested=(Array.isArray(p.lastRequestedFields)&&p.lastRequestedFields.length?p.lastRequestedFields.slice():allRequested.slice()),ops=BTT_devTransportOperations(raw),seen={},captured=0,errors=[];p.lastError="";BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING RESPONSE | candidate="+p.candidateId+" | requested_fields="+requested.join(",")+" | response="+(Number(p.authoringResponses||0)+1)+" | raw_chars="+String(String(raw||"").length)+" | ops_detected="+String(ops.length));BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING RAW PREVIEW | "+BTT_clip(String(raw||"").replace(/\s+/g," "),520));
  for(var i=0;i<ops.length;i++){var op=ops[i],field=op.canonical;if(!field||!BTT_devKnownAtomicField(field)){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING WRAPPER UNKNOWN | key="+String(op.rawKey||"unknown")+" | captured=NO");continue;}if(BTT_devOwnedMechanicalField(field)){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING SCRIPT FIELD IGNORED | field="+field+" | reason=SCRIPT_OWNS_MECHANICS");continue;}if((field==="acquire_1_delivery"&&p.derived.acquire_1_delivery)||(/^track_\d+_delivery$/.test(field)&&p.derived&&p.derived[field])){BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING DERIVED FIELD IGNORED | field="+field+" | derived="+String(p.derived[field]||p.derived.acquire_1_delivery||""));continue;}seen[field]=1;var v=op.incomplete?{valid:false,error:"INCOMPLETE_TRANSPORT_VALUE",payload:""}:BTT_devValidateAtomicField(field,op.payload);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING CAPTURE | field="+field+" | key="+String(op.rawKey||"")+" | container="+op.container+" | quote="+op.quoting+" | repaired="+(op.repaired?"YES":"NO")+" | transport_complete="+(op.incomplete?"NO":"YES")+" | result="+(v.valid?"VALID":"INVALID"+(v.error?":"+v.error:"")));if(v.valid){p.fieldValues[field]=v.payload;p.fieldStatus[field]="valid";p.fieldErrors[field]="";captured++;}else{p.fieldStatus[field]="invalid";p.fieldErrors[field]=v.error;p.lastError=field+":"+v.error;errors.push(p.lastError);}}
  BTT_devRefreshOutcomePlans(p);
  /* Only fields requested in this response are marked missing. Values captured by
     earlier maintenance responses remain available to the final compiler. */
  for(i=0;i<requested.length;i++){var rf=requested[i];if(seen[rf]||BTT_devFieldResolved(p,rf))continue;if(p.fieldStatus[rf]!=="invalid")p.fieldStatus[rf]="missing";if(!p.fieldErrors[rf])p.fieldErrors[rf]="OPERATION_NOT_FOUND";BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING FIELD MISS | field="+rf+" | reason=OPERATION_NOT_FOUND");}
  BTT_devRecomputeSectionStatus(p);p.lastRequestedFields=[];p.authoringResponses=Number(p.authoringResponses||0)+1;p.maintenanceContinues=Math.max(0,p.authoringResponses-1);var reqNow=BTT_devRequestedFields(p),completed=false,completedName="",completedPending=p;if(!reqNow.length){completedName=p.mode==="extension"&&p.extension?String(p.extension.riskName||""):BTT_cleanSpace(p.fieldValues.name||"");completed=BTT_finalizeGeneratedDevRisk(p);if(!completed&&g.pending)p=g.pending;}
  if(completed){g.lastMaintenanceNotice=BTT_devAuthoringNotice(completedPending,completedName,false);return {maintenance:true,completed:true,text:g.lastMaintenanceNotice};}
  if(!g.pending){g.lastMaintenanceNotice=BTT_devAuthoringNotice(completedPending,"",true);return {maintenance:true,aborted:true,text:g.lastMaintenanceNotice};}
  p=g.pending;reqNow=BTT_devRequestedFields(p);if(p.maintenanceContinues>=Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_authoring_continues)){var abortedPending=p;BTT_devRestoreCandidateAfterAbort(p,"AUTHORING_RESPONSE_LIMIT:"+String(p.lastError||reqNow.join(",")||"validation"));g.lastMaintenanceNotice=BTT_devAuthoringNotice(abortedPending,"",true);return {maintenance:true,aborted:true,text:g.lastMaintenanceNotice};}
  if(!p.lastError)p.lastError=reqNow.map(function(x){return x+":"+String(p.fieldErrors[x]||"MISSING");}).join(";");g.lastMaintenanceNotice=BTT_devAuthoringNotice(p,"",false);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING CONTINUE REQUIRED | candidate="+p.candidateId+" | requested_next_fields="+reqNow.join(",")+" | responses="+p.authoringResponses+" | continues_used="+p.maintenanceContinues+"/"+cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_authoring_continues+" | captured_this_response="+captured+" | last_error="+String(p.lastError||"none"));return {maintenance:true,completed:false,text:g.lastMaintenanceNotice};
}
function BTT_devRiskAuthoringPending(){try{return !!BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState().pending;}catch(_e){return false;}}
function BTT_stripDevRiskMaintenanceContext(raw){
  /* Maintenance notices are UI guidance, not story content. Remove every notice
     form before the next Context pass so extension prompts cannot become narrative
     evidence or accumulate across repeated Continue actions. */
  return String(raw||"").replace(/\s*>>>\s*BTT\s+(?:qualified\s+a\s+Developable\s+Risk|is\s+authoring\s+a\s+Developable\s+Risk|finished\s+authoring\s+Developable\s+Risk|paused\s+Developable\s+Risk\s+authoring|detected\s+a\s+qualified\s+new\s+progression\s+track\s+for\s+an\s+existing\s+AI-generated\s+Developable\s+Risk|detected\s+a\s+possible\s+higher\s+progression\s+for\s+an\s+existing\s+AI-generated\s+Developable\s+Risk|added\s+a\s+new\s+progression\s+track\s+to\s+Developable\s+Risk|extended\s+Developable\s+Risk|is\s+extending\s+an\s+existing\s+Developable\s+Risk|paused\s+new-track\s+authoring|paused\s+higher-progression\s+authoring)[\s\S]*?<<<\s*/gi,"\n").replace(/\n{3,}/g,"\n\n");
}
function BTT_processDevRiskMaintenanceOutput(raw){var before=BTT_getTurn(),result=BTT_captureDevRiskGenerationBatch(raw);BTT_writeDevRiskGenerationDebugCard();BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("MAINTENANCE OUTPUT CONSUMED | story_turn="+before+" | turn_advanced=NO | completed="+(result.completed?"YES":"NO")+" | aborted="+(result.aborted?"YES":"NO"));BTT_writeDevRiskGenerationDebugCard();return result.text;}
/* ============================================================================
   5. ENTITY MODEL, PROFILE APPLICATION, AND PREFLIGHT CARD IMPORT
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   This section owns the only card-to-runtime mutation path. Manual edits are
   detected against the last serialized canonical snapshots. When two linked
   representations disagree, the representation the user actually changed is
   authoritative and the linked value is derived from it.
============================================================================ */

var BTT_CUPS = {AA:3,A:5,B:10,C:18,D:22,DD:24,E:24,F:28,G:32,H:38,I:43,J:48,K:53,L:56,M:60,N:63,O:68,P:73,Q:78,R:83,S:88,T:93,U:98};
function BTT_axisToCup(axis){axis=BTT_clamp(axis,0,100);var keys=Object.keys(BTT_CUPS),best="AA",dist=999;for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){var d=Math.abs(BTT_CUPS[keys[i]]-axis);if(d<dist){dist=d;best=keys[i];}}return best;}
function BTT_cupToAxis(c){c=String(c||"").toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g,"");return BTT_CUPS.hasOwnProperty(c)?BTT_CUPS[c]:null;}
function BTT_inchesToAxis(n){n=Number(n);if(!isFinite(n)||n<=0)return 0;return BTT_clamp(20+(n-5)*5,0,100);}
function BTT_axisToInches(a){a=Number(a);if(!isFinite(a)||a<=0)return 0;return Math.max(1,5+(a-20)/5);}
function BTT_assTrait(axis){axis=Number(axis)||0;if(axis<10)return "ass-flat";if(axis<22)return "ass-average";return "ass-curvy";}
function BTT_breastTrait(axis){axis=Number(axis)||0;if(axis<=0)return "flat";return "cup-"+BTT_axisToCup(axis).toLowerCase();}
function BTT_cockTrait(axis){var n=BTT_axisToInches(axis);if(n<=0)return "";var v=Math.round(n*10)/10;return "cock-"+String(v).replace(/\.0$/,'')+"in";}
function BTT_traitAxisHint(slot,traits){
  traits=Array.isArray(traits)?traits:[];var i,m;if(slot==="breasts"){for(i=0;i<traits.length;i++){m=String(traits[i]).match(/^cup-([a-z]+)$/i);if(m){var c=BTT_cupToAxis(m[1]);if(c!==null)return {axis:"breast_size",value:c};}if(/^flat$/i.test(traits[i]))return {axis:"breast_size",value:0};}}
  if(slot==="penis"){for(i=0;i<traits.length;i++){m=String(traits[i]).match(/^cock-(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)in$/i);if(m)return {axis:"cock_size",value:BTT_inchesToAxis(Number(m[1]))};}if(!traits.length||traits.indexOf("none")>=0)return {axis:"cock_size",value:0};}
  if(slot==="ass"){for(i=0;i<traits.length;i++){if(traits[i]==="ass-flat")return {axis:"ass_size",value:5};if(traits[i]==="ass-average")return {axis:"ass_size",value:18};if(traits[i]==="ass-curvy")return {axis:"ass_size",value:28};}}
  return null;
}
function BTT_axisSpecValue(spec,current,seed,heritageMode){
  if(spec===undefined||spec===null)return Number(current)||0;var p=BTT_parseAxisSpec(String(spec));if(!p)return Number(current)||0;
  var roll;if(p.min===p.max)roll=p.min;else{var scale=1000,lo=Math.round(p.min*scale),hi=Math.round(p.max*scale);roll=BTT_hashInt(seed+"|range",lo,hi)/scale;}
  if(heritageMode)return roll;var op=p.op||"=";if(op==="+")return (Number(current)||0)+Math.abs(roll);if(op==="-")return (Number(current)||0)-Math.abs(roll);return roll;
}
function BTT_pickProfileOption(raw,seed){var a=String(raw||"").match(/\[([^\]]+)\]/);var vals=a?BTT_splitList(a[1]):BTT_splitList(raw);if(!vals.length)return "";return vals[BTT_hashInt(seed,0,vals.length-1)];}
function BTT_ensureSlot(meta,slot,baseline){
  var created=!meta.slots[slot];if(created)meta.slots[slot]=BTT_newSlot();var so=meta.slots[slot],origin=BTT_inferSlotOrigin(meta,slot),base=BTT_normId(baseline||"");
  /* Host Heritage is only a provisional origin for a newly created/untyped slot.
     Once acquisition/profile evidence has established a foreign slot origin,
     later ordinary trait additions must never reinsert the host Heritage. */
  if(base&&!origin){if(so.traits.indexOf(base)<0)so.traits.unshift(base);so.origin=base;}
  return so;
}
function BTT_addTraitDirect(meta,slot,trait){slot=BTT_lower(slot);trait=BTT_lower(trait).replace(/\s+/g,"-");if(!trait)return false;if(BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS[trait])slot="head";var s=BTT_ensureSlot(meta,slot,meta&&meta.heritage||"human");if(s.traits.indexOf(trait)>=0)return false;s.traits.push(trait);s.updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();return true;}
function BTT_removeTraitDirect(meta,slot,trait){var s=meta&&meta.slots&&meta.slots[slot];if(!s)return false;var k=s.traits.indexOf(trait);if(k<0)return false;s.traits.splice(k,1);s.updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();return true;}
function BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(marker){return BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(marker)).replace(/\s+/g,"").replace(/^random\((?:f\/m)\)$/,"random(f/m)").replace(/^random\((?:m\/f)\)$/,"random(m/f)");}
function BTT_markerIsNone(marker){return /^(?:n|no|none|false|0)$/.test(BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(marker));}
function BTT_markerIsPositive(marker){marker=BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(marker);return /^(?:y|yes|true|1|m|f|x)$/.test(marker)||marker==="random(m/f)"||marker==="random(f/m)";}
function BTT_resolveRandomMarker(marker,seed){marker=BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(marker);if(marker==="random(m/f)"||marker==="random(f/m)")return BTT_hashInt(seed+"|marker",0,1)===0?"m":"f";return marker;}
function BTT_genderCapabilities(gender){var g=BTT_lower(gender),male=/^(?:male|man|boy)$/.test(g),female=/^(?:female|woman|girl)$/.test(g),mixed=/(?:hermaphrodite|intersex|futanari|futa|dual[- ]?sex|mixed)/.test(g)||(!male&&!female);return {m:male||mixed,f:female||mixed,x:true,mixed:mixed};}
function BTT_slotPresentationCode(genderProfile,slot,gender,seed){var marker=BTT_resolveRandomMarker((genderProfile&&genderProfile.slot_markers&&genderProfile.slot_markers[slot])||(genderProfile&&genderProfile.slots&&genderProfile.slots[slot]&&genderProfile.slots[slot].present)||"",seed+"|"+slot),caps=BTT_genderCapabilities(gender);if(marker==="m"||marker==="f"||marker==="x")return marker;if(marker==="y"||marker==="yes"||marker==="true"||marker==="1"||marker==="g")return caps.m&&caps.f?"x":caps.m?"m":caps.f?"f":"x";return caps.m&&caps.f?"x":caps.m?"m":caps.f?"f":"x";}
function BTT_traitGateAllowed(raw,slotCode,caps){var m=String(raw||"").match(/\(([mfx])\)\s*$/i);if(!m)return true;var g=BTT_lower(m[1]);if(g==="x")return true;if(slotCode===g)return true;if(slotCode==="x")return !!caps[g];return !!caps[g]&&!slotCode;}
function BTT_stripProfileTraitGate(raw){return BTT_lower(String(raw||"").replace(/\s*\([mfx]\)\s*$/i,"").trim()).replace(/\s+/g,"-");}
function BTT_heritageMarkerFor(profile,slot){var sp=profile&&profile.slots&&profile.slots[slot];return BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(profile&&profile.slot_markers&&profile.slot_markers[slot]||(sp&&sp.present)||"");}
function BTT_genderMarkerFor(profile,slot){var sp=profile&&profile.slots&&profile.slots[slot];return BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(profile&&profile.slot_markers&&profile.slot_markers[slot]||(sp&&sp.present)||"");}
function BTT_heritageAllowsNewSlot(hMarker,gMarker,gCode,slot){hMarker=BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(hMarker);gMarker=BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(gMarker);if(slot==="penis"||slot==="vagina")return !BTT_markerIsNone(gMarker)&&gMarker!=="g"&&!!gMarker;if(BTT_markerIsNone(hMarker))return false;if(/^(?:y|yes|true|1|x)$/.test(hMarker))return !BTT_markerIsNone(gMarker);if(hMarker==="g")return BTT_markerIsPositive(gMarker);if(hMarker==="m")return gCode==="m"||gCode==="x";if(hMarker==="f")return gCode==="f"||gCode==="x";return false;}
function BTT_profileFieldChoice(profile,key,seed){var f=profile&&profile.fields||{},direct=f[key],options=f[key+"_options"];if(Array.isArray(direct))options=direct;else if(direct!==undefined&&direct!==null&&BTT_cleanSpace(direct))return BTT_cleanSpace(direct);var vals=[];if(Array.isArray(options))vals=BTT_unique(options);else{var m=String(options||"").match(/\[([^\]]+)\]/);vals=m?BTT_splitList(m[1]):BTT_splitList(options);}if(!vals.length)return "";return vals[BTT_hashInt(seed+"|field|"+key,0,vals.length-1)];}
function BTT_applyProfileFields(meta,p,kind,mode,seed){if(!meta||!p)return {};var changed={},keys=["skin","hair_color","hair_length","eye_color","build","scent"],override=kind==="gender"||/instant|reinit/.test(String(mode||""));for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){var val=BTT_profileFieldChoice(p,keys[i],seed);if(!val)continue;var old=String(meta.appearance.fields[keys[i]]||"");if(override||!old){meta.appearance.fields[keys[i]]=val;if(old!==val)changed[keys[i]]=true;}}return changed;}

function BTT_profileMarkerAllows(marker,meta){
  marker=BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(marker);if(BTT_markerIsNone(marker))return false;if(marker==="random(m/f)"||marker==="random(f/m)")return true;if(marker==="m")return BTT_genderCapabilities(meta&&meta.gender).m;if(marker==="f")return BTT_genderCapabilities(meta&&meta.gender).f;return /^(?:y|yes|true|1|g|m|f|x)$/.test(marker);
}
function BTT_applyProfile(meta,p,kind,mode,seed,peerProfile){
  if(!meta||!p)return;kind=BTT_lower(kind);mode=String(mode||"baseline");seed=String(seed||meta.entity+"|"+p.id+"|"+BTT_getTurn());var hp=kind==="heritage"?p:(peerProfile||BTT_profile("heritage",meta.heritage)),gp=kind==="gender"?p:peerProfile;
  if(kind==="heritage"){meta.heritage=p.id;meta.species=p.id;meta.identity.heritage=p.id;}
  if(kind==="gender"){var gid=BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(p,meta.gender||"hermaphrodite");meta.gender=gid;meta.identity.gender=gid;}
  var caps=BTT_genderCapabilities(meta.gender),profileTraits=kind==="heritage"?(p.heritage_traits||[]):[],fieldsChanged={};
  for(var i=0;i<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;i++){
    var slot=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[i],sp=p.slots&&p.slots[slot],marker=BTT_profileMarkerNormalized((p.slot_markers&&p.slot_markers[slot])||(sp&&sp.present)||""),gMarker=BTT_genderMarkerFor(gp,slot),gCode=BTT_slotPresentationCode(gp,slot,meta.gender,seed+"|presentation");
    if(kind==="heritage"){
      var create=false;if(BTT_markerIsNone(marker)){delete meta.slots[slot];continue;}if(/^(?:y|yes|true|1|x)$/.test(marker))create=!BTT_markerIsNone(gMarker);else if(marker==="g")create=BTT_markerIsPositive(gMarker);else if(marker==="m")create=gCode==="m"||gCode==="x";else if(marker==="f")create=gCode==="f"||gCode==="x";
      if(!create)continue;
    }else{
      if(BTT_markerIsNone(marker)){delete meta.slots[slot];continue;}var exists=!!meta.slots[slot];if(marker==="g"&&!exists)continue;if(!exists){var hm=BTT_heritageMarkerFor(hp,slot);if(!BTT_heritageAllowsNewSlot(hm,marker,gCode,slot))continue;}
    }
    var so=BTT_ensureSlot(meta,slot,meta.heritage||"human"),raw=[];if(sp&&Array.isArray(sp.traits))raw=raw.concat(sp.traits);if(kind==="heritage"&&slot!=="penis"&&slot!=="vagina")raw=raw.concat(profileTraits);
    for(var t=0;t<raw.length;t++){if(!BTT_traitGateAllowed(raw[t],gCode,caps))continue;var tr=BTT_stripProfileTraitGate(raw[t]);if(tr&&so.traits.indexOf(tr)<0)so.traits.push(tr);}so.updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();
  }
  var axes=p.axes||{},ax=meta.appearance.axes;for(var key in axes)if(axes.hasOwnProperty(key)){var akey=BTT_lower(key).replace(/\s+/g,"_");if(akey==="overall")akey="fem";if(!ax.hasOwnProperty(akey)&&akey!=="fem")continue;var parsed=BTT_parseAxisSpec(axes[key]);if(!parsed)continue;var cur=akey==="fem"?meta.overallFem:ax[akey],val=BTT_axisSpecValue(axes[key],cur,seed+"|"+akey,kind==="heritage");if(akey==="fem"){meta.overallFem=BTT_round(BTT_clamp(val,-100,100),2);ax.fem=meta.overallFem;}else ax[akey]=BTT_round(BTT_clamp(val,0,100),2);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"PROFILE AXIS | "+kind+":"+p.id+" | "+akey+"="+BTT_round(akey==="fem"?meta.overallFem:ax[akey],2));if(/instant/.test(mode))BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE PHENOTYPE ROLL | axis="+akey+" | value="+BTT_round(akey==="fem"?meta.overallFem:ax[akey],2)+" | profile="+kind+"|"+p.id);}
  fieldsChanged=BTT_applyProfileFields(meta,p,kind,mode,seed);if(/instant/.test(mode)){for(var fk in fieldsChanged)if(fieldsChanged.hasOwnProperty(fk))BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE FIELD OVERRIDE | "+fk+"="+String(meta.appearance.fields[fk])+" | source="+kind+"|"+p.id);}
  if(kind==="gender"&&p.pronouns){var old=meta.identity.pronouns;if(old!==p.pronouns){meta.identity.previousPronouns=old;meta.identity.pronouns=p.pronouns;meta.identity.pronounGrace=BTT_getConfig().pronoun_grace_turns;}}
  meta.appearance.axes.fem=meta.overallFem;
}
function BTT_captureRefreshTransport(app){
  app=app||{};return {summary_ai:String(app.summary_ai||""),evidence:BTT_clone(app.evidence||[]),debug:BTT_clone(app.debug||[]),delta:BTT_round(Number(app._delta_since_refresh)||0,2),last:isFinite(Number(app._last_refresh_turn))?Number(app._last_refresh_turn):-1,need:!!app._need_ai_refresh,pending:!!app._pendingRefresh,did:!!app._did_ai_init,reason:String(app._refresh_flag_reason||""),requested:isFinite(Number(app._refresh_requested_turn))?Number(app._refresh_requested_turn):-1,captured:isFinite(Number(app._refresh_captured_turn))?Number(app._refresh_captured_turn):-1,requestId:String(app._refresh_request_id||""),signature:String(app._refresh_signature||"")};
}
function BTT_restoreRefreshTransport(app,x){
  if(!app||!x)return;app.summary_ai=String(x.summary_ai||"");app.evidence=BTT_clone(x.evidence||[]);app.debug=BTT_clone(x.debug||[]);app._delta_since_refresh=BTT_round(Number(x.delta)||0,2);app._last_refresh_turn=isFinite(Number(x.last))?Number(x.last):-1;app._need_ai_refresh=!!x.need;app._pendingRefresh=!!x.pending;app._did_ai_init=!!x.did;app._refresh_flag_reason=String(x.reason||"");app._refresh_requested_turn=isFinite(Number(x.requested))?Number(x.requested):-1;app._refresh_captured_turn=isFinite(Number(x.captured))?Number(x.captured):-1;app._refresh_request_id=String(x.requestId||"");app._refresh_signature=String(x.signature||"");
}

function BTT_reinitializeFromProfiles(meta,heritageId,genderId,seed){
  var oldFields=BTT_clone(meta.appearance&&meta.appearance.fields||{}),oldSummary=String(meta.appearance&&meta.appearance.summary_ai||""),oldRefresh=BTT_captureRefreshTransport(meta.appearance),hp=BTT_profile("heritage",heritageId),gp=BTT_profile("gender",genderId)||BTT_profileByGenderLabel(genderId);
  meta.slots={};meta.appearance=BTT_newAppearance();BTT_restoreRefreshTransport(meta.appearance,oldRefresh);meta.overallFem=0;
  if(gp){meta.gender=BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(gp,meta.gender||genderId||"hermaphrodite");meta.identity.gender=meta.gender;}else{meta.gender=genderId||meta.gender||"hermaphrodite";meta.identity.gender=meta.gender;}
  if(hp)BTT_applyProfile(meta,hp,"heritage","reinit",seed+"|heritage",gp);else{meta.heritage=heritageId||"human";meta.species=meta.heritage;meta.identity.heritage=meta.heritage;}
  if(gp)BTT_applyProfile(meta,gp,"gender","reinit",seed+"|gender",hp);
  for(var k in oldFields)if(oldFields.hasOwnProperty(k)&&oldFields[k]&&!meta.appearance.fields[k])meta.appearance.fields[k]=oldFields[k];meta.appearance.summary_ai=oldSummary;meta.initialized=true;
}
function BTT_entityName(entity,meta){meta=meta||BTT_findEntity(entity);var n=meta&&meta.identity&&meta.identity.currentName;return BTT_cleanSpace(n)||(entity==="Player"?"Player":String(entity||"").replace(/^NPC:/,""));}
function BTT_profileByGenderLabel(label){var ps=BTT_profiles("gender"),want=BTT_normId(label),id;for(id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){var aliases=BTT_profileAliases(ps[id]);for(var i=0;i<aliases.length;i++){var a=BTT_normId(aliases[i]);if(a===want||(want==="male"&&/^(?:man|male)$/.test(a))||(want==="female"&&/^(?:woman|female)$/.test(a))||(want==="hermaphrodite"&&/(?:futa|futanari|hermaphrodite|intersex)/.test(a)))return ps[id];}}return null;}
function BTT_humanHeritageProfile(){var ps=BTT_profiles("heritage");if(ps.human)return ps.human;for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){var a=BTT_profileAliases(ps[id]);for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(BTT_normId(a[i])==="human")return ps[id];}return null;}
function BTT_initializeBaseline(meta,text,sourceCard,parse,entity){
  if(meta.initialized)return;entity=entity||meta.entity||"Player";var owned=String(text||""),explicitGender=BTT_normId(sourceCard&&sourceCard.baselineGender||""),explicitHeritage=BTT_normId(sourceCard&&sourceCard.baselineHeritage||""),h=null,g=null,gender="",he=null,ge=null,cardText="";
  if(explicitHeritage){var eh=BTT_profile("heritage",explicitHeritage);if(eh)h={id:eh.id,profile:eh,authority:"character_metadata",score:99,margin:99};}
  if(explicitGender){var egp=BTT_profile("gender",explicitGender)||BTT_profileByGenderLabel(explicitGender);if(egp)g={id:egp.id,profile:egp,authority:"character_metadata",score:99,margin:99};else if(/^(?:male|female|hermaphrodite)$/.test(explicitGender))gender=explicitGender;}
  /* Character-card text is baseline authority for a carded NPC. Otherwise the
     parsed narrative supplies an additive, entity-owned baseline window that
     ends when actual transformation evidence begins. */
  if(sourceCard&&sourceCard.carded&&sourceCard.card)cardText=BTT_characterCardText(sourceCard.card);
  if(!h&&cardText){var ch=BTT_findProfileByIdentity(cardText,"heritage",true);if(ch&&ch.score>=0.72)h={id:ch.id,profile:ch.profile,authority:"character_card_text",score:ch.score,margin:ch.score};}
  if(!g&&!gender&&cardText){var cg=BTT_findProfileByIdentity(cardText,"gender",true);if(cg&&cg.score>=0.72)g={id:cg.id,profile:cg.profile,authority:"character_card_text",score:cg.score,margin:cg.score};}
  if(!h&&parse){he=BTT_baselineProfileEvidence(parse,entity,"heritage",true);if(he&&he.score>=0.72&&he.margin>=0.12)h={id:he.id,profile:he.profile,authority:"confidence",score:he.score,margin:he.margin,boundary:he.boundary};}
  if(!g&&!gender&&parse){ge=BTT_baselineProfileEvidence(parse,entity,"gender",true);if(ge&&ge.score>=0.72&&ge.margin>=0.12)g={id:ge.id,profile:ge.profile,authority:"confidence",score:ge.score,margin:ge.margin,boundary:ge.boundary};}
  /* Raw-text fallback remains additive and profile-defined. It is only used
     when no parsed owned evidence was available; no grammar template or alias
     string length can independently force the result. */
  if(!h&&!parse){var rh=BTT_findProfileByIdentity(owned,"heritage",true);if(rh&&rh.score>=0.72)h={id:rh.id,profile:rh.profile,authority:"text_fallback",score:rh.score,margin:rh.score};}
  if(!g&&!gender&&!parse){var rg=BTT_findProfileByIdentity(owned,"gender",true);if(rg&&rg.score>=0.72)g={id:rg.id,profile:rg.profile,authority:"text_fallback",score:rg.score,margin:rg.score};}
  if(!h){var hp=BTT_humanHeritageProfile();if(hp)h={id:hp.id,profile:hp,authority:"default",score:0,margin:0};}
  var hid=h&&h.id||"human",gid=g&&g.id||gender||"hermaphrodite",boundary=parse?BTT_baselineChangeBoundary(parse,entity):null,seed=entity+"|baseline-init|"+BTT_runSeed();
  if(parse){parse.baselineBoundaries=parse.baselineBoundaries||{};parse.baselineBoundaries[entity]=boundary===null?parse.fragments.length:boundary;if(parse.shared)parse.shared.baselineBoundaries=parse.baselineBoundaries;}
  BTT_reinitializeFromProfiles(meta,hid,gid,seed);
  /* Explicit pre-change appearance beats rolled defaults. Fields without
     supported baseline evidence keep the valid Heritage/Gender profile roll. */
  if(parse&&!(sourceCard&&sourceCard.carded))BTT_baselineAppearanceEvidence(meta,parse,entity,boundary);
  if(!meta.identity.pronouns||meta.identity.pronouns==="they/them/their")meta.identity.pronouns=BTT_defaultPronouns(meta.gender);
  if(sourceCard&&sourceCard.name){meta.identity.currentName=sourceCard.name;meta.identity.originalName=meta.identity.originalName||sourceCard.name;meta.identity.aliases=BTT_unique(meta.identity.aliases.concat(sourceCard.aliases||[]));}
  if(meta.genesis){meta.genesis.cardAuthority=meta.genesis.cardAuthority||{gender:false,heritage:false};meta.genesis.cardAuthority.gender=!!explicitGender;meta.genesis.cardAuthority.heritage=!!explicitHeritage;if(sourceCard&&sourceCard.carded)meta.genesis.baselineSource="character_card";}
  meta.initialized=true;meta.appearance.axes.fem=meta.overallFem;
  if(g&&g.authority==="confidence")BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","BASELINE EVIDENCE | kind=gender | winner="+g.id+" | score="+BTT_round(g.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(g.margin,2)+" | boundary="+(boundary===null?"none":boundary));
  if(h&&h.authority==="confidence")BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","BASELINE EVIDENCE | kind=heritage | winner="+h.id+" | score="+BTT_round(h.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(h.margin,2)+" | boundary="+(boundary===null?"none":boundary));
  BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","BASELINE INIT | gender="+meta.gender+" | heritage="+meta.heritage+" | source="+(sourceCard&&sourceCard.carded?"character_card":"profile_confidence"));
}

function BTT_parseIdentityLines(entry){var out={};var lines=String(entry||"").split(/\n/g);for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++){var m=lines[i].match(/^\s*(Entity ID|Current Name|Original Name|Aliases|Gender|Pronouns|Previous Pronouns|Pronoun Grace|Heritage)\s*:\s*(.*)$/i);if(!m)continue;out[BTT_lower(m[1]).replace(/\s+/g,"_")]=BTT_cleanSpace(m[2]);}return out;}
function BTT_parseAnatomyEntry(entry){
  var out={identity:BTT_parseIdentityLines(entry),slots:{},conditions:[],overallFem:null},lines=String(entry||"").split(/\n/g),inCond=false;for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++){var line=lines[i],m=line.match(/^\s*(HEAD|ARMS|LEGS|BREASTS|GUT|PENIS|VAGINA|ASS|TAIL|WINGS)\s*:\s*(.*)$/i);if(m){var rhs=BTT_cleanSpace(m[2]),traits=/^none$/i.test(rhs)?[]:rhs.split(/\s*;\s*/g).map(function(x){return BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(x)).replace(/\s+/g,"-");}).filter(Boolean);out.slots[BTT_lower(m[1])]=traits;continue;}m=line.match(/^\s*-\s*([a-z][a-z-]*)(?:\s+x(\d+))?\s*\|\s*slot=([^|]+)(?:\|\s*source=([^|]+))?(?:\|\s*turn=(\d+))?/i);if(m){out.conditions.push({type:BTT_lower(m[1]),count:Math.max(1,Number(m[2])||1),slot:BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(m[3])),source:BTT_cleanSpace(m[4]||"unknown"),startedTurn:Number(m[5])||0,active:true});continue;}m=line.match(/^\s*Femininity \(overall\)\s*:\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/i);if(m)out.overallFem=Number(m[1]);}return out;
}
function BTT_parseAppearanceEntry(entry){
  var out={summary_ai:"",species:"",fields:{},axes:{}},t=String(entry||"").trim();if(!t)return out;t=t.replace(/^(?:[A-Z][A-Z0-9 '’._-]*|PLAYER)\s+APPEARANCE\s*\(BTT(?: NPC)?\)\s*/i,"").trim();var lines=t.split(/\n/g),prose=[],i=0;
  for(;i<lines.length;i++){var x=BTT_cleanSpace(lines[i]);if(!x)continue;if(/^Species\s*:/i.test(x)||/^--- DEBUG/i.test(x))break;prose.push(x);}var summary=prose.join("\n").trim();if(summary&&!/^\[(?:Awaiting appearance description from AI refresh|NPC AI appearance refresh disabled|NPC appearance uses structured canonical fields)/i.test(summary))out.summary_ai=summary;
  var map={"hair color":"hair_color","hair length":"hair_length","eye color":"eye_color","skin":"skin","build":"build","scent":"scent","height axis":"height","fat axis":"fat","body fat axis":"fat","gut size":"gut_size","gut size axis":"gut_size","breast size":"breast_size","ass size":"ass_size","cock size":"cock_size","fertility axis":"fertility","virility axis":"virility","fem":"fem"};
  for(;i<lines.length;i++){var m=lines[i].match(/^\s*([^:]+)\s*:\s*(.*)$/);if(!m)continue;var key=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(m[1])),val=BTT_cleanSpace(m[2]);if(key==="species"){out.species=BTT_lower(val);continue;}var dest=map[key];if(!dest)continue;if(["height","fat","gut_size","breast_size","ass_size","cock_size","fertility","virility","fem"].indexOf(dest)>=0){var n=Number(val);if(isFinite(n))out.axes[dest]=n;}else out.fields[dest]=val;}
  return out;
}
function BTT_parseAppearanceRefreshState(desc){
  var raw=String(desc||""),m=raw.match(/--- BTT APPEARANCE REFRESH STATE ---[\s\S]*?Payload JSON:\s*([^\n]+)[\s\S]*?--- END BTT APPEARANCE REFRESH STATE ---/i);if(m){try{var o=JSON.parse(m[1]);if(o&&typeof o==="object")return o;}catch(_e){}}
  var r=raw.match(/REFRESH\s*\|\s*need=(YES|NO)\s*\|\s*pending=(YES|NO)(?:\s*\|\s*init=(YES|NO))?\s*\|\s*last=(-?\d+)\s*\|\s*delta=(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*\|\s*reason=([^\n|]*)/i);if(!r)return null;return {need_ai_refresh:/^YES$/i.test(r[1]),pending:/^YES$/i.test(r[2]),did_ai_init:r[3]?/^YES$/i.test(r[3]):false,last_refresh_turn:Number(r[4]),delta_since_refresh:Number(r[5])||0,reason:BTT_cleanSpace(r[6]),requested_turn:-1,captured_turn:-1};
}
function BTT_applyAppearanceRefreshState(entity,meta,st){
  if(!meta||!meta.appearance)return;if(entity!=="Player"){meta.appearance.summary_ai="";meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=false;meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=false;meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason="npc_refresh_disabled";meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn=-1;meta.appearance._refresh_captured_turn=-1;return;}if(!st)return;
  /* Context may have moved a request from REQUESTED to PENDING after the last
     card write. That transport-only live flag must survive the Output preflight
     or a failed AI block would look as though no injection was attempted. */
  var livePending=!!meta.appearance._pendingRefresh,liveNeed=!!meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh,liveReason=String(meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason||""),liveRequested=Number(meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn),liveRequestId=String(meta.appearance._refresh_request_id||""),liveSignature=String(meta.appearance._refresh_signature||"");
  meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=!!st.need_ai_refresh;meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=!!st.pending;meta.appearance._did_ai_init=!!st.did_ai_init;meta.appearance._last_refresh_turn=isFinite(Number(st.last_refresh_turn))?Number(st.last_refresh_turn):-1;meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh=Number(st.delta_since_refresh)||0;meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason=String(st.reason||"");meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn=isFinite(Number(st.requested_turn))?Number(st.requested_turn):-1;meta.appearance._refresh_captured_turn=isFinite(Number(st.captured_turn))?Number(st.captured_turn):-1;meta.appearance._refresh_request_id=String(st.request_id||meta.appearance._refresh_request_id||"");meta.appearance._refresh_signature=String(st.signature||meta.appearance._refresh_signature||"");
  if(livePending){meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=true;meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=liveNeed||true;if(liveReason)meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason=liveReason;if(isFinite(liveRequested)&&liveRequested>=0)meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn=liveRequested;if(liveRequestId)meta.appearance._refresh_request_id=liveRequestId;if(liveSignature)meta.appearance._refresh_signature=liveSignature;}
}

function BTT_parseLifecyclePayload(desc){
  var m=String(desc||"").match(/--- BTT INSTANT-CHANGE MACHINE STATE ---[\s\S]*?Payload JSON:\s*([^\n]+)[\s\S]*?--- END BTT INSTANT-CHANGE MACHINE STATE ---/i);if(!m)return null;
  try{var p=JSON.parse(m[1]);if(!p||!p.instantchange)return null;return {currentDay:isFinite(Number(p.currentDay))?Number(p.currentDay):null,instantchange:p.instantchange,developableRisks:p.developableRisks||null};}catch(_e){return null;}
}
function BTT_sameJSON(a,b){try{return JSON.stringify(a)===JSON.stringify(b);}catch(_e){return false;}}
function BTT_applyManualAnatomy(meta,parsed,oldParsed){
  if(!parsed)return;oldParsed=oldParsed||{identity:{},slots:{},conditions:[],overallFem:null};var id=parsed.identity||{},oid=oldParsed.identity||{};
  function changed(k){return String(id[k]||"")!==String(oid[k]||"");}
  if(changed("current_name")&&id.current_name){var old=meta.identity.currentName;meta.identity.currentName=id.current_name;meta.identity.aliases=BTT_unique(meta.identity.aliases.concat([old,id.current_name]));BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | current_name "+old+"→"+id.current_name);}
  /* Identity fields written by Anatomy must be reconstructable after a state
     reload. Original Name is stable history, while Previous Pronouns/Grace are
     continuity state for an already-committed pronoun change. */
  if(changed("original_name")&&id.original_name){meta.identity.originalName=id.original_name;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | original_name→"+id.original_name);}
  if(changed("aliases")){var supplied=BTT_splitList(id.aliases||""),required=meta.entity==="Player"?["Player","you","your","yourself",meta.identity.originalName,meta.identity.currentName]:[meta.identity.originalName,meta.identity.currentName];meta.identity.aliases=BTT_unique(required.concat(supplied));BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | aliases="+meta.identity.aliases.join(", "));}
  if(changed("gender")&&id.gender){meta.gender=BTT_lower(id.gender);meta.identity.gender=meta.gender;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | gender→"+meta.gender);}
  if(changed("pronouns")&&id.pronouns){var pp=BTT_pronounSet(id.pronouns);if(pp){var oldPron=meta.identity.pronouns;if(oid.pronouns){meta.identity.previousPronouns=oldPron;meta.identity.pronounGrace=BTT_getConfig().pronoun_grace_turns;}meta.identity.pronouns=pp;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | pronouns "+oldPron+"→"+pp);}}
  if(changed("previous_pronouns")){var oldPrev=meta.identity.previousPronouns;meta.identity.previousPronouns=BTT_pronounSet(id.previous_pronouns)||"";BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | previous_pronouns "+oldPrev+"→"+meta.identity.previousPronouns);}
  if(changed("pronoun_grace")){var oldGrace=meta.identity.pronounGrace,pg=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(id.pronoun_grace)||0));meta.identity.pronounGrace=pg;if(!pg&&!id.previous_pronouns)meta.identity.previousPronouns="";BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | pronoun_grace "+oldGrace+"→"+pg);}
  if(changed("heritage")&&id.heritage){var hp=BTT_profile("heritage",id.heritage);if(hp){var oldHeritage=meta.heritage;meta.heritage=hp.id;meta.species=hp.id;meta.identity.heritage=hp.id;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL IDENTITY | heritage "+oldHeritage+"→"+hp.id);} }
  var slots=parsed.slots||{},oldSlots=oldParsed.slots||{};for(var s=0;s<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;s++){var slot=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[s];if(!slots.hasOwnProperty(slot))continue;if(!BTT_sameJSON(slots[slot],oldSlots[slot])){var incoming=slots[slot];if(!incoming.length)delete meta.slots[slot];else{meta.slots[slot]={traits:incoming.slice(),origin:"",updatedTurn:BTT_getTurn()};BTT_inferSlotOrigin(meta,slot);}var hint=BTT_traitAxisHint(slot,incoming);if(!hint&&!incoming.length&&slot==="breasts")hint={axis:"breast_size",value:0};if(hint){var before=meta.appearance.axes[hint.axis];meta.appearance.axes[hint.axis]=BTT_round(hint.value,2);meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh=BTT_round((Number(meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh)||0)+Math.abs((Number(before)||0)-BTT_round(hint.value,2)),2);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL ANATOMY AUTHORITY | "+slot+" changed; "+hint.axis+"→"+hint.value);}}}
  if(parsed.overallFem!==null&&parsed.overallFem!==oldParsed.overallFem){var oldFem=meta.overallFem;meta.overallFem=BTT_round(BTT_clamp(parsed.overallFem,-100,100),2);meta.appearance.axes.fem=meta.overallFem;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL ANATOMY | overall_fem "+oldFem+"→"+meta.overallFem);}
  if(!BTT_sameJSON(parsed.conditions,oldParsed.conditions)){meta.conditions=BTT_clone(parsed.conditions);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL CONDITIONS | active="+meta.conditions.length);}
}
function BTT_applyManualAppearance(meta,parsed,oldParsed){
  if(!parsed)return;oldParsed=oldParsed||{summary_ai:"",species:"",fields:{},axes:{}};var k;
  if(parsed.summary_ai!==undefined&&String(parsed.summary_ai)!==String(oldParsed.summary_ai||"")&&meta.entity==="Player"){meta.appearance.summary_ai=String(parsed.summary_ai||"");if(meta.appearance.summary_ai){meta.appearance._did_ai_init=true;meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=false;meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=false;}BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL APPEARANCE SUMMARY | len="+meta.appearance.summary_ai.length);}
  if(parsed.species&&parsed.species!==oldParsed.species){var hp=BTT_profile("heritage",parsed.species);if(hp){var oldSpecies=meta.species;meta.heritage=hp.id;meta.species=hp.id;meta.identity.heritage=hp.id;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL APPEARANCE SPECIES | "+oldSpecies+"→"+hp.id);}}
  for(k in parsed.fields)if(parsed.fields.hasOwnProperty(k)&&String(parsed.fields[k])!==String((oldParsed.fields||{})[k]||"")){var prev=meta.appearance.fields[k]||"";meta.appearance.fields[k]=parsed.fields[k];BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL APPEARANCE FIELD | "+k+" "+prev+"→"+parsed.fields[k]);}
  for(k in parsed.axes)if(parsed.axes.hasOwnProperty(k)&&Number(parsed.axes[k])!==Number((oldParsed.axes||{})[k])){var before=k==="fem"?meta.overallFem:meta.appearance.axes[k],val=BTT_round(k==="fem"?BTT_clamp(parsed.axes[k],-100,100):BTT_clamp(parsed.axes[k],0,100),2);if(k==="fem"){meta.overallFem=val;meta.appearance.axes.fem=val;}else meta.appearance.axes[k]=val;meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh=BTT_round((Number(meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh)||0)+Math.abs((Number(before)||0)-val),2);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL APPEARANCE AXIS | "+k+" "+before+"→"+val);}
}
function BTT_importEntityCards(entity,meta){
  if(!BTT_hasStoryCards()||!meta)return;var set=BTT_ensureEntityCardSet(entity,meta);if(!set)return;var anatomyEntry=String(set.anatomy&&set.anatomy.entry||""),appearanceEntry=String(set.appearance&&set.appearance.entry||""),appearanceDesc=String(set.appearance&&set.appearance.description||"");
  if(anatomyEntry&&anatomyEntry!==meta.lastSerialized.anatomy){var parsed=BTT_parseAnatomyEntry(anatomyEntry);if(Object.keys(parsed.slots).length||parsed.identity.current_name){BTT_applyManualAnatomy(meta,parsed,meta.lastSerialized.anatomyParsed);meta.initialized=true;}}
  if(appearanceEntry&&appearanceEntry!==meta.lastSerialized.appearance){var ap=BTT_parseAppearanceEntry(appearanceEntry);if(Object.keys(ap.axes).length||Object.keys(ap.fields).length||ap.summary_ai||ap.species)BTT_applyManualAppearance(meta,ap,meta.lastSerialized.appearanceParsed);}
  if(appearanceDesc&&appearanceDesc!==meta.lastSerialized.appearanceDescription){BTT_applyAppearanceRefreshState(entity,meta,BTT_parseAppearanceRefreshState(appearanceDesc));}
  if(entity!=="Player"){meta.appearance.summary_ai="";BTT_applyAppearanceRefreshState(entity,meta,null);}
  if(set.instantchange){var payload=BTT_parseLifecyclePayload(set.instantchange.description);if(payload&&String(set.instantchange.description||"")!==meta.lastSerialized.instantchange){meta.instantchange=BTT_clone(payload.instantchange);if(payload.developableRisks)meta.developableRisks=BTT_clone(payload.developableRisks);if(!meta.developableRisks)meta.developableRisks={active:[],conditionedTriggers:[],acquisitionHistory:{}};if(meta.instantchange.transformationSeed===undefined)meta.instantchange.transformationSeed=null;if(meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature===undefined)meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature="";if(meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype===undefined)meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype=null;if(isFinite(Number(payload.currentDay)))BTT_ensureState().worldDay=Math.max(Number(BTT_ensureState().worldDay)||0,Number(payload.currentDay));BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","MANUAL LIFECYCLE IMPORT | effects="+String((meta.instantchange.effects||[]).length)+" | dev_risks="+String((meta.developableRisks.active||[]).length)+" | day="+String(BTT_getDay()));}}
  if(meta.initialized)BTT_sealGenesis(meta,"card_rehydration");
}
function BTT_existingEntityIdsFromCards(){
  var out=[],seen={};if(!Array.isArray(storyCards))return out;for(var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++){var e=BTT_cardEntity(storyCards[i]);if(!e||!(e==="Player"||/^NPC:/.test(e))||seen[e])continue;seen[e]=1;out.push(e);}return out;
}
function BTT_preflightImportAll(){
  BTT_ensureConfigCard();BTT_ensureTargetCard();BTT_loadProfiles(true);BTT_loadDevRiskProfiles(true);var s=BTT_ensureState(),ids=BTT_existingEntityIdsFromCards();
  if(ids.indexOf("Player")<0)ids.unshift("Player");
  /* Existing BTT card sets can bootstrap the runtime cache after a reload. The
     marker is BTT-owned authority, so this does not admit arbitrary Character,
     Auto-Cards, or Inner Self cards as entities. */
  for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){var e=ids[i],m=BTT_getEntity(e);BTT_ensureEntityCardSet(e,m);BTT_importEntityCards(e,m);}
  BTT_cleanupPlayerDuplicateNPCs(BTT_getEntity("Player"),"preflight_player_identity_reconciliation");
  /* Configured/carded NPCs that already exist in runtime state are also
     imported even if one of their four cards was repaired during preflight. */
  var sources=BTT_admittedNPCSources();for(var j=0;j<sources.length;j++){var en=sources[j].entity,meta=BTT_findEntity(en);if(meta&&meta.initialized&&ids.indexOf(en)<0)BTT_importEntityCards(en,meta);}
}

/* ============================================================================
   6. SINGLE NARRATIVE PARSER AND SHARED EVIDENCE GRAPH
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Input and output text are parsed once per transaction. All downstream domains
   consume these fragments, entity mentions, role hypotheses, actuality values,
   temporal/reference signals, and product references. Domains may use different
   acceptance thresholds, but they do not reparse the narrative independently.
============================================================================ */

var BTT_EVENT_VERBS = ["take","takes","took","swallow","swallows","swallowed","pop","pops","popped","drink","drinks","drank","down","downs","downed","sip","sips","sipped","eat","eats","ate","inject","injects","injected","apply","applies","applied","rub","rubs","rubbed","inhale","inhales","inhaled","breathe","breathes","breathed","drop","drops","dropped","slip","slips","slipped","put","puts","place","places","placed","mix","mixes","mixed","dissolve","dissolves","dissolved","spike","spikes","spiked","lace","laces","laced","give","gives","gave","hand","hands","handed","transform","transforms","transformed","change","changes","changed","become","becomes","became","grow","grows","grew","sprout","sprouts","sprouted","remove","removes","removed","cleanse","cleanses","cleansed","exorcise","exorcises","exorcised","birth","births","delivers","delivered"];

function BTT_fragmentText(text){
  /* Fragmentation carries quote state directly. Opening/closing quote marks are
     structural boundaries, so quoted dialogue can never inherit narration-level
     actuality merely because the fragment starts on the quote character. */
  text=String(text||"");var out=[],start=0,inQuote=false;
  function push(end,quoted){var raw=BTT_cleanSpace(text.slice(start,end));if(raw)out.push({raw:raw,start:start,end:end,quoted:!!quoted});start=end;}
  for(var i=0;i<text.length;i++){
    var c=text.charAt(i),isQuote=(c==='"'||c==='“'||c==='”');
    if(isQuote){
      if(!inQuote){if(i>start)push(i,false);start=i+1;inQuote=true;}
      else{if(i>start)push(i,true);start=i+1;inQuote=false;}
      continue;
    }
    var boundary=(c==='\n'||c==='.'||c==='!'||c==='?'||c===';'||c==='>');
    if(boundary){push(i+1,inQuote);}
  }
  if(start<text.length)push(text.length,inQuote);if(!out.length&&BTT_cleanSpace(text))out.push({raw:BTT_cleanSpace(text),start:0,end:text.length,quoted:false});return out;
}
function BTT_quoteAt(text,index){var inQuote=false;for(var i=0;i<Math.min(index,text.length);i++){var c=text.charAt(i);if(c==='"'||c==='“'||c==='”')inQuote=!inQuote;}return inQuote;}
function BTT_eventStatus(raw,quoted){
  var t=BTT_lower(raw),mode="ACTUAL",actual=true,confidence=1.0,reason="asserted_event";
  if(quoted){mode="SPEECH";actual=false;confidence=0.20;reason="quoted_speech";}
  /* Epistemic uncertainty is not an actual causal event. This shared gate keeps
     "I think/suspect I've been infected" from committing Conditions, product
     administration, or Developable Risk acquisition before confirmation. */
  if(/\b(?:think|believe|suspect|guess|wonder|fear)\b[^.!?]{0,75}\b(?:been|is|are|am|was|were|has|have|had|might|may|could|possibly|perhaps|seem|appear)\b/i.test(t)||/\b(?:seems?|appears?)\s+(?:to\s+be|to\s+have|like)\b/i.test(t)){mode="UNCERTAIN";actual=false;confidence=Math.min(confidence,0.42);reason="epistemic_uncertainty";}
  if(/\b(?:if|would|could|might|may|maybe|perhaps|suppose|imagine|hypothetical|as if|pretend)\b/i.test(t)){mode="HYPOTHETICAL";actual=false;confidence=Math.min(confidence,0.28);reason="hypothetical_or_modal";}
  if(/\b(?:wait(?:s|ed|ing)?\s+for|ready\s+for|prepare(?:s|d|ing)?\s+for)\b[^.!?]{0,90}\bto\b/i.test(t)||/\b(?:will|going to|about to|plan to|plans to|intend to|intends to|later|next time|tomorrow)\b/i.test(t)){mode="FUTURE";actual=false;confidence=Math.min(confidence,0.22);reason="future_or_anticipated";}
  if(/\b(?:want(?:s|ed)?|need(?:s|ed)?|crave(?:s|d)?|wish(?:es|ed)?|hope(?:s|d)?|beg(?:s|ged)?|ask(?:s|ed)?)\b[^.!?]{0,45}\b(?:to|for)\b/i.test(t)){mode="DESIRED";actual=false;confidence=Math.min(confidence,0.24);reason="desire_or_request";}
  if(/\b(?:think(?:s|ing)? about|talk(?:s|ed|ing)? about|discuss(?:es|ed|ing)?|mention(?:s|ed|ing)?|warn(?:s|ed|ing)?(?:\s+[^.!?]{0,30})?\babout|explain(?:s|ed|ing)?(?:\s+[^.!?]{0,30})?\babout|describe(?:s|d|ing)?|fantasiz(?:e|es|ed|ing)|dream(?:s|ed|ing)? about)\b/i.test(t)){mode="DISCUSSED";actual=false;confidence=Math.min(confidence,0.24);reason="discussion_or_reference";}
  return {mode:mode,actual:actual,confidence:BTT_clamp(confidence,0,1),reason:reason};
}
function BTT_actuality(raw,quoted){
  var t=BTT_lower(raw),a=0.90,status=BTT_eventStatus(raw,quoted);if(quoted)a-=0.20;if(/\b(?:not|never|didn't|doesn't|don't|cannot|can't|won't|wouldn't)\b/i.test(t))a-=0.35;if(/\b(?:if|would|could|might|may|maybe|perhaps|suppose|imagine|hypothetical|as if|pretend)\b/i.test(t))a-=0.28;if(/\b(?:will|going to|plan to|intend to|want to|wants to|need to|needs to|should|must|try to|tries to|about to)\b/i.test(t))a-=0.18;if(/\b(?:already|now|currently|has|have|had|did|was|were|began|started|finished|completed|successfully)\b/i.test(t))a+=0.08;if(!status.actual)a=Math.min(a,status.confidence);return BTT_clamp(a,0,1);
}
function BTT_temporalStatus(raw){var t=BTT_lower(raw);if(/\b(?:wait(?:s|ed|ing)?\s+for|will|going to|about to|later|tomorrow|next time|plan to|intend to|want to|needs? to|should|must)\b/.test(t))return "FUTURE";if(/\b(?:used to|previously|earlier|yesterday|last week|had been)\b/.test(t))return "PAST";return "CURRENT";}
function BTT_stateAssertionConfidence(raw){var t=BTT_lower(raw),s=0.55;if(/\b(?:is|are|am|has|have|now|currently|remains|became|becomes)\b/.test(t))s+=0.25;if(/\bto be\s+[a-z-]+/.test(t))s-=0.30;if(/\b(?:seems|appears|maybe|might|could|possibly)\b/.test(t))s-=0.18;return BTT_clamp(s,0,1);}
function BTT_completionConfidence(raw){
  var t=BTT_lower(raw),c=0.55;
  if(/\b(?:swallow(?:s|ed|ing)?|drink(?:s|ing)?|drank|drunk|down(?:s|ed|ing)?|sip(?:s|ped|ping)?|gulp(?:s|ed|ing)?|ate|eats?|eating|injected|applied|finished|completed|inside|into|within|buried|implanted|laid|deposited|filled|delivered|gave birth)\b/.test(t)||/\btake(?:s|n|ing)?\b[^.!?]{0,18}\b(?:gulp|sip|drink|pill|capsule|tablet|dose)\b/.test(t))c+=0.32;
  if(/\b(?:inseminated|impregnated|fertilized|ejaculated|climaxed|came|deposited|released)\b/.test(t))c+=0.30;
  /* Finite present-tense event predicates are completed actions in AI Dungeon's
     action/narration grammar. Modality is gated separately by BTT_eventStatus,
     so an infinitive/future/desire phrase can score lexical completion without
     becoming an actual causal event. */
  if(/\b(?:inseminate|inseminates|impregnate|impregnates|fertilize|fertilizes|ejaculate|ejaculates|breed|breeds|deposit|deposits|release|releases|implant|implants|oviposit|oviposits)\b/.test(t))c+=0.25;
  if(/\b(?:finish(?:es|ed|ing)?|climax(?:es|ed|ing)?|came|ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing))\b[^.!?]{0,35}\b(?:inside|into|within)\b/.test(t))c+=0.18;
  if(/\b(?:tries?|attempts?|starts? to|begins? to|almost|nearly|waits? for|waiting for|about to|wants? to|needs? to)\b/.test(t))c-=0.30;return BTT_clamp(c,0,1);
}
function BTT_detectPlayerName(text){var m=String(text||"").match(/\bYou\s+are\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’\-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\-]+){0,2})\s*,/);return m?BTT_safeName(m[1]):"";}
function BTT_entityAliases(entity,source){var m=BTT_findEntity(entity),a=[];if(m&&m.identity)a=a.concat(m.identity.aliases||[]);if(source)a=a.concat(source.aliases||[]);if(entity==="Player")a=a.concat(["you","your","yourself"]);return BTT_unique(a);}
function BTT_entityMentionScore(raw,entity,aliases,quoted,addressee){
  var score=0,reasons=[],lower=BTT_lower(raw);for(var i=0;i<aliases.length;i++){var a=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[i]);if(!a)continue;if(entity==="Player"&&/^(?:you|your|yourself)$/i.test(a)){if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,a)){var v=quoted&&addressee&&addressee!=="Player"?0.05:0.92;if(v>score){score=v;reasons=[a+":"+v.toFixed(2)];}}}else if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,a)){var x=a.indexOf(" ")>=0?1.00:0.94;if(x>score){score=x;reasons=[a+":"+x.toFixed(2)];}}}
  return {score:score,reasons:reasons};
}
/*
 * Dialogue speaker attribution is evidence-based rather than name-only. AI
 * Dungeon frequently tags speech with pronouns ("she whispers") or splits a
 * single speaker across adjacent quote fragments. Explicit named/Player tags
 * remain strongest; compatible pronoun tags use recent discourse to separate
 * otherwise ambiguous entities; short adjacent quotes may inherit the last
 * resolved speaker. This resolver only assigns a speaker when the evidence is
 * strong enough to avoid arbitrary cross-entity ownership.
 */
var BTT_SPEECH_VERB_RE="(?:say|says|said|ask|asks|asked|tell|tells|told|reply|replies|replied|answer|answers|answered|murmur|murmurs|murmured|whisper|whispers|whispered|shout|shouts|shouted|command|commands|commanded|breathe|breathes|breathed|gasp|gasps|gasped|moan|moans|moaned|sigh|sighs|sighed|admit|admits|admitted|declare|declares|declared|insist|insists|insisted|plead|pleads|pleaded)";
function BTT_speakerPronounSet(p){p=BTT_lower(p);if(/^(?:he|him|his)$/.test(p))return "he/him/his";if(/^(?:she|her|hers)$/.test(p))return "she/her/her";if(/^(?:they|them|their|theirs)$/.test(p))return "they/them/their";return "";}
function BTT_entitySpeakerPronounCompatible(entity,pronoun){var want=BTT_speakerPronounSet(pronoun),m=BTT_findEntity(entity);if(!want||!m||!m.identity)return false;var current=BTT_pronounSet(m.identity.pronouns||"");if(current===want)return true;return Number(m.identity.pronounGrace||0)>0&&BTT_pronounSet(m.identity.previousPronouns||"")===want;}
function BTT_resolvePronounSpeaker(pronoun,sources,recentSpeaker,recentNPC){var compatible=[];for(var i=0;i<sources.length;i++)if(BTT_entitySpeakerPronounCompatible(sources[i].entity,pronoun))compatible.push(sources[i].entity);if(recentSpeaker&&compatible.indexOf(recentSpeaker)>=0)return {entity:recentSpeaker,confidence:0.96,reason:"pronoun_tag_recent_speaker"};if(recentNPC&&compatible.indexOf(recentNPC)>=0)return {entity:recentNPC,confidence:0.92,reason:"pronoun_tag_recent_npc"};if(compatible.length===1)return {entity:compatible[0],confidence:0.88,reason:"pronoun_tag_unique_match"};return {entity:"",confidence:0,reason:"pronoun_tag_ambiguous"};}
function BTT_findSpeakerForRange(text,start,end,sources,recentSpeaker,recentNPC){
  text=String(text||"");var beforeEnd=(start>0&&/["“”]/.test(text.charAt(start-1)))?start-1:start,afterStart=(end<text.length&&/["“”]/.test(text.charAt(end)))?end+1:end,before=text.slice(Math.max(0,beforeEnd-190),beforeEnd),after=text.slice(afterStart,Math.min(text.length,afterStart+190)),i,a,aliases,alias,esc,re;
  if(new RegExp("\\byou\\s+"+BTT_SPEECH_VERB_RE+"\\b[^.!?]{0,28}[.!?,:]?\\s*$","i").test(before)||new RegExp("^[\\s,;:—-]{0,18}you\\s+"+BTT_SPEECH_VERB_RE+"\\b","i").test(after))return {entity:"Player",confidence:1.0,reason:"explicit_player_speech_tag"};
  for(i=0;i<sources.length;i++){aliases=sources[i].aliases||[];for(a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){alias=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[a]);if(!alias||/^(?:you|your|yourself)$/i.test(alias))continue;esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(alias).replace(/\ /g,"\\s+");re=new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"\\b\\s+"+BTT_SPEECH_VERB_RE+"\\b[^.!?]{0,28}[.!?,:]?\\s*$","i");if(re.test(before))return {entity:sources[i].entity,confidence:1.0,reason:"explicit_named_speech_tag_before"};re=new RegExp("^[\\s,;:—-]{0,18}\\b"+esc+"\\b\\s+"+BTT_SPEECH_VERB_RE+"\\b","i");if(re.test(after))return {entity:sources[i].entity,confidence:1.0,reason:"explicit_named_speech_tag_after"};}}
  var pm=before.match(new RegExp("\\b(he|she|they)\\s+"+BTT_SPEECH_VERB_RE+"\\b[^.!?]{0,28}[.!?,:]?\\s*$","i"));if(pm){var pr=BTT_resolvePronounSpeaker(pm[1],sources,recentSpeaker,recentNPC);if(pr.entity)return pr;}
  pm=after.match(new RegExp("^[\\s,;:—-]{0,18}(he|she|they)\\s+"+BTT_SPEECH_VERB_RE+"\\b","i"));if(pm){var pr2=BTT_resolvePronounSpeaker(pm[1],sources,recentSpeaker,recentNPC);if(pr2.entity)return pr2;}
  return {entity:"",confidence:0,reason:"no_speaker_tag"};
}
function BTT_findAddressee(raw,speaker,sources){
  var t=String(raw||"");for(var i=0;i<sources.length;i++){if(sources[i].entity===speaker)continue;var aliases=sources[i].aliases||[];for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){var al=aliases[a];if(new RegExp("(?:^|[,!?:]\\s*)"+BTT_escapeRegExp(al).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"(?:[,!?:]|$)","i").test(t)||new RegExp("\\b(?:tell|ask|command|say to)\\s+"+BTT_escapeRegExp(al),"i").test(t))return sources[i].entity;}}
  return "";
}
function BTT_subjectHypothesis(raw,mentions,speaker,speakerConfidence,quoted,addressee){
  var lower=BTT_lower(raw),best={entity:"",score:0,reasons:[]};if(/\b(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower)){var e=quoted&&addressee?addressee:"Player",s=quoted&&addressee?0.90:0.95;if(s>best.score)best={entity:e,score:s,reasons:["second_person_subject"]};}
  for(var i=0;i<mentions.length;i++){var m=mentions[i],name=m.alias;if(!name||/^(?:you|your|yourself)$/i.test(name))continue;var re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(name).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"(?:'s)?\\s+(?:"+BTT_EVENT_VERBS.map(BTT_escapeRegExp).join("|")+")\\b","i");if(re.test(raw)&&m.score+0.05>best.score)best={entity:m.entity,score:BTT_clamp(m.score+0.05,0,1),reasons:["named_subject"]};}
  if(!best.entity&&quoted&&speaker)best={entity:speaker,score:BTT_clamp(Number(speakerConfidence)||0.55,0.55,1),reasons:["quote_speaker_fallback"]};return best;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   SHARED EVIDENCE FRAME BUILDER
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   One fragment receives one lexical feature frame. Entity-specific views then
   combine that frame with ownership, discourse continuity, neighboring windows,
   actuality, chronology, and profile availability. The frame is evidence only:
   no slot, trait, axis, identity, product, or Condition can commit here.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function BTT_sharedChangeEvidence(raw){
  var score=0,reasons=[];for(var i=0;i<BTT_SHARED_CHANGE_PATTERNS.length;i++){var p=BTT_SHARED_CHANGE_PATTERNS[i];if(p.re.test(String(raw||""))){score+=Number(p.score)||0;reasons.push(p.reason);}}
  return {score:BTT_clamp(score,-0.4,1.35),reasons:BTT_unique(reasons)};
}
function BTT_sharedBaseSlotEvidence(raw){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),lower=BTT_lower(raw),channels={},reasons={},scores={},slot,i;
  function ensure(sl){if(!channels[sl])channels[sl]={mention:0,location:0,weak:0,bonus:0,penalty:0};if(!reasons[sl])reasons[sl]=[];return channels[sl];}
  function maxc(sl,ch,v,why){var c=ensure(sl);v=Number(v)||0;if(v>Number(c[ch]||0))c[ch]=v;if(why)reasons[sl].push(why);}
  for(i=0;i<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;i++)ensure(BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[i]);
  for(slot in BTT_SLOT_TERMS)if(BTT_SLOT_TERMS.hasOwnProperty(slot)){var terms=BTT_SLOT_TERMS[slot],best="";for(var t=0;t<terms.length;t++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,terms[t])&&terms[t].length>best.length)best=terms[t];if(best)maxc(slot,"mention",cfg.slotMention,"mention:"+best);}
  function locations(table){for(var loc in table)if(table.hasOwnProperty(loc)&&BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,loc)){var map=table[loc];for(var sl in map)if(map.hasOwnProperty(sl))maxc(sl,"location",map[sl],"location:"+loc);}}
  locations(BTT_LOCATION_EVIDENCE);locations(BTT_SHARED_LOCATION_EVIDENCE);
  for(var weak in BTT_WEAK_SLOT_EVIDENCE)if(BTT_WEAK_SLOT_EVIDENCE.hasOwnProperty(weak)&&BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,weak)){var wm=BTT_WEAK_SLOT_EVIDENCE[weak];for(var ws in wm)if(wm.hasOwnProperty(ws))maxc(ws,"weak",Math.max(cfg.slotWeak,wm[ws]),"weak:"+weak);}
  if(/\b(?:tailbone|coccyx|base of (?:your|his|her|their) spine|lower spine)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:tail|appendage|extension|growth|sprout|emerge|form)\b/i.test(raw)){channels.tail.bonus=Math.max(channels.tail.bonus,cfg.tail_spine_appendage_bonus);reasons.tail.push("bonus:spine_appendage");}
  if(/\b(?:from|out of|emerging from|growing from)\b[^.!?]{0,45}\b(?:ass|butt|rear|cleft|cheeks?)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:tail|appendage|extension|growth|sprout|emerge|form)\b/i.test(raw)){channels.tail.bonus=Math.max(channels.tail.bonus,cfg.tail_from_ass_bonus);reasons.tail.push("bonus:posterior_origin");}
  if(/\b(?:inside|within|into|out of)\b[^.!?]{0,35}\b(?:ass|butt|anus|rear)\b/i.test(raw)&&/\b(?:tail|appendage|parasite|object|thing|growth)\b/i.test(raw)){channels.ass.penalty=Math.max(channels.ass.penalty,cfg.ass_container_penalty);reasons.ass.push("penalty:container_not_target");}
  for(slot in channels)if(channels.hasOwnProperty(slot)){var c=channels[slot];scores[slot]=Math.max(0,c.mention+c.location+c.weak+c.bonus-c.penalty);}
  return {channels:channels,reasons:reasons,scores:scores};
}
function BTT_sharedNearestRegexDistance(raw,reA,reB){
  raw=String(raw||"");function pos(re){var flags="g"+(re.ignoreCase?"i":"")+(re.multiline?"m":""),r=new RegExp(re.source,flags),out=[],m;while((m=r.exec(raw))){out.push(m.index);if(!m[0].length)r.lastIndex++;}return out;}var a=pos(reA),b=pos(reB),best=9999;for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)for(var j=0;j<b.length;j++)best=Math.min(best,Math.abs(a[i]-b[j]));return best;
}
function BTT_sharedAxisEvidence(raw){
  var candidates=[];function add(axis,delta,confidence,reason){candidates.push({axis:axis,delta:delta,confidence:confidence,reason:reason});}
  for(var i=0;i<BTT_SHARED_AXIS_FEATURES.length;i++){var p=BTT_SHARED_AXIS_FEATURES[i];if(p.re.test(String(raw||"")))add(p.axis,p.delta,p.confidence,p.reason);}
  /* Regional anchors and morphology are separate feature channels. Direction is
     associated by nearest distance rather than by a fixed phrase template. */
  var t=BTT_lower(raw),posterior=/\b(?:pelvis|hips?|hip bones|rear|ass|butt)\b/i,grow=/\b(?:widen|widens|widened|widening|spread|spreads|spreading|splay|splays|splayed|splaying|round|rounds|rounded|rounding|expand|expands|expanded|expanding)\b/i,reduce=/\b(?:narrow|narrows|narrowed|narrowing|flatten|flattens|flattened|flattening|shrink|shrinks|shrunk|shrinking)\b/i;
