  var dg=BTT_sharedNearestRegexDistance(t,posterior,grow),dr=BTT_sharedNearestRegexDistance(t,posterior,reduce);if(dg<=72&&dg<=dr){add("ass_size",5,0.82,"regional posterior expansion");add("fem",1.4,0.68,"regional posterior presentation");}else if(dr<=72)add("ass_size",-4,0.76,"regional posterior reduction");
  if(BTT_sharedNearestRegexDistance(t,/\b(?:chest|breasts?|breast tissue|pecs)\b/i,/\b(?:fullness|heavy|weight|bloom|blooms|blooming|bud|buds|budding|swell|swells|swelling|grow|grows|growing|expand|expands|expanding)\b/i)<=68)add("breast_size",5,0.82,"regional chest growth");
  if(BTT_sharedNearestRegexDistance(t,/\b(?:shoulders?|upper body|frame)\b/i,/\b(?:narrow|narrows|narrowing|soften|softens|softening|slender|graceful)\b/i)<=62)add("fem",2.4,0.74,"upper-body feminization");
  if(BTT_sharedNearestRegexDistance(t,/\b(?:hands?|fingers?|palms?)\b/i,/\b(?:narrow|narrows|narrowing|taper|tapers|tapering|delicate|dainty|slender|soften|softening)\b/i)<=62)add("fem",1.8,0.66,"extremity feminization");
  /* MAX-merge same-axis/same-direction cues inside one fragment. Repeated or
     overlapping synonyms corroborate confidence but do not multiply magnitude. */
  var best={};for(i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){var c=candidates[i],sign=c.delta>=0?"+":"-",key=c.axis+"|"+sign,authority=Math.abs(c.delta)*c.confidence;if(!best[key]||authority>best[key].authority)best[key]={authority:authority,row:c};else if(c.confidence>best[key].row.confidence)best[key].row.confidence=c.confidence;}
  var out=[];for(var k in best)if(best.hasOwnProperty(k))out.push(best[k].row);return out;
}
function BTT_traitLocalSlots(raw,trait){
  raw=String(raw||"");var lower=BTT_lower(raw),surface=BTT_lower(String(trait||"")).replace(/-/g," "),positions=[],m,re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(surface).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig");while((m=re.exec(lower))){positions.push(m.index);if(!m[0].length)re.lastIndex++;}if(!positions.length)return [];
  var rows=[];for(var slot in BTT_SLOT_TERMS)if(BTT_SLOT_TERMS.hasOwnProperty(slot)&&slot!=="misc"){var terms=BTT_SLOT_TERMS[slot];for(var t=0;t<terms.length;t++){var rr=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(terms[t]).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"\\b","ig"),sm;while((sm=rr.exec(lower))){for(var p=0;p<positions.length;p++){var d=Math.abs(sm.index-positions[p]);if(d<=34){var between=lower.slice(Math.min(sm.index,positions[p]),Math.max(sm.index,positions[p]));var objectOnly=sm.index>positions[p]&&/\b(?:around|against|over|across|onto|into|inside|within)\b/.test(between);rows.push({slot:slot,distance:d,objectOnly:objectOnly});}}if(!sm[0].length)rr.lastIndex++;}}}
  rows=rows.filter(function(x){return !x.objectOnly;});if(!rows.length)return [];rows.sort(function(a,b){return a.distance-b.distance;});var best=rows[0].distance,out=[];for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(rows[i].distance<=best+7&&out.indexOf(rows[i].slot)<0)out.push(rows[i].slot);return out;
}
function BTT_traitExplicitOwner(parse,f,traitEv){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),surface=BTT_escapeRegExp(String(traitEv&&traitEv.trait||"").replace(/-/g," ")).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+"),entities=["Player"],i;if(parse&&parse.admitted)for(i=0;i<parse.admitted.length;i++)entities.push(parse.admitted[i].entity);
  if(f&&f.quoted){if(f.speaker&&new RegExp("\\bmy\\b[^.!?]{0,34}\\b"+surface+"\\b","i").test(raw))return f.speaker;if(f.addressee&&new RegExp("\\byour\\b[^.!?]{0,34}\\b"+surface+"\\b","i").test(raw))return f.addressee;}
  else if(new RegExp("\\b(?:your|my)\\b[^.!?]{0,34}\\b"+surface+"\\b","i").test(raw)||new RegExp("\\b"+surface+"\\b[^.!?]{0,20}\\b(?:your|my)\\s+(?:head|face|arms?|forearms?|legs?|thighs?|breasts?|chest|gut|belly|penis|cock|vagina|pussy|ass|anus|tail|wings?)\\b","i").test(raw))return "Player";
  for(i=0;i<entities.length;i++){var e=entities[i];if(e==="Player")continue;var aliases=BTT_entityAliases(e);for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){var al=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[a]);if(!al||/^(?:he|she|they|him|her|them|his|their)$/i.test(al))continue;var esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(al).replace(/\ /g,"\\s+");if(new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"(?:['’]s)?\\b[^.!?]{0,34}\\b"+surface+"\\b","i").test(raw))return e;}}
  var pron=(f&&f.mentions||[]).filter(function(x){return x.alias==="pronoun"&&x.score>=0.50;});if(pron.length===1&&new RegExp("\\b(?:his|her|their)\\b[^.!?]{0,28}\\b"+surface+"\\b","i").test(raw))return pron[0].entity;return "";
}
/**
 * OWNER-BOUND OBSERVATION ROUTER
 * ------------------------------
 * Shared lexical parsing intentionally remains clause-based because actuality,
 * chronology, grammar, and causal language are useful single-pass features.
 * Canonical consumers do not receive those lexical features directly. Each
 * physical/profile/axis observation must first resolve to one entity and, when
 * applicable, one anatomical slot. Ambiguous ties are dropped here rather than
 * copied into several entity frames and allowed to accumulate independently.
 */
function BTT_sharedEntityList(parse){
  var out=[],seen={};function add(e){e=String(e||"");if(!e||seen[e])return;seen[e]=1;out.push(e);}add("Player");
  for(var i=0;i<(parse&&parse.sources||[]).length;i++)add(parse.sources[i].entity);
  for(i=0;i<(parse&&parse.admitted||[]).length;i++)add(parse.admitted[i].entity);
  return out;
}
function BTT_fragmentEntityEvidenceScore(f,entity){
  var score=0,reasons=[];for(var i=0;i<(f&&f.mentions||[]).length;i++){var m=f.mentions[i];if(m.entity!==entity)continue;score=Math.max(score,Number(m.score)||0);reasons.push("mention:"+(m.alias||"entity"));}
  if(f&&f.subject===entity){score=Math.max(score,Number(f.subjectConfidence)||0);reasons.push("subject");}
  if(f&&f.quoted){if(f.speaker===entity&&/\b(?:my|mine|I|me)\b/i.test(f.raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.94);reasons.push("speaker_first_person");}if(f.addressee===entity&&/\b(?:your|yours|you)\b/i.test(f.raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.90);reasons.push("quoted_addressee");}}
  else if(entity==="Player"&&/\b(?:your|you|yourself|my|myself)\b/i.test(String(f&&f.raw||""))){score=Math.max(score,0.88);reasons.push("player_second_person");}
  if(entity==="Player"&&f&&f.phase==="input"&&!f.quoted&&/\b(?:I|me|my|mine|myself)\b/i.test(String(f.raw||""))){score=Math.max(score,0.96);reasons.push("input_first_person_player");}
  var aliases=BTT_entityAliases(entity);for(i=0;i<aliases.length;i++){var a=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[i]);if(!a||/^(?:you|your|yourself|he|she|they|him|her|them|his|their)$/i.test(a))continue;var esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(a).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");if(new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"(?:['’]s)?\\b","i").test(String(f&&f.raw||""))){score=Math.max(score,0.93);reasons.push("named_entity");break;}}
  return {entity:entity,score:BTT_clamp(score,0,1.08),reasons:reasons};
}
function BTT_pickResolvedObservation(rows,minScore,minMargin){
  rows=(rows||[]).filter(function(x){return x&&x.entity&&Number(x.score)>0;});rows.sort(function(a,b){return Number(b.score)-Number(a.score);});if(!rows.length)return {entity:"",confidence:0,margin:0,reason:"unresolved"};
  var top=rows[0],next=rows[1]||{score:0},margin=Number(top.score)-Number(next.score);if(Number(top.score)<Number(minScore||0.62)||margin<Number(minMargin||0.10))return {entity:"",confidence:Number(top.score)||0,margin:BTT_round(margin,3),reason:"owner_ambiguous",runner:next.entity||""};
  return {entity:top.entity,confidence:BTT_clamp(Number(top.score)||0,0,1.08),margin:BTT_round(margin,3),reason:top.reason||"resolved",slotConfidence:Number(top.slotConfidence)||0};
}
function BTT_resolveGenericObservationOwner(parse,f){
  var entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse),rows=[];for(var i=0;i<entities.length;i++){var r=BTT_fragmentEntityEvidenceScore(f,entities[i]);rows.push({entity:r.entity,score:r.score,reason:(r.reasons||[]).join(",")||"entity_evidence"});}
  var direct=BTT_pickResolvedObservation(rows,0.62,0.12);if(direct.entity)return direct;
  /* A clause may omit the subject after a preceding/following clause established
     it. Clause continuity is supporting evidence only: it must still produce one
     winner with a margin, and any explicit competing participant blocks borrowing. */
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),support={};for(var d=1;d<=cfg.clause_window;d++){for(var side=-1;side<=1;side+=2){var ni=Number(f.index)+side*d;if(ni<0||ni>=parse.fragments.length)continue;var nf=parse.fragments[ni];for(i=0;i<entities.length;i++){var e=entities[i],nr=BTT_fragmentEntityEvidenceScore(nf,e);if(nr.score<0.78)continue;if(BTT_fragmentHasCompetingEntity(nf,e)&&nr.score<0.94)continue;var weighted=nr.score*Math.pow(0.76,d);if(!support[e]||weighted>support[e].score)support[e]={entity:e,score:weighted,reason:"clause_owner_continuity:C"+ni};}}}
  var continuity=[];for(var e in support)if(support.hasOwnProperty(e))continuity.push(support[e]);return BTT_pickResolvedObservation(continuity,0.55,0.12);
}
function BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,slot){
  slot=BTT_lower(slot);var entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse),explicit=[],fallback=[];
  for(var i=0;i<entities.length;i++){var e=entities[i],owned=BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,f.index,e),best=null;for(var j=0;j<owned.length;j++)if(owned[j].slot===slot&&(!best||owned[j].confidence>best.confidence))best=owned[j];if(best)explicit.push({entity:e,score:Number(best.confidence)||0,slotConfidence:Number(best.confidence)||0,reason:best.reason||"explicit_owned_slot"});var g=BTT_fragmentEntityEvidenceScore(f,e);fallback.push({entity:e,score:g.score,slotConfidence:0,reason:(g.reasons||[]).join(",")||"entity_evidence"});}
  /* Once any character owns this slot explicitly, clause-level subject/focus is
     not allowed to create a competing owner for the same anatomical observation. */
  if(explicit.length)return BTT_pickResolvedObservation(explicit,0.82,0.08);
  var base=f&&f.shared&&f.shared.slotBase&&f.shared.slotBase.scores?Number(f.shared.slotBase.scores[slot])||0:0;if(base<0.08)return {entity:"",confidence:0,margin:0,reason:"slot_not_anchored"};
  for(i=0;i<fallback.length;i++)fallback[i].score=BTT_clamp(fallback[i].score+Math.min(0.10,base*0.16),0,1.08);
  return BTT_pickResolvedObservation(fallback,0.66,0.12);
}
function BTT_bindTraitObservation(parse,f,traitEv){
  var ev=BTT_clone(traitEv||{}),tr=ev.trait||"",explicitOwner=BTT_traitExplicitOwner(parse,f,ev),candidates=[],localSlots=BTT_traitLocalSlots(f.raw,tr);
  if(BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS[tr])candidates=["head"];
  else if(ev.slots&&ev.slots.length){
    /* Shared semantic feature tables describe which slots a trait CAN inhabit;
       local lexical anchors decide which slot this observation actually describes.
       When both exist, intersect them so a broad feature such as `slick` cannot
       fan out from a tail into a nearby ass/penis/vagina merely because all four
       are valid trait domains in the abstract. */
    var allowed=ev.slots.slice();candidates=localSlots.length?localSlots.filter(function(x){return allowed.indexOf(x)>=0;}):allowed;
  }else candidates=localSlots;
  candidates=BTT_unique(candidates);if(!candidates.length&&ev.allowDominant!==false){var pairs=[],scores=f.shared&&f.shared.slotBase&&f.shared.slotBase.scores||{};for(var s in scores)if(scores.hasOwnProperty(s)&&s!=="misc"&&Number(scores[s])>=0.12)pairs.push({slot:s,score:Number(scores[s])});pairs.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});if(pairs.length)candidates=[pairs[0].slot];}
  if(!candidates.length)return null;var rows=[],baseScores=f.shared&&f.shared.slotBase&&f.shared.slotBase.scores||{};
  for(var i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){var slot=candidates[i],own=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,slot);if(!own.entity)continue;if(explicitOwner&&own.entity!==explicitOwner)continue;var base=Number(baseScores[slot])||0,score=Number(own.confidence)||0;if(BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS[tr]&&slot==="head"&&base<0.08)score+=0.04;else score+=Math.min(0.22,base*0.36);rows.push({entity:own.entity,slot:slot,score:score,ownerConfidence:own.confidence,ownerMargin:own.margin,slotConfidence:Math.max(Number(own.slotConfidence)||0,base),reason:own.reason});}
  rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});if(!rows.length)return null;var top=rows[0],next=rows[1]||{score:0};if(top.score<0.68||(top.score-next.score)<0.10)return null;
  ev.boundOwner=top.entity;ev.boundSlot=top.slot;ev.ownerConfidence=BTT_round(top.ownerConfidence,3);ev.ownerMargin=BTT_round(top.ownerMargin,3);ev.slotConfidence=BTT_round(top.slotConfidence,3);ev.bindingReason=top.reason||"owner_slot_resolved";return ev;
}
function BTT_axisAnchorSlots(axis){var m={breast_size:["breasts"],ass_size:["ass","legs"],cock_size:["penis"],gut_size:["gut"],fertility:["vagina"],virility:["penis"],fem:["head","arms","legs","breasts","gut","penis","vagina","ass","tail","wings"]};return m[axis]||[];}
function BTT_axisAnchorSlot(axis){var a=BTT_axisAnchorSlots(axis);return a.length?a[0]:"";}
function BTT_bindAxisObservation(parse,f,axisEv){
  var ev=BTT_clone(axisEv||{}),anchors=BTT_axisAnchorSlots(ev.axis),scores=f.shared&&f.shared.slotBase&&f.shared.slotBase.scores||{},rows=[];
  /* Numeric deltas are observations too. Resolve them from an explicitly owned
     anatomical anchor when one exists (e.g. "Leandra's hips widen") before any
     fragment-level owner fallback is considered. This prevents a nearby observer
     from inheriting another character's axis change. */
  for(var ai=0;ai<anchors.length;ai++){var slot=anchors[ai],base=Number(scores[slot])||0,owned=[];var entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse);for(var ei=0;ei<entities.length;ei++){var oe=BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,f.index,entities[ei]);for(var oi=0;oi<oe.length;oi++)if(oe[oi].slot===slot)owned.push({entity:entities[ei],slot:slot,score:Number(oe[oi].confidence)||0,base:base,reason:oe[oi].reason});}if(owned.length){owned.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});var top=owned[0],next=owned[1]||{score:0};if(top.score>=0.82&&top.score-next.score>=0.08)rows.push({entity:top.entity,slot:slot,score:top.score+Math.min(0.10,base*0.20),confidence:top.score,margin:top.score-next.score,reason:top.reason});}else if(base>=0.08){var so=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,slot);if(so.entity)rows.push({entity:so.entity,slot:slot,score:Number(so.confidence)+Math.min(0.08,base*0.16),confidence:so.confidence,margin:so.margin,reason:so.reason});}}
  rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});var best=rows[0],runner=rows[1]||{score:0};if(best&&best.score-runner.score>=0.08){ev.boundOwner=best.entity;ev.boundSlot=best.slot;ev.ownerConfidence=BTT_round(best.confidence,3);ev.ownerMargin=BTT_round(best.margin,3);ev.slotConfidence=BTT_round(Number(scores[best.slot])||0,3);ev.bindingReason=best.reason||"owned_axis_anchor";return ev;}
  var own=BTT_resolveGenericObservationOwner(parse,f);if(!own.entity)return null;ev.boundOwner=own.entity;ev.boundSlot="";ev.ownerConfidence=BTT_round(own.confidence,3);ev.ownerMargin=BTT_round(own.margin,3);ev.slotConfidence=0;ev.bindingReason=own.reason||"owner_resolved";return ev;
}
function BTT_bindProfileReference(parse,f,ref){
  var ev=BTT_clone(ref||{}),slots=BTT_traitLocalSlots(f.raw,ev.alias||ev.id),best=null;if(slots.length){for(var i=0;i<slots.length;i++){var own=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,slots[i]);if(!own.entity)continue;var base=Number(f.shared&&f.shared.slotBase&&f.shared.slotBase.scores&&f.shared.slotBase.scores[slots[i]])||0,row={entity:own.entity,slot:slots[i],score:Number(own.confidence)+Math.min(0.18,base*0.30),owner:own};if(!best||row.score>best.score)best=row;}}
  if(best){ev.boundOwner=best.entity;ev.boundSlot=best.slot;ev.ownerConfidence=BTT_round(best.owner.confidence,3);ev.ownerMargin=BTT_round(best.owner.margin,3);ev.slotConfidence=BTT_round(Math.max(best.owner.slotConfidence||0,Number(f.shared.slotBase.scores[best.slot])||0),3);return ev;}
  var generic=BTT_resolveGenericObservationOwner(parse,f);if(!generic.entity)return null;ev.boundOwner=generic.entity;ev.boundSlot="";ev.ownerConfidence=BTT_round(generic.confidence,3);ev.ownerMargin=BTT_round(generic.margin,3);return ev;
}
function BTT_bindSharedObservations(parse){
  for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){var f=parse.fragments[i],sh=f.shared||{},entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse);sh.boundSlotBase={};sh.boundTraits=[];sh.boundAxes=[];sh.boundProfileRefs={heritage:[],gender:[]};sh.boundStructure={};sh.unresolved=[];
    var base=sh.slotBase&&sh.slotBase.scores||{};for(var slot in base)if(base.hasOwnProperty(slot)&&slot!=="misc"&&Number(base[slot])>0){var so=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,slot);if(so.entity){if(!sh.boundSlotBase[so.entity])sh.boundSlotBase[so.entity]={scores:{},reasons:{}};sh.boundSlotBase[so.entity].scores[slot]=Number(base[slot])||0;sh.boundSlotBase[so.entity].reasons[slot]=(sh.slotBase.reasons&&sh.slotBase.reasons[slot]||[]).concat(["owner:"+so.entity,"owner_conf:"+BTT_round(so.confidence,2)]);}else sh.unresolved.push("slot:"+slot+":"+so.reason);}
    for(var t=0;t<(sh.traits||[]).length;t++){var bt=BTT_bindTraitObservation(parse,f,sh.traits[t]);if(bt)sh.boundTraits.push(bt);else sh.unresolved.push("trait:"+String(sh.traits[t].trait||"?")+":owner_or_slot");}
    for(var a=0;a<(sh.axes||[]).length;a++){var ba=BTT_bindAxisObservation(parse,f,sh.axes[a]);if(ba)sh.boundAxes.push(ba);else sh.unresolved.push("axis:"+String(sh.axes[a].axis||"?")+":owner");}
    for(var kind in sh.profileRefs)if(sh.profileRefs.hasOwnProperty(kind)){for(var r=0;r<(sh.profileRefs[kind]||[]).length;r++){var br=BTT_bindProfileReference(parse,f,sh.profileRefs[kind][r]);if(br)sh.boundProfileRefs[kind].push(br);else sh.unresolved.push("profile:"+kind+":"+String(sh.profileRefs[kind][r].id||"?")+":owner");}}
    for(slot in (sh.structure||{}))if(sh.structure.hasOwnProperty(slot)){var os=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,slot);if(os.entity){var bs=BTT_clone(sh.structure[slot]);bs.boundOwner=os.entity;bs.boundSlot=slot;bs.ownerConfidence=BTT_round(os.confidence,3);bs.ownerMargin=BTT_round(os.margin,3);bs.slotConfidence=BTT_round(Math.max(os.slotConfidence||0,Number(base[slot])||0),3);sh.boundStructure[slot]=bs;}else sh.unresolved.push("structure:"+slot+":"+os.reason);}
    f.shared=sh;
  }return parse;
}
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   CANONICAL ATOMIC OBSERVATION GRAPH
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Every domain consumes the same owner-bound evidence envelope. Lexical extraction
   may be fragment-wide, but an observation is not eligible for accumulation until
   owner/role/anchor resolution has selected one receiver and, when present, one
   source/mechanism. Input and Output therefore differ only by provenance/actuality,
   not by which evidence architecture they use.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function BTT_observationTokens(text){var stop={the:1,a:1,an:1,and:1,or:1,to:1,of:1,in:1,on:1,at:1,for:1,with:1,from:1,that:1,this:1,it:1,is:1,are:1,was:1,were:1,be:1,been:1,being:1,you:1,your:1,i:1,my:1,me:1,he:1,she:1,they:1,his:1,her:1,their:1,as:1,but:1,not:1};var xs=BTT_lower(text).replace(/[^a-z0-9-]+/g," ").split(/\s+/g),out=[],seen={};for(var i=0;i<xs.length;i++){var x=xs[i];if(!x||x.length<3||stop[x]||seen[x])continue;seen[x]=1;out.push(x);}return out;}
function BTT_observationTextSimilarity(a,b){var aa=BTT_observationTokens(a),bb=BTT_observationTokens(b),set={},inter=0;if(!aa.length||!bb.length)return 0;for(var i=0;i<aa.length;i++)set[aa[i]]=1;for(i=0;i<bb.length;i++)if(set[bb[i]])inter++;return BTT_clamp(inter/Math.max(aa.length,bb.length),0,1);}
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   GENERALIZED RESPONSE-CHANNEL HYPOTHESES
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   These are shared canonical-observation features, not Dev-Risk-only flags.
   Each actual owner-side response may support several compatible channels. A
   response event can therefore exist before BTT knows its exact causal source.
   Domain consumers decide how much each channel matters to their own confidence.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function BTT_responseMorphologicalConfidence(raw){
  /* Morphological evidence requires a body/structure change predicate. Ordinary
     orientation/expression language must not become anatomy evidence. */
  var text=String(raw||""),body="(?:body|form|shape|figure|frame|skin|flesh|chest|breasts?|bust|waist|hips?|ass|butt|belly|gut|abdomen|penis|cock|vagina|tail|wings?|arms?|legs?|face|hair|voice|muscles?|bones?|organ|tissue)";
  var change="(?:transform(?:s|ed|ing)?|reshape(?:s|d|ing)?|morph(?:s|ed|ing)?|mutat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|grow(?:s|ing|n)?|enlarge(?:s|d|ing)?|shrink(?:s|ing|shrunk)?|widen(?:s|ed|ing)?|narrow(?:s|ed|ing)?|expand(?:s|ed|ing)?|swell(?:s|ing|ollen)?|flatten(?:s|ed|ing)?|develop(?:s|ed|ing)?|sprout(?:s|ed|ing)?|become(?:s)?|change(?:s|d|ing)?|alter(?:s|ed|ing)?|soften(?:s|ed|ing)?)";
  var direct=new RegExp("\\b(?:your|my|his|her|their|the|[A-Z][a-z]+['’]s)?\\s*"+body+"\\b[^.!?]{0,34}\\b"+change+"\\b","i").test(text);
  var caused=new RegExp("\\b"+change+"\\b[^.!?]{0,34}\\b(?:your|my|his|her|their|the|[A-Z][a-z]+['’]s)?\\s*"+body+"\\b","i").test(text);
  var explicit=/\b(?:new (?:limb|organ|tail|wing)|physical changes?|body changes?|changes? (?:in|to) (?:your|my|his|her|their) (?:body|form|shape|skin|flesh)|takes? on a new (?:shape|form)|(?:body|form|shape|figure|frame|skin|flesh|chest|breasts?|waist|hips?|tail|wings?)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bturn(?:s|ed|ing)?\s+into\b)\b/i.test(text);
  if(!direct&&!caused&&!explicit)return 0;
  if(/\b(?:eyes?|expression|smile|smirk|gaze|features?)\b[^.!?]{0,18}\b(?:soften|widen|narrow|drop|fall|remain)\w*/i.test(text)&&!new RegExp("\\b(?:body|form|shape|skin|flesh|chest|breasts?|waist|hips?|penis|vagina|tail|wings?)\\b","i").test(text))return 0;
  return explicit?0.58:0.46;
}
function BTT_responseAdaptiveConfidence(raw,baseScores){
  /* Adaptive/progressive evidence is contextual. Bare words like "again" or
     "remain" only matter when an actual owner-side response/effect is already
     present. Strong adaptation vocabulary may stand on its own. */
  var text=String(raw||""),t=BTT_lower(text),base=baseScores||{},other=0,k;for(k in base)if(base.hasOwnProperty(k)&&k!=="adaptive")other=Math.max(other,Number(base[k])||0);
  if(/\b(?:adapt\w*|attun\w*|acclimat\w*|sensitiz\w*|tolerance|primed|lowered inhibition|reduced inhibition|less able to resist|harder to resist|reduced resistance|easier to (?:obey|submit|accept)|more automatic|increasingly automatic)\b/i.test(text))return 0.42;
  if(other>=0.30&&/\b(?:again|repeated\w*|each time|every time|over time|increasingly|gradual\w*|progressiv\w*|continued|successive|more strongly|stronger than before|easier|harder to resist)\b/i.test(text))return 0.34;
  if(other>=0.30&&/\b(?:effect|change|response|sensation|urge|compulsion|pain|pleasure|warmth|pressure|craving|trait|behavior|behaviour|reaction)\b[^.!?]{0,28}\b(?:persist\w*|remain\w*|last\w*|linger\w*|residual)\b/i.test(text))return 0.34;
  return 0;
}
function BTT_responseChannelHypotheses(raw){
  var t=BTT_lower(String(raw||"")),scores={},cues={};
  function add(id,val,re,label){if(!re.test(t))return;scores[id]=BTT_clamp((Number(scores[id])||0)+Number(val||0),0,1);cues[id]=cues[id]||[];if(label&&cues[id].indexOf(label)<0)cues[id].push(label);}
  function put(id,val,label){if(!(Number(val)>0))return;scores[id]=BTT_clamp(Math.max(Number(scores[id])||0,Number(val)||0),0,1);cues[id]=cues[id]||[];if(label&&cues[id].indexOf(label)<0)cues[id].push(label);}
  /* Direct experience / cognitive response. */
  add("sensory",0.34,/\b(?:feel|feels|felt|feeling|sense|senses|sensed|sensation|notice|notices|noticed|tingl\w*|numb\w*|sensitive|sensitivity|hypersens\w*|itch\w*|throb\w*|warmth|heat|cold|coolness|pressure)\b/,"direct_sensation");
  add("mental",0.42,/\b(?:compuls\w*|urge|urges|urged|crav\w*|fixat\w*|obsess\w*|intrusive|thoughts?|mind|mental|judg(?:e|ment)|inhibit\w*|instinct\w*|primed|resist\w*|willpower|attention)\b/,"mental_response");
  add("behavioral",0.38,/\b(?:obey\w*|compl(?:y|ies|ied|ying|iance|iant)|submit\w*|yield\w*|resist\w*|hesitat\w*|instinctively|automatically|without thinking|before .* realize|find(?:s)? (?:yourself|himself|herself|themselves)|cannot stop|can't stop|keep(?:s|ing)? .*\b(?:do|return|seek|use|take)|seek\w*|return\w*|avoid\w*)\b/,"behavioral_response");
  add("reward",0.38,/\b(?:pleasur\w*|pleasant|relief|relieved|sooth\w*|comfort\w*|euphor\w*|bliss\w*|reward\w*|satisf\w*|enjoy\w*|good feeling|rightness|calm\w*|relax\w*)\b/,"reward_response");
  add("aversive",0.38,/\b(?:pain\w*|discomfort|uncomfortable|hurt\w*|sting\w*|burn\w*|ache\w*|agony|nause\w*|panic\w*|distress\w*|punish\w*|pressure|constrict\w*|tighten\w*)\b/,"aversive_response");
  add("physiological",0.36,/\b(?:twitch\w*|shiver\w*|trembl\w*|pulse\w*|heartbeat|heart rate|breath\w*|muscles?|relax\w*|tense\w*|fatigue\w*|dizz\w*|hunger|hungry|thirst\w*|sweat\w*|sedat\w*|sleepy|arous\w*)\b/,"physiological_response");
  put("morphological",BTT_responseMorphologicalConfidence(raw),"morphological_change");
  /* Cross-mechanism addiction/dependency evidence. Chemical language strengthens
     this channel but is not required: magical, biological, sensory, and social
     mechanisms can all produce dependency-like progressions. */
  add("dependency",0.50,/\b(?:addict\w*|dependen\w*|withdrawal|tolerance|sensitiz\w*|crav\w*|need(?:s|ed|ing)? another|want(?:s|ed|ing)? another|cannot go without|can't go without|harder to stop|harder to resist|less able to resist|reduced resistance)\b/,"dependency_progression");
  add("chemical",0.34,/\b(?:chemical|drug|dose|pill|potion|tonic|serum|intoxic\w*|metabol\w*|stimulant|sedative|hormone|withdrawal|tolerance)\b/,"chemical_response");
  add("host",0.46,/\b(?:parasite\w*|host\w*|infest\w*|coloniz\w*|symbio\w*|implant\w*|burrow\w*|organism|larva\w*|egg\w*|slime\w*|tentacle\w*|spawn\w*|breed\w*|bred\b|gestat\w*)\b/,"biological_host_response");
  put("adaptive",BTT_responseAdaptiveConfidence(raw,scores),"adaptive_progression");
  /* Direct causal-response constructions increase the affected response channels
     without requiring a specific fiction mechanism. */
  if(/\b(?:you|I|he|she|they|[A-Z][a-z]+)\b[^.!?]{0,28}\b(?:feel|sense|experience|react|respond|twitch|shiver|resist|obey|comply|crave|relax|tense|hurt|ache|tingle|panic)\w*/i.test(String(raw||""))){
    if(scores.sensory)scores.sensory=BTT_clamp(scores.sensory+0.08,0,1);
    if(scores.mental)scores.mental=BTT_clamp(scores.mental+0.08,0,1);
    if(scores.behavioral)scores.behavioral=BTT_clamp(scores.behavioral+0.08,0,1);
    if(scores.physiological)scores.physiological=BTT_clamp(scores.physiological+0.08,0,1);
  }
  var rows=[];for(var id in scores)if(scores.hasOwnProperty(id))rows.push({id:id,score:BTT_round(scores[id],3),cues:BTT_clone(cues[id]||[])});rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});return rows;
}
function BTT_responseChannelMap(raw){var rows=BTT_responseChannelHypotheses(raw),out={};for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++)out[rows[i].id]=rows[i].score;return out;}
function BTT_responseChannelSimilarity(a,b){a=a||{};b=b||{};var keys={},num=0,den=0,k;for(k in a)if(a.hasOwnProperty(k))keys[k]=1;for(k in b)if(b.hasOwnProperty(k))keys[k]=1;for(k in keys){var av=Number(a[k])||0,bv=Number(b[k])||0;num+=Math.min(av,bv);den+=Math.max(av,bv);}return den?BTT_clamp(num/den,0,1):0;}
function BTT_responseDominantFamily(ch){ch=ch||{};var host=Number(ch.host)||0,dep=Number(ch.dependency)||0,morph=Number(ch.morphological)||0,mental=Number(ch.mental)||0,beh=Number(ch.behavioral)||0,reward=Number(ch.reward)||0,aversive=Number(ch.aversive)||0,sens=Number(ch.sensory)||0,phys=Number(ch.physiological)||0,adapt=Number(ch.adaptive)||0;
  if(host>=0.46&&Math.max(adapt,phys,morph)>=0.30)return "biological-host-adaptation";
  if(dep>=0.46)return "dependency";
  if(morph>=0.46)return "morphological-adaptation";
  if(Math.max(mental,beh)>=0.38&&(reward>=0.30||aversive>=0.30||adapt>=0.30))return "behavioral-conditioning";
  if(sens>=0.34&&adapt>=0.30)return "sensory-sensitization";
  if(phys>=0.36&&adapt>=0.30)return "physiological-adaptation";
  if(Math.max(reward,aversive)>=0.38)return "reinforcement-response";
  if(Math.max(mental,beh)>=0.38)return "mental-behavioral-response";
  if(phys>=0.36)return "physiological-response";
  if(sens>=0.34)return "sensory-response";
  return "response";
}
function BTT_responseDirectCue(raw){return /\b(?:feel\w*|sens\w*|experienc\w*|react\w*|respond\w*|twitch\w*|shiver\w*|trembl\w*|resist\w*|obey\w*|compl(?:y|ies|ied|ying|iance|iant)|submit\w*|yield\w*|crav\w*|need\w*|relax\w*|tens\w*|ache\w*|hurt\w*|tingl\w*|panic\w*|instinct\w*|automatic\w*|find yourself|unable to prevent|cannot prevent|can't prevent)\b/i.test(String(raw||""));}
function BTT_resolveResponseObservationOwner(parse,f,channels){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),rows=[],phase=String(parse&&parse.phase||""),direct=BTT_responseDirectCue(raw),bestScore=0,k;for(k in (channels||{}))if(channels.hasOwnProperty(k))bestScore=Math.max(bestScore,Number(channels[k])||0);if(bestScore<0.28)return {entity:"",confidence:0,margin:0,reason:"no_response_channel"};
  /* Direct first/second-person affected-owner evidence outranks generic subject
     resolution. The response channel itself is enough to establish that "you"
     or "I" is experiencing the effect; a separate effect-verb gate is not
     required. This keeps input statements such as "unable to prevent your body
     from complying" and "pleasure flowing into you" owner-bound to Player. */
  if(phase==="input"&&!f.quoted&&/\b(?:I|me|my|mine|myself)\b/i.test(raw))rows.push({entity:"Player",score:1.02,reason:"input_first_person_response"});
  if(!f.quoted&&/\b(?:you|your|yourself)\b/i.test(raw))rows.push({entity:"Player",score:0.99,reason:"second_person_response"});
  var entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse);for(var ei=0;ei<entities.length;ei++){var entity=entities[ei],aliases=BTT_entityAliases(entity);for(var ai=0;ai<aliases.length;ai++){var a=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[ai]);if(!a||/^(?:you|your|yourself|he|she|they|him|her|them|his|their)$/i.test(a))continue;var esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(a).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");var ownerSide=new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"(?:['’]s)?\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:feel\\w*|sens\\w*|experienc\\w*|react\\w*|respond\\w*|twitch\\w*|shiver\\w*|resist\\w*|obey\\w*|compli\\w*|submit\\w*|crav\\w*|relax\\w*|tens\\w*|hurt\\w*|ache\\w*|tingl\\w*|panic\\w*|change\\w*|adapt\\w*)","i");var towardOwner=new RegExp("\\b(?:affect\\w*|compel\\w*|urge\\w*|reward\\w*|hurt\\w*|change\\w*|adapt\\w*|flow\\w* into|spread\\w* through|pressure .* on)\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b"+esc+"\\b","i");if(ownerSide.test(raw)||towardOwner.test(raw)){rows.push({entity:entity,score:0.95,reason:"named_direct_response"});break;}}}
  if(!rows.length&&direct){var generic=BTT_resolveGenericObservationOwner(parse,f);if(generic.entity)rows.push({entity:generic.entity,score:Number(generic.confidence)||0,reason:"generic_direct_response_owner"});}
  var by={},fold=[];for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++){var r=rows[i];if(!by[r.entity]||r.score>by[r.entity].score)by[r.entity]=r;}for(var e in by)if(by.hasOwnProperty(e))fold.push(by[e]);return BTT_pickResolvedObservation(fold,0.55,0.08);
}
function BTT_buildResponseObservation(parse,f){
  var status=f.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(f.raw,f.quoted);if(!status.actual||Number(f.actuality)<0.45)return null;var channels=BTT_responseChannelMap(f.raw),keys=Object.keys(channels),cfg=BTT_getConfig(),top=0;for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++)top=Math.max(top,Number(channels[keys[i]])||0);if(top<Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_channel_min||0.30))return null;var owner=BTT_resolveResponseObservationOwner(parse,f,channels);if(!owner.entity||Number(owner.confidence)<Number(cfg.dev_risk_ai_response_owner_min||0.55))return null;var family=BTT_responseDominantFamily(channels),base=BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"response","response",{boundOwner:owner.entity,affectedOwner:owner.entity,receiver:owner.entity,ownerConfidence:owner.confidence,ownerMargin:owner.margin},family);base.responseChannels=BTT_clone(channels);base.responseFamily=family;base.responseStrength=BTT_round(top,3);base.payload=base.payload||{};base.payload.responseChannels=BTT_clone(channels);base.payload.responseFamily=family;base.payload.responseStrength=base.responseStrength;base.semanticFingerprint=[base.mechanism,family].concat(keys.sort()).join("|");return base;
}
function BTT_observationMechanismTags(raw){
  raw=String(raw||"");var t=BTT_lower(raw),out=[];function tag(id,re){if(re.test(t)&&out.indexOf(id)<0)out.push(id);}
  var clothingRe=/\b(?:clothes?|clothing|garment|fabric|silk|dress|skirt|blouse|bra|lingerie|outfit|wardrobe|heels?|hosiery|weave|tailor|chemise|robe|slip|slippers?|shoes?|stockings?|panties|underwear|corset|bodice|gown|socks?)\b/;
  tag("contract",/\b(?:pact|contract|wager|bet|binding|bound|terms?)\b/);tag("clothing",clothingRe);
  if(/\b(?:curse|cursed|hex|hexed|spell|cursecraft|glamour)\b/.test(t)||(!clothingRe.test(t)&&/\b(?:enchant(?:ment|ed|ing)?|magic(?:al)?)\b/.test(t)))tag("curse",/./);
  tag("product",/\b(?:pill|capsule|tablet|dose|tonic|potion|drug|elixir|serum|injection|tincture|draught|nectar)\b/);tag("chemical",/\b(?:chemical|drug|dose|intoxic\w*|withdrawal|tolerance|sensitiz\w*|stimulant|sedative|hormone|tincture|draught|nectar)\b/);tag("parasite",/\b(?:parasite|parasitic|infest(?:ed|ation|ing)?|implant(?:ed|ation|ing)?|host|coloniz\w*|symbio\w*|slime\w*|tentacle\w*)\b/);tag("conditioning",/\b(?:condition(?:ing|ed)?|habit(?:ual|uation)?|attun(?:e|es|ed|ing|ement)|adapt(?:s|ed|ing|ation)?|learn(?:s|ed|ing)?|reinforc(?:e|es|ed|ing|ement)|automatic(?:ally)?|default)\b/);tag("obedience",/\b(?:obey|obeys|obeyed|obedience|command(?:s|ed|ing)?|order(?:s|ed|ing)?|comply|complies|complied|compliance|submit(?:s|ted|ting)?|submission)\b/);tag("transformation",/\b(?:transform(?:s|ed|ing|ation)?|reshap(?:e|es|ed|ing)|morph(?:s|ed|ing)?|femini[sz](?:e|es|ed|ing|ation)?|chang(?:e|es|ed|ing)|alter(?:s|ed|ing)?|develop(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/);tag("persistent",/\b(?:persist(?:s|ed|ing|ent)?|remain(?:s|ed|ing)?|lasting|durable|residual|between shifts|after removal|long-term)\b/);tag("repeated",/\b(?:repeat(?:s|ed|ing)?|again|each time|every time|another|over time|gradual(?:ly)?|progressive(?:ly)?|continued|successive)\b/);tag("reward",/\b(?:reward(?:s|ed|ing)?|relief|warmth|rightness|approval|praise|comfort|pleasur\w*|sooth\w*)\b/);tag("aversive",/\b(?:punish(?:es|ed|ing|ment)?|sting|pain|pressure|burn(?:s|ed|ing)?|constrict(?:s|ed|ing)?|discomfort|hurt\w*)\b/);tag("dependency",/\b(?:addict\w*|dependen\w*|withdrawal|tolerance|sensitiz\w*|crav\w*|reduced resistance|lowered inhibition)\b/);tag("social",/\b(?:address(?:es|ed|ing)?|presentation|grooming|dress-code|dress code|social|approval|role)\b/);tag("behavioral",/\b(?:behavior|behaviour|routine|posture|movement|mannerism|habit|automatic|urge|compulsion|resist\w*|inhibit\w*)\b/);return out;
}
function BTT_observationMechanism(raw,tags){tags=tags||BTT_observationMechanismTags(raw);var order=["parasite","product","chemical","contract","clothing","curse","obedience","social","behavioral","conditioning","transformation"];for(var i=0;i<order.length;i++)if(tags.indexOf(order[i])>=0)return order[i];return tags[0]||"narrative";}
function BTT_atomicMechanismLexicalCue(raw,mechanism){raw=String(raw||"");mechanism=String(mechanism||"");if(mechanism==="contract")return /\b(?:pact|contract|binding|compulsion|wager)\b/i.test(raw);if(mechanism==="clothing")return /\b(?:cloth(?:ing)?|garment|fabric|silk|chemise|robe|slip|slipper|shoe|heel|stocking|hosiery|lingerie|underwear|corset|bodice|gown|weave|embroidery)\b/i.test(raw);if(mechanism==="curse")return /\b(?:curse|hex|spell|cursecraft|glamour|enchantment|magic(?:al)?)\b/i.test(raw);if(mechanism==="product"||mechanism==="chemical")return /\b(?:pill|potion|tincture|draught|nectar|drug|dose|serum|chemical|liquid|elixir)\b/i.test(raw);if(mechanism==="parasite"||mechanism==="biological-host")return /\b(?:parasite|slime|symbiote|organism|tentacle|host|infestation|implant|colony)\b/i.test(raw);return false;}
function BTT_atomicSourceContinuityScore(o,q,distance){var raw=String(o&&o.raw||""),mechanism=String(q&&q.mechanism||""),ch=o&&o.responseChannels||o&&o.payload&&o.payload.responseChannels||{},score=0,lex=BTT_atomicMechanismLexicalCue(raw,mechanism),direct=BTT_responseDirectCue(raw),relevant=0;if(mechanism==="contract")relevant=Math.max(Number(ch.mental)||0,Number(ch.behavioral)||0,Number(ch.reward)||0,Number(ch.aversive)||0);else if(mechanism==="clothing")relevant=Math.max(Number(ch.sensory)||0,Number(ch.physiological)||0,Number(ch.morphological)||0,Number(ch.adaptive)||0,Number(ch.reward)||0,Number(ch.aversive)||0);else if(mechanism==="chemical"||mechanism==="product")relevant=Math.max(Number(ch.chemical)||0,Number(ch.dependency)||0,Number(ch.reward)||0,Number(ch.physiological)||0,Number(ch.sensory)||0);else if(mechanism==="biological-host"||mechanism==="parasite")relevant=Math.max(Number(ch.host)||0,Number(ch.physiological)||0,Number(ch.morphological)||0,Number(ch.adaptive)||0,Number(ch.behavioral)||0);else relevant=Math.max.apply(null,[0].concat(Object.keys(ch).map(function(k){return Number(ch[k])||0;})));var competing=/\b(?:cool air|warm air|room air|her breath|his breath|their breath|perfume|scent|her tail|his tail|their tail|her eyes|his eyes|their eyes|her gaze|his gaze|their gaze|her voice|his voice|their voice)\b/i.test(raw);if(competing&&!lex)return 0.08;if(lex)score+=0.46;if(relevant>=0.30)score+=0.30;if(direct)score+=0.12;if(Number(distance)<=1)score+=0.12;else if(Number(distance)<=2)score+=0.07;return BTT_clamp(score,0,1);}
function BTT_resolveObservationSource(parse,f,owner){var best={entity:"",descriptor:"",confidence:0,reason:""},raw=String(f&&f.raw||"");if(f&&f.subject&&f.subject!==owner&&Number(f.subjectConfidence)>=0.66&&/\b(?:command|order|tell|make|force|cast|hex|curse|enchant|apply|give|hand|dose|inject|dress|put|press|touch|cause|trigger|infest|implant|expose|feed|administer)\w*\b/i.test(raw))best={entity:f.subject,descriptor:"",confidence:BTT_clamp(Number(f.subjectConfidence),0,1),reason:"causal_subject"};if(f&&f.quoted&&f.speaker&&f.speaker!==owner&&f.addressee===owner&&/\b(?:must|will|do|put|wear|obey|stand|sit|come|go|take|drink|let me|I want)\b/i.test(raw)&&Number(f.speakerConfidence)>=best.confidence)best={entity:f.speaker,descriptor:"",confidence:BTT_clamp(Number(f.speakerConfidence),0,1),reason:"command_speaker"};if(!best.entity){var other=BTT_bestEntityNearFragment(f,owner);if(other.entity&&other.score>=0.82&&/\b(?:because|from|by|through|causes?|makes?|forces?|casts?|orders?|commands?|administers?|exposes?|under\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:influence|effect|control|curse|spell|pact|contract))\b/i.test(raw))best={entity:other.entity,descriptor:"",confidence:BTT_clamp(other.score*0.88,0,0.9),reason:"causal_near_entity"};}
  if(!best.entity){if(/\b(?:pact|contract|binding|wager)\b/i.test(raw))best={entity:"",descriptor:"binding-pact",confidence:0.82,reason:"mechanism_descriptor"};else if(/\b(?:living|enchanted|cursed|sympathetic)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\b(?:cloth|clothing|garment|fabric|silk|dress|skirt|outfit|weave|chemise|robe|slip|slipper|shoe|heel|stocking|hosiery|lingerie|underwear|corset|bodice|gown)\b|\b(?:garment|fabric|silk|dress|skirt|outfit|weave|chemise|robe|slip|slipper|shoe|heel|stocking|hosiery|lingerie|underwear|corset|bodice|gown)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\b(?:learn|attun|adapt|tighten|reshape|encourage)/i.test(raw))best={entity:"",descriptor:"enchanted-clothing",confidence:0.82,reason:"mechanism_descriptor"};else if(/\b(?:curse|hex|spell|cursecraft|glamour)\b/i.test(raw))best={entity:"",descriptor:"curse-magic",confidence:0.78,reason:"mechanism_descriptor"};else if(/\b(?:pill|capsule|tablet|dose|tonic|potion|drug|elixir|serum|chemical|intoxic\w*|stimulant|sedative)\b/i.test(raw))best={entity:"",descriptor:"chemical-agent",confidence:0.78,reason:"mechanism_descriptor"};else if(/\b(?:parasite|infestation|parasitic|host|coloniz\w*|symbio\w*|slime\w*|tentacle\w*|organism|implant\w*)\b/i.test(raw))best={entity:"",descriptor:"biological-host-source",confidence:0.80,reason:"mechanism_descriptor"};}
  return best;}
function BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,kind,domain,row,name){row=row||{};var owner=String(row.boundOwner||row.owner||row.affectedOwner||row.receiver||""),source=BTT_resolveObservationSource(parse,f,owner),tags=BTT_observationMechanismTags(f.raw),mechanism=BTT_observationMechanism(f.raw,tags),slot=String(row.boundSlot||row.slot||""),phase=String(parse&&parse.phase||f.phase||""),action=BTT_nowAction()||BTT_getTurn(),ownerConf=Number(row.ownerConfidence||row.receiverConfidence||row.confidence)||0,ownerMargin=Number(row.ownerMargin)||0,slotConf=Number(row.slotConfidence)||0,actor=String(row.actor||row.sourceEntity||source.entity||((f&&f.subject&&f.subject!==owner)?f.subject:owner)||""),receiver=String(row.receiver||owner),affectedOwner=String(row.affectedOwner||owner),semantic=[mechanism].concat(tags).concat([kind,String(name||"")]).filter(Boolean).join("|");return {kind:String(kind||"event"),domain:String(domain||"narrative"),name:String(name||""),owner:owner,affectedOwner:affectedOwner,receiver:receiver,actor:actor,ownerConfidence:BTT_round(ownerConf,3),ownerMargin:BTT_round(ownerMargin,3),sourceEntity:String(source.entity||""),sourceDescriptor:String(source.descriptor||""),sourceConfidence:BTT_round(Number(source.confidence)||0,3),sourceReason:String(source.reason||""),slot:slot,slotConfidence:BTT_round(slotConf,3),phase:phase,turn:BTT_getTurn(),action:action,fragmentIndex:Number(f.index)||0,raw:String(f.raw||""),actuality:Number(f.actuality)||0,eventStatus:BTT_clone(f.eventStatus||{}),assertion:Number(f.assertion)||0,completion:Number(f.completion)||0,changeScore:Number(f.shared&&f.shared.change&&f.shared.change.score)||0,mechanism:mechanism,mechanismTags:tags,semanticFingerprint:semantic,provenance:{phase:phase,index:Number(f.index)||0,start:Number(f.start)||0,end:Number(f.end)||0,raw:BTT_clip(f.raw,260)},payload:BTT_clone(row)};}
function BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,obs){if(!parse||!obs||!obs.owner)return null;parse.observations=Array.isArray(parse.observations)?parse.observations:[];parse._observationSeen=parse._observationSeen||{};obs.signature="obs|"+BTT_hash([obs.phase,obs.action,obs.fragmentIndex,obs.kind,obs.name,obs.owner,obs.sourceEntity||obs.sourceDescriptor,obs.slot,obs.raw].join("|"));if(parse._observationSeen[obs.signature])return null;parse._observationSeen[obs.signature]=1;obs.eventKey=[obs.action,obs.owner,obs.sourceEntity||obs.sourceDescriptor||"",obs.mechanism||"",obs.slot||"",obs.kind||""].join("|");parse.observations.push(obs);return obs;}
function BTT_linkAtomicObservationContinuity(parse){if(!parse||!parse.observations)return parse;var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),core={contract:1,clothing:1,curse:1,product:1,chemical:1,parasite:1,"biological-host":1},generic={reward:1,aversive:1,conditioning:1,obedience:1,social:1,behavioral:1,transformation:1,persistent:1,repeated:1,narrative:1,response:1};for(var i=0;i<parse.observations.length;i++){var o=parse.observations[i];if(!o.owner||o.sourceEntity||o.sourceDescriptor||!generic[o.mechanism])continue;var candidateMap={};for(var j=0;j<parse.observations.length;j++){if(i===j)continue;var q=parse.observations[j],distance=Math.abs(Number(q.fragmentIndex)-Number(o.fragmentIndex));if(q.owner!==o.owner||distance<1||distance>cfg.clause_window||(!q.sourceEntity&&!q.sourceDescriptor)||!core[q.mechanism])continue;var lo=Math.min(Number(q.fragmentIndex),Number(o.fragmentIndex)),hi=Math.max(Number(q.fragmentIndex),Number(o.fragmentIndex)),blocked=false;for(var k=lo+1;k<hi;k++)if(BTT_fragmentHasCompetingEntity(parse.fragments[k],o.owner)){blocked=true;break;}if(blocked)continue;var local=BTT_atomicSourceContinuityScore(o,q,distance);if(local<0.42)continue;var tagOverlap=BTT_devTagSimilarity?BTT_devTagSimilarity(o.mechanismTags||[],q.mechanismTags||[]):0,score=Math.pow(0.76,distance)*(0.48+0.20*Number(q.sourceConfidence||0)+0.12*tagOverlap+0.20*local),ck=[q.fragmentIndex,q.sourceEntity||q.sourceDescriptor,q.mechanism].join("|");if(!candidateMap[ck]||score>candidateMap[ck].score)candidateMap[ck]={o:q,score:score,local:local};}var candidates=[];for(var ck in candidateMap)if(candidateMap.hasOwnProperty(ck))candidates.push(candidateMap[ck]);candidates.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});if(!candidates.length)continue;var top=candidates[0],next=candidates[1]||{score:0};if(top.score<0.48||top.score-next.score<0.08)continue;o.sourceEntity=top.o.sourceEntity;o.sourceDescriptor=top.o.sourceDescriptor;o.sourceConfidence=BTT_round(Math.min(Number(top.o.sourceConfidence)||0.72,top.score+0.18),3);o.sourceReason="atomic_clause_continuity";o.mechanism=top.o.mechanism;o.mechanismTags=BTT_unique([top.o.mechanism].concat(o.mechanismTags||[]));o.semanticFingerprint=[o.mechanism].concat(o.mechanismTags||[]).concat([o.kind,o.name]).filter(Boolean).join("|");o.eventKey=[o.action,o.owner,o.sourceEntity||o.sourceDescriptor||"",o.mechanism,o.slot||"",o.kind].join("|");}return parse;}
function BTT_buildAtomicObservations(parse){
  if(!parse)return parse;parse.observations=[];parse._observationSeen={};
  for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){
    var f=parse.fragments[i],sh=f.shared||{},generic=BTT_resolveGenericObservationOwner(parse,f),slot,entity;
    /* Direct owner-side response events are canonical shared evidence.
       They are created before domain-specific observations so every subsystem can
       consume the same mental/behavioral/sensory/physiological/body-response fact. */
    var response=BTT_buildResponseObservation(parse,f);if(response)BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,response);
    if(generic.entity){var ge=BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"event","narrative",{boundOwner:generic.entity,ownerConfidence:generic.confidence,ownerMargin:generic.margin},"event");BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,ge);if(sh.change&&Math.max(0,Number(sh.change.score)||0)>=0.12)BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"change","shared",{boundOwner:generic.entity,ownerConfidence:generic.confidence,ownerMargin:generic.margin},"change"));}
    for(entity in (sh.boundSlotBase||{}))if(sh.boundSlotBase.hasOwnProperty(entity)){var sb=sh.boundSlotBase[entity];for(slot in (sb.scores||{}))if(sb.scores.hasOwnProperty(slot)){BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"slot_anchor","shared",{boundOwner:entity,boundSlot:slot,ownerConfidence:Math.max(BTT_fragmentEntityEvidenceScore(f,entity).score,0.66),slotConfidence:Number(sb.scores[slot])||0},slot));}}
    for(var t=0;t<(sh.boundTraits||[]).length;t++)BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"trait","anatomy",sh.boundTraits[t],sh.boundTraits[t].trait));
    for(var a=0;a<(sh.boundAxes||[]).length;a++)BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"axis","appearance",sh.boundAxes[a],sh.boundAxes[a].axis));
    for(var kind in (sh.boundProfileRefs||{}))if(sh.boundProfileRefs.hasOwnProperty(kind)){var refs=sh.boundProfileRefs[kind]||[];for(var r=0;r<refs.length;r++)BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,kind==="heritage"?"heritage":"gender","identity",refs[r],refs[r].id));}
    for(slot in (sh.boundStructure||{}))if(sh.boundStructure.hasOwnProperty(slot))BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"structure","anatomy",sh.boundStructure[slot],slot));
    if((f.products||[]).length&&generic.entity)for(var pr=0;pr<f.products.length;pr++)BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,"product_reference","product",{boundOwner:generic.entity,ownerConfidence:generic.confidence,ownerMargin:generic.margin},String(f.products[pr].id||f.products[pr].trigger||"product")));
  }
  return BTT_linkAtomicObservationContinuity(parse);
}function BTT_observationsFor(parse,filters){var out=[],a=parse&&parse.observations||[];filters=filters||{};for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var o=a[i];if(filters.owner!==undefined&&o.owner!==filters.owner)continue;if(filters.fragmentIndex!==undefined&&Number(o.fragmentIndex)!==Number(filters.fragmentIndex))continue;if(filters.kind!==undefined&&o.kind!==filters.kind)continue;if(filters.domain!==undefined&&o.domain!==filters.domain)continue;if(filters.slot!==undefined&&o.slot!==filters.slot)continue;out.push(o);}return out;}
function BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,f,fields){if(!parse||!f||!fields)return null;var resolvedOwner=String(fields.affectedOwner||fields.receiver||fields.owner||""),row={boundOwner:resolvedOwner,receiver:String(fields.receiver||resolvedOwner),affectedOwner:resolvedOwner,actor:String(fields.actor||fields.sourceEntity||""),ownerConfidence:Number(fields.ownerConfidence||fields.receiverConfidence||fields.confidence)||0,boundSlot:String(fields.slot||fields.receiverSlot||""),slotConfidence:Number(fields.slotConfidence)||0},o=BTT_atomicObservationBase(parse,f,String(fields.kind||"resolved_event"),String(fields.domain||"event"),row,String(fields.name||fields.type||"event"));o.sourceEntity=String(fields.sourceEntity||fields.actor||o.sourceEntity||"");o.sourceDescriptor=String(fields.sourceDescriptor||o.sourceDescriptor||"");o.sourceConfidence=BTT_round(Number(fields.sourceConfidence)||o.sourceConfidence||0,3);o.actor=String(fields.actor||o.sourceEntity||o.actor||"");o.receiver=String(fields.receiver||resolvedOwner);o.affectedOwner=String(fields.affectedOwner||resolvedOwner);o.mechanism=String(fields.mechanism||o.mechanism||"narrative");if(Array.isArray(fields.mechanismTags))o.mechanismTags=BTT_unique(fields.mechanismTags);o.semanticFingerprint=[o.mechanism].concat(o.mechanismTags||[]).concat([o.kind,o.name]).filter(Boolean).join("|");o.eventKey=[o.action,o.receiver||o.owner,o.sourceEntity||o.sourceDescriptor||"",o.mechanism,o.slot||"",o.kind].join("|");return BTT_addAtomicObservation(parse,o);}
/**
 * Emits bounded diagnostics for evidence that occurs in a mixed-entity clause but
 * is routed to exactly one owner. This is intentionally diagnostic-only: the
 * losing entity never receives the observation, history credit, or a commit
 * candidate. The log therefore distinguishes "candidate was rejected" from the
 * stronger invariant "candidate was never generated for that owner."
 */
function BTT_logOwnerBoundObservationRouting(parse,work){
  if(!parse||!work)return;var entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse),seen={},rows=parse.observations||[];
  for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++){var o=rows[i];if(["structure","trait","heritage","axis"].indexOf(o.kind)<0||Number(o.ownerConfidence)<0.70)continue;for(var e=0;e<entities.length;e++){var other=entities[e];if(other===o.owner||!work[other])continue;var f=parse.fragments[o.fragmentIndex],competing=BTT_fragmentEntityEvidenceScore(f,other);if(competing.score<0.62)continue;var key=o.signature+"|"+other;if(seen[key])continue;seen[key]=1;var line="OBS ROUTE AWAY | "+o.kind+":"+o.name+" | phase="+String(o.phase||"").toUpperCase()+" | resolved_owner="+o.owner+(o.slot?" | slot="+o.slot:"")+" | owner_conf="+BTT_round(o.ownerConfidence,2)+" | owner_margin="+BTT_round(o.ownerMargin,2)+" | candidate_for="+other+"=NO";if(o.domain==="appearance"){BTT_touchClauseGroup(work[other],"appearance",f,true);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(work[other],line);}else{BTT_touchClauseGroup(work[other],"anatomy",f,false);BTT_touchDebug(work[other],"anatomy",line);}}}
}

function BTT_sharedTraitEvidence(raw){
  var out=[],seen={},reg=BTT_profileTraitRegistry(),heritageProfiles=BTT_profiles("heritage"),genderProfiles=BTT_profiles("gender"),lower=BTT_lower(raw),tr;
  function push(x){var key=x.trait+"|"+(x.remove?"remove":"add");if(!seen[key]||Number(seen[key].confidence)<Number(x.confidence)){seen[key]=x;}}
  for(tr in reg)if(reg.hasOwnProperty(tr)){
    /* Gender-profile identity words remain Identity-only. Heritage/profile nouns
       are valid Anatomy descriptors when the owner/slot router can bind them to
       one body anchor; a separate slot-origin arbiter decides whether such a noun
       is merely a trait or actually replaces that slot's Heritage origin. */
    if(genderProfiles[tr])continue;
    var surface=tr.replace(/-/g," ");if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,surface)||BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,tr)){
      var direct=0.58;if(tr==="slime"||tr==="ooze")direct=/\b(?:body|skin|flesh|limbs?|form)\b[^.!?]{0,55}\b(?:becomes?|turns? into|changes? into|is now|becoming)\b[^.!?]{0,35}\b(?:slime|ooze|gelatinous)\b/i.test(raw)?0.78:0.18;
      push({trait:tr,confidence:direct,slots:BTT_traitLocalSlots(raw,tr),allowDominant:false,remove:BTT_removeLanguage(raw,tr),reason:"canonical_trait_surface"});}}
  for(var i=0;i<BTT_SHARED_TRAIT_FEATURES.length;i++){var p=BTT_SHARED_TRAIT_FEATURES[i];if(p.re.test(String(raw||""))&&reg[p.trait])push({trait:p.trait,confidence:p.confidence,slots:(p.slots||[]).slice(),allowDominant:true,remove:!!p.remove,reason:p.reason});}
  for(var k in seen)if(seen.hasOwnProperty(k)&&Number(seen[k].confidence)>=0.22)out.push(seen[k]);return out;
}
function BTT_sharedStructureEvidence(raw,slotBase,traits,change){
  var out={},text=String(raw||""),formation=/\b(?:now has|has developed|develops?|forms?|formed|emerges?|emerged|sprouts?|sprouted|grows?|grew|appears?|manifest(?:s|ed)?|becomes?|turns? into|changes? into)\b/i.test(text),phys={
    vagina:{strong:/\b(?:womb|uterus|cervix|vaginal canal|labia|clitoris)\b/i,weak:/\b(?:ovulat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|slit|opening)\b/i},
    penis:{strong:/\b(?:penis|cock|dick|shaft|phallus|erection|testes?|testicles?)\b/i,weak:null},
    breasts:{strong:/\b(?:breasts?|breast buds?|nipples?|areolae?)\b/i,weak:/\bchest\b/i},
    tail:{strong:/\btail\b/i,weak:/\b(?:tailbone|coccyx)\b/i},
    wings:{strong:/\bwings?\b/i,weak:/\b(?:wing joints?|shoulder blades?)\b/i}
  };
  for(var s in phys)if(phys.hasOwnProperty(s)){var strong=phys[s].strong&&phys[s].strong.test(text),weak=phys[s].weak&&phys[s].weak.test(text);if(!strong&&!(weak&&formation))continue;var base=Number(slotBase.scores[s]||0),score=base+Math.max(0,Number(change.score)||0)*0.32;if(formation)score+=0.32;if(strong)score+=0.18;if(s==="vagina"&&/\b(?:ovulat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|fertility|fertile|womb|uterus|cervix)\b/i.test(text))score+=0.18;out[s]={confidence:BTT_clamp(score,0,1.4),reason:strong?"explicit_structural_or_physiological_presence":"change_supported_structure"};}
  /* Traits may corroborate a structure that was independently observed, but a
     descriptor/physiology trait is never allowed to bootstrap a missing slot. */
  for(var i=0;i<(traits||[]).length;i++){var tr=traits[i];for(var j=0;j<(tr.slots||[]).length;j++){var sl=tr.slots[j],r=out[sl];if(!r)continue;r.confidence=BTT_clamp(r.confidence+Number(tr.confidence||0)*0.18,0,1.4);out[sl]=r;}}
  return out;
}

function BTT_sharedProfileReferences(raw,kind){
  var out=[],ps=BTT_profiles(kind),lower=BTT_lower(raw),change=BTT_sharedChangeEvidence(raw),hasBody=false;for(var sl in BTT_SLOT_TERMS)if(BTT_SLOT_TERMS.hasOwnProperty(sl)){for(var q=0;q<BTT_SLOT_TERMS[sl].length;q++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,BTT_SLOT_TERMS[sl][q])){hasBody=true;break;}if(hasBody)break;}
  for(var id in ps)if(ps.hasOwnProperty(id)){var p=ps[id];if(!p||p.enabled===false)continue;var aliases=BTT_profileAliases(p),best="";for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,aliases[a])&&aliases[a].length>best.length)best=aliases[a];if(!best)continue;var score=0.48+(hasBody?0.14:0)+Math.min(0.20,Math.max(0,change.score)*0.24);out.push({kind:kind,id:id,profile:p,alias:best,confidence:BTT_clamp(score,0,1),reason:"available_profile_reference"});}
  out.sort(function(a,b){return b.confidence-a.confidence;});return out;
}
function BTT_sharedLexicalFrame(fragment){
  var change=BTT_sharedChangeEvidence(fragment.raw),slotBase=BTT_sharedBaseSlotEvidence(fragment.raw),traits=BTT_sharedTraitEvidence(fragment.raw),axes=BTT_sharedAxisEvidence(fragment.raw),profileRefs={heritage:BTT_sharedProfileReferences(fragment.raw,"heritage"),gender:BTT_sharedProfileReferences(fragment.raw,"gender")};
  return {change:change,slotBase:slotBase,traits:traits,axes:axes,profileRefs:profileRefs,structure:BTT_sharedStructureEvidence(fragment.raw,slotBase,traits,change),provenance:{index:fragment.index,start:fragment.start,end:fragment.end,raw:BTT_clip(fragment.raw,240)}};
}
function BTT_buildSharedEvidence(parse){
  parse.shared={entityCache:{},ownedSlotEvidence:{},ownedSlotEntities:{},baselineBoundaries:parse.baselineBoundaries||{}};
  for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){var f=parse.fragments[i];f.shared=BTT_sharedLexicalFrame(f);f.shared.participantSubject=f.subject||"";f.shared.participantSubjectConfidence=Number(f.subjectConfidence)||0;}
  /* Owner→slot possessive syntax is raw lexical evidence, so it belongs here:
     extract it exactly once before any owner/slot binding or domain consumer. */
  BTT_buildOwnedSlotEvidenceCache(parse);
  BTT_bindSharedObservations(parse);BTT_buildAtomicObservations(parse);return parse;
}
function BTT_sharedPhase(parse,entity,index){var b=parse&&parse.baselineBoundaries&&parse.baselineBoundaries.hasOwnProperty(entity)?Number(parse.baselineBoundaries[entity]):null;if(b!==null&&isFinite(b))return index<b?"BASELINE":"POST_BASELINE_CHANGE";return "CURRENT";}
function BTT_sharedEntityFrame(parse,entity,index){
  if(!parse||!parse.fragments||index<0||index>=parse.fragments.length)return null;parse.shared=parse.shared||{entityCache:{}};parse.shared.entityCache=parse.shared.entityCache||{};var key=entity+"|"+index;if(parse.shared.entityCache[key])return parse.shared.entityCache[key];
  var f=parse.fragments[index],cfg=BTT_getConfig(),direct=BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity),sh=f.shared||{},localObs=BTT_observationsFor(parse,{owner:entity,fragmentIndex:index}),scores={},reasons={},traits=[],axes=[],profileRefs={heritage:[],gender:[]},structure={},slot,boundOwner=0;
  for(var oi=0;oi<localObs.length;oi++){var ob=localObs[oi],pl=BTT_clone(ob.payload||{});boundOwner=Math.max(boundOwner,Number(ob.ownerConfidence)||0);if(ob.kind==="slot_anchor"&&ob.slot){scores[ob.slot]=Math.max(Number(scores[ob.slot])||0,Number(ob.slotConfidence)||0);reasons[ob.slot]=(reasons[ob.slot]||[]).concat(["atomic_slot_anchor:"+ob.slot]);continue;}if(ob.kind==="trait"){pl.boundOwner=ob.owner;pl.boundSlot=ob.slot;pl.ownerConfidence=ob.ownerConfidence;pl.ownerMargin=ob.ownerMargin;pl.slotConfidence=ob.slotConfidence;traits.push(pl);if(ob.slot){scores[ob.slot]=Math.max(Number(scores[ob.slot])||0,Math.min(0.44,Number(ob.slotConfidence)||0));reasons[ob.slot]=(reasons[ob.slot]||[]).concat(["trait_anchor:"+String(pl.trait||ob.name)]);}continue;}if(ob.kind==="axis"){pl.boundOwner=ob.owner;pl.boundSlot=ob.slot;pl.ownerConfidence=ob.ownerConfidence;pl.ownerMargin=ob.ownerMargin;pl.slotConfidence=ob.slotConfidence;axes.push(pl);if(ob.slot){scores[ob.slot]=Math.max(Number(scores[ob.slot])||0,0.16);reasons[ob.slot]=(reasons[ob.slot]||[]).concat(["axis_anchor:"+String(pl.axis||ob.name)]);}continue;}if(ob.kind==="heritage"||ob.kind==="gender"){pl.boundOwner=ob.owner;pl.boundSlot=ob.slot;pl.ownerConfidence=ob.ownerConfidence;pl.ownerMargin=ob.ownerMargin;pl.slotConfidence=ob.slotConfidence;profileRefs[ob.kind].push(pl);if(ob.slot){scores[ob.slot]=Math.max(Number(scores[ob.slot])||0,0.16);reasons[ob.slot]=(reasons[ob.slot]||[]).concat(["profile_anchor:"+String(pl.id||ob.name)]);}continue;}if(ob.kind==="structure"&&ob.slot){pl.boundOwner=ob.owner;pl.boundSlot=ob.slot;pl.ownerConfidence=ob.ownerConfidence;pl.ownerMargin=ob.ownerMargin;pl.slotConfidence=ob.slotConfidence;structure[ob.slot]=pl;scores[ob.slot]=Math.max(Number(scores[ob.slot])||0,Math.min(0.44,Number(ob.slotConfidence)||0));reasons[ob.slot]=(reasons[ob.slot]||[]).concat(["structure_anchor:"+ob.slot]);}}
  var hasLocalBound=traits.length||axes.length||profileRefs.heritage.length||profileRefs.gender.length||Object.keys(structure).length||Object.keys(scores).length,ownership=Math.max(Number(direct)||0,boundOwner),continuity=false,competing=BTT_fragmentHasCompetingEntity(f,entity);
  /* Clause continuity may support context only when this clause has not already
     resolved its physical observations to another participant. It never imports
     the neighboring clause's unbound lexical bags. */
  if(ownership<0.55&&!hasLocalBound&&!competing){for(var d=1;d<=cfg.clause_window;d++){var pi=index-d;if(pi<0)break;var pf=parse.fragments[pi],prevObs=BTT_observationsFor(parse,{owner:entity,fragmentIndex:pi}),pHas=prevObs.some(function(x){return ["slot_anchor","trait","axis","structure","heritage","gender"].indexOf(x.kind)>=0;});if(!pHas&&BTT_fragmentHasCompetingEntity(pf,entity))break;var po=BTT_fragmentMention(pf,entity);if(pHas)po=Math.max(po,0.72);if(po>=0.55){ownership=Math.max(ownership,po*Math.pow(0.72,d));continuity=ownership>=0.40;break;}}}
  var windowChange=0,windowChangeReason="";for(var dist=1;dist<=cfg.clause_window;dist++){for(var side=-1;side<=1;side+=2){var ni=index+side*dist;if(ni<0||ni>=parse.fragments.length)continue;var nf=parse.fragments[ni],neighborObs=BTT_observationsFor(parse,{owner:entity,fragmentIndex:ni});if(!neighborObs.length)continue;var factor=Math.pow(0.58,dist);for(var no=0;no<neighborObs.length;no++){var nob=neighborObs[no];if(nob.kind==="change"){var nchange=Math.max(0,Number(nob.changeScore)||0)*factor;if(nchange>windowChange){windowChange=nchange;windowChangeReason="window:"+dist+":atomic:change";}continue;}if(!nob.slot||["slot_anchor","trait","axis","structure","heritage","gender"].indexOf(nob.kind)<0)continue;var baseSlotScore=Math.max(Number(nob.slotConfidence)||0,nob.kind==="slot_anchor"?0.44:0.16),borrowed=baseSlotScore*factor;if(borrowed>(scores[nob.slot]||0)){scores[nob.slot]=borrowed;reasons[nob.slot]=["window:"+dist+":atomic:"+nob.kind+":"+nob.name];}}}}
  var localChangeScore=0,localChangeReasons=[];for(var co=0;co<localObs.length;co++)if(localObs[co].kind==="change"){localChangeScore=Math.max(localChangeScore,Number(localObs[co].changeScore)||0);localChangeReasons.push("atomic_change_owner:"+entity);}var change={score:Math.max(localChangeScore,windowChange),reasons:localChangeReasons};if(windowChange>localChangeScore&&windowChangeReason)change.reasons.push(windowChangeReason);
  var frame={fragment:f,index:index,entity:entity,ownership:ownership,directOwnership:direct,boundOwnership:boundOwner,continuity:continuity,hasBoundEvidence:!!hasLocalBound,phase:BTT_sharedPhase(parse,entity,index),actuality:Number(f.actuality)||0,eventStatus:f.eventStatus,assertion:Number(f.assertion)||0,completion:Number(f.completion)||0,change:change,slots:{scores:scores,reasons:reasons},traits:traits,axes:axes,profileRefs:profileRefs,structure:structure,products:BTT_clone(f.products||[]),subject:f.subject,subjectConfidence:f.subjectConfidence,addressee:f.addressee,speaker:f.speaker,quoted:f.quoted,provenance:sh.provenance||{index:index,raw:BTT_clip(f.raw,240)},unresolved:BTT_clone(sh.unresolved||[]),observations:BTT_clone(localObs)};
  parse.shared.entityCache[key]=frame;return frame;
}
function BTT_sharedOwnedFrames(parse,entity,minOwnership){var out=[],min=minOwnership===undefined?0.40:Number(minOwnership);for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){var fr=BTT_sharedEntityFrame(parse,entity,i);if(fr&&fr.hasBoundEvidence&&fr.ownership>=min)out.push(fr);}return out;}
function BTT_sharedHistoryKey(domain,parts){return String(domain||"shared")+"|"+(Array.isArray(parts)?parts.join("|"):String(parts||""));}
function BTT_sharedHistoryScore(meta,key){var h=meta&&meta.sharedEvidenceHistory&&meta.sharedEvidenceHistory[key];if(!h)return 0;var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),age=Math.max(0,BTT_getTurn()-Number(h.turn||0));if(age>cfg.retained_turns)return 0;return Number(h.score||0)*Math.pow(cfg.decay_base,age);}
function BTT_sharedStoreHistory(meta,key,score,raw){if(!meta)return;meta.sharedEvidenceHistory=meta.sharedEvidenceHistory||{};meta.sharedEvidenceHistory[key]={score:BTT_clamp(Number(score)||0,0,2.5),turn:BTT_getTurn(),raw:BTT_clip(raw,140)};}
function BTT_sharedClearHistory(meta,key){if(meta&&meta.sharedEvidenceHistory)delete meta.sharedEvidenceHistory[key];}
function BTT_sharedBestSlot(frame){var best="",score=0;for(var s in frame.slots.scores)if(frame.slots.scores.hasOwnProperty(s)&&frame.slots.scores[s]>score){score=frame.slots.scores[s];best=s;}return {slot:best,score:score};}

function BTT_parseNarrative(text,phase){
  text=String(text||"");phase=String(phase||"");var detectedPlayerName=BTT_detectPlayerName(text),admitted=BTT_admittedNPCSources(detectedPlayerName),sources=[{entity:"Player",name:detectedPlayerName||BTT_entityName("Player",BTT_getEntity("Player")),aliases:BTT_unique(BTT_entityAliases("Player").concat(detectedPlayerName?[detectedPlayerName,detectedPlayerName.split(/\s+/)[0]]:[]))}];
  for(var i=0;i<admitted.length;i++){admitted[i].aliases=BTT_entityAliases(admitted[i].entity,admitted[i]);sources.push(admitted[i]);}
  var discourse=BTT_ensureState().discourse,base=BTT_fragmentText(text),frags=[],recentNPC=discourse.recentNPC||"",recentSpeaker=discourse.recentSpeaker||"",lastQuotedEnd=-1;for(var f=0;f<base.length;f++){var b=base[f],quoted=!!b.quoted,speakerInfo=quoted?BTT_findSpeakerForRange(text,b.start,b.end,sources,recentSpeaker,recentNPC):{entity:"",confidence:0,reason:"not_quoted"};
    if(quoted&&!speakerInfo.entity&&recentSpeaker&&lastQuotedEnd>=0&&(b.start-lastQuotedEnd)<=190){var between=text.slice(lastQuotedEnd,b.start);if(!/\b(?:meanwhile|elsewhere|another voice|someone else|a different voice)\b/i.test(between))speakerInfo={entity:recentSpeaker,confidence:0.74,reason:"adjacent_quote_continuity"};}
    var speaker=speakerInfo.entity||"",addressee=BTT_findAddressee(b.raw,speaker,sources),mentions=[];
    for(var s=0;s<sources.length;s++){var aliases=sources[s].aliases||[],hit=BTT_entityMentionScore(b.raw,sources[s].entity,aliases,quoted,addressee);if(hit.score>0){var bestAlias="";for(var aa=0;aa<aliases.length;aa++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(b.raw,aliases[aa])&&aliases[aa].length>bestAlias.length)bestAlias=aliases[aa];mentions.push({entity:sources[s].entity,alias:bestAlias||sources[s].name,score:hit.score,reasons:hit.reasons});}}
    if(phase==="input"&&!quoted&&/\b(?:I|me|my|mine|myself)\b/i.test(b.raw)&&!mentions.some(function(x){return x.entity==="Player";}))mentions.push({entity:"Player",alias:"first-person",score:0.96,reasons:["input_first_person_player"]});
    if(recentNPC&&/\b(?:he|him|his|she|her|hers|they|them|their|theirs)\b/i.test(b.raw)&&!mentions.some(function(x){return x.entity===recentNPC;}))mentions.push({entity:recentNPC,alias:"pronoun",score:0.56,reasons:["recent_npc_pronoun"]});
    var subj=BTT_subjectHypothesis(b.raw,mentions,speaker,speakerInfo.confidence,quoted,addressee);if(phase==="input"&&!quoted&&/\bI\b/i.test(b.raw)&&(subj.score<0.96||!subj.entity))subj={entity:"Player",score:0.96,reasons:["input_first_person_subject"]};var products=BTT_profileProductHits(b.raw),status=BTT_eventStatus(b.raw,quoted);frags.push({index:f,start:b.start,end:b.end,raw:b.raw,lower:BTT_lower(b.raw),phase:phase,quoted:quoted,speaker:speaker,speakerConfidence:speakerInfo.confidence||0,speakerReason:speakerInfo.reason||"",addressee:addressee,mentions:mentions,subject:subj.entity,subjectConfidence:subj.score,subjectReasons:subj.reasons,eventStatus:status,actuality:BTT_actuality(b.raw,quoted),temporal:BTT_temporalStatus(b.raw),assertion:BTT_stateAssertionConfidence(b.raw),completion:BTT_completionConfidence(b.raw),products:products});
    for(var mm=0;mm<mentions.length;mm++)if(/^NPC:/.test(mentions[mm].entity)&&mentions[mm].score>=0.90)recentNPC=mentions[mm].entity;if(quoted){lastQuotedEnd=b.end;if(speaker){recentSpeaker=speaker;if(/^NPC:/.test(speaker))recentNPC=speaker;}}
  }
  var parse={text:text,phase:String(phase||""),fragments:frags,sources:sources,admitted:admitted,playerName:detectedPlayerName,createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),baselineBoundaries:{}};BTT_buildSharedEvidence(parse);discourse.recentNPC=recentNPC;discourse.recentSpeaker=recentSpeaker;return parse;
}
function BTT_fragmentMention(fragment,entity){var best=0;for(var i=0;i<(fragment.mentions||[]).length;i++)if(fragment.mentions[i].entity===entity)best=Math.max(best,fragment.mentions[i].score);if(fragment.subject===entity)best=Math.max(best,fragment.subjectConfidence);if(fragment.addressee===entity)best=Math.max(best,0.90);return best;}
function BTT_ownedFragments(parse,entity){var out=[];for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){var f=parse.fragments[i],score=BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity),obs=BTT_observationsFor(parse,{owner:entity,fragmentIndex:i});for(var j=0;j<obs.length;j++)score=Math.max(score,Number(obs[j].ownerConfidence)||0);if(score>=0.55)out.push({fragment:f,ownership:score,observations:obs});}return out;}
function BTT_bestEntityNearFragment(fragment,exclude){var best={entity:"",score:0};for(var i=0;i<(fragment.mentions||[]).length;i++){var m=fragment.mentions[i];if(m.entity===exclude)continue;if(m.score>best.score)best={entity:m.entity,score:m.score};}if(fragment.subject&&fragment.subject!==exclude&&fragment.subjectConfidence>best.score)best={entity:fragment.subject,score:fragment.subjectConfidence};return best;}
function BTT_resolvePossessor(raw,parse){
  var best={entity:"",score:0,reasons:[]};if(/\byour\s+(?:coffee|tea|water|drink|mug|cup|glass|bottle|can)\b/i.test(raw))best={entity:"Player",score:0.72,reasons:["player_possessive"]};for(var i=0;i<parse.admitted.length;i++){var src=parse.admitted[i],aliases=src.aliases||[];for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){var re=new RegExp("\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(aliases[a]).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+")+"['’]s\\s+(?:coffee|tea|water|drink|mug|cup|glass|bottle|can|food|meal)\\b","i");if(re.test(raw)&&1.0>best.score)best={entity:src.entity,score:1.0,reasons:["explicit_possessive"]};}}
  return best;
}
function BTT_eventActual(fragment,min){return !!(fragment&&(!fragment.eventStatus||fragment.eventStatus.actual!==false)&&fragment.actuality>=Number(min||0.62)&&fragment.temporal!=="FUTURE");}
function BTT_eventSignature(domain,fragment,extra){
  /* Input and output for one AI Dungeon action share info.actionCount. Using
     that logical action id makes product, risk, Condition, and administration
     events idempotent across the two hooks. */
  var logical=BTT_nowAction();if(!logical)logical=BTT_getTurn();
  return domain+"|"+logical+"|"+String(fragment&&fragment.index)+"|"+BTT_hash(String(fragment&&fragment.raw||"")+"|"+String(extra||""));
}
function BTT_markEventOnce(sig){var s=BTT_ensureState();if(s.processedEvents[sig])return false;s.processedEvents[sig]=BTT_getTurn();var ks=Object.keys(s.processedEvents);if(ks.length>500){ks.sort(function(a,b){return Number(s.processedEvents[b])-Number(s.processedEvents[a]);});for(var i=350;i<ks.length;i++)delete s.processedEvents[ks[i]];}return true;}

/* ============================================================================
   7. ENTITY ADMISSION AND BASELINE MATERIALIZATION
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Admission is separate from detection. The Player is always materialized.
   NPCs must be allowed by the BTT NPC Targets card or a Character card and also
   participate in meaningful current evidence before their four-card schema is
   created. Once admitted, Player and NPCs use the same downstream processors.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_fragmentIsActionableForNPC(f,entity){
  var own=BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity);if(own<0.80)return false;if((f.products||[]).length)return true;if(/\b(?:transform|change|morph|grow|sprout|become|became|gender|pronouns?|male|female|woman|man|human|heritage|pregnan|seed|egg|parasite|possess|corrupt|infect|infest|drink|swallow|take|inject|apply|coffee|mug|body|breasts?|penis|vagina|tail|wings?)\b/i.test(f.raw))return true;if(f.subject===entity&&/\b(?:is|was|has|had|looks?|appears?|says?|asks?|drinks?|takes?)\b/i.test(f.raw))return true;return false;
}

/**
 * Stores a short, entity-owned pre-transformation narrative buffer for NPC
 * genesis. The buffer is evidence only; it is discarded once the first causal
 * transformation snapshot is sealed.
 */
function BTT_sealGenesis(meta,source){
  if(!meta||!meta.genesis)return;meta.genesis.state="sealed";meta.genesis.sealedTurn=BTT_getTurn();meta.genesis.admissionTurn=null;meta.genesis.buffer=[];if(source)meta.genesis.baselineSource=String(source);
}
function BTT_genesisRemember(meta,raw){
  /* Baseline genesis is transaction-local admission evidence. It is never a
     reusable phenotype lifecycle state and cannot reopen after materialization. */
  if(!meta||!meta.genesis||meta.genesis.state!=="admission"||Number(meta.genesis.admissionTurn)!==BTT_getTurn())return;raw=BTT_cleanSpace(raw);if(!raw)return;
  var arr=meta.genesis.buffer=Array.isArray(meta.genesis.buffer)?meta.genesis.buffer:[],sig=BTT_hash(raw);for(var i=0;i<arr.length;i++)if(arr[i].hash===sig)return;
  arr.push({turn:BTT_getTurn(),hash:sig,raw:BTT_clip(raw,500)});while(arr.length>10)arr.shift();
}
/**
 * Resolves a newly admitted target-list NPC's best baseline from evidence that
 * belongs to that one admission transaction. Character-card baselines are
 * already sealed before this stage. Whether or not stronger evidence is found,
 * admission is sealed at the end of this call and can never run again.
 */
function BTT_prepareGenesis(work,parse){
  for(var entity in work)if(work.hasOwnProperty(entity)&&/^NPC:/.test(entity)){
    var meta=work[entity];if(!meta||!meta.genesis||meta.genesis.state!=="admission"||Number(meta.genesis.admissionTurn)!==BTT_getTurn())continue;
    var owned=BTT_ownedFragments(parse,entity);for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++)BTT_genesisRemember(meta,owned[i].fragment.raw);
    var joined=(meta.genesis.buffer||[]).map(function(x){return x.raw;}).join("\n"),male=0,female=0,herm=0;
    male+=(joined.match(/\b(?:male|man|boy)\b/gi)||[]).length*2.4;female+=(joined.match(/\b(?:female|woman|girl)\b/gi)||[]).length*2.4;herm+=(joined.match(/\b(?:hermaphrodite|intersex|futanari|futa)\b/gi)||[]).length*2.4;
    for(var b=0;b<(meta.genesis.buffer||[]).length;b++){var r=meta.genesis.buffer[b].raw;male+=(r.match(/\b(?:he|him|his)\b/gi)||[]).length*0.9;female+=(r.match(/\b(?:she|her|hers)\b/gi)||[]).length*0.9;herm+=(r.match(/\b(?:they|them|their|theirs)\b/gi)||[]).length*0.35;}
    var gender="",score=0,next=0;if(male>=female&&male>=herm){gender="male";score=male;next=Math.max(female,herm);}else if(female>=male&&female>=herm){gender="female";score=female;next=Math.max(male,herm);}else{gender="hermaphrodite";score=herm;next=Math.max(male,female);}
    if(score>=0.85&&score-next>=0.35){
      var sig=gender+"|"+String(meta.heritage||"human");if(meta.genesis.baselineSignature!==sig){var gp=BTT_profileByGenderLabel(gender);if(gp){BTT_reinitializeFromProfiles(meta,meta.heritage||"human",gp.id,entity+"|baseline-admission|"+gender);meta.gender=gender;meta.identity.gender=gender;if(!meta.identity.pronouns||meta.identity.pronouns==="they/them/their")meta.identity.pronouns=BTT_defaultPronouns(gender);}else{meta.gender=gender;meta.identity.gender=gender;meta.identity.pronouns=BTT_defaultPronouns(gender);}meta.genesis.baselineSignature=sig;meta.genesis.sourceText=BTT_clip(joined,700);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","NPC BASELINE ADMISSION | gender="+gender+" | evidence="+BTT_round(score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(score-next,2));}
    }
    BTT_sealGenesis(meta,"target_allowlist_admission");BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","NPC BASELINE SEALED | source=target_allowlist | turn="+BTT_getTurn());
  }
}
function BTT_materializeEntities(parse){
  var player=BTT_getEntity("Player");
  if(parse.playerName&&parse.playerName!=="Player"){var old=player.identity.currentName;player.identity.currentName=parse.playerName;player.identity.originalName=player.identity.originalName==="Player"?parse.playerName:player.identity.originalName;player.identity.aliases=BTT_unique(player.identity.aliases.concat([old,parse.playerName,parse.playerName.split(/\s+/)[0]]));}
  BTT_cleanupPlayerDuplicateNPCs(player,"character_card_matches_player_identity");
  if(!player.initialized){BTT_initializeBaseline(player,parse.text,null,parse,"Player");BTT_sealGenesis(player,"player_baseline");}else if(player.genesis&&player.genesis.state!=="sealed")BTT_sealGenesis(player,"existing_player");BTT_ensureEntityCardSet("Player",player);
  var work={Player:player};
  for(var i=0;i<parse.admitted.length;i++){
    var src=parse.admitted[i],actionable=false,owned=[];for(var f=0;f<parse.fragments.length;f++)if(BTT_fragmentIsActionableForNPC(parse.fragments[f],src.entity)){actionable=true;owned.push(parse.fragments[f].raw);}
    var existing=BTT_findEntity(src.entity);if(!actionable&&!existing)continue;
    var meta=BTT_getEntity(src.entity);meta.identity.aliases=BTT_unique(meta.identity.aliases.concat(src.aliases||[]));
    if(!meta.initialized){
      var cardText=src.card?BTT_characterCardText(src.card):"",initText=(cardText?cardText+"\n":"")+owned.join("\n");
      BTT_initializeBaseline(meta,initText,src,parse,src.entity);if(src.carded&&src.card)BTT_applyCharacterCardBaselineDetails(meta,src.card);meta.genesis.buffer=[];meta.genesis.baselineSignature=meta.gender+"|"+String(meta.heritage||"human");
      if(src.carded&&cardText){meta.genesis.sourceText=BTT_clip(cardText,700);meta.genesis.cardAuthority=meta.genesis.cardAuthority||{gender:false,heritage:false};BTT_sealGenesis(meta,"character_card");BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","CHARACTER BASELINE SEALED | gender="+meta.gender+" | heritage="+meta.heritage+" | metadata_gender="+(src.baselineGender||"none")+" | metadata_heritage="+(src.baselineHeritage||"none"));}
      else{meta.genesis.state="admission";meta.genesis.admissionTurn=BTT_getTurn();meta.genesis.firstTurn=BTT_getTurn();for(var q=0;q<owned.length;q++)BTT_genesisRemember(meta,owned[q]);}
      BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","NPC MATERIALIZED | source="+(src.carded?"character_card":"target_allowlist")+" | evidence="+owned.length);
    }else if(meta.genesis&&meta.genesis.state!=="sealed")BTT_sealGenesis(meta,"existing_runtime");
    BTT_ensureEntityCardSet(src.entity,meta);work[src.entity]=meta;
  }
  return work;
}

/* ============================================================================
   8. PHYSICAL PRODUCT, CONTAINER, AND ADMINISTRATION RESOLUTION
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Product recognition does not itself imply a dose. Physical product instances
   move through possession, placement, container association, and consumption.
   Generic product-family language remains valid evidence, but a longer/more
   specific card-defined product phrase wins when the phrases overlap.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_prunePhysicalGraph(){
  var s=BTT_ensureState(),cfg=BTT_getConfig(),turn=BTT_getTurn(),expire=Math.max(1,cfg.dosed_container_expiration_turns),grace=Math.max(0,cfg.dosed_container_revival_grace_turns),objs=s.discourse.productObjects,cs=s.discourse.containers,k;
  for(k in objs)if(objs.hasOwnProperty(k)){var o=objs[k],age=turn-Number(o.lastTurn||0);if(age>expire+grace)delete objs[k];else o.expired=age>expire;}
  for(k in cs)if(cs.hasOwnProperty(k)){var c=cs[k],age2=turn-Number(c.lastTurn||0);if(age2>expire+grace)delete cs[k];else c.expired=age2>expire;}
}
function BTT_containerNoun(raw){var m=String(raw||"").match(/\b(coffee|tea|water|drink|beverage|mug|cup|glass|bottle|can|food|meal|shake|smoothie|juice|soda)\b/i);return m?BTT_lower(m[1]):"";}
