function BTT_newContainer(owner,label,fragment){var s=BTT_ensureState(),id="container-"+(s.discourse.nextContainer++),c={containerId:id,owner:owner||"",intended:owner||"",label:label||"container",productObjects:[],state:"available",expired:false,createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastText:BTT_clip(fragment&&fragment.raw,260)};s.discourse.containers[id]=c;if(owner)s.discourse.lastContainerByEntity[owner]=id;return c;}
function BTT_findRecentContainer(entity,label,allowExpired){
  var s=BTT_ensureState(),best=null,id=s.discourse.lastContainerByEntity[entity];if(id&&s.discourse.containers[id])best=s.discourse.containers[id];
  if(best&&best.expired&&!allowExpired)best=null;if(best&&label&&best.label!==label&&BTT_getTurn()-best.lastTurn>1)best=null;
  if(!best){for(var k in s.discourse.containers)if(s.discourse.containers.hasOwnProperty(k)){var c=s.discourse.containers[k];if(c.owner!==entity)continue;if(c.expired&&!allowExpired)continue;if(label&&c.label!==label&&label!=="drink")continue;if(!best||c.lastTurn>best.lastTurn)best=c;}}
  return best;
}
function BTT_componentHits(hits){var byKind={},out=[];for(var i=0;i<(hits||[]).length;i++){var h=hits[i];if(!h||!h.profile)continue;if(!byKind[h.kind]||h.specificity>byKind[h.kind].specificity)byKind[h.kind]=h;}for(var k in byKind)if(byKind.hasOwnProperty(k))out.push(byKind[k]);return out;}
function BTT_productComponentKey(components){var out=[];for(var i=0;i<(components||[]).length;i++){var c=components[i];if(!c)continue;out.push(String(c.kind||"")+":"+String(c.id||""));}out.sort();return out.join("|");}
function BTT_explicitNewProductCue(raw){return /\b(?:another|additional|extra|second|third|fourth|different|separate|new)\b(?:\s+[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9-]*){0,4}\s+(?:pill|capsule|tablet|vial|dose|sample)\b/i.test(String(raw||""));}
function BTT_mergeProductComponents(o,components){
  if(!o)return o;var incoming=components||[],stored=o.components=Array.isArray(o.components)?o.components:[];
  for(var i=0;i<incoming.length;i++){var h=incoming[i],idx=-1;for(var j=0;j<stored.length;j++)if(stored[j].kind===h.kind){idx=j;break;}var row={kind:h.kind,id:h.id,trigger:h.trigger,specificity:Number(h.specificity)||0,suppressedCandidates:BTT_clone(h.suppressedCandidates||[])};
    if(idx<0)stored.push(row);else if((Number(stored[idx].specificity)||0)<row.specificity){row.suppressedCandidates=(stored[idx].suppressedCandidates||[]).concat(row.suppressedCandidates||[]);stored[idx]=row;}
  }return o;
}
function BTT_findActionProductObject(components,holder,intended,fragment){
  if(BTT_explicitNewProductCue(fragment&&fragment.raw))return null;var st=BTT_ensureState(),action=BTT_nowAction(),key=BTT_productComponentKey(components),best=null,fallback=null;if(!action)return null;
  for(var id in st.discourse.productObjects)if(st.discourse.productObjects.hasOwnProperty(id)){var o=st.discourse.productObjects[id];if(Number(o.createdAction||0)!==action)continue;if(intended&&o.intended&&o.intended!==intended&&o.holder!==intended)continue;if(holder&&o.createdHolder&&o.createdHolder!==holder&&o.holder!==holder&&o.intended!==holder)continue;var okey=BTT_productComponentKey(o.components);if(key&&okey===key){best=o;break;}var overlap=false;for(var a=0;a<(components||[]).length;a++)for(var b=0;b<(o.components||[]).length;b++)if(components[a].kind===o.components[b].kind){overlap=true;break;}if(overlap&&(!fallback||Number(o.lastTurn||0)>=Number(fallback.lastTurn||0)))fallback=o;}
  return best||fallback;
}
function BTT_newProductObject(components,holder,intended,fragment){var st=BTT_ensureState(),id="product-"+(st.discourse.nextObject++),o={objectId:id,components:[],holder:holder||"",createdHolder:holder||"",intended:intended||"",containerId:"",state:"possessed",route:"",expired:false,createdTurn:BTT_getTurn(),createdAction:BTT_nowAction(),createdPhase:st.currentTransaction&&st.currentTransaction.phase||"",lastTurn:BTT_getTurn(),lastText:BTT_clip(fragment&&fragment.raw,320),possessionAction:null,placementAction:null,consumedAction:null};BTT_mergeProductComponents(o,components);st.discourse.productObjects[id]=o;st.discourse.lastProductObject=id;return o;}
function BTT_materializeProductObject(components,holder,intended,fragment){var existing=BTT_findActionProductObject(components,holder,intended,fragment);if(existing){BTT_mergeProductComponents(existing,components);existing.expired=false;existing.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();existing.lastText=BTT_clip(fragment&&fragment.raw,320);return {object:existing,reused:true};}return {object:BTT_newProductObject(components,holder,intended,fragment),reused:false};}
function BTT_productObjectComponents(o){var out=[];for(var i=0;i<(o&&o.components||[]).length;i++){var c=o.components[i],p=BTT_profile(c.kind,c.id);if(p)out.push({kind:c.kind,id:c.id,profile:p,trigger:c.trigger,specificity:c.specificity||0,reversal:!!p.reversal,suppressedCandidates:BTT_clone(c.suppressedCandidates||[])});}return out;}
function BTT_recentProductObject(holder,allowExpired){var st=BTT_ensureState(),best=null;if(st.discourse.lastProductObject&&st.discourse.productObjects[st.discourse.lastProductObject])best=st.discourse.productObjects[st.discourse.lastProductObject];if(best&&best.expired&&!allowExpired)best=null;if(best&&holder&&best.holder!==holder&&best.intended!==holder)best=null;if(!best){for(var k in st.discourse.productObjects)if(st.discourse.productObjects.hasOwnProperty(k)){var o=st.discourse.productObjects[k];if(o.expired&&!allowExpired)continue;if(holder&&o.holder!==holder&&o.intended!==holder)continue;if(!best||o.lastTurn>best.lastTurn)best=o;}}return best;}
function BTT_entityFromSurface(surface,parse,fragment){
  var v=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(surface));if(!v)return "";if(/^(?:you|your|yourself)$/.test(v))return fragment&&fragment.quoted&&fragment.addressee?fragment.addressee:"Player";
  if(/^(?:he|him|his|she|her|hers|they|them|their|theirs)$/.test(v)){var best="",score=0;for(var i=0;i<(fragment&&fragment.mentions||[]).length;i++){var m=fragment.mentions[i];if(/^NPC:/.test(m.entity)&&m.score>score){best=m.entity;score=m.score;}}return best||BTT_ensureState().discourse.recentNPC||"";}
  for(var s=0;s<(parse&&parse.sources||[]).length;s++){var src=parse.sources[s],aliases=src.aliases||[];for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++)if(BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[a]))===v)return src.entity;}return "";
}
function BTT_resolvePredicateActor(fragment,parse,verbSource){
  var raw=String(fragment&&fragment.raw||""),re=new RegExp("\\b("+verbSource+")\\b","i"),m=re.exec(raw);if(!m)return {entity:"",confidence:0,index:-1,verb:""};var idx=m.index,before=raw.slice(Math.max(0,idx-80),idx),best={entity:"",confidence:0,index:idx,verb:m[0],distance:999};
  var subjMatch=before.match(/(?:^|[^A-Za-z])((?:you|he|she|they)|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\\-]+(?:\\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\\-]+){0,2})\\s+(?:to\\s+)?$/i);if(subjMatch){var ent=BTT_entityFromSurface(subjMatch[1],parse,fragment);if(ent)best={entity:ent,confidence:/^(?:you|he|she|they)$/i.test(subjMatch[1])?0.88:0.98,index:idx,verb:m[0],distance:0};}
  if(best.distance>0){for(var i=0;i<(parse&&parse.sources||[]).length;i++){var src=parse.sources[i],aliases=src.aliases||[];for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){var al=aliases[a],pos=BTT_lower(before).lastIndexOf(BTT_lower(al));if(pos<0)continue;var dist=before.length-(pos+al.length);if(dist<=32&&(dist<best.distance||(dist===best.distance&&0.96>best.confidence)))best={entity:src.entity,confidence:0.96,index:idx,verb:m[0],distance:dist};}}}
  if(!best.entity&&fragment.subject){var sharedSubject=fragment.shared&&fragment.shared.participantSubjectConfidence;best={entity:fragment.subject,confidence:Math.max(fragment.subjectConfidence||0.72,Number(sharedSubject)||0),index:idx,verb:m[0],distance:999};}if(!best.entity){var near=BTT_bestEntityNearFragment(fragment,"");if(near.entity)best={entity:near.entity,confidence:near.score,index:idx,verb:m[0],distance:999};}delete best.distance;return best;
}
function BTT_resolveActor(fragment){if(fragment.subject)return {entity:fragment.subject,confidence:fragment.subjectConfidence};var b=BTT_bestEntityNearFragment(fragment,"");return b.entity?b:{entity:"Player",score:0.35};}
function BTT_resolveRecipient(fragment,parse,actor){var poss=BTT_resolvePossessor(fragment.raw,parse);if(poss.entity&&poss.entity!==actor)return {entity:poss.entity,confidence:poss.score,reasons:poss.reasons};if(fragment.addressee&&fragment.addressee!==actor)return {entity:fragment.addressee,confidence:0.90,reasons:["addressee"]};var b=BTT_bestEntityNearFragment(fragment,actor);return {entity:b.entity,confidence:b.score,reasons:b.entity?["nearest_entity"]:[]};}
function BTT_directRoute(raw){var t=BTT_lower(raw);if(/\b(?:inject|injected|needle|syringe)\b/.test(t))return "injection";if(/\b(?:rub|apply|smear|coat|skin|topical)\b/.test(t))return "topical";if(/\b(?:inhale|breathe|sniff|vapor|gas)\b/.test(t))return "inhalation";if(/\b(?:under (?:the )?tongue|sublingual)\b/.test(t))return "sublingual";if(/\b(?:swallow|drink|down|sip|eat|pop|take)\b/.test(t))return "oral";return "";}
function BTT_isPossessionFragment(f){return /\b(?:grab(?:s|bed|bing)?|take(?:s|n)?|took|pick(?:s|ed|ing)? up|palm(?:s|ed|ing)?|hold(?:s|ing)?|held|retrieve(?:s|d|ing)?|pull(?:s|ed|ing)? out|remove(?:s|d|ing)? from|select(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/i.test(f.raw)&&/\b(?:pill|capsule|tablet|vial|dose|sample)\b/i.test(f.raw);}
function BTT_isPlacementFragment(f){return /\b(?:sneak(?:s|ed|ing)?|drop(?:s|ped|ping)?|slip(?:s|ped|ping)?|put(?:s|ting)?|place(?:s|d|ing)?|deposit(?:s|ed|ing)?|mix(?:es|ed|ing)?|dissolv(?:e|es|ed|ing)|spike(?:s|d|ing)?|lace(?:s|d|ing)?|stir(?:s|red|ring)?|dose(?:s|d|ing)?|add(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/i.test(f.raw)&&/\b(?:into|inside|in|within|through)\b/i.test(f.raw)&&!!BTT_containerNoun(f.raw);}
function BTT_isConsumptionFragment(f){return /\b(?:swallow(?:s|ed|ing)?|drink(?:s|ing)?|drank|drunk|down(?:s|ed|ing)?|sip(?:s|ped|ping)?|eat(?:s|ing)?|ate|pop(?:s|ped|ping)?|take(?:s|n|ing)?|took|gulp(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/i.test(f.raw);}
function BTT_isDirectAdministrationFragment(f){return /\b(?:inject(?:s|ed|ing)?|apply(?:ies|ied|ing)?|rub(?:s|bed|bing)?|inhale(?:s|d|ing)?|force-feed(?:s|ing)?|administer(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/i.test(f.raw);}
function BTT_isTransferFragment(f){return /\b(?:give(?:s|n|ing)?|gave|hand(?:s|ed|ing)?|pass(?:es|ed|ing)?|offer(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/i.test(f.raw)&&/\b(?:pill|capsule|tablet|vial|dose|sample|it|this|that)\b/i.test(f.raw);}
function BTT_resolveProductEvents(parse){
  var events=[],st=BTT_ensureState(),logicalAction=BTT_nowAction();BTT_prunePhysicalGraph();
  for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){
    var f=parse.fragments[i],hits=BTT_componentHits(f.products),actor=BTT_resolveActor(f),recipient=BTT_resolveRecipient(f,parse,actor.entity),actual=BTT_eventActual(f,0.60),fragmentObject=null;
    if(hits.length&&BTT_isPossessionFragment(f)&&actual){
      var possessionActor=/\byou\s+(?:grab|take|pick up|palm|hold|retrieve|pull out|remove from|select)\b/i.test(f.raw)?"Player":actor.entity,placementPoss=BTT_isPlacementFragment(f)?BTT_resolvePossessor(f.raw,parse):{entity:""},possessionIntended=placementPoss.entity||recipient.entity||"";
      var pm=BTT_materializeProductObject(hits,possessionActor,possessionIntended,f),posObj=pm.object;fragmentObject=posObj;if(posObj&&Number(posObj.possessionAction||0)!==logicalAction&&!(posObj.state==="consumed"&&Number(posObj.consumedAction||0)===logicalAction)){var psig=BTT_eventSignature("possession",f,posObj.objectId);if(BTT_markEventOnce(psig)){posObj.possessionAction=logicalAction||BTT_getTurn();events.push({type:"possession",fragment:f,actor:possessionActor,recipient:possessionIntended,object:posObj,reused:pm.reused});}}
    }
    if(BTT_isTransferFragment(f)&&actual){var transferObj=fragmentObject||BTT_recentProductObject(actor.entity,false);if(!transferObj&&hits.length)transferObj=BTT_materializeProductObject(hits,actor.entity,recipient.entity||"",f).object;var transferTarget=recipient.entity;if(transferObj&&transferTarget&&transferTarget!==actor.entity&&!(transferObj.state==="consumed"&&Number(transferObj.consumedAction||0)===logicalAction)){var tsig=BTT_eventSignature("transfer",f,transferObj.objectId+"|"+transferTarget);if(BTT_markEventOnce(tsig)){BTT_mergeProductComponents(transferObj,hits);transferObj.holder=transferTarget;transferObj.intended=transferTarget;transferObj.state="transferred";transferObj.expired=false;transferObj.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();fragmentObject=transferObj;events.push({type:"transfer",fragment:f,actor:actor.entity,recipient:transferTarget,object:transferObj,confidence:recipient.confidence});}}}
    if(BTT_isPlacementFragment(f)&&actual){
      var poss=BTT_resolvePossessor(f.raw,parse),owner=poss.entity||recipient.entity,label=BTT_containerNoun(f.raw)||"container",placementActor=actor.entity||"Player",obj2=fragmentObject;
      if(!obj2&&hits.length){var sameAction=BTT_findActionProductObject(hits,placementActor,owner||"",f);obj2=sameAction||BTT_materializeProductObject(hits,placementActor,owner||"",f).object;}if(!obj2)obj2=BTT_recentProductObject(placementActor,false);if(obj2&&hits.length)BTT_mergeProductComponents(obj2,hits);
      if(obj2&&owner&&!(obj2.state==="consumed"&&Number(obj2.consumedAction||0)===logicalAction)){var cont=BTT_findRecentContainer(owner,label,true)||BTT_newContainer(owner,label,f),alreadyPlaced=Number(obj2.placementAction||0)===logicalAction&&obj2.containerId===cont.containerId&&obj2.intended===owner;if(!alreadyPlaced){var sig2=BTT_eventSignature("placement",f,obj2.objectId+"|"+owner);if(BTT_markEventOnce(sig2)){if(cont.productObjects.indexOf(obj2.objectId)<0)cont.productObjects.push(obj2.objectId);cont.expired=false;cont.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();cont.lastText=BTT_clip(f.raw,260);obj2.holder=owner;obj2.intended=owner;obj2.containerId=cont.containerId;obj2.state="mixed_into_container";obj2.expired=false;obj2.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();obj2.placementAction=logicalAction||BTT_getTurn();st.discourse.lastContainerByEntity[owner]=cont.containerId;events.push({type:"placement",fragment:f,actor:placementActor,recipient:owner,object:obj2,container:cont,confidence:Math.min(f.actuality,poss.score||recipient.confidence||0.7)});}}fragmentObject=obj2;}
    }
    if(BTT_isConsumptionFragment(f)){
      var cp=BTT_resolvePredicateActor(f,parse,"swallow(?:s|ed|ing)?|drink(?:s|ing)?|drank|drunk|down(?:s|ed|ing)?|sip(?:s|ped|ping)?|eat(?:s|ing)?|ate|pop(?:s|ped|ping)?|take(?:s|n|ing)?|took|gulp(?:s|ed|ing)?"),consumer=cp.entity||actor.entity,consumeActual=actual&&!!consumer;
      if(consumeActual){var components=hits,obj3=fragmentObject,cont2=null;if(!components.length){var label2=BTT_containerNoun(f.raw)||"";cont2=BTT_findRecentContainer(consumer,label2,!!label2);if(cont2&&cont2.expired){cont2.expired=false;cont2.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();}if(cont2&&cont2.productObjects.length){for(var po=cont2.productObjects.length-1;po>=0;po--){var oo=st.discourse.productObjects[cont2.productObjects[po]];if(oo&&oo.state!=="consumed"&&(!oo.expired||!!label2)){oo.expired=false;obj3=oo;components=BTT_productObjectComponents(oo);break;}}}if(!components.length){obj3=BTT_recentProductObject(consumer,false);if(obj3&&obj3.state!=="consumed")components=BTT_productObjectComponents(obj3);}}else if(!obj3){var cm=BTT_materializeProductObject(components,consumer,consumer,f);obj3=cm.object;}if(obj3&&hits.length)BTT_mergeProductComponents(obj3,hits);
        if(components.length&&obj3&&!(obj3.state==="consumed"&&Number(obj3.consumedAction||0)===logicalAction)){components=BTT_productObjectComponents(obj3);var sig3=BTT_eventSignature("consumption",f,obj3.objectId+"|"+consumer);if(BTT_markEventOnce(sig3)){obj3.holder=consumer;obj3.intended=consumer;obj3.state="consumed";obj3.route="oral";obj3.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();obj3.consumedAction=logicalAction||BTT_getTurn();fragmentObject=obj3;if(cont2){cont2.state="consumed";cont2.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();}events.push({type:"administration",route:"oral",fragment:f,actor:consumer,recipient:consumer,recipientConfidence:Math.max(cp.confidence||0,fragmentMentionSafe(f,consumer),consumer===f.subject?(f.subjectConfidence||0):0),components:components,object:obj3,actuality:f.actuality,completion:f.completion});}}
      }
    }
    if(hits.length&&BTT_isDirectAdministrationFragment(f)&&actual){var route=BTT_directRoute(f.raw),target=recipient.entity||actor.entity;if(target){var directObj=fragmentObject||BTT_recentProductObject(actor.entity,false);if(!directObj)directObj=BTT_materializeProductObject(hits,actor.entity,target,f).object;BTT_mergeProductComponents(directObj,hits);if(directObj&&!(directObj.state==="consumed"&&Number(directObj.consumedAction||0)===logicalAction)){var sig4=BTT_eventSignature("direct-admin",f,directObj.objectId+"|"+target);if(BTT_markEventOnce(sig4)){directObj.holder=target;directObj.intended=target;directObj.state="consumed";directObj.expired=false;directObj.route=route||"direct";directObj.lastTurn=BTT_getTurn();directObj.consumedAction=logicalAction||BTT_getTurn();events.push({type:"administration",route:route||"direct",fragment:f,actor:actor.entity,recipient:target,recipientConfidence:recipient.entity?recipient.confidence:actor.confidence,components:BTT_productObjectComponents(directObj),object:directObj,actuality:f.actuality,completion:f.completion});}}}}
  }return events;
}
/**
 * Converts the product graph's role-resolved events into the same canonical
 * observation envelope used by Identity/Anatomy/Appearance and AI discovery.
 * Product resolution remains the authority for possession/administration; this
 * record is provenance and a shared downstream evidence representation, not a
 * second product detector.
 */
function BTT_recordProductEventObservations(parse,events){for(var i=0;i<(events||[]).length;i++){var ev=events[i],f=ev.fragment;if(!f)continue;var owner=String(ev.recipient||ev.actor||""),source=String(ev.actor&&ev.actor!==owner?ev.actor:""),route=String(ev.route||ev.type||"product"),confidence=Number(ev.recipientConfidence||ev.confidence||0);if(!confidence&&owner)confidence=Math.max(0.58,BTT_fragmentMention(f,owner));BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,f,{kind:"product_"+String(ev.type||"event"),domain:"product",name:String(ev.type||"event"),owner:owner,ownerConfidence:confidence,sourceEntity:source,sourceConfidence:source?Math.max(0.58,BTT_fragmentMention(f,source)):0,mechanism:route,mechanismTags:BTT_unique(["product",route,String(ev.type||"event")])});}}
function fragmentMentionSafe(f,e){return BTT_fragmentMention(f,e)||0;}

/* ============================================================================
   9. INSTANT-CHANGE LIFECYCLE AND PROFILE REINITIALIZATION
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Accepted administration mutates canonical phenotype before ordinary prose is
   interpreted. Temporary components share a pre-change source snapshot. A new
   component of the same kind replaces the previous component and the body is
   rebuilt from the source snapshot plus all currently authoritative components.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_snapshotBody(meta){
  return {gender:meta.gender,heritage:meta.heritage,species:meta.species,overallFem:meta.overallFem,slots:BTT_clone(meta.slots),appearance:{summary_ai:String(meta.appearance.summary_ai||""),fields:BTT_clone(meta.appearance.fields||{}),axes:BTT_clone(meta.appearance.axes||{})},identity:{gender:meta.identity.gender,pronouns:meta.identity.pronouns,previousPronouns:meta.identity.previousPronouns,pronounGrace:meta.identity.pronounGrace,heritage:meta.identity.heritage}};
}
function BTT_restoreBody(meta,snap){
  if(!meta||!snap)return false;var transport=BTT_captureRefreshTransport(meta.appearance);meta.gender=snap.gender;meta.heritage=snap.heritage;meta.species=snap.species;meta.overallFem=Number(snap.overallFem)||0;meta.slots=BTT_clone(snap.slots||{});var app=BTT_newAppearance(),sa=snap.appearance||{};app.fields=BTT_clone(sa.fields||{});app.axes=BTT_clone(sa.axes||app.axes);app.summary_ai=String(sa.summary_ai||"");BTT_restoreRefreshTransport(app,transport);app.summary_ai=String(sa.summary_ai||transport.summary_ai||"");meta.appearance=app;meta.identity.gender=snap.identity&&snap.identity.gender||snap.gender;meta.identity.heritage=snap.identity&&snap.identity.heritage||snap.heritage;if(snap.identity&&snap.identity.pronouns){meta.identity.pronouns=snap.identity.pronouns;meta.identity.previousPronouns=snap.identity.previousPronouns||"";meta.identity.pronounGrace=Number(snap.identity.pronounGrace)||0;}return true;
}
function BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(p,current){
  if(!p)return current||"hermaphrodite";var explicit=BTT_normId(p.target_gender||p.gender||"");if(/^(?:male|man)$/.test(explicit))return "male";if(/^(?:female|woman)$/.test(explicit))return "female";if(/(?:hermaphrodite|intersex|futanari|futa|dual-sex)/.test(explicit))return "hermaphrodite";
  var pen=BTT_genderMarkerFor(p,"penis"),vag=BTT_genderMarkerFor(p,"vagina"),penOn=BTT_markerIsPositive(pen),vagOn=BTT_markerIsPositive(vag),penNo=BTT_markerIsNone(pen),vagNo=BTT_markerIsNone(vag);if(vagOn&&penNo)return "female";if(penOn&&vagNo)return "male";if(penOn&&vagOn)return "hermaphrodite";
  var overall=BTT_profileMarkerNormalized(p.slot_markers&&p.slot_markers.overall||"");if(overall==="f")return "female";if(overall==="m")return "male";if(overall==="x")return "hermaphrodite";
  var id=BTT_normId(p.id||p.name);if(/(?:hermaphrodite|intersex|futanari|futa|purple)/.test(id))return "hermaphrodite";if(/(?:female|woman|pink)/.test(id))return "female";if(/(?:male|man|blue)/.test(id))return "male";
  var fem=BTT_parseAxisSpec(p.axes&&p.axes.fem||p.axes&&p.axes.overall||"");if(fem){var mid=(fem.min+fem.max)/2;if(fem.op==="+")return "female";if(fem.op==="-")return "male";if(mid>20)return "female";if(mid<-20)return "male";}return current||"hermaphrodite";
}
function BTT_rollDuration(profile,seed){if(!profile||!profile.duration)return null;return profile.duration.min===profile.duration.max?profile.duration.min:BTT_hashInt(seed,profile.duration.min,profile.duration.max);}
function BTT_activeComponents(meta){
  var out=[],canon=meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile&&meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile.components||[];for(var c=0;c<canon.length;c++)out.push({kind:canon[c].kind,profileId:canon[c].profileId,permanent:true,transformationSeed:canon[c].transformationSeed});for(var i=0;i<(meta.instantchange.effects||[]).length;i++){var e=meta.instantchange.effects[i];if(e&&!e.permanent)out.push({kind:e.kind,profileId:e.profileId,permanent:false,transformationSeed:e.transformationSeed});}out.sort(function(a,b){return String(a.kind).localeCompare(String(b.kind));});return out;
}
var BTT_HAIR_SCALE=["bald","shaved","cropped","short","medium","shoulder-length","long","very long","waist-length"];
function BTT_normalizeHairLength(v){var s=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(v)).replace(/_/g,"-");if(/^(?:buzzed|buzz cut|buzz-cut)$/.test(s))return "shaved";if(/^(?:moderate|medium-length|medium length)$/.test(s))return "medium";if(s==="shoulder length")return "shoulder-length";if(s==="waist length")return "waist-length";return s;}
function BTT_genderDirection(profile,oldGender){var next=BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(profile,oldGender),o=BTT_lower(oldGender);if(next==="female"&&o!=="female")return 1;if(next==="male"&&o!=="male")return -1;return 0;}
function BTT_profileSpecifiesField(profile,key){var f=profile&&profile.fields||{};return !!(f[key]!==undefined&&BTT_cleanSpace(f[key])||f[key+"_options"]!==undefined&&BTT_cleanSpace(f[key+"_options"]));}
function BTT_applyHairTrend(meta,profile,oldGender,seed){if(!meta||!profile||BTT_profileSpecifiesField(profile,"hair_length"))return null;var dir=BTT_genderDirection(profile,oldGender),cur=BTT_normalizeHairLength(meta.appearance.fields.hair_length),ix=BTT_HAIR_SCALE.indexOf(cur);if(!dir||ix<0)return null;var steps=1+BTT_hashInt(seed+"|hair-trend-steps",0,1),next=BTT_clamp(ix+(dir>0?steps:-steps),0,BTT_HAIR_SCALE.length-1);next=Math.round(next);if(next===ix)return null;var to=BTT_HAIR_SCALE[next];meta.appearance.fields.hair_length=to;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE HAIR TREND | "+cur+"→"+to+" | direction="+(dir>0?"longer":"shorter")+" | steps="+Math.abs(next-ix));return {from:cur,to:to,steps:Math.abs(next-ix),direction:dir};}
function BTT_prepareGenderAxes(meta,profile){var axes=profile&&profile.axes||{},reset={fem:1,overall:1,fertility:1,virility:1,breast_size:1,ass_size:1,cock_size:1};for(var key in axes)if(axes.hasOwnProperty(key)){var a=BTT_lower(key).replace(/\s+/g,"_");if(!reset[a])continue;if(a==="overall")a="fem";if(a==="fem"){meta.overallFem=0;meta.appearance.axes.fem=0;}else meta.appearance.axes[a]=0;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE PHENOTYPE BASE | axis="+a+" | value=0 | reason=target_gender_reinit");}}
function BTT_componentSignature(comps){return (comps||[]).map(function(c){return String(c.kind)+":"+String(c.profileId);}).sort().join("|");}
function BTT_componentSeed(comps,fallback){var xs=(comps||[]).map(function(c){return String(c.kind)+":"+String(c.profileId)+":"+String(c.transformationSeed===undefined?"":c.transformationSeed);}).sort();return BTT_hash(String(fallback||"")+"|"+xs.join("|"));}
function BTT_committedPhenotype(meta){return {gender:meta.gender,heritage:meta.heritage,species:meta.species,overallFem:meta.overallFem,slots:BTT_clone(meta.slots),fields:BTT_clone(meta.appearance.fields||{}),axes:BTT_clone(meta.appearance.axes||{})};}
/**
 * Logs every profile-owned reroll performed by an Instant-Change rebuild. A
 * value is reported even when the deterministic reroll lands on the same value,
 * making it possible to distinguish "not processed" from "rerolled unchanged".
 */
function BTT_logInstantReinitDelta(meta,before,components,seed){
  if(!meta||!before)return;var after=BTT_committedPhenotype(meta),profiles=[],ownedAxes={},ownedFields={},ownedSlots={},i,k;
  for(i=0;i<(components||[]).length;i++){var c=components[i],p=BTT_profile(c.kind,c.profileId);if(!p)continue;profiles.push(c.kind+":"+c.profileId);for(k in (p.axes||{}))if((p.axes||{}).hasOwnProperty(k)){var ak=BTT_lower(k).replace(/\s+/g,"_");if(ak==="overall")ak="fem";ownedAxes[ak]=1;}var fks=["skin","hair_color","hair_length","eye_color","build","scent"];for(var fi=0;fi<fks.length;fi++)if(BTT_profileSpecifiesField(p,fks[fi]))ownedFields[fks[fi]]=1;for(var si=0;si<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;si++){var sl=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[si],sp=p.slots&&p.slots[sl],mk=(p.slot_markers&&p.slot_markers[sl])||(sp&&sp.present);if(mk!==undefined&&String(mk)!=="")ownedSlots[sl]=1;}}
  var profileLabel=profiles.join(",")||"profile";
  for(k in ownedSlots)if(ownedSlots.hasOwnProperty(k)){var bt=before.slots&&before.slots[k]?before.slots[k].traits||[]:[],at=after.slots&&after.slots[k]?after.slots[k].traits||[]:[],same=BTT_sameJSON(bt,at);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"anatomy","REINIT SLOT | "+k+" | "+(bt.length?bt.join(";"):"none")+"→"+(at.length?at.join(";"):"none")+" | result="+(same?"REROLLED_SAME":"CHANGED")+" | profile="+profileLabel+" | seed="+String(seed));}
  for(k in ownedAxes)if(ownedAxes.hasOwnProperty(k)){var bv=k==="fem"?Number(before.overallFem):Number(before.axes&&before.axes[k]),av=k==="fem"?Number(after.overallFem):Number(after.axes&&after.axes[k]);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"REINIT AXIS | "+k+" | "+BTT_round(bv,3)+"→"+BTT_round(av,3)+" | result="+(bv===av?"REROLLED_SAME":"CHANGED")+" | profile="+profileLabel+" | seed="+String(seed));}
  for(k in ownedFields)if(ownedFields.hasOwnProperty(k)){var bf=String(before.fields&&before.fields[k]||""),af=String(after.fields&&after.fields[k]||"");BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"REINIT FIELD | "+k+" | "+bf+"→"+af+" | result="+(bf===af?"REROLLED_SAME":"CHANGED")+" | profile="+profileLabel+" | seed="+String(seed));}
  if(String(before.gender)!==String(after.gender))BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","REINIT IDENTITY | gender "+String(before.gender)+"→"+String(after.gender)+" | profile="+profileLabel+" | seed="+String(seed));
  if(String(before.heritage)!==String(after.heritage))BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","REINIT IDENTITY | heritage "+String(before.heritage)+"→"+String(after.heritage)+" | profile="+profileLabel+" | seed="+String(seed));
}
function BTT_markInstantAppearanceChange(meta,beforeAxes){var ax=meta.appearance.axes||{},delta=0;for(var k in ax)if(ax.hasOwnProperty(k)&&typeof ax[k]==="number")delta+=Math.abs(Number(ax[k])-(Number(beforeAxes&&beforeAxes[k])||0));meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh=BTT_round((Number(meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh)||0)+delta,2);if(meta.entity==="Player"){meta.appearance.summary_ai="";BTT_requestAppearanceRefresh(meta,"instant_change");}else meta.appearance.summary_ai="";}

function BTT_rebuildInstantBody(meta,seedOverride){
  var comps=BTT_activeComponents(meta);if(!comps.length)return false;var sig=BTT_componentSignature(comps),x=meta.instantchange;if(x.phenotypeSignature===sig&&x.committedPhenotype){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE PHENOTYPE REUSE | signature="+sig+" | body_reroll=NO");return false;}
  var beforePhenotype=BTT_committedPhenotype(meta),source=x.sourceSnapshot||BTT_snapshotBody(meta),oldGender=String(source.gender||meta.gender||"hermaphrodite"),beforeAxes=BTT_clone(meta.appearance.axes||{}),seed=Number(seedOverride);if(!isFinite(seed))seed=BTT_componentSeed(comps,meta.entity+"|"+sig);seed=seed>>>0;
  BTT_restoreBody(meta,source);var hp=null,gp=null,heritageId=String(source.heritage||meta.heritage||"human");for(var i=0;i<comps.length;i++){var p=BTT_profile(comps[i].kind,comps[i].profileId);if(!p)continue;if(comps[i].kind==="heritage"){hp=p;heritageId=p.id;}else if(comps[i].kind==="gender")gp=p;}
  if(!hp)hp=BTT_profile("heritage",heritageId)||BTT_humanHeritageProfile();
  if(hp&&heritageId!==String(source.heritage||"")){var transport=BTT_captureRefreshTransport(meta.appearance),sourceFields=BTT_clone(meta.appearance.fields||{});meta.slots={};meta.appearance=BTT_newAppearance();BTT_restoreRefreshTransport(meta.appearance,transport);meta.appearance.fields=sourceFields;meta.overallFem=0;if(gp){meta.gender=BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(gp,oldGender);meta.identity.gender=meta.gender;}BTT_applyProfile(meta,hp,"heritage","instant-reinit",String(seed)+"|heritage",gp);}else{meta.heritage=heritageId;meta.species=heritageId;meta.identity.heritage=heritageId;}
  if(gp){var target=BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(gp,oldGender);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE TARGET GENDER | product="+gp.id+" | resolved="+target+" | source=profile_anatomy");meta.gender=target;meta.identity.gender=target;BTT_prepareGenderAxes(meta,gp);var hairBefore=String(meta.appearance.fields.hair_length||""),preserved={skin:String(meta.appearance.fields.skin||""),hair_color:String(meta.appearance.fields.hair_color||""),eye_color:String(meta.appearance.fields.eye_color||"")};BTT_applyProfile(meta,gp,"gender","instant-reinit",String(seed)+"|gender",hp);BTT_applyHairTrend(meta,gp,oldGender,String(seed));for(var pk in preserved)if(preserved.hasOwnProperty(pk)&&preserved[pk]&&!BTT_profileSpecifiesField(gp,pk))BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE FIELD PRESERVE | "+pk+"="+preserved[pk]+" | reason="+(hp&&heritageId!==String(source.heritage||"")?"profile_unspecified":"gender_only"));}
  else if(hp&&heritageId!==String(source.heritage||"")){var generic=BTT_profileByGenderLabel(oldGender);if(generic)BTT_applyProfile(meta,generic,"gender","instant-reinit",String(seed)+"|gender-preserve",hp);}
  meta.initialized=true;meta.appearance.axes.fem=meta.overallFem;BTT_logInstantReinitDelta(meta,beforePhenotype,comps,seed);BTT_markInstantAppearanceChange(meta,beforeAxes);x.transformationSeed=seed;x.phenotypeSignature=sig;x.committedPhenotype=BTT_committedPhenotype(meta);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE PHENOTYPE LINK | seed="+String(seed)+" | gender="+String(meta.gender)+" | heritage="+String(meta.heritage));BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE REINIT | gender="+String(meta.gender)+" | heritage="+String(meta.heritage)+" | components="+sig);return true;
}
function BTT_forceRevert(meta,reason){var had=!!meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot;if(had)BTT_restoreBody(meta,meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot);meta.instantchange.effects=[];meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile=null;meta.instantchange.persistentRiskProfiles=[];meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot=null;meta.instantchange.transformationSeed=null;meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature="";meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype=null;if(meta.entity==="Player"){meta.appearance.summary_ai="";BTT_requestAppearanceRefresh(meta,"reversion");}else meta.appearance.summary_ai="";BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE REVERT | reason="+String(reason||"reversal")+" | restored="+(had?"YES":"NO"));return had;}
function BTT_applyReversionTarget(meta,profile,kind,seed){
  var target=BTT_normId(profile&&profile.reversion_target||"");if(!target)return false;var gp=BTT_profile("gender",target),hp=BTT_profile("heritage",target);if(gp)BTT_reinitializeFromProfiles(meta,meta.heritage||"human",gp.id,seed);else if(hp)BTT_reinitializeFromProfiles(meta,hp.id,meta.gender||"hermaphrodite",seed);else return false;meta.instantchange.effects=[];meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile=null;meta.instantchange.persistentRiskProfiles=[];meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot=null;meta.instantchange.transformationSeed=null;meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature="";meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype=null;if(meta.entity==="Player"){meta.appearance.summary_ai="";BTT_requestAppearanceRefresh(meta,"reversion_target");}return true;
}

function BTT_addCanonicalComponent(meta,hit,modifier,cause,transformationSeed){
  var p=hit.profile,x=meta.instantchange,cp=x.canonicalProfile||{components:[],profileName:"",establishedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),permanenceCause:"",sourceTrigger:""};for(var i=cp.components.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(cp.components[i].kind===hit.kind)cp.components.splice(i,1);cp.components.push({kind:hit.kind,profileId:hit.id,durationModifier:modifier?BTT_clone(modifier):null,transformationSeed:transformationSeed===undefined?null:Number(transformationSeed)>>>0});cp.profileName=p.name||hit.id;cp.establishedTurn=BTT_getTurn();cp.permanenceCause=String(cause||"permanent profile");cp.sourceTrigger=hit.trigger||hit.id;if(modifier)cp.durationModifier=BTT_clone(modifier);x.canonicalProfile=cp;
  if(p.risk_tracks&&p.risk_tracks.length){var found=null;for(var r=0;r<x.persistentRiskProfiles.length;r++)if(x.persistentRiskProfiles[r].kind===hit.kind&&x.persistentRiskProfiles[r].profileId===hit.id){found=x.persistentRiskProfiles[r];break;}if(!found)x.persistentRiskProfiles.push({kind:hit.kind,profileId:hit.id,profileName:p.name||hit.id,risk_tracks:BTT_clone(p.risk_tracks),riskState:{}});}
}
function BTT_applyInstantAdministration(event){
  var entity=event.recipient,meta=BTT_findEntity(entity)||BTT_getEntity(entity);if(!meta.initialized)BTT_initializeBaseline(meta,event.fragment.raw,null,null,event.recipient);var comps=BTT_componentHits(event.components||[]);if(!comps.length)return false;for(var pd=0;pd<comps.length;pd++){var sup=comps[pd].suppressedCandidates||[];if(sup.length)BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","PRODUCT RESOLUTION | candidates="+sup.map(function(x){return x.id;}).concat([comps[pd].id]).join(",")+" | winner="+comps[pd].id+" | reason=SPECIFIC_SAME_PRODUCT_SURFACE");}
  for(var rv=0;rv<comps.length;rv++)if(comps[rv].profile&&comps[rv].profile.reversal){if(!BTT_forceRevert(meta,"reversal product "+comps[rv].id))BTT_applyReversionTarget(meta,comps[rv].profile,comps[rv].kind,entity+"|reversal|"+BTT_nowAction());return true;}
  var effects=meta.instantchange.effects||[],bodyChanged=false,hasPermanent=false,seedParts=[];
  for(var i=0;i<comps.length;i++){
    var h=comps[i],p=h.profile;if(!p)continue;var mod=BTT_resolveDurationModifier(p,event.fragment.raw,h.trigger),same=null,canonSame=false,cp=meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile;
    for(var q=0;q<effects.length;q++)if(effects[q].kind===h.kind&&effects[q].profileId===h.id){same=effects[q];break;}if(cp&&Array.isArray(cp.components))for(var cs=0;cs<cp.components.length;cs++)if(cp.components[cs].kind===h.kind&&cp.components[cs].profileId===h.id)canonSame=true;
    if(same&&!(mod&&mod.permanent)){var resetDays=mod&&mod.timedDays!==undefined?mod.timedDays:BTT_rollDuration(p,entity+"|"+h.kind+"|"+h.id+"|redose|"+BTT_nowAction());same.startedTurn=BTT_getTurn();same.startedDay=BTT_getDay();same.minimumRevertTurn=BTT_getTurn()+1;same.durationDays=resetDays;same.revertDay=resetDays===null?null:BTT_getDay()+resetDays;same.sourceTrigger=h.trigger||same.sourceTrigger;if(mod)same.durationModifier=BTT_clone(mod);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE REDOSE | "+h.kind+":"+h.id+" | duration="+(resetDays===null?"permanent":resetDays+"d")+" | body_reroll=NO");continue;}
    if(canonSame&&!same){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE REDOSE | "+h.kind+":"+h.id+" | canonical_already_active=YES | body_reroll=NO");continue;}
    if(!meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot)meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot=BTT_snapshotBody(meta);
    /* A new component of an axis replaces both timed and permanent authority of
       that same axis. If the old state was permanent, the current canonical body
       has already been captured as the fresh source snapshot above. */
    for(var x=effects.length-1;x>=0;x--)if(effects[x].kind===h.kind)effects.splice(x,1);if(cp&&Array.isArray(cp.components)){for(var cc=cp.components.length-1;cc>=0;cc--)if(cp.components[cc].kind===h.kind)cp.components.splice(cc,1);if(!cp.components.length)meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile=null;}
    var objectId=event.object&&event.object.objectId||"direct",componentSeed=BTT_hash(entity+"|"+h.kind+"|"+h.id+"|object="+objectId+"|action="+BTT_nowAction()+"|turn="+BTT_getTurn());seedParts.push(componentSeed);
    if(mod&&mod.permanent){BTT_addCanonicalComponent(meta,h,mod,"duration modifier: "+String(mod.label||mod.alias||"configured permanent modifier"),componentSeed);hasPermanent=true;bodyChanged=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE COMPONENT | "+h.kind+":"+h.id+" | modifier="+String(mod.label||mod.alias)+" | duration=permanent | route="+event.route);continue;}
    var days=mod&&mod.timedDays!==undefined?mod.timedDays:BTT_rollDuration(p,entity+"|"+h.kind+"|"+h.id+"|"+BTT_nowAction()+"|duration"),effect={kind:h.kind,profileId:h.id,profileName:p.name||h.id,manufacturer:p.manufacturer||"",startedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),startedDay:BTT_getDay(),minimumRevertTurn:BTT_getTurn()+1,durationDays:days,revertDay:days===null?null:BTT_getDay()+days,permanent:false,sourceTrigger:h.trigger||h.id,durationModifier:mod?BTT_clone(mod):null,risk_tracks:BTT_clone(p.risk_tracks||[]),riskState:{},transformationSeed:componentSeed};effects.push(effect);bodyChanged=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE COMPONENT | "+h.kind+":"+h.id+" | "+(mod?"modifier="+String(mod.label||mod.alias)+" | ":"")+"duration="+(days===null?"permanent":days+"d")+" | route="+event.route);
  }
  meta.instantchange.effects=effects;if(bodyChanged){meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature="";meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype=null;BTT_rebuildInstantBody(meta,seedParts.length?BTT_hash(seedParts.join("|")):undefined);}if(hasPermanent&&!effects.length)meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot=null;BTT_sealGenesis(meta,"preexisting_baseline");BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE TRANSACTION COMMIT | target="+BTT_entityName(entity,meta)+" | components="+comps.map(function(h){return h.kind+":"+h.id;}).join(",")+" | phenotype_reinit=ATOMIC");return true;
}
function BTT_processInstantEvents(events){for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++)if(events[i].type==="administration"&&events[i].recipient&&events[i].recipientConfidence>=0.55&&events[i].actuality>=0.60&&events[i].completion>=0.52)BTT_applyInstantAdministration(events[i]);}
function BTT_advanceWorldDay(text){var t=BTT_lower(text),max=0,m;if(/\b(?:next day|following day|tomorrow|the day after)\b/.test(t))max=1;var re=/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty)\s+days?\s+(?:later|pass|passes|passed|afterward|afterwards)\b/g,map={one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,eleven:11,twelve:12,twenty:20};while((m=re.exec(t))){var n=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(n))n=map[m[1]]||0;max=Math.max(max,n);}var rw=/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+weeks?\s+(?:later|pass|passes|passed)\b/g;while((m=rw.exec(t))){var w=Number(m[1]);if(!isFinite(w))w=map[m[1]]||0;max=Math.max(max,w*7);}if(max>0)BTT_ensureState().worldDay+=max;return max;}
function BTT_triggerMatch(text,phrase){return phrase&&BTT_wordBoundaryHas(text,phrase);}
function BTT_permanencyConditionName(trigger){
  var raw=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(trigger)),m=raw.match(/^condition\s*:\s*([a-z-]+)$/i),known={pregnancy:1,incubation:1,seeded:1,infestation:1,possession:1,infection:1,corruption:1,pollination:1};if(m)return known[BTT_conditionType(m[1])]?BTT_conditionType(m[1]):"";var t=BTT_conditionType(raw);return known[t]?t:"";
}
function BTT_lifecycleTriggerFragment(meta,parse,phrase){
  var owned=BTT_ownedFragments(parse,meta.entity);for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){var f=owned[i].fragment,status=f.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(f.raw,f.quoted);if(owned[i].ownership<0.55||!status.actual||f.actuality<0.55)continue;if(BTT_triggerMatch(f.raw,phrase))return f;}return null;
}
function BTT_commitPermanentEffect(meta,e,p,cause){
  if(!meta||!e||!p)return false;var cp=meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile||{components:[],profileName:"",establishedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),permanenceCause:""};if(!cp.components.some(function(x){return x.kind===e.kind&&x.profileId===e.profileId;}))cp.components.push({kind:e.kind,profileId:e.profileId,transformationSeed:e.transformationSeed});cp.profileName=p.name||e.profileId;cp.establishedTurn=BTT_getTurn();cp.permanenceCause=String(cause||"permanent");meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile=cp;
  /* Permanence freezes the actual live phenotype, including valid narrative and
     risk changes accumulated while the temporary profile was active. */
  meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype=BTT_committedPhenotype(meta);
  if(p.risk_tracks&&p.risk_tracks.length&&!meta.instantchange.persistentRiskProfiles.some(function(r){return r.kind===e.kind&&r.profileId===e.profileId;}))meta.instantchange.persistentRiskProfiles.push({kind:e.kind,profileId:e.profileId,profileName:p.name||e.profileId,risk_tracks:BTT_clone(p.risk_tracks),riskState:BTT_clone(e.riskState||{})});
  BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","PERMANENCE COMMIT | "+e.kind+":"+e.profileId+" | cause="+String(cause||"permanent"));return true;
}
function BTT_applyLifecycleText(meta,parse){
  var effects=meta.instantchange.effects||[],changed=false;for(var i=effects.length-1;i>=0;i--){var e=effects[i],p=BTT_profile(e.kind,e.profileId);if(!p)continue;
    for(var a=0;a<(p.extend_time_conditions||[]).length;a++){var ex=p.extend_time_conditions[a],fx=BTT_lifecycleTriggerFragment(meta,parse,ex.trigger);if(fx&&e.revertDay!==null){var sx=BTT_eventSignature("lifecycle-extend",fx,meta.entity+"|"+e.profileId+"|"+ex.trigger);if(BTT_markEventOnce(sx)){e.revertDay+=Math.abs(ex.days);e.durationDays=Math.max(1,e.revertDay-e.startedDay);changed=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","DURATION EXTEND | "+e.profileId+" | +"+Math.abs(ex.days));}}}
    for(var b=0;b<(p.reduce_time_triggers||[]).length;b++){var rd=p.reduce_time_triggers[b],fr=BTT_lifecycleTriggerFragment(meta,parse,rd.trigger);if(fr&&e.revertDay!==null){var sr=BTT_eventSignature("lifecycle-reduce",fr,meta.entity+"|"+e.profileId+"|"+rd.trigger);if(BTT_markEventOnce(sr)){e.revertDay=Math.max(BTT_getDay(),e.revertDay-Math.abs(rd.days));changed=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","DURATION REDUCE | "+e.profileId+" | -"+Math.abs(rd.days));}}}
    /* Known Condition names are never raw narrative permanence triggers. They are
       resolved later by BTT_conditionPermanence from committed Condition state. */
    var why="",pf=null;for(var c=0;c<(p.permanency_triggers||[]).length;c++){var trig=p.permanency_triggers[c];if(BTT_permanencyConditionName(trig))continue;var ff=BTT_lifecycleTriggerFragment(meta,parse,trig);if(ff){why="trigger:"+trig;pf=ff;break;}}
    if(why&&pf){var sp=BTT_eventSignature("lifecycle-permanence",pf,meta.entity+"|"+e.profileId+"|"+why);if(BTT_markEventOnce(sp)){BTT_commitPermanentEffect(meta,e,p,why);effects.splice(i,1);changed=true;}}
  }
  meta.instantchange.effects=effects;if(changed&&!effects.length&&meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile)meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot=null;return changed;
}
function BTT_applyExpirations(meta){
  var effects=meta.instantchange.effects||[],removed=false;for(var i=effects.length-1;i>=0;i--){var e=effects[i];if(e.revertDay!==null&&BTT_getDay()>=Number(e.revertDay)&&BTT_getTurn()>=Number(e.minimumRevertTurn||0)){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"instantchange","INSTANT_CHANGE EXPIRE | "+e.kind+":"+e.profileId+" | day="+BTT_getDay());effects.splice(i,1);removed=true;}}
  if(removed){meta.instantchange.effects=effects;if(!effects.length){if(meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile&&meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile.components&&meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile.components.length){var nextSig=BTT_componentSignature(BTT_activeComponents(meta));if(nextSig!==meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature){meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature="";meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype=null;BTT_rebuildInstantBody(meta);}meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot=null;}else BTT_forceRevert(meta,"timed expiration");}else{meta.instantchange.phenotypeSignature="";meta.instantchange.committedPhenotype=null;BTT_rebuildInstantBody(meta);}}return removed;
}

/* ============================================================================
   10. IDENTITY, NAME, GENDER, PRONOUN, AND HERITAGE EVIDENCE
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Identity is separate from physical phenotype. Pronouns are resolved from
   owned dialogue/narration evidence and may retain a short grace window after a
   strong change. Heritage can only commit to an enabled Heritage profile that
   exists in the scenario; unsupported prose never invents canonical species.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_selfDeclaredName(raw,allowIAmNow){
  /* The declaration phrase is case-insensitive, while the captured name is
     validated separately by BTT_safeName. This accepts natural sentence-start
     capitalization such as "My name is Chloe" without weakening name syntax. */
  var text=String(raw||""),prefix=allowIAmNow?/\b(?:my name is|I am now|call me)\s+/ig:/\b(?:my name is|call me)\s+/ig,m;
  while((m=prefix.exec(text))){var tail=text.slice(m.index+m[0].length),n=tail.match(/^([A-Z][A-Za-z'’\-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\-]+){0,2})\b/);if(n){var safe=BTT_safeName(n[1]);if(safe)return safe;}}
  return "";
}
function BTT_findRename(raw,entity,meta){
  var old=BTT_entityName(entity,meta),m,selfName=BTT_selfDeclaredName(raw,entity==="Player");if(selfName)return selfName;
  if(entity!=="Player"){
    var esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(old);m=String(raw||"").match(new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"\\b[^.!?]{0,80}\\b(?:name is now|renames? (?:himself|herself|themself) as|goes by|is now called)\\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’\\-]+(?:\\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\\-]+){0,2})","i"));if(m)return BTT_safeName(m[1]);
  }
  return "";
}
function BTT_profileIdentityEvidence(fragment,entity,kind,ownership){
  var hits=BTT_fragmentProfileCandidates(fragment,kind,false,ownership,false,entity),out=[],raw=String(fragment&&fragment.raw||""),wholeCue=0,partCue=0,chg=BTT_changeEvidence(raw).score;
  if(fragment&&fragment.subject===entity)wholeCue+=0.18;
  if(/\b(?:identity|gender|heritage|species|race|form|body|person|man|woman|boy|girl)\b/i.test(raw))wholeCue+=0.08;
  if(/\b(?:is|are|am|was|were|become|becomes|became|turn|turns|turned|now|currently|remains)\b/i.test(raw))wholeCue+=0.10;
  if(kind==="heritage"&&/\b(?:heritage|species|race)\b/i.test(raw))wholeCue+=0.18;
  if(chg>=0.45)wholeCue+=0.10;
  if(/\b(?:head|face|skin|hair|eyes?|arms?|hands?|legs?|feet|chest|breasts?|gut|stomach|penis|cock|vagina|cunt|ass|hips?|tail|wings?)\b/i.test(raw))partCue+=0.10;
  for(var i=0;i<hits.length;i++){
    var h=hits[i];if(Number(h.relation||0)<0.18&&!/\b(?:gender|heritage|species|race)\s*[:=]/i.test(raw))continue;
    /* The candidate already contains alias/ownership/assertion/actuality/relation
       evidence. Runtime identity adds whole-body cues and subtracts part-only
       cues without multiplying the candidate by another confidence scalar. */
    var score=h.score+wholeCue-partCue-0.18;
    if(kind==="heritage"){for(var a=0;a<h.aliases.length;a++){var al=BTT_cleanSpace(h.aliases[a]),esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(al).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");if(new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"['’]s\\b","i").test(raw)||new RegExp("\\b(?:like|resembling|similar to)\\s+(?:an?\\s+)?"+esc+"\\b","i").test(raw))score-=0.34;}}
    out.push({id:h.id,profile:h.profile,score:BTT_clamp(score,0,1.65),raw:raw});
  }
  out.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});return out;
}
function BTT_explicitPronouns(raw){var m=String(raw||"").match(/\bpronouns?\s*(?:are|is|become|becomes|changed?\s+to|set\s+to|:)?\s*(he\s*\/\s*him(?:\s*\/\s*his)?|she\s*\/\s*her(?:\s*\/\s*her)?|they\s*\/\s*them(?:\s*\/\s*their)?)/i);return m?BTT_pronounSet(m[1]):"";}
/* Ordinary references to a Heritage are not whole-entity identity changes.
   A body part may take on traits legal to an available Heritage without
   replacing the entity's canonical Heritage. Only explicit whole-identity
   assertions (for example, "she becomes a dragonkin" or "heritage: elf")
   are eligible for canonical Heritage commits. */
function BTT_explicitHeritageIdentity(raw,hit){
  /* Adapter for callers outside Identity. It
     returns only a generic whole-entity evidence decision and never treats a
     does not treat a high alias score as ownership authority. */
  if(!hit||!hit.profile)return false;var text=String(raw||""),aliases=BTT_profileAliases(hit.profile),whole=/\b(?:heritage|species|race|form|body|become|becomes|became|turned|turns|is|are|was|were)\b/i.test(text),part=/\b(?:head|skin|hair|arms?|legs?|breasts?|penis|vagina|ass|tail|wings?)\b/i.test(text);
  for(var i=0;i<aliases.length;i++){var a=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[i]);if(!a)continue;var esc=BTT_escapeRegExp(a).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");if(new RegExp("\\b"+esc+"['’]s\\b","i").test(text)||new RegExp("\\b(?:like|resembling|similar to)\\s+(?:an?\\s+)?"+esc+"\\b","i").test(text))return false;}
  return !!(whole&&!part);
}

/**
 * Returns current gender-profile authority, if any. Timed and canonical
 * Instant-Change components own physical gender until they expire/revert.
 */
function BTT_activeGenderAuthority(meta){
  if(!meta||!meta.instantchange)return null;var comps=BTT_activeComponents(meta),best=null;for(var i=0;i<comps.length;i++)if(comps[i].kind==="gender"){var p=BTT_profile("gender",comps[i].profileId);if(p)best={profileId:comps[i].profileId,profile:p,gender:BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(p,meta.gender||"hermaphrodite")};}return best;
}
function BTT_deliberateGenderIdentity(raw){return /\b(?:gender\s+identity\s+(?:is|becomes?|changed?\s+to)|identif(?:y|ies|ied)\s+(?:myself|himself|herself|themself|themselves)?\s*as|declares?\s+(?:his|her|their|my)\s+gender\s+(?:as|to be))\b/i.test(String(raw||""));}
function BTT_pronounEvidence(raw){var counts={"he/him/his":0,"she/her/her":0,"they/them/their":0};var m=String(raw||"").match(/\b(he|him|his|she|her|hers|they|them|their|theirs)\b/gi)||[];for(var i=0;i<m.length;i++){var p=BTT_lower(m[i]);if(/^(?:he|him|his)$/.test(p))counts["he/him/his"]++;else if(/^(?:she|her|hers)$/.test(p))counts["she/her/her"]++;else counts["they/them/their"]++;}return counts;}
function BTT_processIdentity(meta,entity,parse,causalIndex){
  var owned=BTT_ownedFragments(parse,entity),pron={"he/him/his":0,"she/her/her":0,"they/them/their":0},genderScores={},genderRaw={},heritageScores={},heritageRaw={},rename="",renameConf=0,boundary=BTT_baselineChangeBoundary(parse,entity);
  for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){
    var f=owned[i].fragment,shared=BTT_sharedEntityFrame(parse,entity,f.index),selfSpeech=!!(f.quoted&&f.speaker===entity),weight=selfSpeech?Math.max(shared.ownership,Number(f.speakerConfidence)||0):Math.min(shared.ownership,shared.actuality);if(shared.ownership<0.34||(!selfSpeech&&shared.actuality<0.34))continue;if(causalIndex!==null&&f.index<causalIndex&&!selfSpeech)weight=Math.max(0,weight-0.42);
    /* Resolved self-authored dialogue is an identity assertion, not a causal
       world event. Quoted speech therefore keeps ordinary event actuality low
       for Conditions/Risks while a direct self-name declaration can still be
       authoritative for Identity. */
    var selfSpeechWeight=selfSpeech?BTT_clamp(Math.max(weight,Number(owned[i].ownership)||0)+Math.max(0,(Number(f.speakerConfidence)||0)-0.50)*0.35,0,1.25):weight,rn=BTT_findRename(f.raw,entity,meta);if(rn&&selfSpeechWeight>renameConf){rename=rn;renameConf=selfSpeechWeight;}
    /* When one transaction contains a stable starting state followed by an
       actual transformation, pre-boundary profile words describe the old state.
       They remain useful for baseline initialization but are weak evidence for
       the post-transaction current identity. */
    var baselinePenalty=(shared.phase==="BASELINE")?0.58:0;
    var gc=BTT_profileIdentityEvidence(f,entity,"gender",owned[i].ownership);for(var gi=0;gi<gc.length;gi++){var target=BTT_targetGenderFromProfile(gc[gi].profile,meta.gender||"hermaphrodite"),gs=Math.max(0,gc[gi].score-baselinePenalty);genderScores[target]=(genderScores[target]||0)+gs;if(!genderRaw[target]||gs>genderRaw[target].score)genderRaw[target]={raw:f.raw,score:gs,profileId:gc[gi].id};}
    var hc=BTT_profileIdentityEvidence(f,entity,"heritage",owned[i].ownership);for(var hi=0;hi<hc.length;hi++){var hs=Math.max(0,hc[hi].score-baselinePenalty);heritageScores[hc[hi].id]=(heritageScores[hc[hi].id]||0)+hs;if(!heritageRaw[hc[hi].id]||hs>heritageRaw[hc[hi].id].score)heritageRaw[hc[hi].id]={raw:f.raw,score:hs,profile:hc[hi].profile};}
    var ep=BTT_explicitPronouns(f.raw);if(ep)pron[ep]+=1.5*(f.quoted&&f.speaker===entity?selfSpeechWeight:weight);if(!(f.quoted&&f.speaker===entity)&&entity!=="Player"){var focusedPron=(f.mentions||[]).some(function(x){return x.entity===entity&&x.alias==="pronoun"&&x.score>=0.50;});if(focusedPron){var pe=BTT_pronounEvidence(f.raw);for(var k in pe)if(pe.hasOwnProperty(k))pron[k]+=pe[k]*0.22*weight;}}
  }
  if(rename&&rename!==meta.identity.currentName&&renameConf>=0.66){var old=meta.identity.currentName;meta.identity.currentName=rename;meta.identity.aliases=BTT_unique(meta.identity.aliases.concat([old,rename,rename.split(/\s+/)[0]]));BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","NAME COMMIT | "+old+"→"+rename+" | confidence="+BTT_round(renameConf,2));}
  var hr=[];for(var hid in heritageScores)if(heritageScores.hasOwnProperty(hid))hr.push({id:hid,score:heritageScores[hid],raw:heritageRaw[hid]});hr.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});if(hr.length){var hb=hr[0],hn=hr.length>1?hr[1].score:0;if(hb.score>=0.86&&(hb.score-hn)>=0.16&&hb.id!==meta.heritage){meta.heritage=hb.id;meta.species=hb.id;meta.identity.heritage=hb.id;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","HERITAGE COMMIT | "+hb.id+" | confidence="+BTT_round(hb.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(hb.score-hn,2)+" | profile=AVAILABLE");}}
  var gr=[];for(var gg in genderScores)if(genderScores.hasOwnProperty(gg))gr.push({gender:gg,score:genderScores[gg],raw:genderRaw[gg]});gr.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});if(gr.length){var gb=gr[0],gn=gr.length>1?gr[1].score:0;if(gb.score>=0.82&&(gb.score-gn)>=0.14&&gb.gender!==meta.gender){var ga=BTT_activeGenderAuthority(meta),rawInfo=gb.raw||{};if(ga&&gb.gender!==ga.gender&&!BTT_deliberateGenderIdentity(rawInfo.raw||"")){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","GENDER HOLD | candidate="+gb.gender+" | confidence="+BTT_round(gb.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(gb.score-gn,2)+" | active_profile="+ga.profileId+" | authority="+ga.gender+" | reason=ACTIVE_GENDER_PROFILE_AUTHORITY");if(meta.gender!==ga.gender){meta.gender=ga.gender;meta.identity.gender=ga.gender;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","GENDER REASSERT | "+ga.gender+" | active_profile="+ga.profileId);}}else{meta.gender=gb.gender;meta.identity.gender=gb.gender;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","GENDER COMMIT | "+gb.gender+" | confidence="+BTT_round(gb.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(gb.score-gn,2)+(ga?" | override=DELIBERATE_IDENTITY":""));}}}
  var winner="",wscore=0,next=0;for(var p in pron)if(pron.hasOwnProperty(p)){var sc=pron[p];if(sc>wscore){next=wscore;wscore=sc;winner=p;}else if(sc>next)next=sc;}if(winner&&wscore>=0.72&&(wscore-next)>=0.18&&winner!==meta.identity.pronouns){var prev=meta.identity.pronouns;meta.identity.previousPronouns=prev;meta.identity.pronouns=winner;meta.identity.pronounGrace=BTT_getConfig().pronoun_grace_turns;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"identity","PRONOUN COMMIT | "+prev+"→"+winner+" | score="+BTT_round(wscore,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(wscore-next,2));}else if(meta.identity.pronounGrace>0)meta.identity.pronounGrace--;
}

/* ============================================================================
   11. ORDINARY ANATOMY EVIDENCE WITH HISTORY AND DECAY
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Ordinary trait commits are confidence-based. The engine collects slot
   mention/ownership/location evidence, transformation/status evidence, and
   promotable trait tokens from entity-owned fragments. Recent failed evidence
   can reinforce later evidence through bounded decay instead of being forgotten
   immediately or permanently accumulated.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_changeEvidence(raw){return BTT_sharedChangeEvidence(raw);}
function BTT_slotScores(raw,entity,ownership){
  /* Adapter for domain code that only has raw text. It consumes
     the same shared slot feature extractor; entity-aware contextual windows are
     available through BTT_sharedEntityFrame when parse/index are known. */
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),base=BTT_sharedBaseSlotEvidence(raw),scores={},reasons={},channels={},slot;
  for(slot in base.scores)if(base.scores.hasOwnProperty(slot)){scores[slot]=base.scores[slot];reasons[slot]=(base.reasons[slot]||[]).slice();channels[slot]=BTT_clone(base.channels[slot]||{});}
  var lower=BTT_lower(raw),poss=/(?:\byour\b|\bmy\b|\bhis\b|\bher\b|\btheir\b)/i.test(raw);if(poss){for(slot in BTT_SLOT_TERMS)if(BTT_SLOT_TERMS.hasOwnProperty(slot)){var terms=BTT_SLOT_TERMS[slot];for(var i=0;i<terms.length;i++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(lower,terms[i])){scores[slot]=(scores[slot]||0)+cfg.slotOwnership*Math.max(0.4,Number(ownership)||0);reasons[slot]=(reasons[slot]||[]).concat(["ownership:"+terms[i]]);break;}}}
  return {scores:scores,reasons:reasons,channels:channels};
}
function BTT_promotableTraitEvidence(raw){return BTT_sharedTraitEvidence(raw);}
function BTT_promotableTraits(raw){var ev=BTT_promotableTraitEvidence(raw),out=[];for(var i=0;i<ev.length;i++)if(Number(ev[i].confidence)>=0.34)out.push(ev[i].trait);return BTT_unique(out);}

function BTT_traitDescriptor(tr){return /^(?:smooth|slick|glistening|sticky|wet|oily|velvety|opalescent|ribbed|ridged|knotted|muscular|sinuous)$/.test(tr);}
function BTT_offslotGenital(slot,tr){return (tr==="vaginal"&&slot!=="vagina")||(tr==="penile"&&slot!=="penis");}
function BTT_selfSlotTrait(slot,tr){return (slot==="vagina"&&/^(?:vaginal|vagina|pussy|slit)$/.test(tr))||(slot==="penis"&&/^(?:penile|penis|cock|dick|shaft)$/.test(tr))||(slot==="ass"&&/^(?:anal|anus|ass|butt)$/.test(tr));}
function BTT_historyKey(slot,trait,mode){return BTT_sharedHistoryKey("trait",[slot,trait,mode||"add"]);}
function BTT_historyScore(meta,key){return BTT_sharedHistoryScore(meta,key);}
function BTT_storeHistory(meta,key,score,raw){BTT_sharedStoreHistory(meta,key,score,raw);}
function BTT_removeLanguage(raw,trait){var surface=trait.replace(/-/g," ");var re=new RegExp("\\b(?:lose|loses|lost|remove|removes|removed|shed|sheds|shed|no longer has|without)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}\\b"+BTT_escapeRegExp(surface).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+"),"i");return re.test(raw);}
function BTT_partialHeritageSlotProfile(frame,slot){
  if(!frame)return null;var refs=frame.profileRefs&&frame.profileRefs.heritage||[];for(var i=0;i<refs.length;i++){var p=refs[i].profile,m=BTT_heritageMarkerFor(p,slot);if(refs[i].boundOwner===frame.entity&&refs[i].boundSlot===slot&&Number(refs[i].confidence)>=0.60&&Number(refs[i].ownerConfidence)>=0.72&&!BTT_markerIsNone(m))return p;}return null;
}
function BTT_initializeCreatedSlotFromEvidence(meta,slot,frame){
  /* Heritage slot markers constrain baseline/profile initialization only. A later
     transformation may create any slot once structural evidence passes. When the
     current clause clearly references an available partial Heritage profile, use
     that profile as the new slot's origin/default trait source; otherwise the
     host Heritage is only a provisional origin and may be replaced by stronger
     acquisition/profile evidence later in the same transaction. */
  var hp=BTT_partialHeritageSlotProfile(frame,slot)||(BTT_profile("heritage",meta.heritage)||BTT_humanHeritageProfile()),origin=hp&&hp.id||meta.heritage||"human",so=BTT_ensureSlot(meta,slot,origin),sp=hp&&hp.slots&&hp.slots[slot],raw=sp&&Array.isArray(sp.traits)?sp.traits:[],caps=BTT_genderCapabilities(meta.gender),gCode=slot==="vagina"?"f":(slot==="penis"?"m":(caps.f&&!caps.m?"f":(caps.m&&!caps.f?"m":"x")));
  for(var i=0;i<raw.length;i++){if(!BTT_traitGateAllowed(raw[i],gCode,caps))continue;var tr=BTT_stripProfileTraitGate(raw[i]);if(tr&&so.traits.indexOf(tr)<0)so.traits.push(tr);}so.updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();return so;
}
function BTT_structurePresence(meta,slot,raw,score,frame){
  if(meta.slots[slot])return false;var sh=frame&&frame.structure&&frame.structure[slot];if(!sh||sh.boundOwner!==meta.entity||sh.boundSlot!==slot||Number(sh.ownerConfidence)<0.72)return false;var conf=Math.max(Number(score)||0,Number(sh.confidence)||0);
  if(conf<0.82)return false;
  if(frame&&frame.fragment)BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"anatomy",frame.fragment,false);
  BTT_initializeCreatedSlotFromEvidence(meta,slot,frame);
  var cues=[];if(sh&&sh.reason)cues.push(sh.reason);if(frame&&frame.slots&&frame.slots.reasons&&frame.slots.reasons[slot])cues=cues.concat(frame.slots.reasons[slot]);
  BTT_touchDebug(meta,"anatomy","STRUCTURE "+slot+" | PASS → CREATE | owner="+meta.entity+" | owner_conf="+BTT_round(sh.ownerConfidence,2)+" | owner_margin="+BTT_round(sh.ownerMargin,2)+" | confidence="+BTT_round(conf,2)+(cues.length?" | cues="+BTT_debugCueText(cues):""));return true;
}

function BTT_fragmentHasCompetingEntity(fragment,entity){
  for(var i=0;i<(fragment.mentions||[]).length;i++)if(fragment.mentions[i].entity!==entity&&fragment.mentions[i].score>=0.70)return true;
  if(fragment.subject&&fragment.subject!==entity&&fragment.subjectConfidence>=0.70)return true;
  if(fragment.addressee&&fragment.addressee!==entity)return true;
  return false;
}
function BTT_anatomyFragments(parse,entity){var frames=BTT_sharedOwnedFrames(parse,entity,0.40),out=[];for(var i=0;i<frames.length;i++){var fr=frames[i];if(fr.phase==="BASELINE")continue;var relevant=(fr.traits&&fr.traits.length)||(fr.change&&fr.change.score>0.12)||BTT_sharedBestSlot(fr).score>0.08||Object.keys(fr.structure||{}).length;if(relevant)out.push({fragment:fr.fragment,ownership:fr.ownership,continuity:fr.continuity,frame:fr});}return out;}
function BTT_traitCandidateTargets(frame,traitEv,ss){
  if(!frame||!traitEv||traitEv.boundOwner!==frame.entity||!traitEv.boundSlot)return [];var slot=traitEv.boundSlot,score=Math.max(Number(ss&&ss.scores&&ss.scores[slot])||0,Math.min(0.48,Number(traitEv.slotConfidence)||0));return [{slot:slot,score:score}];
}
function BTT_processSlotHeritageEvidence(meta,frame,preCausal){
  var refs=frame&&frame.profileRefs&&frame.profileRefs.heritage||[],bySlot={};for(var i=0;i<refs.length;i++){var r=refs[i],slot=r.boundSlot;if(!slot||r.boundOwner!==meta.entity)continue;var score=Number(r.confidence||0)+Math.max(0,Number(r.ownerConfidence||0)-0.55)*0.28+Math.max(0,Number(r.slotConfidence||0))*0.18+Math.max(0,Number(frame.change&&frame.change.score)||0)*0.26+Math.max(0,Number(frame.actuality||0)-0.50)*0.16-(preCausal?0.40:0),row={ref:r,slot:slot,score:score};if(!bySlot[slot])bySlot[slot]=[];bySlot[slot].push(row);}
  for(var slot in bySlot)if(bySlot.hasOwnProperty(slot)&&meta.slots[slot]){var rows=bySlot[slot];rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});var top=rows[0],next=rows[1]||{score:0},candidate=BTT_normId(top.ref.id),old=BTT_inferSlotOrigin(meta,slot),margin=top.score-next.score,change=Number(frame.change&&frame.change.score)||0;if(!candidate||candidate===old)continue;
    /* Replacing an established slot origin is a transformation/correction event,
       not a side effect of mentioning the host's Heritage elsewhere. A foreign
       origin receives the same protection as any other canonical origin. */
    var changeRequired=!!old,changePass=!changeRequired||change>=0.28,pass=top.score>=1.08&&margin>=0.12&&changePass&&!preCausal;BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"anatomy",frame.fragment,false);
    if(pass){BTT_setSlotOriginCanonical(meta,slot,candidate);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"anatomy","SLOT HERITAGE "+slot+" | PASS → "+candidate+" | previous="+(old||"none")+" | owner="+meta.entity+" | score="+BTT_round(top.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(margin,2)+" | change="+BTT_round(change,2));}
    else BTT_touchDebug(meta,"anatomy","SLOT HERITAGE "+slot+" | HOLD | candidate="+candidate+" | previous="+(old||"none")+" | score="+BTT_round(top.score,2)+" | margin="+BTT_round(margin,2)+(changePass?"":" | reason=SLOT_HERITAGE_CHANGE_REQUIRED"));
  }
}

function BTT_processAnatomy(meta,entity,parse,causalIndex){
  var items=BTT_anatomyFragments(parse,entity),cfg=BTT_getConfig();
  for(var i=0;i<items.length;i++){
    var item=items[i],frame=item.frame,f=frame.fragment,own=Number(frame.ownership)||0;if(own<0.34||frame.actuality<0.34)continue;
    var preCausal=causalIndex!==null&&f.index<causalIndex,ss={scores:BTT_clone(frame.slots.scores||{}),reasons:BTT_clone(frame.slots.reasons||{})},change=Math.max(0,Number(frame.change&&frame.change.score)||0),traitEvs=frame.traits||[];
    for(var st in frame.structure)if(frame.structure.hasOwnProperty(st)){
      var stScore=(ss.scores[st]||0)+change*0.30+Math.max(0,frame.actuality-0.5)*0.22+Number(frame.structure[st].confidence||0)*0.42-(preCausal?0.38:0);
      BTT_structurePresence(meta,st,f.raw,stScore,frame);
    }
    BTT_processSlotHeritageEvidence(meta,frame,preCausal);
    for(var t=0;t<traitEvs.length;t++){
      var tev=traitEvs[t],tr=tev.trait,targets=BTT_traitCandidateTargets(frame,tev,ss);
      for(var ti=0;ti<targets.length;ti++){
        var slot=targets[ti].slot,slotScore=targets[ti].score;if(BTT_selfSlotTrait(slot,tr))continue;if(BTT_profile("heritage",tr))continue;if(BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS[tr]&&slot!=="head")continue;if(tev.slots&&tev.slots.length&&tev.slots.indexOf(slot)<0&&!BTT_MENTAL_TRAITS[tr])continue;
        var removal=!!tev.remove||BTT_removeLanguage(f.raw,tr),current=slotScore+Math.min(0.44,change*0.42)+Number(tev.confidence||0)*0.54+Math.max(0,(frame.actuality-0.5)*0.30)+Math.max(0,own-0.55)*0.22-(preCausal?0.36:0);if(BTT_traitDescriptor(tr))current-=0.06;current=Math.max(0,current);
        var key=BTT_historyKey(slot,tr,removal?"remove":"add"),prior=Math.min(0.55,BTT_historyScore(meta,key)),total=current+prior,threshold=removal?cfg.threshold_trait_remove:(BTT_offslotGenital(slot,tr)?cfg.threshold_offslot_genital_add:(BTT_traitDescriptor(tr)?cfg.threshold_descriptor_add:cfg.threshold_trait_add)),cues=[tev.reason].concat((ss.reasons[slot]||[]).slice(0,2));cues.push("owner:"+tev.boundOwner,"owner_conf:"+BTT_round(tev.ownerConfidence,2),"slot_conf:"+BTT_round(tev.slotConfidence,2));
        BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"anatomy",f,false);
        if(total>=threshold){
          if(!meta.slots[slot]&&!BTT_structurePresence(meta,slot,f.raw,total,frame)){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"anatomy","TRAIT "+slot+":"+tr+" | BLOCK | current="+BTT_round(current,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(prior,2)+" | total="+BTT_round(total,2)+" ≥ "+BTT_round(threshold,2)+" | reason=STRUCTURE_ABSENT");continue;}
          var changed=removal?BTT_removeTraitDirect(meta,slot,tr):BTT_addTraitDirect(meta,slot,tr);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"anatomy","TRAIT "+slot+":"+tr+" | PASS → "+(removal?"REMOVE":"ADD")+" | current="+BTT_round(current,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(prior,2)+" | total="+BTT_round(total,2)+" ≥ "+BTT_round(threshold,2)+" | result="+(changed?"COMMIT":"ALREADY_CANONICAL")+(cues.length?" | cues="+BTT_debugCueText(cues):""));BTT_sharedClearHistory(meta,key);
        }else if(total>0.18){
          var retained=Math.min(0.55,Math.max(prior,current*0.48));BTT_storeHistory(meta,key,retained,f.raw);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"anatomy","TRAIT "+slot+":"+tr+" | HOLD | current="+BTT_round(current,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(prior,2)+" | total="+BTT_round(total,2)+" < "+BTT_round(threshold,2)+(preCausal?" | reason=BEFORE_CAUSAL_EVENT":"")+(cues.length?" | cues="+BTT_debugCueText(cues):""));
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================================
   12. DECOUPLED APPEARANCE FIELDS AND AXES
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Appearance axes are independent from ordinary trait thresholds. Shared evidence
   is evaluated chronologically per clause. Explicit numeric/cup assertions set
   that clause's endpoint; softer body-shape language produces one bounded nominal
   delta per axis/direction after confidence passes. Confidence never scales the
   magnitude of a passed physical change. Player appearance refresh is handled later as a
   tagged transport, while NPC appearance remains deterministic.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_setAxis(meta,axis,value,reason,source){
  var ax=meta.appearance.axes,old=BTT_round(axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:Number(ax[axis])||0,2),val=axis==="fem"?BTT_clamp(value,-100,100):BTT_clamp(value,0,100);val=BTT_round(val,2);
  if(axis==="fem"){meta.overallFem=val;ax.fem=val;}else ax[axis]=val;
  if(old!==val){meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh=BTT_round((Number(meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh)||0)+Math.abs(val-old),2);if(source!=="NARRATIVE_CLAUSE")BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"AXIS "+axis+" | "+old+"→"+val+" | source="+String(source||"TURN")+" | reason="+reason);}
}
function BTT_addAxis(meta,axis,delta,reason,source){var cur=axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:meta.appearance.axes[axis];BTT_setAxis(meta,axis,BTT_round((Number(cur)||0)+BTT_round(delta,2),2),reason,source);}
function BTT_setAppearanceField(meta,key,value,reason,fragment){
  value=BTT_cleanSpace(value);if(!value)return;var old=meta.appearance.fields[key]||"";if(old===value)return;meta.appearance.fields[key]=value;
  if(fragment){BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"appearance",fragment,true);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"FIELD "+key+" | PASS | "+(old||"∅")+" → "+value+" | cues="+reason);}else BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"FIELD "+key+" | "+old+"→"+value+" | reason="+reason);
}
function BTT_numberWord(s){var map={one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,eleven:11,twelve:12};s=BTT_lower(s);return map.hasOwnProperty(s)?map[s]:Number(s);}
function BTT_processAppearance(meta,entity,parse,causalIndex){
  var frames=BTT_sharedOwnedFrames(parse,entity,0.40),cfg=BTT_getConfig(),threshold=Number(cfg.axis_evidence_threshold)||0.78,storeMin=Number(cfg.axis_evidence_store_min)||0.50,historyMax=Number(cfg.axis_history_bonus_max)||0.35;
  for(var i=0;i<frames.length;i++){
    var frame=frames[i],f=frame.fragment;if(frame.phase==="BASELINE")continue;var own=Number(frame.ownership)||0,actual=Number(frame.actuality)||0;if(own<0.40||actual<0.40)continue;var preCausal=causalIndex!==null&&f.index<causalIndex,raw=f.raw,m,exactAxes={},genericAppearanceOwner=BTT_resolveGenericObservationOwner(parse,f),headAppearanceOwner=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,"head");
    /* Explicit numeric endpoints are authoritative for their clause only. Later
       clauses may still change the same axis chronologically. */
    m=raw.match(/\b([A-U]{1,2})\s*-?cup\b/i);if(m&&/\b(?:breast|breasts|boob|boobs|chest|cup)\b/i.test(raw)){var cupOwner=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,"breasts");if(cupOwner.entity===entity){var ca=BTT_cupToAxis(m[1]);if(ca!==null){var oldCup=BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.breast_size,2);BTT_setAxis(meta,"breast_size",ca,"explicit cup size","NARRATIVE_CLAUSE");BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"appearance",f,true);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"AXIS breast_size | PASS → SET | "+oldCup+" → "+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.breast_size,2)+" | owner="+entity+" | owner_conf="+BTT_round(cupOwner.confidence,2)+" | cues=explicit cup size");exactAxes.breast_size=true;}}}
    m=raw.match(/\b(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:inch|inches|in\.)\b/i);if(m&&/\b(?:penis|cock|dick|shaft|member)\b/i.test(raw)){var cockOwner=BTT_resolveSlotObservationOwner(parse,f,"penis");if(cockOwner.entity===entity){var oldCock=BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.cock_size,2),setCock=BTT_inchesToAxis(Number(m[1]));BTT_setAxis(meta,"cock_size",setCock,"explicit penile length","NARRATIVE_CLAUSE");BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"appearance",f,true);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"AXIS cock_size | PASS → SET | "+oldCock+" → "+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.cock_size,2)+" | owner="+entity+" | owner_conf="+BTT_round(cockOwner.confidence,2)+" | cues=explicit penile length");exactAxes.cock_size=true;}}

    /* Same-clause cues were already MAX-merged by axis/direction in the shared
       frame. Evaluate them now in narrative order instead of transaction-wide. */
    var rows=[];for(var a=0;a<(frame.axes||[]).length;a++){var ev=frame.axes[a];if(exactAxes[ev.axis])continue;var dir=Number(ev.delta||0)>=0?"+":"-",key=BTT_sharedHistoryKey("axis",[ev.axis,dir]),prior=Math.min(historyMax,BTT_sharedHistoryScore(meta,key)),current=Number(ev.confidence||0)+BTT_additiveContextSupport(own,actual,Number(frame.change&&frame.change.score)||0)-(preCausal?0.42:0),total=Math.max(0,current)+prior;rows.push({ev:ev,dir:dir,key:key,prior:prior,current:Math.max(0,current),total:total});}
    /* Opposing cues for one axis in one clause require winner separation rather
       than cancellation. A close conflict is held and stored separately. */
    var byAxis={};for(var r=0;r<rows.length;r++){var rr=rows[r],axn=rr.ev.axis;if(!byAxis[axn])byAxis[axn]=[];byAxis[axn].push(rr);}
    for(var axis in byAxis)if(byAxis.hasOwnProperty(axis)){var ars=byAxis[axis];ars.sort(function(x,y){return y.total-x.total;});var winner=ars[0],runner=ars.length>1&&ars[1].dir!==winner.dir?ars[1]:null;if(runner&&winner.total-runner.total<0.14){BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"appearance",f,true);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"AXIS "+axis+" | HOLD | "+winner.dir+"="+BTT_round(winner.total,2)+" vs "+runner.dir+"="+BTT_round(runner.total,2)+" | reason=DIRECTION_CONFLICT");continue;}
      var w=winner,pass=w.total>=threshold&&!preCausal,cues=[w.ev.reason];BTT_touchClauseGroup(meta,"appearance",f,true);
      if(pass){var before=BTT_round(axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:meta.appearance.axes[axis],2),delta=BTT_round(Number(w.ev.delta)||0,2);BTT_addAxis(meta,axis,delta,w.ev.reason,"NARRATIVE_CLAUSE");var after=BTT_round(axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:meta.appearance.axes[axis],2);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"AXIS "+axis+" | PASS | "+(delta>=0?"+":"")+delta+" | "+before+" → "+after+" | current="+BTT_round(w.current,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(w.prior,2)+" | total="+BTT_round(w.total,2)+" ≥ "+BTT_round(threshold,2)+" | cues="+BTT_debugCueText(cues));BTT_sharedClearHistory(meta,w.key);BTT_sharedClearHistory(meta,BTT_sharedHistoryKey("axis",[axis,w.dir==="+"?"-":"+"]));}
      else if(w.total>=storeMin){var retained=Math.min(historyMax,Math.max(w.prior,w.current*0.42));BTT_sharedStoreHistory(meta,w.key,retained,raw);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"AXIS "+axis+" | HOLD | current="+BTT_round(w.current,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(w.prior,2)+" | total="+BTT_round(w.total,2)+" < "+BTT_round(threshold,2)+(preCausal?" | reason=BEFORE_CAUSAL_EVENT":"")+" | cues="+BTT_debugCueText(cues));}
    }

    m=raw.match(/\b(pale|olive|tan|tanned|brown|dark|fair|ebony|porcelain|smooth|slick|green|gray|grey|crimson|violet|blue|bronze|golden)\s+skin\b/i);if(m&&frame.change.score>0.10&&genericAppearanceOwner.entity===entity&&genericAppearanceOwner.confidence>=0.62)BTT_setAppearanceField(meta,"skin",BTT_lower(m[1]).replace("grey","gray")+" skin","owned transformed description",f);
    m=raw.match(/\b(blond|blonde|black|brown|dark brown|red|auburn|white|silver|pink|blue|green|purple|gray|grey)\s+(?:hair|locks)\b/i);if(m&&frame.change.score>0.10&&headAppearanceOwner.entity===entity&&headAppearanceOwner.confidence>=0.72)BTT_setAppearanceField(meta,"hair_color",BTT_lower(m[1]).replace("grey","gray"),"owned transformed description",f);
    m=raw.match(/\b(bald|shaved|cropped|short|shoulder-length|medium|moderate|long|very long|waist-length)\s+(?:hair|locks)\b/i);if(m&&frame.change.score>0.10&&headAppearanceOwner.entity===entity&&headAppearanceOwner.confidence>=0.72)BTT_setAppearanceField(meta,"hair_length",BTT_normalizeHairLength(BTT_lower(m[1])),"owned transformed description",f);
    m=raw.match(/\b(blue|green|brown|hazel|gray|grey|silver|gold|amber|red|pink|purple|black)\s+eyes?\b/i);if(m&&frame.change.score>0.10&&headAppearanceOwner.entity===entity&&headAppearanceOwner.confidence>=0.72)BTT_setAppearanceField(meta,"eye_color",BTT_lower(m[1]).replace("grey","gray"),"owned transformed description",f);
    m=raw.match(/\b(broad|slender|lean|stocky|athletic|curvy|petite|muscular|thick|soft|lithe)\s+(?:build|frame)\b/i);if(m&&frame.change.score>0.10&&genericAppearanceOwner.entity===entity&&genericAppearanceOwner.confidence>=0.62)BTT_setAppearanceField(meta,"build",BTT_lower(m[1])+" build","owned transformed description",f);
    m=raw.match(/\b(?:scent|smell|aroma)\b[^.!?]{0,28}\b(?:becomes?|turns?|changes? to|is now)\b[^.!?]{0,20}\b(sweet|musky|honeyed|floral|earthy|citrus|spicy|ozone)\b/i);if(m&&genericAppearanceOwner.entity===entity&&genericAppearanceOwner.confidence>=0.62)BTT_setAppearanceField(meta,"scent",BTT_lower(m[1]),"owned transformed description",f);
  }
}

/* ============================================================================
   13. STATEFUL CONDITIONS, REPRODUCTION, AND CONDITION REMOVAL
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Conditions are canonical structured state, not ordinary slot traits. Causal
   Conditions resolve receiver, source, receiving slot, mechanism, actuality,
   and completion from the shared parse graph. Reproductive type is determined
   from both source/mechanism and receiver biology. Specialized implantation can
   bypass ordinary fertility/virility rules when the mechanism itself supports it.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_conditionType(t){t=BTT_normId(t);if(/^(?:pregnant|pregnancy|fertilized|fertilization)$/.test(t))return "pregnancy";if(/^(?:incubating|incubation|egg-bearing|eggs|gravid|brooding)$/.test(t))return "incubation";if(/^(?:seeded|seeding|seed-bearing)$/.test(t))return "seeded";if(/^(?:infested|infestation|parasite|parasitic)$/.test(t))return "infestation";if(/^(?:possessed|possession)$/.test(t))return "possession";if(/^(?:infected|infection)$/.test(t))return "infection";if(/^(?:corrupted|corruption)$/.test(t))return "corruption";if(/^(?:pollinated|pollination)$/.test(t))return "pollination";return t||"condition";}
