function BTT_conditionTrait(t,count){t=BTT_conditionType(t);var map={pregnancy:"pregnant",incubation:"incubating",seeded:"seeded",infestation:"infested",possession:"possessed",infection:"infected",corruption:"corrupted",pollination:"pollinated"},x=map[t]||t;return count>1?x+" x"+count:x;}
function BTT_conditionFamily(t){t=BTT_conditionType(t);if(["pregnancy","incubation","seeded","pollination"].indexOf(t)>=0)return "reproductive";if(["infestation","infection"].indexOf(t)>=0)return "biological";if(t==="possession")return "spiritual";if(t==="corruption")return "corruption";return t;}
function BTT_allMetaTraits(meta){var set={};for(var s in meta.slots)if(meta.slots.hasOwnProperty(s)){var a=meta.slots[s].traits||[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)set[BTT_lower(a[i])]=1;}return set;}
function BTT_hasAnyTrait(meta,traits){var set=BTT_allMetaTraits(meta);for(var i=0;i<traits.length;i++)if(set[BTT_lower(traits[i])])return true;return false;}
function BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,trait){var s=meta&&meta.slots&&meta.slots[slot];return !!(s&&s.traits&&s.traits.indexOf(trait)>=0);}
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   SHARED EXPLICIT OWNER→SLOT EXTRACTION
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   This is lexical evidence used by the combined confidence engine. Expensive
   raw-text work is performed once per fragment/entity during
   BTT_buildSharedEvidence(). Downstream gameplay domains may only read
   BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(); they must never rebuild possessor/slot regexes.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
var BTT_OWNED_SLOT_LEXICON=null;
function BTT_ownedSlotLexicon(){
  if(BTT_OWNED_SLOT_LEXICON)return BTT_OWNED_SLOT_LEXICON;var termSlots={},surfaces=[];
  for(var slot in BTT_SLOT_TERMS)if(BTT_SLOT_TERMS.hasOwnProperty(slot)&&slot!=="misc"){
    var terms=BTT_SLOT_TERMS[slot]||[];for(var i=0;i<terms.length;i++){var surface=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(terms[i]));if(!surface)continue;if(!termSlots[surface]){termSlots[surface]=[];surfaces.push(surface);}if(termSlots[surface].indexOf(slot)<0)termSlots[surface].push(slot);}
  }
  /* Longest-first keeps multiword anatomy such as "shoulder blades" intact. */
  surfaces.sort(function(a,b){return b.length-a.length;});var pattern=surfaces.map(function(x){return BTT_escapeRegExp(x).replace(/\ /g,"\\s+");}).join("|");
  BTT_OWNED_SLOT_LEXICON={pattern:pattern,termSlots:termSlots};return BTT_OWNED_SLOT_LEXICON;
}
function BTT_entitySlotPrefixes(entity,f,parse){
  var xs=[],quoted=!!(f&&f.quoted);
  if(entity==="Player"){
    if(!quoted)xs.push("your","my");if(quoted&&f.speaker===entity)xs.push("my");if(quoted&&f.addressee===entity)xs.push("your");
  }else{
    var aliases=BTT_entityAliases(entity),sources=(parse&&parse.sources||[]).concat(parse&&parse.admitted||[]);
    for(var si=0;si<sources.length;si++)if(sources[si]&&sources[si].entity===entity)aliases=aliases.concat(sources[si].aliases||[],sources[si].name||[]);
    aliases=BTT_unique(aliases);for(var i=0;i<aliases.length;i++){var av=BTT_cleanSpace(aliases[i]);if(av&&!/^(?:he|she|they|him|her|them|his|their)$/i.test(av))xs.push(BTT_escapeRegExp(av).replace(/\ /g,"\\s+")+"(?:['’]s)?");}
    if(quoted&&f.speaker===entity)xs.push("my");if(quoted&&f.addressee===entity)xs.push("your");if((f&&f.mentions||[]).some(function(m){return m.entity===entity&&m.alias==="pronoun"&&m.score>=0.50;}))xs.push("his","her","their");
  }
  return BTT_unique(xs).sort(function(a,b){return b.length-a.length;});
}
function BTT_extractOwnedSlotEvidence(f,entity,parse){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),prefixes=BTT_entitySlotPrefixes(entity,f,parse),rows=[];if(!raw||!prefixes.length)return rows;
  var lex=BTT_ownedSlotLexicon(),prefixPattern="(?:"+prefixes.join("|")+")",ownerRx=new RegExp("\\b"+prefixPattern+"((?:\\s+[A-Za-z][A-Za-z'-]*){1,5})","ig"),termRx=new RegExp("\\b("+lex.pattern+")\\b","ig"),m;
  function add(slot,score,term,why,index){rows.push({slot:slot,confidence:score,term:term,reason:why,index:Number(index)||0});}
  /* Capture each owned anatomical phrase once, then inspect its short local word
     window. A slot term is eligible only when at most three descriptor words sit
     between possessor and slot, preserving the former ownership grammar without
     constructing a new regex for every prefix × slot × term combination. */
  while((m=ownerRx.exec(raw))){var segment=String(m[1]||""),segmentStart=m.index+m[0].length-segment.length;termRx.lastIndex=0;var tm;
    while((tm=termRx.exec(segment))){var before=BTT_cleanSpace(segment.slice(0,tm.index)),words=before?before.split(/\s+/).length:0;if(words<=3){var surface=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(tm[1])),slots=lex.termSlots[surface]||[];for(var si=0;si<slots.length;si++)add(slots[si],0.99,surface,"explicit_owned_slot",segmentStart+tm.index);}if(!tm[0].length)termRx.lastIndex++;}
    if(!m[0].length)ownerRx.lastIndex++;
  }
  /* Preserve the higher-confidence compound semantics used by off-slot anatomy. */
  var tailRx=new RegExp("\\b"+prefixPattern+"(?:\\s+[A-Za-z][A-Za-z'-]*){0,2}\\s+tail\\s+(?:pussy|vagina|slit|opening)\\b","i"),tailMatch=raw.match(tailRx);if(tailMatch)add("tail",1.04,"tail pussy","compound_offslot_genital",tailMatch.index);
  var oralRx=new RegExp("\\b"+prefixPattern+"(?:\\s+[A-Za-z][A-Za-z'-]*){0,2}\\s+(?:mouth|throat)\\b","i"),oralMatch=raw.match(oralRx);if(oralMatch)add("head",1.01,"mouth/throat","owned_oral_slot",oralMatch.index);
  rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.confidence-a.confidence||a.index-b.index;});var ded=[];for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(!ded.some(function(x){return x.slot===rows[i].slot;}))ded.push(rows[i]);return ded;
}
function BTT_buildOwnedSlotEvidenceCache(parse){
  if(!parse||!parse.fragments)return parse;parse.shared=parse.shared||{};parse.shared.ownedSlotEvidence={};parse.shared.ownedSlotEntities={};var entities=BTT_sharedEntityList(parse);
  for(var fi=0;fi<parse.fragments.length;fi++){var f=parse.fragments[fi],present=[];for(var ei=0;ei<entities.length;ei++){var entity=entities[ei],rows=BTT_extractOwnedSlotEvidence(f,entity,parse),key=String(fi)+"|"+entity;parse.shared.ownedSlotEvidence[key]=rows;if(rows.length)present.push(entity);}parse.shared.ownedSlotEntities[String(fi)]=present;}
  return parse;
}
function BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,index,entity){
  if(!parse||!parse.shared||!parse.shared.ownedSlotEvidence)return [];var key=String(Number(index)||0)+"|"+String(entity||"");return parse.shared.ownedSlotEvidence[key]||[];
}
function BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEntities(parse,index){
  if(!parse||!parse.shared||!parse.shared.ownedSlotEntities)return [];return parse.shared.ownedSlotEntities[String(Number(index)||0)]||[];
}
function BTT_deliveryReachEvidence(f,entrySlot,receiver,parse){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),lower=BTT_lower(raw),finalSlot=entrySlot,score=0.55,reasons=[],owned=BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,f&&f.index,receiver),ownedGut=owned.some(function(x){return x.slot==="gut";});
  var directedGut=/\b(?:into|inside|within|down into|down to|reaches?|reach(?:es|ed|ing)?|fills?|floods?)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\b(?:the\s+)?(?:gut|stomach|abdomen|belly|intestines?|digestive tract)\b/i.test(raw);
  if(ownedGut||directedGut){finalSlot="gut";score=0.96;reasons.push("explicit_receiver_internal_destination");}
  else if(entrySlot==="head"&&/\b(?:swallow|swallows|swallowed|ingest|ingests|ingested|down (?:your|his|her|their) throat|into (?:your|his|her|their) throat|deep in (?:your|his|her|their) throat)\b/i.test(raw)){finalSlot="gut";score=0.86;reasons.push("oral_to_gut_ingestion");}
  else if(entrySlot==="ass"&&/\b(?:deep|fully|far inside|rectum|intestinal|flood(?:s|ed|ing)?|fill(?:s|ed|ing)?|pump(?:s|ed|ing)?|large amount|copious)\b/i.test(lower)){finalSlot="gut";score=0.76;reasons.push("anal_to_gut_depth_amount");}
  return {entrySlot:entrySlot,finalSlot:finalSlot,confidence:score,reasons:reasons};
}
function BTT_deliveryDestination(f,sourceEntity,parse,work){
  var byReceiver={},transfer=/\b(?:into|inside|within|engulf|engulfs|engulfed|take|takes|took|receive|receives|received|flood|floods|flooded|fill|fills|filled|deposit|deposits|deposited|release|releases|released|ejaculat|swallow|ingest|penetrat|insert|enter)\b/i.test(String(f&&f.raw||"")),candidates=BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEntities(parse,f&&f.index);
  for(var ci=0;ci<candidates.length;ci++){
    var entity=candidates[ci];if(!work||!work.hasOwnProperty(entity)||entity===sourceEntity)continue;var owned=BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,f.index,entity),mention=BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity),best=null;
    for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){var r=owned[i],reach=BTT_deliveryReachEvidence(f,r.slot,entity,parse),score=BTT_clamp(r.confidence+(transfer?0.10:0)+Math.max(0,mention-0.55)*0.08,0,1.08),row={receiver:entity,entrySlot:r.slot,finalSlot:reach.finalSlot,confidence:score,reachConfidence:reach.confidence,reasons:[r.reason].concat(reach.reasons)};if(!best||row.confidence>best.confidence)best=row;}
    if(best)byReceiver[entity]=best;
  }
  var rows=[];for(var e in byReceiver)if(byReceiver.hasOwnProperty(e))rows.push(byReceiver[e]);rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.confidence-a.confidence;});var top=rows[0]||{receiver:"",entrySlot:"",finalSlot:"",confidence:0,reachConfidence:0,reasons:[]},next=rows[1]||{confidence:0};top.margin=BTT_round(top.confidence-next.confidence,3);return top;
}
function BTT_reproductiveSourceSlot(f,source,receiver,sourceMeta,parse){
  if(!source||!source.entity||!sourceMeta)return {slot:"",confidence:0,reason:"source_slot_unresolved"};var owned=BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,f&&f.index,source.entity);if(owned.length)return {slot:owned[0].slot,confidence:owned[0].confidence,reason:owned[0].reason};var fr=BTT_sharedEntityFrame(parse,source.entity,f.index),best=BTT_sharedBestSlot(fr);if(best.slot&&best.score>=0.48&&sourceMeta.slots[best.slot])return {slot:best.slot,confidence:BTT_clamp(best.score,0,0.78),reason:"source_frame_slot"};return {slot:"",confidence:0,reason:"source_slot_unresolved"};
}
function BTT_slotHasAny(meta,slot,traits){for(var i=0;i<(traits||[]).length;i++)if(BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,BTT_normId(traits[i])))return true;return false;}
function BTT_uniqueSlotWithTraits(meta,traits){var rows=[];for(var sl in (meta&&meta.slots||{}))if(meta.slots.hasOwnProperty(sl)){var ok=true;for(var i=0;i<(traits||[]).length;i++)if(!BTT_slotHas(meta,sl,BTT_normId(traits[i]))){ok=false;break;}if(ok)rows.push(sl);}return rows.length===1?{slot:rows[0],confidence:0.64,reason:"unique_source_trait_slot"}:{slot:"",confidence:0,reason:rows.length?"ambiguous_source_trait_slots":"no_source_trait_slot"};}
function BTT_conditionSlotFromFragment(f,receiver,parse){var scores=parse?BTT_sharedEntityFrame(parse,receiver,f.index).slots:BTT_slotScores(f.raw,receiver,BTT_fragmentMention(f,receiver)),best="",bs=0;for(var s in scores.scores)if(scores.scores.hasOwnProperty(s)&&scores.scores[s]>bs){bs=scores.scores[s];best=s;}if(best&&bs>=0.24)return {slot:best,confidence:BTT_clamp(bs,0,1)};return {slot:"",confidence:0};}
function BTT_reproductiveReceivingSlot(f,receiver,source,meta,parse){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),rows=[],owned=BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,f&&f.index,receiver),frame=parse?BTT_sharedEntityFrame(parse,receiver,f.index):null;
  function add(slot,score,why){if(meta&&meta.slots&&meta.slots[slot])rows.push({slot:slot,confidence:score,reason:why});}
  for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){var os=owned[i];if(BTT_slotHas(meta,os.slot,"vaginal")||BTT_slotHas(meta,os.slot,"receptive")||os.slot==="vagina")add(os.slot,Math.max(0.90,os.confidence),os.reason+":eligible_reproductive_slot");}
  if(frame){for(var sl in meta.slots)if(meta.slots.hasOwnProperty(sl)&&(BTT_slotHas(meta,sl,"vaginal")||BTT_slotHas(meta,sl,"receptive")||sl==="vagina")){var sc=Number(frame.slots.scores[sl]||0);if(sc>=0.28)add(sl,BTT_clamp(0.62+sc*0.32,0,0.92),"shared_frame_reproductive_slot");}}
  if(!rows.length&&meta&&meta.slots.vagina&&/\b(?:inseminat|impregnat|fertiliz|ejaculat|breed|bred|deposit|release|fill|flood|reproductive material|semen|cum)\b/i.test(raw))add("vagina",0.68,"receiver_default_reproductive_slot");
  if(!rows.length){var generic=BTT_conditionSlotFromFragment(f,receiver,parse);if(generic.slot&&(!source||generic.slot!=="penis"||receiver===String(source.entity||"")))rows.push({slot:generic.slot,confidence:Math.min(0.62,generic.confidence),reason:"generic_fallback"});}
  rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.confidence-a.confidence;});return rows[0]||{slot:"",confidence:0,reason:""};
}
function BTT_conditionReceiver(f,parse){
  var best={entity:"",confidence:0};if(f.addressee){best={entity:f.addressee,confidence:0.90};}if(f.subject&&f.subjectConfidence>best.confidence)best={entity:f.subject,confidence:f.subjectConfidence};var poss=BTT_resolvePossessor(f.raw,parse);if(poss.entity&&poss.score>best.confidence)best={entity:poss.entity,confidence:poss.score};for(var i=0;i<(f.mentions||[]).length;i++)if(f.mentions[i].score>best.confidence)best={entity:f.mentions[i].entity,confidence:f.mentions[i].score};return best;
}
/**
 * Reproductive conditions use directional receiver evidence instead of treating
 * every tracked entity as the receiver in a separate pass. This prevents one
 * two-party event from being replayed with source and receiver reversed.
 */
function BTT_reproductiveReceiverCandidates(f,parse,work){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),rows=[],objectPronoun=/\b(?:her|him|them)\b/i,possessivePronoun=/\b(?:her|his|their)\s+(?:womb|uterus|vagina|cervix|abdomen|belly|body|host|cavity)\b/i,destinationPronoun=/\b(?:inside|into|within)\s+(?:her|him|them)\b/i;
  function aliasesFor(entity){return BTT_entityAliases(entity).filter(function(a){return a&&!/^(?:you|your|yourself)$/i.test(a);});}
  function isPronounFocus(entity){return (f.mentions||[]).some(function(m){return m.entity===entity&&m.alias==="pronoun"&&m.score>=0.50;});}
  function transitiveObjectPronoun(){return /\b(?:inseminat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|impregnat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|fertiliz(?:e|es|ed|ing)|breed(?:s|ing|ed)?|bred|ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|deposit(?:s|ed|ing)?|release(?:s|d|ing)?|fill(?:s|ed|ing)?|implant(?:s|ed|ing)?|oviposit(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\b(?:her|him|them)\b/i.test(raw);}
  for(var entity in work)if(work.hasOwnProperty(entity)){
    var score=0,reasons=[],meta=work[entity],mention=BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity),focused=isPronounFocus(entity),aliases=aliasesFor(entity),a;
    if(mention>0){score=Math.max(score,mention*0.48);reasons.push("mention="+BTT_round(mention*0.48,2));}
    for(a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){
      var nm=BTT_escapeRegExp(aliases[a]).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");
      if(new RegExp("\\b(?:inseminat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|impregnat(?:e|es|ed|ing)|fertiliz(?:e|es|ed|ing)|breed(?:s|ing|ed)?|bred|implant(?:s|ed|ing)?|oviposit(?:s|ed|ing)?)\\b[^.!?]{0,55}\\b"+nm+"\\b","i").test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.98);reasons.push("directional_verb_name");}
      if(new RegExp("\\b(?:inside|into|within)\\s+"+nm+"\\b","i").test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.94);reasons.push("named_destination_receiver");}
      if(new RegExp("\\b"+nm+"(?:'s)?\\b[^.!?]{0,45}\\b(?:womb|uterus|vagina|cervix|body|abdomen|belly|gut|host|cavity)\\b","i").test(raw)&&/\b(?:inside|into|within|fill|flood|deposit|implant|inseminat|fertiliz|pregnan|release|ejaculat)\b/i.test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.95);reasons.push("named_receiving_body");}
      if(new RegExp("\\b"+nm+"\\b[^.!?]{0,35}\\b(?:is|was|becomes?|became|left)\\b[^.!?]{0,15}\\bpregnant\\b","i").test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.97);reasons.push("named_pregnancy_result");}
    }
    /* Pronouns are only promoted when the shared discourse parser already tied
       them to this NPC. This preserves generalized entity resolution while
       allowing grammatical direct objects such as "inseminate them" to carry
       the same directional authority as a repeated proper name. */
    if(entity!=="Player"&&focused){
      if(transitiveObjectPronoun()&&objectPronoun.test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.97);reasons.push("direct_object_pronoun");}
      if(possessivePronoun.test(raw)&&/\b(?:inside|into|within|fill|flood|deposit|implant|inseminat|fertiliz|release|ejaculat)\b/i.test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.96);reasons.push("possessive_receiving_anatomy");}
      if(destinationPronoun.test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.94);reasons.push("pronoun_destination_receiver");}
    }
    if(entity==="Player"&&/\b(?:your womb|your uterus|your vagina|your cervix|inside you|into you|within you)\b/i.test(raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.96);reasons.push("second_person_receiving_body");}
    /* A strong transitive causal subject is normally the source. Do not let the
       generic second-person/name mention score compete as receiver when the
       clause has an independently resolved object receiver. Passive/reflexive
       constructions are deliberately exempt. */
    if(f.subject===entity&&f.subjectConfidence>=0.75&&transitiveObjectPronoun()&&!/\b(?:yourself|himself|herself|themselves)\b/i.test(raw)&&!new RegExp("\\b(?:you|"+aliases.map(BTT_escapeRegExp).join("|")+")\\s+(?:are|were|get|got)\\s+(?:inseminated|impregnated|fertilized)","i").test(raw)){
      score=Math.min(score,0.18);reasons.push("source_subject_exclusion");
    }
    var sl=BTT_reproductiveReceivingSlot(f,entity,null,meta,parse);if(sl.slot&&BTT_receivingSlotEligible(meta,sl.slot,"ordinary",sl.confidence)){var actualTransfer=/\b(?:inseminat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|impregnat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|fertiliz(?:e|es|ed|ing|ation)|ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|breed(?:s|ing|ed)?|bred|deposit(?:s|ed|ing)?|releas(?:e|es|ed|ing)|fill(?:s|ed|ing)?|flood(?:s|ed|ing)?|finish(?:es|ed|ing)?|climax(?:es|ed|ing)?|semen|cum)\b/i.test(raw);if(actualTransfer&&BTT_sharedOwnedSlotEvidence(parse,f&&f.index,entity).some(function(x){return x.slot===sl.slot;})){score=Math.max(score,0.96);reasons.push("explicit_owned_receiving_slot:"+sl.slot);}else{score=Math.min(1,score+0.06);reasons.push("eligible_receiving_slot:"+sl.slot);}}
    rows.push({entity:entity,confidence:BTT_clamp(score,0,1),reasons:reasons});
  }
  rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.confidence-a.confidence;});var top=rows[0]||{entity:"",confidence:0,reasons:[]},next=rows[1]||{confidence:0};top.margin=BTT_round(top.confidence-next.confidence,3);top.ranking=rows;return top;
}
function BTT_conditionSourceCandidates(f,parse,receiver,work){
  var rows=[];for(var entity in work)if(work.hasOwnProperty(entity)&&entity!==receiver){var score=0,reasons=[];
    if(f.subject===entity){score=Math.max(score,f.subjectConfidence);reasons.push("causal_subject");}
    for(var i=0;i<(f.mentions||[]).length;i++)if(f.mentions[i].entity===entity){score=Math.max(score,f.mentions[i].score*0.70);reasons.push("mention");}
    var aliases=BTT_entityAliases(entity);for(var a=0;a<aliases.length;a++){var nm=BTT_escapeRegExp(aliases[a]).replace(/\\ /g,"\\s+");if(new RegExp("\\b"+nm+"\\b[^.!?]{0,55}\\b(?:inseminat|impregnat|fertiliz|ejaculat|breed|bred|implant|deposit|release|oviposit)","i").test(f.raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.96);reasons.push("directional_source_name");}}
    if(entity==="Player"&&/^\s*(?:>\s*)?you\b/i.test(String(f.raw||""))&&/\b(?:inseminat|impregnat|fertiliz|ejaculat|breed|bred|implant|deposit|release|fill|finish|climax)\b/i.test(f.raw)){score=Math.max(score,0.96);reasons.push("second_person_causal_subject");}
    rows.push({entity:entity,confidence:BTT_clamp(score,0,1),descriptor:"tracked",reasons:reasons});}
  rows.sort(function(a,b){return b.confidence-a.confidence;});var top=rows[0]||{entity:"",confidence:0,descriptor:"",reasons:[]},next=rows[1]||{confidence:0};if(!top.entity){var fallback=BTT_conditionSource(f,parse,receiver);top=fallback;}top.margin=BTT_round(Number(top.confidence||0)-Number(next.confidence||0),3);return top;
}
/**
 * Builds an action-local logical reproductive event key. Input and Output hooks
 * often restate the same causal event with different wording, so exact-text
 * hashes are deliberately excluded unless the prose explicitly marks another
 * distinct exposure. This keeps paraphrases idempotent without collapsing an
 * authored "again/another/second" event in the same action.
 */
function BTT_reproductiveLogicalSignature(f,receiver,source,type,mechanism){
  var raw=String(f&&f.raw||""),repeat=/\b(?:again|another|additional|second|third|separate|separately|once more|twice|two times|three times)\b/i.test(raw),suffix=repeat?("|repeat="+BTT_hash(BTT_lower(raw).replace(/\s+/g," "))):"";
  return "condition-repro|"+String(BTT_nowAction()||BTT_getTurn())+"|"+receiver+"|"+String(source&&source.entity||source&&source.descriptor||"")+"|"+String(type||"")+"|"+String(mechanism||"")+suffix;
}
function BTT_conditionSource(f,parse,receiver){
  var best={entity:"",confidence:0,descriptor:""};
  if(f.subject&&f.subject!==receiver&&f.subjectConfidence>best.confidence)best={entity:f.subject,confidence:f.subjectConfidence,descriptor:"subject"};
  for(var i=0;i<(f.mentions||[]).length;i++){var m=f.mentions[i];if(m.entity!==receiver&&m.score>best.confidence)best={entity:m.entity,confidence:m.score,descriptor:"mention"};}
  if(best.entity)return best;
  /* Untracked sources are allowed as event-local evidence. This captures a
     causal noun phrase without creating an NPC or mutating canonical Heritage.
     Available Heritage cards may later describe its biology for this event. */
  var raw=String(f.raw||""),m2=raw.match(/\b(?:a|an|the)\s+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z'’\-]*(?:\s+[A-Za-z][A-Za-z'’\-]*){0,3})\s+(?:injects?|plants?|lays?|deposits?|implants?|inseminates?|impregnates?|breeds?|fertilizes?|ejaculates?|pollinates?|infests?|possesses?|corrupts?|infects?)\b/i);
  if(m2){var d=BTT_cleanSpace(m2[1]);if(d&&!/^(?:you|he|she|they|player)$/i.test(d))return {entity:"",confidence:0.76,descriptor:d};}
  m2=raw.match(/\b(plant(?: creature)?|vine|flower|parasite|creature|spirit|demon|ghost|ovipositor)\b/i);if(m2)return {entity:"",confidence:0.62,descriptor:BTT_cleanSpace(m2[1])};
  return best;
}
/**
 * Builds an ephemeral source-biological view for one Condition event. It can
 * consume an available Heritage/Gender profile named in prose without admitting
 * a new NPC and without committing that prose to canonical story state.
 */
function BTT_eventLocalSourceMeta(f,source){
  if(source&&source.entity)return BTT_findEntity(source.entity);var text=BTT_cleanSpace((source&&source.descriptor?source.descriptor+" ":"")+String(f&&f.raw||"")),hp=BTT_findProfileByIdentity(text,"heritage",false),gp=BTT_findProfileByIdentity(text,"gender",false);if(!hp&&!gp)return null;var m=BTT_newMeta("EVENT_SOURCE","event source"),hid=hp&&hp.id||((BTT_humanHeritageProfile()||{}).id||"human"),gid=gp&&gp.id||"hermaphrodite";BTT_reinitializeFromProfiles(m,hid,gid,"event-source|"+hid+"|"+gid);m.initialized=true;return m;
}
function BTT_eggTransferInward(raw){
  /* Direction matters: laying/depositing eggs into a receiver is an implantation
     event, while a receiver laying their existing eggs is lifecycle resolution.
     The transferred quantity may appear between the verb and the word "eggs". */
  var text=String(raw||"");
  if(/\b(?:oviposits?|oviposited|lays?|laid|deposits?|deposited|implants?|implanted)\b[^.!?]{0,36}\b(?:(?:\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|a|an|some|several|multiple)\s+)?eggs?\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:inside|into|within)\b[^.!?]{0,20}(?:(?:your|his|her|their|the)\s+|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’\-]+['’]s\s+)?(?:vagina|womb|uterus|body|abdomen|belly|gut|stomach|rectum|ass|anus|cavity|host)\b/i.test(text))return true;
  return /\boviposit(?:s|ed|ing|ion)?\b[^.!?]{0,60}\b(?:inside|into|within)\b[^.!?]{0,30}(?:vagina|womb|uterus|body|abdomen|belly|gut|stomach|rectum|ass|anus|cavity|host)\b/i.test(text);
}
function BTT_mechanismEvidence(f,sourceMeta,receiverMeta,sourceSlot){
  var raw=BTT_lower(f.raw),ordinary=/\b(?:inseminat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|breed|breeds|breeding|bred|ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|internal reproductive exposure|fertiliz(?:e|es|ed|ing|ation)|impregnat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|creampie(?:s|d)?|semen|cum|finish(?:es|ed|ing)?\s+(?:inside|in)|climax(?:es|ed|ing)?\s+(?:inside|in))\b/.test(raw),internal=/\b(?:inside|into|within|fill|flood|deposit|implant|release|ejaculat)\b/.test(raw);
  if(BTT_eggTransferInward(f.raw)||/\b(?:ovipositor|egg[- ]stuff|egg implantation)\b/.test(raw))return {kind:"egg",confidence:0.97,reasons:["explicit_egg_transfer"]};
  var literalSeed=/\b(?:plants?|planted|injects?|injected|implants?|implanted|deposits?|deposited)\b[^.!?]{0,28}\b(?:seeds|spores)\b|\b(?:seeds|spores)\b[^.!?]{0,28}\b(?:inside|into|within)\b/.test(raw);if(literalSeed)return {kind:"seed",confidence:0.96,reasons:["explicit_seed_transfer"]};
  var explicitParasite=/\b(?:infests?|infested|infestation|transmits?\s+(?:a\s+)?parasite|parasites?\s+(?:take|takes|took)\s+hold|implants?\s+(?:a\s+|the\s+)?parasite|deposits?\s+larvae|larvae?\s+(?:inside|into|within)|parasitic\s+implantation|parasitic\s+conception)\b/.test(raw);if(explicitParasite)return {kind:"parasite",confidence:0.96,reasons:["explicit_parasite_propagation_or_implantation"]};
  if(/\b(?:pollen|pollinat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion))\b/.test(raw))return {kind:"pollen",confidence:0.93,reasons:["explicit_pollination"]};
  var sl=sourceSlot&&sourceSlot.slot||"",slotParasitic=!!(sourceMeta&&sl&&BTT_slotHas(sourceMeta,sl,"parasitic-conception")),inferredParasitic=!sl&&sourceMeta?BTT_uniqueSlotWithTraits(sourceMeta,["parasitic-conception"]):{slot:"",confidence:0};
  /* Specialized biology is supporting evidence, not a mode switch. Without
     event-local infestation/implantation semantics, an ordinary reproductive
     transfer remains ordinary conception. */
  if(ordinary&&internal&&slotParasitic&&/\b(?:parasite|parasitic|infest|implant|larva|propagat)\b/.test(raw))return {kind:"parasite",confidence:0.88,reasons:["source_slot_parasitic_conception","specialized_event_semantics"]};
  if(ordinary&&internal&&!sl&&inferredParasitic.slot&&Number(inferredParasitic.confidence)>=0.60&&/\b(?:parasitic conception|parasite-bearing reproductive|transmissible parasite|parasite implantation)\b/.test(raw))return {kind:"parasite",confidence:0.84,reasons:["explicit_specialized_semantics","unique_source_slot_parasitic_conception"]};
  if(sourceMeta&&(ordinary||/\bseed\b/.test(raw))){if(sl&&BTT_slotHasAny(sourceMeta,sl,["oviparous","egg-laying","ovipositor","clutch-bearing"]))return {kind:"egg",confidence:0.90,reasons:["source_slot_egg_reproductive_mode"]};if(sl&&BTT_slotHasAny(sourceMeta,sl,["seed-bearing","pollinating"]))return {kind:"seed",confidence:0.90,reasons:["source_slot_seed_reproductive_mode"]};}
  if(ordinary||/\b(?:seed|reproductive material)\b/.test(raw)&&/\b(?:inside|into|within|womb|uterus|vagina|cervix)\b/.test(raw))return {kind:"ordinary",confidence:0.90,reasons:slotParasitic?["ordinary_reproduction","specialized_biology_without_specialized_event_evidence"]:["ordinary_reproduction"]};
  return {kind:"ordinary",confidence:0.55,reasons:[]};
}
function BTT_receivingSlotEligible(meta,slot,mechanism,slotConfidence){
  if(!meta||!slot)return false;if(slot==="vagina"&&meta.slots.vagina)return true;if(BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"vaginal")||BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"receptive"))return true;if(mechanism!=="ordinary"&&slotConfidence>=0.52&&meta.slots[slot])return true;return false;
}
function BTT_slotFertility(meta,slot,mechanism){var f=Number(meta.appearance.axes.fertility)||0;if(slot!=="vagina"&&BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"vaginal")&&!BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"sterile")&&!BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"infertile"))f=Math.max(f,20);if(BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"fertile"))f+=16;if(BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"receptive"))f+=16;if(BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"infertile"))f-=25;if(BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"sterile"))f=0;if(mechanism!=="ordinary"&&BTT_slotHas(meta,slot,"host-womb"))f+=20;return BTT_clamp(f,0,100);}
function BTT_reproDerived(axis){axis=BTT_clamp(axis,0,100);if(axis<=0)return 0;if(axis<=20)return Math.pow(axis/20,1.65)*50;return 50*Math.pow(2.25,(axis-20)/20);}
function BTT_nativeReproductiveType(receiverMeta,receivingSlot){var sl=String(receivingSlot||"");if(sl&&BTT_slotHasAny(receiverMeta,sl,["egg-laying","oviparous","ovipositor","clutch-bearing","clutch-prone","hyper-clutch"]))return "incubation";if(sl&&BTT_slotHasAny(receiverMeta,sl,["plant","flowering","pollen-receptive","seedbed"]))return "pollination";return "pregnancy";}
function BTT_resolveReproductiveType(receiverMeta,sourceMeta,mechanism,suggested,receivingSlot){if(mechanism.kind==="seed"&&mechanism.confidence>=0.82)return "seeded";if(mechanism.kind==="egg"&&mechanism.confidence>=0.82)return "incubation";if(mechanism.kind==="parasite"&&mechanism.confidence>=0.82)return "infestation";if(mechanism.kind==="pollen"&&mechanism.confidence>=0.82)return "pollination";suggested=BTT_conditionType(suggested);if(["seeded","incubation","pollination"].indexOf(suggested)>=0&&mechanism.confidence>=0.82)return suggested;return BTT_nativeReproductiveType(receiverMeta,receivingSlot);}
function BTT_explicitQuantity(raw){var map={one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,eleven:11,twelve:12},m=String(raw||"").match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve)\s+(?:baby|babies|child|children|offspring|eggs?|parasites?|seeds?|spirits?)\b/i);if(!m)return 0;var n=Number(m[1]);return isFinite(n)?BTT_clamp(n,1,99):(map[BTT_lower(m[1])]||0);}
function BTT_multiplicityProfile(receiverMeta,sourceMeta,resolvedType,mechanism){
  var r=BTT_allMetaTraits(receiverMeta),s=sourceMeta?BTT_allMetaTraits(sourceMeta):{},p={name:"ordinary",additionalMin:25,max:99,specialSecond:25,specialThird:25};if(r.singleton||r["single-birth"])return {name:"singleton",additionalMin:101,max:1,specialSecond:101,specialThird:101};if(r["twin-prone"])p={name:"twin-prone",additionalMin:25,max:99,specialSecond:10,specialThird:25};if(r["triplet-prone"])p={name:"triplet-prone",additionalMin:25,max:99,specialSecond:10,specialThird:10};if(r["litter-prone"]||r["litter-bearing"])p={name:"litter-bearing",additionalMin:15,max:99,specialSecond:15,specialThird:15};if(r["brood-prone"]||r.broodmother)p={name:"broodmother",additionalMin:10,max:99,specialSecond:10,specialThird:10};if(r["hyper-litter"])p={name:"hyper-litter",additionalMin:1,max:99,specialSecond:1,specialThird:1};if(resolvedType==="incubation"&&(r["clutch-bearing"]||r["clutch-prone"]||s["clutch-bearing"]||s["clutch-prone"]))p={name:"clutch-bearing",additionalMin:10,max:99,specialSecond:10,specialThird:10};if(resolvedType==="incubation"&&(r["hyper-clutch"]||s["hyper-clutch"]))p={name:"hyper-clutch",additionalMin:1,max:99,specialSecond:1,specialThird:1};if(mechanism==="seed"&&s["seed-bearing"])p={name:"seed-bearing",additionalMin:10,max:99,specialSecond:10,specialThird:10};return p;
}
function BTT_progressiveMultiplicity(score,seed,receiverMeta,sourceMeta,resolvedType,mechanism){
  score=Math.max(0,Number(score)||0);var profile=BTT_multiplicityProfile(receiverMeta,sourceMeta,resolvedType,mechanism),remaining=score,count=0,rolls=[],ordinal=1;while(remaining>0&&count<99){var min=1;if(ordinal>1){if(ordinal===2)min=profile.specialSecond;else if(ordinal===3)min=profile.specialThird;else min=profile.additionalMin;if(min>100)break;}var roll=BTT_hashInt(seed+"|multiplicity|"+ordinal,min,100),success=remaining>=roll;rolls.push({ordinal:ordinal,min:min,max:100,roll:roll,success:success,before:BTT_round(remaining,2),remaining:success?BTT_round(remaining-roll,2):BTT_round(remaining,2)});if(!success)break;count++;remaining-=roll;ordinal++;}return {count:count,rolls:rolls,profile:profile};
}
function BTT_conditionExisting(meta,type,slot){for(var i=0;i<meta.conditions.length;i++){var c=meta.conditions[i];if(c.active!==false&&BTT_conditionType(c.type)===BTT_conditionType(type)&&String(c.slot||"")===String(slot||""))return c;}return null;}
function BTT_slotTraitSet(meta,slot){var out={},s=meta&&meta.slots&&meta.slots[slot],a=s&&s.traits||[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)out[BTT_lower(a[i])]=1;return out;}
/**
 * Natural conception occupancy is slot-local. A normal receiving slot accepts
 * one active natural conception episode at a time. Traits that explicitly imply
 * repeat carrying capacity may accept another distinct episode while one is
 * already active. Multiplicity within an episode is calculated separately by
 * BTT_progressiveMultiplicity and never grants repeat-event permission itself.
 */
function BTT_naturalConceptionPolicy(meta,slot,resolvedType){
  var traits=BTT_slotTraitSet(meta,slot),active=[];for(var i=0;i<(meta.conditions||[]).length;i++){var c=meta.conditions[i];if(c.active===false||String(c.slot||"")!==String(slot||""))continue;var t=BTT_conditionType(c.type);if(t==="pregnancy"||t==="incubation")active.push(c);}
  var repeatTraits=[];["litter-bearing","broodmother","hyper-litter","egg-laying","oviparous","clutch-bearing","hyper-clutch"].forEach(function(t){if(traits[t])repeatTraits.push(t);});
  var repeatCapable=repeatTraits.length>0;
  return {allow:active.length===0||repeatCapable,activeEpisodes:active.length,repeatCapable:repeatCapable,repeatTraits:repeatTraits,type:BTT_conditionType(resolvedType)};
}
function BTT_addCondition(meta,row){
  row.active=true;row.type=BTT_conditionType(row.type);row.count=Math.max(1,Math.min(99,Number(row.count)||1));if(row.conceptionEvents!==undefined)row.conceptionEvents=Math.max(1,Math.min(99,Number(row.conceptionEvents)||1));
  var same=BTT_conditionExisting(meta,row.type,row.slot);if(same&&String(same.sourceEntityId||"")===String(row.sourceEntityId||"")){
    if(row.accumulateCount){same.count=Math.min(99,Math.max(1,Number(same.count)||1)+row.count);same.conceptionEvents=Math.min(99,Math.max(1,Number(same.conceptionEvents)||1)+Math.max(1,Number(row.conceptionEvents)||1));}
    else same.count=Math.max(same.count,row.count);
    for(var k in row)if(row.hasOwnProperty(k)&&k!=="count"&&k!=="conceptionEvents"&&k!=="accumulateCount")same[k]=row[k];return same;
  }
  delete row.accumulateCount;meta.conditions.push(row);return row;
}
function BTT_reproductiveSemanticEvidence(raw){
  raw=String(raw||"");var score=0,trigger="";
  if(/\b(?:inseminat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|impregnat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|fertiliz(?:e|es|ed|ing|ation))\b/i.test(raw)){score=0.96;trigger="direct conception predicate";}
  if(/\b(?:ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|creampie(?:s|d)?)\b/i.test(raw)&&score<0.90){score=0.90;trigger="reproductive release predicate";}
  if(/\b(?:breed(?:s|ing|ed)?|bred)\b/i.test(raw)&&score<0.72){score=0.72;trigger="breeding language";}
  if(/\b(?:finish(?:es|ed|ing)?|climax(?:es|ed|ing)?|came|ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing))\b[^.!?]{0,40}\b(?:inside|into|within)\b/i.test(raw)&&score<0.92){score=0.92;trigger="completed internal release";}
  var action=/\b(?:deposit(?:s|ed|ing)?|release(?:s|d|ing)?|fill(?:s|ed|ing)?|flood(?:s|ed|ing)?|inject(?:s|ed|ing)?|paint(?:s|ed|ing)?|pump(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/i.test(raw),material=/\b(?:semen|cum|reproductive material|seed)\b/i.test(raw),destination=/\b(?:inside|into|within|womb|uterus|vagina|cervix|reproductive tract)\b/i.test(raw);
  if(action&&material&&destination&&score<0.90){score=0.90;trigger="transfer+material+destination";}
  if(material&&destination&&/\b(?:puls(?:e|es|ed|ing)|flow(?:s|ed|ing)?|spill(?:s|ed|ing)?|pour(?:s|ed|ing)?|pump(?:s|ed|ing)?)\b/i.test(raw)&&score<0.84){score=0.84;trigger="material movement into receiver";}
  return {confidence:score,trigger:trigger};
}
function BTT_possessionSemanticEvidence(raw){var t=String(raw||""),score=0,trigger="";if(/\b(?:possessive|possessively|possessiveness|possessions?)\b/i.test(t)&&!/\b(?:possessed|possessing|possesses)\b/i.test(t))return {confidence:0,trigger:""};if(/\b(?:spirit|demon|ghost|entity|presence)\b[^.!?]{0,45}\b(?:possess(?:es|ed|ing)?|inhabit(?:s|ed|ing)?|enters?|takes? over|seizes? control)\b/i.test(t)||/\b(?:possess(?:es|ed|ing)?|inhabit(?:s|ed|ing)?|enters?)\b[^.!?]{0,45}\b(?:body|mind|soul|host)\b/i.test(t)){score=0.97;trigger="explicit inhabitation/control";}else if(/\b(?:possessed by|taken over by|inhabited by)\b[^.!?]{0,35}\b(?:spirit|demon|ghost|entity|presence)\b/i.test(t)){score=0.98;trigger="explicit possessor";}return {confidence:score,trigger:trigger};}
function BTT_conditionSemantics(f){
  var out=[],raw=f.raw,lower=BTT_lower(raw),eggInward=BTT_eggTransferInward(raw),repro=BTT_reproductiveSemanticEvidence(raw);function add(type,mode,trigger,semantic){out.push({type:type,mode:mode,trigger:trigger,semantic:semantic});}
  if(repro.confidence>0)add("reproductive-exposure","event",repro.trigger||"reproductive exposure",repro.confidence);
  if(eggInward||/\b(?:egg implantation|egg[- ]stuff)\b/i.test(raw))add("reproductive-exposure","event","oviposition",0.96);
  if(/\b(?:plants? seeds?|planted seeds?|injects? seeds?|injected seeds?|parasitic seeds?|spores? .*?(?:inside|into)|seeds? .*?(?:inside|into))\b/i.test(raw))add("reproductive-exposure","event","seeding",0.96);
  if(/\b(?:implants?\s+(?:a\s+|the\s+)?parasites?|parasitic implantation|parasites?\s+(?:enters?|burrows?|takes? hold|is implanted|gets? inside)|infests?)\b/i.test(raw))add("reproductive-exposure","event","parasitic implantation",0.94);
  if(/\b(?:is|are|am|now|currently|becomes?|became)\s+(?:pregnant|pregnancy|incubating|egg-bearing|seeded|infested|possessed|corrupted|infected|pollinated)\b/i.test(raw)){var m=raw.match(/\b(pregnant|pregnancy|incubating|egg-bearing|seeded|infested|possessed|corrupted|infected|pollinated)\b/i);if(m)add(BTT_conditionType(m[1]),"state_assertion",m[1],0.88);}
  if(/\bto be\s+(?:pregnant|incubating|seeded|infested|possessed|corrupted|infected|pollinated)\b/i.test(raw)){var m2=raw.match(/\bto be\s+(pregnant|incubating|seeded|infested|possessed|corrupted|infected|pollinated)\b/i);if(m2)add(BTT_conditionType(m2[1]),"future_assertion",m2[1],0.42);}
  var poss=BTT_possessionSemanticEvidence(raw);if(poss.confidence>0&&!(/\bpossessed of\b/i.test(lower)))add("possession","event",poss.trigger||"possession",poss.confidence);
  if(/\b(?:infects?|infected by|infection takes hold)\b/i.test(raw))add("infection","event","infection",0.86);
  if(/\b(?:corrupts?|corrupted by|corruption takes hold)\b/i.test(raw))add("corruption","event","corruption",0.86);

  /* Resolution verbs accept optional determiners/quantities. A lay/laid phrase
     is outbound resolution only when the same phrase is not an inward transfer
     into a receiver's body. This prevents one oviposition sentence from both
     creating and immediately erasing the incubation Condition. */
  if(/\b(?:gives? birth|gave birth|delivers?[^.!?]{0,30}(?:baby|babies|child|children|offspring)|pregnancy ends?|terminates?[^.!?]{0,20}pregnancy)\b/i.test(raw))add("pregnancy","resolution","birth/delivery",0.95);
  var eggResolve=/\b(?:extract(?:s|ed|ing)?|remov(?:e|es|ed|ing))[^.!?]{0,30}\beggs?\b|\beggs?\s+hatch(?:es|ed)?\b/i.test(raw)||(!eggInward&&/\b(?:lays?|laid)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\beggs?\b/i.test(raw));
  if(eggResolve)add("incubation","resolution","egg resolution",0.93);
  if(/\b(?:purg(?:e|es|ed|ing)|remov(?:e|es|ed|ing)|cleans(?:e|es|ed|ing)|extract(?:s|ed|ing)?)[^.!?]{0,30}\bseeds?\b/i.test(raw))add("seeded","resolution","seed purge",0.92);
  if(/\b(?:cleans(?:e|es|ed|ing)|purg(?:e|es|ed|ing)|extract(?:s|ed|ing)?|remov(?:e|es|ed|ing))[^.!?]{0,30}\bparasites?\b/i.test(raw))add("infestation","resolution","parasite cleanse",0.93);
  if(/\b(?:cures?|cured|clears?|cleared|treats?|treated)\s+(?:the\s+)?infection\b/i.test(raw))add("infection","resolution","infection cure",0.93);
  if(/\b(?:exorcises?|exorcised|banishes?|banished)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:spirit|demon|possession)|drives? out\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:spirit|demon)\b/i.test(raw))add("possession","resolution","exorcism",0.96);
  if(/\b(?:purifies?|purified|cleanses?|cleansed)\s+(?:the\s+)?corruption|removes?\s+(?:the\s+)?corruption\b/i.test(raw))add("corruption","resolution","purification",0.92);
  return out;
}
function BTT_conditionEventBaseScore(semantic,f){return Math.max(0,Number(semantic&&semantic.semantic||0))+Math.max(0,Number(f&&f.actuality||0))+Math.max(0,Number(f&&f.completion||0));}
function BTT_conditionHistoryKey(receiver,source,mechanism,slot,type,raw){var repeat=/\b(?:again|another|additional|second|third|separate|separately|once more|twice|two times|three times)\b/i.test(String(raw||""))?"repeat":"base";return ["reproductive",String(receiver||""),String(source&&source.entity||source&&source.descriptor||""),String(mechanism||"ordinary"),String(slot||""),String(type||"reproductive-exposure"),repeat].join("|");}
function BTT_conditionHistoryRecord(meta,key){meta.conditionEvidenceHistory=meta.conditionEvidenceHistory||{};var h=meta.conditionEvidenceHistory[key];if(!h)return null;var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),age=Math.max(0,BTT_getTurn()-Number(h.turn||0));if(age>cfg.retained_turns){delete meta.conditionEvidenceHistory[key];return null;}return {bonus:BTT_clamp(Number(h.bonus||0)*Math.pow(cfg.decay_base,age),0,cfg.condition_history_bonus_max),turn:Number(h.turn||0),seen:h.seen||{}};}
function BTT_conditionHistoryContribution(base,cfg){if(base<cfg.condition_event_store_min)return 0;var span=Math.max(0.01,cfg.condition_event_threshold-cfg.condition_event_store_min),r=BTT_clamp((base-cfg.condition_event_store_min)/span,0,1);return BTT_clamp(0.12+r*0.18,0.12,0.30);}
function BTT_storeConditionHistory(meta,key,base,raw){var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),old=BTT_conditionHistoryRecord(meta,key)||{bonus:0,seen:{}},sig=String(BTT_nowAction()||BTT_getTurn())+"|"+BTT_hash(BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(raw)));if(old.seen[sig])return old.bonus;var add=BTT_conditionHistoryContribution(base,cfg);if(add<=0)return old.bonus;old.seen[sig]=1;var keys=Object.keys(old.seen);if(keys.length>20)for(var i=0;i<keys.length-20;i++)delete old.seen[keys[i]];meta.conditionEvidenceHistory[key]={bonus:BTT_clamp(old.bonus+add,0,cfg.condition_history_bonus_max),turn:BTT_getTurn(),seen:old.seen,raw:BTT_clip(raw,140)};return meta.conditionEvidenceHistory[key].bonus;}
function BTT_clearConditionHistory(meta,key){if(meta&&meta.conditionEvidenceHistory)delete meta.conditionEvidenceHistory[key];}
function BTT_resolutionAmount(raw,condition){var q=BTT_explicitQuantity(raw);return q||Math.max(1,Number(condition&&condition.count)||1);}
function BTT_applyConditionResolution(meta,entity,f,semantic,parse){
  var candidates=[];for(var i=0;i<meta.conditions.length;i++){var c=meta.conditions[i];if(c.active===false||BTT_conditionType(c.type)!==BTT_conditionType(semantic.type))continue;candidates.push(c);}var sl=BTT_conditionSlotFromFragment(f,entity,parse);if(sl.slot)candidates=candidates.filter(function(c){return String(c.slot)===sl.slot;});var source=BTT_conditionSource(f,parse,entity);if(source.entity&&source.entity!==entity)candidates=candidates.filter(function(c){return String(c.sourceEntityId||"")===source.entity;});if(candidates.length!==1){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION RESOLUTION HOLD | type="+semantic.type+" | candidates="+candidates.length+" | reason="+(candidates.length?"ambiguous_episode":"no_matching_episode"));return false;}var c=candidates[0],amount=BTT_resolutionAmount(f.raw,c),before=c.count,after=Math.max(0,before-amount);if(after>0){c.count=after;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION REDUCE | "+c.type+" | "+before+"→"+after+" | trigger="+semantic.trigger);}else{var idx=meta.conditions.indexOf(c);if(idx>=0)meta.conditions.splice(idx,1);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION RESOLVE | "+c.type+" | "+before+"→0 | trigger="+semantic.trigger);}BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,f,{kind:"condition_resolution",domain:"conditions",name:BTT_conditionType(c.type),owner:entity,ownerConfidence:Math.max(0.72,BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity)),sourceEntity:source.entity||"",sourceDescriptor:source.descriptor||"",sourceConfidence:Number(source.confidence)||0,slot:String(c.slot||sl.slot||"system"),slotConfidence:Number(sl.confidence)||0.72,mechanism:"resolution",mechanismTags:["resolution","condition"]});return true;
}
function BTT_applyReproductiveCondition(meta,entity,f,semantic,parse,receiver,source,eventEvidence){
  receiver=receiver||{entity:entity,confidence:BTT_fragmentMention(f,entity),margin:0};source=source||BTT_conditionSource(f,parse,entity);eventEvidence=eventEvidence||{};
  var sourceMeta=BTT_eventLocalSourceMeta(f,source),sourceSlot=eventEvidence.sourceSlot||BTT_reproductiveSourceSlot(f,source,entity,sourceMeta,parse),slot=eventEvidence.slot||BTT_reproductiveReceivingSlot(f,entity,source,meta,parse),mech=eventEvidence.mechanism||BTT_mechanismEvidence(f,sourceMeta,meta,sourceSlot),cfg=BTT_getConfig(),eventConf=Number(eventEvidence.score||BTT_conditionEventBaseScore(semantic,f));
  if(receiver.confidence<cfg.condition_receiver_min||Number(receiver.margin||0)<0.12){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION REJECT | reproductive | reason=RECEIVER_CONFIDENCE | event_conf="+BTT_round(eventConf,2)+" | receiver_conf="+BTT_round(receiver.confidence,2)+" | receiver_margin="+BTT_round(receiver.margin||0,2));return false;}
  if(Number(source.confidence||0)<cfg.condition_source_min||(source.entity&&Number(source.margin||0)<0.12)){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION REJECT | reproductive | reason=SOURCE_CONFIDENCE | source_conf="+BTT_round(source.confidence,2)+" | source_margin="+BTT_round(source.margin||0,2));return false;}
  if(!slot.slot||!BTT_receivingSlotEligible(meta,slot.slot,mech.kind,slot.confidence)){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION REJECT | reproductive | reason=RECEIVING_SLOT | slot="+slot.slot+" | slot_conf="+BTT_round(slot.confidence,2));return false;}
  var type=BTT_resolveReproductiveType(meta,sourceMeta,mech,semantic.type,slot.slot),naturalPolicy=null;
  if(mech.kind==="ordinary"&&(type==="pregnancy"||type==="incubation")){naturalPolicy=BTT_naturalConceptionPolicy(meta,slot.slot,type);if(!naturalPolicy.allow){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION BLOCKED | type="+type+" | slot="+slot.slot+" | reason=NATURAL_CONCEPTION_SLOT_OCCUPIED | active_episodes="+naturalPolicy.activeEpisodes+" | repeat_capable=NO");return false;}if(naturalPolicy.activeEpisodes>0)BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONCEPTION REPEAT ELIGIBLE | type="+type+" | slot="+slot.slot+" | active_episodes="+naturalPolicy.activeEpisodes+" | traits="+naturalPolicy.repeatTraits.join(","));}
  var baseF=Number(meta.appearance.axes.fertility)||0,slotF=BTT_slotFertility(meta,slot.slot,mech.kind),vir=sourceMeta?Number(sourceMeta.appearance.axes.virility)||0:null,special=/^(?:egg|seed|parasite|pollen)$/.test(mech.kind),fd,vd,score;
  if(special){fd=Math.max(35,BTT_reproDerived(Math.max(10,slotF)));vd=Math.max(50,100*BTT_clamp(Math.max(mech.confidence,source.confidence),0,1));score=(fd+vd)/2;}else{if((vir===null||vir<=0)&&/\b(?:inseminat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|ejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|semen|cum|fertiliz(?:e|es|ed|ing|ation)|impregnat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)|breed|breeds|breeding|bred|creampie(?:s|d)?|finish(?:es|ed|ing)?\s+(?:inside|in)|climax(?:es|ed|ing)?\s+(?:inside|in))\b/i.test(f.raw))vir=20;if(vir===null||vir<=0||slotF<=0){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION REJECT | type="+type+" | reason=NONVIABLE | slot_fertility="+slotF+" | source_virility="+String(vir));return false;}fd=BTT_reproDerived(slotF);vd=BTT_reproDerived(vir);score=(fd+vd)/2;}
  var seed=entity+"|"+(source.entity||source.descriptor)+"|"+slot.slot+"|"+type+"|"+BTT_clip(f.raw,180)+"|"+BTT_getTurn(),multi=BTT_progressiveMultiplicity(score,seed,meta,sourceMeta,type,mech.kind),count=multi.count,explicit=BTT_explicitQuantity(f.raw);if(special&&explicit>0)count=explicit;if(special&&count<1&&f.actuality>=0.78&&f.completion>=0.55&&mech.confidence>=0.82)count=1;
  var logicalSig=BTT_reproductiveLogicalSignature(f,entity,source,type,mech.kind);if(BTT_ensureState().processedEvents[logicalSig]){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION DEDUPE | type="+type+" | receiver="+entity+" | source="+String(source.entity||source.descriptor||"")+" | reason=LOGICAL_EVENT_ALREADY_RESOLVED");return false;}
  /* Once an event has crossed the evidence gate, its conception roll is resolved
     once. A failed roll is still a resolved natural event and must not be rerolled
     by a later paraphrase of the same action. */
  BTT_markEventOnce(logicalSig);
  if(count<1){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION CHECK | type="+type+" | event_conf="+BTT_round(eventConf,2)+" | receiver_conf="+BTT_round(receiver.confidence,2)+" | receiver_margin="+BTT_round(receiver.margin||0,2)+" | source_conf="+BTT_round(source.confidence,2)+" | source_margin="+BTT_round(source.margin||0,2)+" | slot_conf="+BTT_round(slot.confidence,2)+" | source_slot="+(sourceSlot.slot||"unresolved")+" | source_slot_conf="+BTT_round(sourceSlot.confidence||0,2)+" | mechanism_conf="+BTT_round(mech.confidence,2)+" | reproductive_score="+BTT_round(score,2)+" | rolls="+multi.rolls.map(function(r){return r.roll+(r.success?"✓":"✗");}).join(",")+" | success=NO");return false;}
  var repeatNatural=!!(naturalPolicy&&naturalPolicy.activeEpisodes>0&&naturalPolicy.repeatCapable),row={type:type,slot:slot.slot,count:Math.min(99,count),source:source.entity?BTT_entityName(source.entity,sourceMeta):(source.descriptor||"event-local source"),sourceEntityId:source.entity||"",confidence:BTT_round(Math.min(1,eventConf/3),2),mechanism:mech.kind,mechanismConfidence:BTT_round(mech.confidence,2),receiverConfidence:BTT_round(receiver.confidence,2),receiverMargin:BTT_round(receiver.margin||0,2),sourceConfidence:BTT_round(source.confidence,2),sourceMargin:BTT_round(source.margin||0,2),slotConfidence:BTT_round(slot.confidence,2),eventConfidence:BTT_round(eventConf,2),fertility:baseF,slotFertility:slotF,virility:vir===null?"bypassed":vir,reproductiveScore:BTT_round(score,2),rolls:BTT_clone(multi.rolls),multiplicityProfile:multi.profile.name,conceptionEvents:1,accumulateCount:repeatNatural,startedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),trigger:semantic.trigger,active:true};
  var committed=BTT_addCondition(meta,row);BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,f,{kind:"condition_event",domain:"conditions",name:type,owner:entity,ownerConfidence:receiver.confidence,sourceEntity:source.entity||"",sourceDescriptor:source.descriptor||"",sourceConfidence:source.confidence,slot:slot.slot,slotConfidence:slot.confidence,mechanism:mech.kind,mechanismTags:BTT_unique(["condition","reproductive",mech.kind].concat(mech.kind!=="ordinary"?["transformation"]:[]))});BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION COMMIT | type="+type+" | slot="+slot.slot+" | source="+String(row.sourceEntityId||row.source)+" | mechanism="+mech.kind+" | event_conf="+BTT_round(eventConf,2)+" | receiver_conf="+BTT_round(receiver.confidence,2)+" | receiver_margin="+BTT_round(receiver.margin||0,2)+" | source_conf="+BTT_round(source.confidence,2)+" | source_margin="+BTT_round(source.margin||0,2)+" | slot_conf="+BTT_round(slot.confidence,2)+" | source_slot="+(sourceSlot.slot||"unresolved")+" | source_slot_conf="+BTT_round(sourceSlot.confidence||0,2)+" | mechanism_conf="+BTT_round(mech.confidence,2)+" | reproductive_score="+BTT_round(score,2)+" | rolls="+multi.rolls.map(function(r){return r.roll+(r.success?"✓":"✗");}).join(",")+" | event_count="+String(committed.conceptionEvents||1)+" | count="+committed.count);return true;
}
function BTT_logReproductiveHold(work,f,sem,status,base,historyBonus,total,reason){
  var logged={};for(var lm=0;lm<(f.mentions||[]).length;lm++){var ent=f.mentions[lm].entity;if(logged[ent]||f.mentions[lm].score<0.55)continue;var mm=work[ent]||BTT_findEntity(ent);if(mm){logged[ent]=1;BTT_touchDebug(mm,"conditions","CONDITION EVENT HOLD | reproductive | reason="+reason+" | mode="+status.mode+" | semantic_conf="+BTT_round(sem.semantic,2)+" | actuality="+BTT_round(f.actuality,2)+" | completion="+BTT_round(f.completion,2)+" | base="+BTT_round(base,2)+" | history="+BTT_round(historyBonus,2)+" | event_conf="+BTT_round(total,2));}}
}
function BTT_processReproductiveConditions(work,parse,cfg){
  for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){var f=parse.fragments[i],sems=BTT_conditionSemantics(f);for(var s=0;s<sems.length;s++){var sem=sems[s];if(sem.type!=="reproductive-exposure"||sem.mode!=="event")continue;var status=f.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(f.raw,f.quoted),base=BTT_conditionEventBaseScore(sem,f);
    if(!status.actual||Number(f.actuality||0)<cfg.condition_actuality_min||Number(f.completion||0)<cfg.condition_completion_floor){BTT_logReproductiveHold(work,f,sem,status,base,0,base,!status.actual?"MODALITY":(Number(f.actuality||0)<cfg.condition_actuality_min?"ACTUALITY":"COMPLETION_FLOOR"));continue;}
    var receiver=BTT_reproductiveReceiverCandidates(f,parse,work),meta=receiver.entity?(work[receiver.entity]||BTT_findEntity(receiver.entity)):null;if(!receiver.entity||!meta){BTT_logReproductiveHold(work,f,sem,status,base,0,base,"NO_RECEIVER");continue;}var source=BTT_conditionSourceCandidates(f,parse,receiver.entity,work),sourceMeta=BTT_eventLocalSourceMeta(f,source),sourceSlot=BTT_reproductiveSourceSlot(f,source,receiver.entity,sourceMeta,parse),slot=BTT_reproductiveReceivingSlot(f,receiver.entity,source,meta,parse),mech=BTT_mechanismEvidence(f,sourceMeta,meta,sourceSlot),hkey=BTT_conditionHistoryKey(receiver.entity,source,mech.kind,slot.slot,sem.type,f.raw),hist=BTT_conditionHistoryRecord(meta,hkey),historyBonus=hist?hist.bonus:0,total=base+historyBonus;
    if(total<cfg.condition_event_threshold){if(receiver.confidence>=0.40&&slot.confidence>=0.40&&(source.confidence>=0.35||mech.confidence>=0.82))BTT_storeConditionHistory(meta,hkey,base,f.raw);BTT_logReproductiveHold(work,f,sem,status,base,historyBonus,total,"ADDITIVE_THRESHOLD");continue;}
    BTT_clearConditionHistory(meta,hkey);f.conditionEventConfidence=total;f.conditionEventResolved=true;f.conditionEventReceiver=receiver.entity;f.conditionEventSource=source.entity||source.descriptor||"";BTT_applyReproductiveCondition(meta,receiver.entity,f,sem,parse,receiver,source,{score:total,base:base,historyBonus:historyBonus,slot:slot,sourceSlot:sourceSlot,mechanism:mech});
  }}
}
function BTT_processConditions(work,parse){
  var cfg=BTT_getConfig();BTT_processReproductiveConditions(work,parse,cfg);for(var entity in work)if(work.hasOwnProperty(entity)){var meta=work[entity],owned=BTT_ownedFragments(parse,entity);for(var i=0;i<owned.length;i++){var f=owned[i].fragment,sems=BTT_conditionSemantics(f);for(var s=0;s<sems.length;s++){var sem=sems[s],ownership=Number(owned[i].ownership||0),eventScore=Number(sem.semantic||0)+Number(f.actuality||0)+ownership;if(sem.type==="reproductive-exposure")continue;
    if(sem.mode==="future_assertion"){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION REJECT | type="+sem.type+" | reason=FUTURE_OR_INFINITIVE_ASSERTION");continue;}
    if(sem.mode==="resolution"){if((Number(sem.semantic||0)+Number(f.actuality||0)+ownership)>=2.05&&BTT_eventActual(f,0.60))BTT_applyConditionResolution(meta,entity,f,sem,parse);continue;}
    if(sem.mode==="state_assertion"){if(f.temporal!=="CURRENT"||f.assertion<cfg.condition_state_min||ownership<0.55){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION REJECT | type="+sem.type+" | reason=STATE_ASSERTION_NOT_CURRENT");continue;}var slot=BTT_conditionSlotFromFragment(f,entity,parse);if(!slot.slot&&BTT_conditionFamily(sem.type)==="reproductive"&&meta.slots.vagina)slot={slot:"vagina",confidence:0.65};if(!slot.slot)slot={slot:"system",confidence:0.5};var stateConf=BTT_clamp((Number(sem.semantic||0)+Number(f.assertion||0)+ownership)/3,0,1);BTT_addCondition(meta,{type:sem.type,slot:slot.slot,count:1,source:"narrative assertion",sourceEntityId:"",confidence:BTT_round(stateConf,2),startedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),trigger:sem.trigger,active:true});BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,f,{kind:"condition_state",domain:"conditions",name:sem.type,owner:entity,ownerConfidence:ownership,slot:slot.slot,slotConfidence:slot.confidence||0.72,mechanism:"state-assertion",mechanismTags:["condition","persistent"]});BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION CONFIRM | type="+sem.type+" | slot="+slot.slot+" | authority=current_state_assertion");continue;}
    if(sem.mode==="event"){var status=f.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(f.raw,f.quoted);if(!status.actual||f.actuality<cfg.condition_actuality_min||eventScore<cfg.condition_event_threshold){if(eventScore>=cfg.condition_event_store_min)BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION EVENT HOLD | type="+sem.type+" | mode="+status.mode+" | semantic_conf="+BTT_round(sem.semantic,2)+" | actuality="+BTT_round(f.actuality,2)+" | ownership="+BTT_round(ownership,2)+" | event_conf="+BTT_round(eventScore,2)+" | threshold="+cfg.condition_event_threshold);continue;}var src=BTT_conditionSource(f,parse,entity);if((sem.type==="possession"||sem.type==="infection"||sem.type==="corruption")&&src.entity===entity)src={entity:"",confidence:0,descriptor:""};var chance=BTT_clamp((eventScore/3)*100,5,98),roll=BTT_hashRoll(entity+"|condition|"+sem.type+"|"+BTT_getTurn()+"|"+f.index);if(roll<=chance){var dst=sem.type==="possession"?{receiver:entity,finalSlot:"head",entrySlot:"head",confidence:1}:BTT_deliveryDestination(f,src.entity||"",parse,work),condSlot=dst&&dst.receiver===entity&&dst.finalSlot?dst.finalSlot:"system";BTT_addCondition(meta,{type:sem.type,slot:condSlot,count:1,source:src.entity?BTT_entityName(src.entity,BTT_findEntity(src.entity)):(src.descriptor||"unknown"),sourceEntityId:src.entity||"",confidence:BTT_round(BTT_clamp(eventScore/3,0,1),2),chance:BTT_round(chance,1),roll:roll,startedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),trigger:sem.trigger,active:true});BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,f,{kind:"condition_event",domain:"conditions",name:sem.type,owner:entity,ownerConfidence:ownership,sourceEntity:src.entity||"",sourceDescriptor:src.descriptor||"",sourceConfidence:src.confidence||0,slot:condSlot,slotConfidence:dst&&dst.receiver===entity?Number(dst.confidence)||0.72:0.6,mechanism:BTT_conditionFamily(sem.type)||"condition",mechanismTags:["condition",BTT_conditionFamily(sem.type)||"event"]});BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION COMMIT | type="+sem.type+" | slot="+condSlot+(dst&&dst.entrySlot&&dst.entrySlot!==condSlot?" | entry_slot="+dst.entrySlot:"")+" | event_conf="+BTT_round(eventScore,2)+" | chance="+BTT_round(chance,1)+" | roll="+roll);}else BTT_touchDebug(meta,"conditions","CONDITION MISS | type="+sem.type+" | event_conf="+BTT_round(eventScore,2)+" | chance="+BTT_round(chance,1)+" | roll="+roll);}
  }}}
}

/* ============================================================================
   14. PROFILE-DRIVEN RISK TRACKS WITH STRICT DESTINATION SEMANTICS
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Risks consume the same fragments already parsed for the transaction. Each
   milestone tracks current-body and snapshot destinations independently.
   target=both is strict AND: a current-body match never satisfies a missing
   snapshot requirement while a reversible snapshot is still applicable.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_riskOwners(meta){var out=[];for(var i=0;i<(meta.instantchange.effects||[]).length;i++)out.push(meta.instantchange.effects[i]);for(var j=0;j<(meta.instantchange.persistentRiskProfiles||[]).length;j++)out.push(meta.instantchange.persistentRiskProfiles[j]);return out;}
function BTT_riskState(owner,id){owner.riskState=owner.riskState||{};if(!owner.riskState[id])owner.riskState[id]={exposureCount:0,successCount:0,lastExposureTurn:null,seen:{},milestones:{},applied:{},complete:false,lastRoll:null};if(!owner.riskState[id].applied)owner.riskState[id].applied={};return owner.riskState[id];}
function BTT_reproductiveRiskTerm(term){return /(?:inseminat|impregnat|fertiliz|creampie|ejaculat|breed|bred|oviposit|seeded|insemination|fertilization|impregnation)/i.test(String(term||""));}
function BTT_riskTermMatches(raw,term){
  var t=BTT_lower(BTT_cleanSpace(term));if(!t)return false;if(/inseminat/.test(t))return /\binseminat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)\b/i.test(raw);if(/impregnat/.test(t))return /\bimpregnat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)\b/i.test(raw);if(/fertiliz/.test(t))return /\bfertiliz(?:e|es|ed|ing|ation)\b/i.test(raw);if(/ejaculat/.test(t))return /\bejaculat(?:e|es|ed|ing|ion)\b/i.test(raw);if(/^(?:breed|breeding|bred)$/.test(t))return /\b(?:breed|breeds|breeding|bred)\b/i.test(raw);if(/creampie/.test(t))return /\bcreampie(?:s|d)?\b/i.test(raw);if(/oviposit/.test(t))return /\boviposit(?:s|ed|ing|ion)?\b/i.test(raw);return BTT_wordBoundaryHas(raw,term);
}
function BTT_riskTriggerConfidence(track,fragment,entity,parse,work){
  var best={confidence:0,reason:"",threshold:0.76,chanceBonus:0};
  for(var i=0;i<(track.triggers||[]).length;i++){var tr=track.triggers[i];
    for(var t=0;t<tr.terms.length;t++){var term=tr.terms[t];if(!BTT_riskTermMatches(fragment.raw,term))continue;
      var conf=0.58+0.30*fragment.actuality+0.12*BTT_fragmentMention(fragment,entity),contextHit=0;
      for(var c=0;c<tr.context.length;c++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(fragment.raw,tr.context[c]))contextHit++;
      if(tr.requireContext&&!contextHit)conf-=0.30;else conf+=Math.min(0.15,contextHit*0.05);
      /* Reproductive lexical triggers are causal events. A configured word in
         explanatory prose is not an exposure. Resolve the event's participant
         role and completion through the same shared evidence engine Conditions
         use. Existing profiles default to receiver-role semantics; creators may
         explicitly request source/participant/any with trigger.role. */
      if(BTT_reproductiveRiskTerm(term)){
        var sem=BTT_reproductiveSemanticEvidence(fragment.raw),role=tr.role||"receiver",receiver=BTT_reproductiveReceiverCandidates(fragment,parse,work),source=BTT_conditionSourceCandidates(fragment,parse,receiver.entity||entity,work),roleOK=false,cfg=BTT_getConfig(),eventScore=Math.max(Number(fragment.conditionEventConfidence||0),BTT_conditionEventBaseScore({semantic:sem.confidence},fragment)),status=fragment.eventStatus||BTT_eventStatus(fragment.raw,fragment.quoted);
        if(sem.confidence<=0||!status.actual||fragment.actuality<cfg.condition_actuality_min||fragment.completion<cfg.condition_completion_floor||eventScore<cfg.condition_event_threshold)continue;
        if(role==="receiver")roleOK=receiver.entity===entity&&receiver.confidence>=BTT_getConfig().condition_receiver_min;
        else if(role==="source")roleOK=source.entity===entity&&Number(source.confidence||0)>=BTT_getConfig().condition_source_min;
        else if(role==="participant")roleOK=(receiver.entity===entity&&receiver.confidence>=0.55)||(source.entity===entity&&Number(source.confidence||0)>=0.55);
        else roleOK=true;
        if(!roleOK)continue;conf=Math.max(conf,0.45*sem.confidence+0.30*fragment.completion+0.25*(role==="source"?Number(source.confidence||0):Number(receiver.confidence||0)));
      }
      if(conf>best.confidence)best={confidence:BTT_clamp(conf,0,1),reason:term,threshold:Number(tr.threshold)||0.76,chanceBonus:Number(tr.chanceBonus)||0};
    }
  }return best;
}
function BTT_snapshotApplicable(meta){return !!(meta.instantchange&&meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot&&(meta.instantchange.effects||[]).length);}
function BTT_riskOutcomeKey(outcome){return outcome.type+"|"+(outcome.slot||outcome.axis||"")+"|"+(outcome.traits||[]).join(",")+"|"+String(outcome.steps||0)+"|"+outcome.successAt+"|"+outcome.target;}
function BTT_outcomeSatisfied(meta,outcome,st){
  var applicable=BTT_snapshotApplicable(meta),snapState=(outcome.target==="reversion_snapshot"||outcome.target==="both")?(applicable?"AVAILABLE":"PENDING_OR_UNAVAILABLE"):"N/A",cur=true,snap=true,key=BTT_riskOutcomeKey(outcome),applied=st&&st.applied&&st.applied[key]||{};
  if(outcome.type==="buildShift"){
    cur=outcome.target==="current"||outcome.target==="both"?!!applied.current:true;snap=outcome.target==="reversion_snapshot"||outcome.target==="both"?(applicable&&!!applied.snapshot):true;
  }else{
    var slot=outcome.slot,traits=outcome.traits||[];if(outcome.target==="current"||outcome.target==="both")for(var i=0;i<traits.length;i++)if(!(meta.slots[slot]&&meta.slots[slot].traits.indexOf(traits[i])>=0))cur=false;
    if(outcome.target==="reversion_snapshot"||outcome.target==="both"){if(!applicable)snap=false;else{var ss=meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot.slots&&meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot.slots[slot];for(var j=0;j<traits.length;j++)if(!(ss&&ss.traits&&ss.traits.indexOf(traits[j])>=0))snap=false;}}
  }
  var overall=outcome.target==="current"?cur:outcome.target==="reversion_snapshot"?snap:(cur&&snap);return {current:cur,snapshot:snap,overall:overall,snapshotState:snapState};
}
function BTT_snapshotAxisDelta(meta,axis,delta,trackId){
  if(!BTT_snapshotApplicable(meta))return false;var snap=meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot;snap.appearance=snap.appearance||{fields:{},axes:{},summary_ai:""};snap.appearance.axes=snap.appearance.axes||{};var old=BTT_round(axis==="fem"?Number(snap.overallFem)||0:Number(snap.appearance.axes[axis])||0,2),val=BTT_round(axis==="fem"?BTT_clamp(old+delta,-100,100):BTT_clamp(old+delta,0,100),2);if(axis==="fem"){snap.overallFem=val;snap.appearance.axes.fem=val;}else snap.appearance.axes[axis]=val;if(old===val)return false;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK SNAPSHOT AXIS | track="+trackId+" | axis="+axis+" | "+old+"→"+val);return true;
}
function BTT_applyRiskOutcome(meta,outcome,st,trackId){
  var changed=false,key=BTT_riskOutcomeKey(outcome);st.applied=st.applied||{};st.applied[key]=st.applied[key]||{};
  if(outcome.type==="buildShift"){
    var axis=outcome.axis||"ass_size",delta=(Number(outcome.steps)||1)*2;if(outcome.target==="current"||outcome.target==="both"){var before=axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:Number(meta.appearance.axes[axis])||0;BTT_addAxis(meta,axis,delta,"risk build shift","RISK");var after=axis==="fem"?meta.overallFem:Number(meta.appearance.axes[axis])||0;if(after!==before){changed=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK CURRENT AXIS | track="+trackId+" | axis="+axis+" | "+before+"→"+after);}st.applied[key].current=true;}
    if((outcome.target==="reversion_snapshot"||outcome.target==="both")&&BTT_snapshotApplicable(meta)){changed=BTT_snapshotAxisDelta(meta,axis,delta,trackId)||changed;st.applied[key].snapshot=true;}return changed;
  }
  var sat=BTT_outcomeSatisfied(meta,outcome,st),slot=outcome.slot,traits=outcome.traits||[];
  if((outcome.target==="current"||outcome.target==="both")&&!sat.current){for(var i=0;i<traits.length;i++)if(BTT_addTraitDirect(meta,slot,traits[i])){changed=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK CURRENT TRAIT ADD | track="+trackId+" | slot="+slot+" | trait="+traits[i]);}st.applied[key].current=true;}
  if((outcome.target==="reversion_snapshot"||outcome.target==="both")&&!sat.snapshot&&BTT_snapshotApplicable(meta)){var snap=meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot;snap.slots=snap.slots||{};if(!snap.slots[slot])snap.slots[slot]=BTT_newSlot();for(var j=0;j<traits.length;j++)if(snap.slots[slot].traits.indexOf(traits[j])<0){snap.slots[slot].traits.push(traits[j]);snap.slots[slot].updatedTurn=BTT_getTurn();changed=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK SNAPSHOT TRAIT ADD | track="+trackId+" | slot="+slot+" | trait="+traits[j]);}st.applied[key].snapshot=true;}
  return changed;
}
function BTT_trackMilestones(meta,track,st){var all=(track.outcomes||[]).concat(track.progression||[]),complete=true;for(var i=0;i<all.length;i++){var o=all[i],key=BTT_riskOutcomeKey(o),ss=BTT_outcomeSatisfied(meta,o,st);st.milestones[key]={satisfied:ss.overall,current:ss.current,snapshot:ss.snapshot,snapshotState:ss.snapshotState,successAt:o.successAt,target:o.target};if(!ss.overall)complete=false;}st.complete=complete;return complete;}
function BTT_applyRiskSuccess(meta,track,st){
  st.successCount++;var all=(track.outcomes||[]).concat(track.progression||[]);
  for(var o=0;o<all.length;o++){var outcome=all[o];if(st.successCount<outcome.successAt)continue;var before=BTT_outcomeSatisfied(meta,outcome,st);
    if(!before.overall){BTT_applyRiskOutcome(meta,outcome,st,track.id);var after=BTT_outcomeSatisfied(meta,outcome,st);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK OUTCOME | track="+track.id+" | target="+outcome.target+" | current="+(after.current?"SATISFIED":"UNSATISFIED")+" | snapshot="+(after.snapshot?"SATISFIED":after.snapshotState)+" | overall="+(after.overall?"SATISFIED":"UNSATISFIED"));}
  }
}
function BTT_pruneRiskSeen(st,track){
  var now=BTT_getTurn(),memory=Math.max(1,Number(track.memoryTurns)||12);for(var k in st.seen)if(st.seen.hasOwnProperty(k)&&now-Number(st.seen[k])>memory)delete st.seen[k];
}
function BTT_riskDependencySatisfied(owner,track){
  if(!track.requiresRecentTrack)return true;var req=BTT_riskState(owner,track.requiresRecentTrack),last=req.lastExposureTurn;
  if(last===null||last===undefined)return false;return BTT_getTurn()-Number(last)<=Math.max(0,Number(track.withinTurns)||0);
}
function BTT_executeRiskExposure(meta,owner,track,st,fragment,confidence,reason,chanceBonus,signatureSuffix){
  var sig=BTT_eventSignature("risk:"+track.id,fragment,meta.entity+"|"+(owner.profileId||"")+"|"+String(signatureSuffix||reason||""));
  if(st.seen[sig])return false;st.seen[sig]=BTT_getTurn();st.exposureCount++;st.lastExposureTurn=BTT_getTurn();
  var chance=Math.min(track.maximumChance,track.baseChance+Math.max(0,st.exposureCount-1)*track.repeatIncrease+(Number(chanceBonus)||0)),
      roll=BTT_hashRoll(meta.entity+"|risk|"+track.id+"|"+st.exposureCount+"|"+BTT_nowAction()+"|"+String(signatureSuffix||fragment.index)),
      success=roll<=chance;st.lastRoll={turn:BTT_getTurn(),chance:chance,roll:roll,success:success,trigger:reason};
  BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK ROLL | track="+track.id+" | exposure="+st.exposureCount+" | confidence="+BTT_round(confidence,2)+" | chance="+BTT_round(chance,2)+" | roll="+roll+" | result="+(success?"SUCCESS":"MISS"));
  if(success)BTT_applyRiskSuccess(meta,track,st);BTT_trackMilestones(meta,track,st);return true;
}
/**
 * Handles configured passive risk pressure without interpreting it as a free
 * narrative event. Pressure advances once per logical turn; story relief terms
 * are still gated by ordinary asserted/actual fragment evidence.
 */
function BTT_processPassiveRisk(meta,owner,track,st,parse){
  var p=track.passive;if(!p||BTT_lower(p.kind)!=="milkpressure")return;
  if(st.pressure===undefined)st.pressure=0;if(st.lastPassiveTurn===undefined)st.lastPassiveTurn=null;if(st.lastPassiveExposureTurn===undefined)st.lastPassiveExposureTurn=null;
  var max=Math.max(1,BTT_num(p.pressureMax,100)),per=Math.max(0,BTT_num(p.pressurePerTurn,1)),now=BTT_getTurn();
  if(st.lastPassiveTurn!==now){st.pressure=BTT_clamp(Number(st.pressure)||0,0,max);st.pressure=BTT_clamp(st.pressure+per,0,max);st.lastPassiveTurn=now;}
  var full=BTT_splitList(p.completeReliefTerms||["fully drained","completely drained","drained dry","fully emptied","completely emptied","fully expressed","completely expressed","fully milked","completely milked"]),
      decrease=BTT_splitList(p.decreaseTerms||p.reliefTerms||["drained","expressed","milked","relieved"]),increase=BTT_splitList(p.increaseTerms||["aching","engorged","overfull","pressure builds"]);
  for(var i=0;i<parse.fragments.length;i++){var f=parse.fragments[i];if(BTT_fragmentMention(f,meta.entity)<0.55||f.actuality<0.60||f.assertion<0.50)continue;
    var hit=false;for(var a=0;a<full.length;a++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(f.raw,full[a])){st.pressure=0;hit=true;BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","PASSIVE PRESSURE | track="+track.id+" | relief=COMPLETE | pressure=0");break;}if(hit)continue;
    for(var d=0;d<decrease.length;d++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(f.raw,decrease[d])){st.pressure=BTT_clamp(st.pressure-Math.max(0,BTT_num(p.reliefAmount,2)),0,max);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","PASSIVE PRESSURE | track="+track.id+" | relief=-"+Math.max(0,BTT_num(p.reliefAmount,2))+" | pressure="+BTT_round(st.pressure,2));break;}
    for(var u=0;u<increase.length;u++)if(BTT_wordBoundaryHas(f.raw,increase[u])){st.pressure=BTT_clamp(st.pressure+Math.max(0,BTT_num(p.storyIncreaseAmount,1)),0,max);BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","PASSIVE PRESSURE | track="+track.id+" | story=+"+Math.max(0,BTT_num(p.storyIncreaseAmount,1))+" | pressure="+BTT_round(st.pressure,2));break;}
  }
  var every=Math.max(1,Math.floor(BTT_num(p.passiveExposureEveryTurns,1))),threshold=Math.max(0,BTT_num(p.rollWhenPressureAbove,0));
  if(st.complete||st.pressure<=threshold||(st.lastPassiveExposureTurn!==null&&now-Number(st.lastPassiveExposureTurn)<every))return;
  var synthetic={raw:"passive pressure "+track.id,index:-1000-track.id.length,actuality:1,assertion:1,mentions:[{entity:meta.entity,score:1}],subject:meta.entity,subjectConfidence:1};
  var bonus=Math.max(0,BTT_num(p.pressureChancePerPoint,2))*Math.max(0,st.pressure-threshold);if(BTT_executeRiskExposure(meta,owner,track,st,synthetic,1,"passive-pressure",bonus,"pressure-"+now)){st.lastPassiveExposureTurn=now;}
}
function BTT_processRisks(work,parse){
  for(var entity in work)if(work.hasOwnProperty(entity)){
    var meta=work[entity],owners=BTT_riskOwners(meta);
    for(var oi=0;oi<owners.length;oi++){var owner=owners[oi],tracks=owner.risk_tracks||[];
      for(var ti=0;ti<tracks.length;ti++){
        var track=tracks[ti],st=BTT_riskState(owner,track.id);BTT_pruneRiskSeen(st,track);BTT_trackMilestones(meta,track,st);
        BTT_processPassiveRisk(meta,owner,track,st,parse);
        if(st.complete){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK "+track.id+" | state=COMPLETE | no_roll=all_milestones_satisfied");continue;}
        if(!BTT_riskDependencySatisfied(owner,track)){BTT_touchDebug(meta,"risks","RISK "+track.id+" | gate=WAITING_FOR_RECENT_TRACK | requires="+track.requiresRecentTrack);continue;}
        var best={confidence:0,fragment:null,reason:"",threshold:0.76,chanceBonus:0};
        for(var fi=0;fi<parse.fragments.length;fi++){var f=parse.fragments[fi],tr=BTT_riskTriggerConfidence(track,f,entity,parse,work);if(tr.confidence>best.confidence)best={confidence:tr.confidence,fragment:f,reason:tr.reason,threshold:tr.threshold,chanceBonus:tr.chanceBonus};}
        if(!best.fragment||best.confidence<best.threshold)continue;
        if(BTT_executeRiskExposure(meta,owner,track,st,best.fragment,best.confidence,best.reason,best.chanceBonus,"narrative")){BTT_recordResolvedObservation(parse,best.fragment,{kind:"profile_risk_exposure",domain:"risks",name:track.id,owner:entity,ownerConfidence:Math.max(best.confidence,BTT_fragmentMention(best.fragment,entity)),sourceDescriptor:String(owner.profileName||owner.name||owner.profileId||"instant-change risk"),sourceConfidence:0.90,mechanism:"profile-risk",mechanismTags:["profile-risk","conditioning","repeated"]});}
      }
    }
  }
}
function BTT_conditionPermanence(meta){
  var effects=meta.instantchange.effects||[];for(var i=effects.length-1;i>=0;i--){var e=effects[i],p=BTT_profile(e.kind,e.profileId);if(!p)continue;var why="";for(var t=0;t<(p.permanency_triggers||[]).length;t++){var ct=BTT_permanencyConditionName(p.permanency_triggers[t]);if(!ct)continue;for(var c=0;c<meta.conditions.length;c++)if(meta.conditions[c].active!==false&&BTT_conditionType(meta.conditions[c].type)===ct){why="condition:"+ct;break;}if(why)break;}if(!why)continue;BTT_commitPermanentEffect(meta,e,p,why);effects.splice(i,1);}meta.instantchange.effects=effects;if(!effects.length&&meta.instantchange.canonicalProfile)meta.instantchange.sourceSnapshot=null;
}

/* ============================================================================
   15. CANONICAL RECONCILIATION
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Reconciliation is the sole place where linked state is normalized before a
   write. It does not read cards. Conditions remain separate from ordinary slot
   traits; display overlays are derived later by the Anatomy renderer.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_sanitizeExclusiveTraits(meta){
  for(var s in meta.slots)if(meta.slots.hasOwnProperty(s)){var traits=meta.slots[s].traits||[];for(var f=0;f<BTT_EXCLUSIVE_TRAIT_FAMILIES.length;f++){var fam=BTT_EXCLUSIVE_TRAIT_FAMILIES[f],last=-1;for(var i=0;i<traits.length;i++)if(fam.indexOf(traits[i])>=0)last=i;if(last>=0){for(var j=traits.length-1;j>=0;j--)if(j!==last&&fam.indexOf(traits[j])>=0)traits.splice(j,1);}}meta.slots[s].traits=BTT_unique(traits.map(function(x){return BTT_lower(x).replace(/\s+/g,"-");}));BTT_inferSlotOrigin(meta,s);}
}
function BTT_sanitizeSlotHeritageTraits(meta){
  var profiles=BTT_profiles("heritage");for(var slot in (meta&&meta.slots||{}))if(meta.slots.hasOwnProperty(slot)){var so=meta.slots[slot],traits=Array.isArray(so.traits)?so.traits.slice():[],origin=BTT_normId(so.origin||"");if(!origin){for(var i=0;i<traits.length;i++){var id=BTT_normId(traits[i]);if(id==="human"||profiles[id]){origin=id;break;}}}
    if(!origin)continue;var out=[origin];for(i=0;i<traits.length;i++){var tr=BTT_normId(traits[i]);if(!tr||tr===origin)continue;if(tr==="human"||profiles[tr])continue;out.push(tr);}so.origin=origin;so.traits=BTT_unique(out);
  }
}
function BTT_removeConditionTraitsFromSlots(meta){var cond={pregnant:1,incubating:1,seeded:1,infested:1,possessed:1,infected:1,corrupted:1,pollinated:1};for(var s in meta.slots)if(meta.slots.hasOwnProperty(s)){var a=meta.slots[s].traits||[],out=[];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(!cond[a[i]]&&!/^pregnant x\d+$/i.test(a[i])&&!/^incubating x\d+$/i.test(a[i]))out.push(a[i]);meta.slots[s].traits=out;}}
function BTT_enforceHeritageAvailability(meta){var ps=BTT_profiles("heritage");if(meta.heritage&&ps[meta.heritage])return;var hp=BTT_humanHeritageProfile();if(hp){meta.heritage=hp.id;meta.species=hp.id;meta.identity.heritage=hp.id;}else if(!meta.heritage){meta.heritage="human";meta.species="human";meta.identity.heritage="human";}}
function BTT_pruneEvidenceHistory(meta){var cfg=BTT_getConfig(),now=BTT_getTurn();for(var k in meta.evidenceHistory)if(meta.evidenceHistory.hasOwnProperty(k)&&now-Number(meta.evidenceHistory[k].turn||0)>cfg.retained_turns)delete meta.evidenceHistory[k];meta.conditionEvidenceHistory=meta.conditionEvidenceHistory||{};for(var ck in meta.conditionEvidenceHistory)if(meta.conditionEvidenceHistory.hasOwnProperty(ck)&&now-Number(meta.conditionEvidenceHistory[ck].turn||0)>cfg.retained_turns)delete meta.conditionEvidenceHistory[ck];}
function BTT_reconcileEntity(meta){
  if(!meta)return;BTT_enforceHeritageAvailability(meta);BTT_sanitizeExclusiveTraits(meta);BTT_sanitizeSlotHeritageTraits(meta);BTT_removeConditionTraitsFromSlots(meta);meta.conditions=meta.conditions.filter(function(c){return c&&c.active!==false&&Number(c.count||0)>0;});
  var ax=meta.appearance&&meta.appearance.axes||{};for(var k in ax)if(ax.hasOwnProperty(k)&&isFinite(Number(ax[k])))ax[k]=BTT_round(Number(ax[k]),2);
  meta.overallFem=BTT_round(BTT_clamp(ax.fem!==undefined?ax.fem:meta.overallFem,-100,100),2);ax.fem=meta.overallFem;if(meta.appearance)meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh=BTT_round(Number(meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh)||0,2);
  meta.identity.gender=meta.gender;meta.identity.heritage=meta.heritage;meta.identity.aliases=BTT_unique(meta.identity.aliases.concat([meta.identity.currentName]));BTT_pruneEvidenceHistory(meta);
}

/* ============================================================================
   16. FOUR-CARD SERIALIZATION AND HUMAN-READABLE DEBUG
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Every materialized entity owns exactly four cards. This writer is the only
   function that renders canonical runtime state into story cards. Source cards
   have blank triggers; only the NPC AI-Facing projection receives entity aliases.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_slotRenderTraits(meta,slot){
