  var so=meta.slots[slot],traits=so&&Array.isArray(so.traits)?so.traits.slice():[];if(so){if(slot==="breasts"){traits=traits.filter(function(x){return !/^cup-|^flat$/i.test(x);});traits.push(BTT_breastTrait(meta.appearance.axes.breast_size));}if(slot==="penis"){traits=traits.filter(function(x){return !/^cock-\d/i.test(x);});var ct=BTT_cockTrait(meta.appearance.axes.cock_size);if(ct)traits.push(ct);}if(slot==="ass"){traits=traits.filter(function(x){return !/^ass-(?:flat|average|curvy)$/i.test(x);});traits.push(BTT_assTrait(meta.appearance.axes.ass_size));}}
  for(var i=0;i<meta.conditions.length;i++){var c=meta.conditions[i];if(String(c.slot||"")===slot){var tr=BTT_conditionTrait(c.type,c.count);if(traits.indexOf(tr)<0)traits.push(tr);}}
  return BTT_unique(traits);
}
function BTT_identityLines(entity,meta,compact){
  var id=meta.identity,a=id.aliases||[],lines=["Identity:","  Entity ID: "+entity,"  Current Name: "+BTT_entityName(entity,meta),"  Original Name: "+String(id.originalName||BTT_entityName(entity,meta))];if(a.length&&!compact)lines.push("  Aliases: "+a.slice(0,8).join(", "));lines.push("  Gender: "+String(meta.gender||"unknown"));lines.push("  Pronouns: "+String(id.pronouns||"they/them/their"));if(id.previousPronouns&&id.pronounGrace>0&&!compact){lines.push("  Previous Pronouns: "+id.previousPronouns);lines.push("  Pronoun Grace: "+id.pronounGrace);}lines.push("  Heritage: "+String(meta.heritage||"human"));return lines;
}
function BTT_compactConditionLines(meta){var lines=["BTT Conditions:"];if(!meta.conditions.length){lines.push("  Active: none");return lines;}lines.push("  Active:");for(var i=0;i<meta.conditions.length;i++){var c=meta.conditions[i],line="    - "+BTT_conditionType(c.type)+(Number(c.count)>1?" x"+Number(c.count):"")+" | slot="+String(c.slot||"system");if(c.sourceEntityId)line+=" | source="+String(c.sourceEntityId);else if(c.source&&c.source!=="unknown")line+=" | source="+BTT_clip(c.source,28);line+=" | turn="+String(c.startedTurn||0);lines.push(line);}return lines;}
function BTT_anatomyEntry(entity,meta,compact){
  var npc=/^NPC:/.test(entity),name=BTT_entityName(entity,meta),lines=[String(name||entity).toUpperCase()+" ANATOMY ("+(npc?"BTT NPC":"BTT")+")",""];lines=lines.concat(BTT_identityLines(entity,meta,compact),[""]);for(var i=0;i<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;i++){var sl=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[i],traits=BTT_slotRenderTraits(meta,sl);lines.push(sl.toUpperCase()+": "+(traits.length?traits.join("; "):"none"));}lines.push("");lines=lines.concat(BTT_compactConditionLines(meta),["","Femininity (overall): "+BTT_round(meta.overallFem,2)]);if(npc){lines.push("Entity: "+entity);lines.push("BTT NPC Version: "+BTT_VERSION);}return lines.join("\n").replace(/\n{4,}/g,"\n\n\n");
}
function BTT_describeHeight(v){v=Number(v)||20;if(v<14)return "very short height";if(v<18)return "short height";if(v<=22)return "average height";if(v<=27)return "tall height";return "very tall height";}
function BTT_describeChest(v){v=Number(v)||0;if(v<=0)return "flat chest";return BTT_axisToCup(v)+" cup chest";}
function BTT_describeRear(v){v=Number(v)||0;if(v<10)return "flat rear";if(v<22)return "average rear";return "rounded rear";}
function BTT_describePenis(v){var n=BTT_axisToInches(v);if(n<=0)return "no penis";return String(Math.round(n*10)/10).replace(/\.0$/,'')+" inch penis";}
function BTT_describeFem(v){v=Number(v)||0;if(v>60)return "strongly feminine presentation";if(v>20)return "feminine presentation";if(v>=-20)return "androgynous presentation";if(v>=-60)return "masculine presentation";return "strongly masculine presentation";}
function BTT_describeFertility(v){v=Number(v)||0;if(v<=0)return "no fertility";if(v<15)return "low fertility";if(v<35)return "average fertility";if(v<60)return "high fertility";return "extreme fertility";}
function BTT_describeVirility(v){v=Number(v)||0;if(v<=0)return "no virility";if(v<15)return "low virility";if(v<35)return "average virility";if(v<60)return "high virility";return "extreme virility";}

function BTT_appearanceEntry(entity,meta){
  var f=meta.appearance.fields,ax=meta.appearance.axes,npc=/^NPC:/.test(entity),summary=entity==="Player"?(String(meta.appearance.summary_ai||"").trim()||"[Awaiting appearance description from AI refresh]"):"[NPC AI appearance refresh disabled; structured canonical fields follow.]",lines=[BTT_entityName(entity,meta).toUpperCase()+" APPEARANCE ("+(npc?"BTT NPC":"BTT")+")","",summary,"","Species: "+String(meta.species||meta.heritage||"human"),"Hair Color: "+String(f.hair_color||""),"Hair Length: "+String(f.hair_length||""),"Eye Color: "+String(f.eye_color||""),"Skin: "+String(f.skin||""),"Build: "+String(f.build||""),"Scent: "+String(f.scent||""),"Height: "+BTT_describeHeight(ax.height),"Chest: "+BTT_describeChest(ax.breast_size),"Rear: "+BTT_describeRear(ax.ass_size),"Penis: "+BTT_describePenis(ax.cock_size),"Presentation: "+BTT_describeFem(ax.fem),"Fertility: "+BTT_describeFertility(ax.fertility),"Virility: "+BTT_describeVirility(ax.virility),"Height Axis: "+BTT_round(ax.height,2),"Fat Axis: "+BTT_round(ax.fat,2),"Gut Size: "+BTT_round(ax.gut_size,2),"Breast Size: "+BTT_round(ax.breast_size,2),"Ass Size: "+BTT_round(ax.ass_size,2),"Cock Size: "+BTT_round(ax.cock_size,2),"Fertility Axis: "+BTT_round(ax.fertility,2),"Virility Axis: "+BTT_round(ax.virility,2),"Fem: "+BTT_round(ax.fem,2)];return lines.join("\n");
}
function BTT_effectLabel(e){var p=BTT_profile(e.kind,e.profileId);return String(p&&p.name||e.profileName||e.profileId);}
function BTT_snapshotReadableLines(snap){
  if(!snap)return ["Source Snapshot: none"];
  var lines=["Identity:","  Gender: "+String(snap.gender||"unknown"),"  Pronouns: "+String(snap.identity&&snap.identity.pronouns||"unknown"),"  Heritage: "+String(snap.heritage||"unknown"),"","Anatomy:"];
  for(var i=0;i<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;i++){var slot=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[i],row=snap.slots&&snap.slots[slot],traits=row&&Array.isArray(row.traits)?row.traits:[];lines.push("  "+slot.toUpperCase()+": "+(traits.length?traits.join("; "):"none"));}
  var app=snap.appearance||{},fields=app.fields||{},axes=app.axes||{};lines=lines.concat(["","Appearance:","  Skin: "+String(fields.skin||""),"  Hair Color: "+String(fields.hair_color||""),"  Hair Length: "+String(fields.hair_length||""),"  Eye Color: "+String(fields.eye_color||""),"  Build: "+String(fields.build||""),"  Scent: "+String(fields.scent||""),"  Height Axis: "+BTT_round(axes.height,2),"  Fat Axis: "+BTT_round(axes.fat,2),"  Gut Size: "+BTT_round(axes.gut_size,2),"  Breast Size: "+BTT_round(axes.breast_size,2),"  Ass Size: "+BTT_round(axes.ass_size,2),"  Cock Size: "+BTT_round(axes.cock_size,2),"  Fertility Axis: "+BTT_round(axes.fertility,2),"  Virility Axis: "+BTT_round(axes.virility,2),"  Fem: "+BTT_round(snap.overallFem!==undefined?snap.overallFem:axes.fem,2)]);return lines;
}
function BTT_lifecycleEntry(entity,meta){
  var x=meta.instantchange,lines=[BTT_entityName(entity,meta).toUpperCase()+" INSTANT-CHANGE STATE (BTT)","","[BTT-managed lifecycle record. Current anatomy remains editable on Anatomy BTT.]","","Current Day: "+BTT_getDay()];
  if(x.effects&&x.effects.length){lines.push("Active Effect:");for(var i=0;i<x.effects.length;i++){var e=x.effects[i],timing=e.revertDay===null?"permanent until a defined reversal":"revert day "+e.revertDay;
      lines.push("  - "+BTT_effectLabel(e)+" ("+e.kind+") | started day "+e.startedDay+" turn "+e.startedTurn+" | "+timing);
      if(e.durationModifier)lines.push("    Duration Modifier: "+String(e.durationModifier.label||e.durationModifier.alias||"configured")+" -> "+(e.durationModifier.permanent?"permanent":String(e.durationModifier.timedDays)+"d"));
    }}else lines.push("Active Effect: none");
  if(x.canonicalProfile&&x.canonicalProfile.components&&x.canonicalProfile.components.length){lines.push("","Permanent Canonical State: "+String(x.canonicalProfile.profileName||x.canonicalProfile.components.map(function(c){return c.kind+":"+c.profileId;}).join(", ")));
    if(x.canonicalProfile.durationModifier)lines.push("Duration Modifier: "+String(x.canonicalProfile.durationModifier.label||x.canonicalProfile.durationModifier.alias||"configured")+" -> "+(x.canonicalProfile.durationModifier.permanent?"permanent":String(x.canonicalProfile.durationModifier.timedDays)+"d"));
    lines.push("Permanence Cause: "+String(x.canonicalProfile.permanenceCause||"unknown"));lines.push("Established Turn: "+String(x.canonicalProfile.establishedTurn||0));
  }
  lines.push("","REVERSION SOURCE SNAPSHOT");lines=lines.concat(BTT_snapshotReadableLines(x.sourceSnapshot));
  return lines.join("\n");
}
function BTT_riskSummary(meta){var lines=["--- RISKS (Current State) ---"],owners=BTT_riskOwners(meta),any=false;for(var oi=0;oi<owners.length;oi++){var owner=owners[oi],tracks=owner.risk_tracks||[];for(var t=0;t<tracks.length;t++){any=true;var st=BTT_riskState(owner,tracks[t].id);lines.push((owner.profileName||owner.profileId||"Profile")+" / "+tracks[t].id+": "+(st.complete?"COMPLETE":"ACTIVE")+" | exposures="+st.exposureCount+" | successes="+st.successCount+(st.lastRoll?" | last="+st.lastRoll.roll+"/"+st.lastRoll.chance:""));}}if(!any)lines.push("No active or persistent Instant-Change risk state.");return lines;}
function BTT_machinePayload(meta){var instant=BTT_clone(meta.instantchange);instant.currentDay=BTT_getDay();return {version:BTT_VERSION,currentDay:BTT_getDay(),instantchange:instant,developableRisks:BTT_clone(meta.developableRisks||{active:[],conditionedTriggers:[],acquisitionHistory:{}})};}
function BTT_debugSection(title,lines){if(!lines||!lines.length)return [];return ["--- DEBUG ("+title+") ---"].concat(lines);}
function BTT_descriptionForAnatomy(meta){var out=[],domains=["manual","identity","anatomy","conditions"];for(var i=0;i<domains.length;i++){var lines=BTT_recentDebug(meta,domains[i]);if(lines.length){if(out.length)out.push("");out=out.concat(BTT_debugSection("BTT "+domains[i].charAt(0).toUpperCase()+domains[i].slice(1),lines));}}return out.join("\n");}
function BTT_appearanceRefreshPayload(meta){var a=meta&&meta.appearance||{};return {need_ai_refresh:!!a._need_ai_refresh,pending:!!a._pendingRefresh,did_ai_init:!!a._did_ai_init,last_refresh_turn:isFinite(Number(a._last_refresh_turn))?Number(a._last_refresh_turn):-1,delta_since_refresh:BTT_round(Number(a._delta_since_refresh)||0,2),reason:String(a._refresh_flag_reason||""),requested_turn:isFinite(Number(a._refresh_requested_turn))?Number(a._refresh_requested_turn):-1,captured_turn:isFinite(Number(a._refresh_captured_turn))?Number(a._refresh_captured_turn):-1,request_id:String(a._refresh_request_id||""),signature:String(a._refresh_signature||"")};}

function BTT_descriptionForAppearance(entity,meta){
  var out=[],app=meta.appearance;if(entity==="Player")out.push(app.summary_ai?"BTT-managed appearance description. AI-authored via tagged refresh capture.":"BTT-managed appearance description. Awaiting AI refresh block capture.");else out.push("BTT NPC structured appearance record. AI-authored refresh is disabled for NPCs.");
  var dbg=[],a=app.debug||[],min=BTT_getTurn()-BTT_getConfig().debug_keep_turns+1;for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(Number(a[i].turn)>=min)dbg.push(a[i].line);if(dbg.length)out=out.concat(["","--- DEBUG (Appearance Changes) ---"],dbg);
  if(entity==="Player"){var status=app.summary_ai&&app._refresh_captured_turn>=0?"CAPTURED":app._pendingRefresh?"PENDING":app._need_ai_refresh?"REQUESTED":"IDLE";out=out.concat(["","--- DEBUG (Appearance Refresh) ---","REFRESH | status="+status+" | need="+(app._need_ai_refresh?"YES":"NO")+" | pending="+(app._pendingRefresh?"YES":"NO")+" | init="+(app._did_ai_init?"YES":"NO")+" | requested="+String(app._refresh_requested_turn)+" | request_id="+String(app._refresh_request_id||"")+" | captured="+String(app._refresh_captured_turn)+" | last="+String(app._last_refresh_turn)+" | delta="+BTT_round(app._delta_since_refresh,2)+" | reason="+String(app._refresh_flag_reason||""),"","--- BTT APPEARANCE REFRESH STATE ---","Payload JSON: "+JSON.stringify(BTT_appearanceRefreshPayload(meta)),"--- END BTT APPEARANCE REFRESH STATE ---"]);}
  return out.join("\n");
}
function BTT_descriptionForLifecycle(entity,meta){
  var out=["BTT lifecycle persistence. Instant-Change and Developable Risk runtimes are separate systems serialized in one machine payload.",""]
    .concat(BTT_riskSummary(meta),[""],BTT_devRiskSummary(meta)),
    idbg=BTT_recentDebug(meta,"instantchange"),rdbg=BTT_recentDebug(meta,"risks"),ddbg=BTT_recentDebug(meta,"devrisks");
  if(idbg.length)out=out.concat([""],BTT_debugSection("BTT Instant-Change",idbg));
  if(rdbg.length)out=out.concat([""],BTT_debugSection("BTT Instant-Change Risks",rdbg));
  if(ddbg.length)out=out.concat([""],BTT_debugSection("BTT Developable Risks",ddbg));
  out=out.concat(["","--- BTT INSTANT-CHANGE MACHINE STATE ---","Payload JSON: "+JSON.stringify(BTT_machinePayload(meta)),"--- END BTT INSTANT-CHANGE MACHINE STATE ---","",BTT_roleMarker(entity,"instantchange")]);
  return out.join("\n");
}
function BTT_aiFacingEntry(entity,meta){
  var lines=[BTT_entityName(entity,meta).toUpperCase()+" AI-FACING ("+(/^NPC:/.test(entity)?"BTT NPC":"BTT")+")",""];
  if(entity==="Player"&&String(meta.appearance.summary_ai||"").trim())lines.push("Appearance Description: "+String(meta.appearance.summary_ai).trim(),"");
  lines.push("Identity: Gender="+meta.gender+"; Pronouns="+meta.identity.pronouns+"; Heritage="+meta.heritage,
    "Appearance Facts: Height="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.height,2)+"; Fat="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.fat,2)+"; Gut Size="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.gut_size,2)+"; Breast Size="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.breast_size,2)+"; Ass Size="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.ass_size,2)+"; Cock Size="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.cock_size,2)+"; Fertility="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.fertility,2)+"; Virility="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.virility,2)+"; Fem="+BTT_round(meta.appearance.axes.fem,2),"Anatomy:");
  for(var i=0;i<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;i++){var sl=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[i],traits=BTT_slotRenderTraits(meta,sl);lines.push(sl.toUpperCase()+": "+(traits.length?traits.join("; "):"none"));}
  if(meta.conditions.length)lines.push("Conditions: "+meta.conditions.map(function(c){return BTT_conditionTrait(c.type,c.count);}).join(", "));
  var dr=meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.active||[];if(dr.length)lines.push("Developable Risks: "+dr.map(function(x){return x.name||x.id;}).join(", "));
  var tr=meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers||[];if(tr.length)lines.push("Persistent Triggers: "+tr.map(function(x){return x.id+"="+x.description;}).join("; "));
  return lines.join("\n");
}
function BTT_aiKeys(entity,meta){if(entity==="Player")return "";return BTT_unique(meta.identity.aliases.concat([meta.identity.currentName])).filter(function(x){return x&&x.length>=2&&!/^(?:you|your|he|him|his|she|her|they|them|their)$/i.test(x);}).join(",");}
function BTT_writeEntity(entity,meta){
  if(!meta||!meta.initialized)return;BTT_reconcileEntity(meta);var set=BTT_ensureEntityCardSet(entity,meta);if(!set||!set.anatomy||!set.appearance||!set.instantchange||!set.ai)throw new Error("Unable to materialize complete BTT four-card set for "+entity);var anatomy=BTT_anatomyEntry(entity,meta,false),cfg=BTT_getConfig();if(anatomy.length>cfg.anatomy_warning_chars)anatomy=BTT_anatomyEntry(entity,meta,true);if(anatomy.length>=1000)BTT_touchDebug(meta,"manual","ANATOMY SIZE WARNING | chars="+anatomy.length+" | AI Dungeon manual editor limit=1000");
  set.anatomy.entry=anatomy;set.anatomy.description=BTT_descriptionForAnatomy(meta)+"\n\n"+BTT_roleMarker(entity,"anatomy");set.anatomy.keys="";
  set.appearance.entry=BTT_appearanceEntry(entity,meta);set.appearance.description=BTT_descriptionForAppearance(entity,meta)+"\n\n"+BTT_roleMarker(entity,"appearance");set.appearance.keys="";
  set.instantchange.entry=BTT_lifecycleEntry(entity,meta);set.instantchange.description=BTT_descriptionForLifecycle(entity,meta);set.instantchange.keys="";
  set.ai.entry=BTT_aiFacingEntry(entity,meta);set.ai.description=BTT_aiMarker(entity);set.ai.keys=BTT_aiKeys(entity,meta);
  var type=BTT_cardType(entity,meta);set.anatomy.type=type;set.appearance.type=type;set.instantchange.type=type;set.ai.type=type;if("cardType" in set.anatomy)set.anatomy.cardType=type;if("cardType" in set.appearance)set.appearance.cardType=type;if("cardType" in set.instantchange)set.instantchange.cardType=type;if("cardType" in set.ai)set.ai.cardType=type;
  meta.lastSerialized.anatomy=set.anatomy.entry;meta.lastSerialized.appearance=set.appearance.entry;meta.lastSerialized.appearanceDescription=set.appearance.description;meta.lastSerialized.instantchange=set.instantchange.description;meta.lastSerialized.ai=set.ai.entry;meta.lastSerialized.anatomyParsed=BTT_parseAnatomyEntry(set.anatomy.entry);meta.lastSerialized.appearanceParsed=BTT_parseAppearanceEntry(set.appearance.entry);
}

/* ============================================================================
   17. CONTEXT PROJECTION AND PLAYER APPEARANCE REFRESH TRANSPORT
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Context reads finalized live state only. It does not rerun narrative evidence.
   Only the Player may request AI-authored appearance prose. A first-output
   player_appearance metadata operation is injected on demand, stripped from
   visible output, and stored once.
   NPCs never request AI refreshes and no entity receives fabricated prose.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_refreshSignature(meta){var app=meta&&meta.appearance||{},slots={};for(var i=0;i<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;i++){var sl=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[i],so=meta&&meta.slots&&meta.slots[sl];slots[sl]=so&&so.traits?so.traits.slice():[];}return String(BTT_hash(JSON.stringify({gender:meta&&meta.gender||"",heritage:meta&&meta.heritage||"",overallFem:meta&&meta.overallFem||0,slots:slots,fields:app.fields||{},axes:app.axes||{}})));}
function BTT_refreshRequestId(meta,turn){return "apr-"+String(turn)+"-"+BTT_refreshSignature(meta);}
function BTT_requestAppearanceRefresh(meta,reason){
  /* Refresh scheduling is signature-based, but one transaction may legitimately
     update several appearance fields before context is built. Changes that occur
     during the same request turn are therefore coalesced into one pending request
     instead of emitting a chain of self-superseding refresh records. */
  if(!meta||meta.entity!=="Player"||!meta.appearance)return false;
  var app=meta.appearance,turn=BTT_getTurn(),sig=BTT_refreshSignature(meta),requested=Number(app._refresh_requested_turn),captured=Number(app._refresh_captured_turn),active=!!app._need_ai_refresh&&isFinite(requested)&&requested>=0&&(!isFinite(captured)||captured<requested),changed=active&&String(app._refresh_signature||"")!==sig;
  app._need_ai_refresh=true;app._refresh_flag_reason=String(reason||app._refresh_flag_reason||"requested");
  if(!active){
    app._pendingRefresh=false;app._refresh_requested_turn=turn;app._refresh_signature=sig;app._refresh_request_id=BTT_refreshRequestId(meta,turn);
    BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"REFRESH REQUESTED | turn="+turn+" | request_id="+app._refresh_request_id+" | reason="+app._refresh_flag_reason);
  }else if(changed){
    var oldId=String(app._refresh_request_id||""),sameTurn=requested===turn;
    app._refresh_signature=sig;app._refresh_request_id=BTT_refreshRequestId(meta,sameTurn?requested:turn);
    if(!sameTurn){
      app._pendingRefresh=false;app._refresh_requested_turn=turn;
      BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"REFRESH SUPERSEDED | turn="+turn+" | request_id="+app._refresh_request_id+" | prior="+(oldId||"none")+" | reason="+app._refresh_flag_reason);
    }
  }else{
    if(!app._refresh_signature)app._refresh_signature=sig;
    if(!app._refresh_request_id)app._refresh_request_id=BTT_refreshRequestId(meta,requested);
  }
  return true;
}
function BTT_shouldRefreshAppearance(entity,meta,cfg){
  if(entity!=="Player"||!meta||!meta.appearance)return false;cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();if(cfg.appearance_refresh_enabled===false)return false;if(meta.appearance._pendingRefresh||meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh)return true;if(cfg.appearance_refresh_force_init!==false&&(!meta.appearance._did_ai_init||!String(meta.appearance.summary_ai||"").trim()))return true;var last=isFinite(Number(meta.appearance._last_refresh_turn))?Number(meta.appearance._last_refresh_turn):-1,turnRule=cfg.appearance_refresh_turns>0&&BTT_getTurn()-last>=cfg.appearance_refresh_turns,deltaRule=cfg.appearance_refresh_delta>0&&Number(meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh||0)>=cfg.appearance_refresh_delta;return turnRule||deltaRule;
}
function BTT_updateAppearanceRefresh(entity,meta,cfg){
  if(!meta||!meta.appearance)return false;if(entity!=="Player"){meta.appearance.summary_ai="";meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=false;meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=false;meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason="npc_refresh_disabled";meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn=-1;meta.appearance._refresh_captured_turn=-1;meta.appearance._refresh_request_id="";meta.appearance._refresh_signature="";return false;}cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();if(cfg.appearance_refresh_enabled===false){meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=false;meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=false;meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason="refresh_disabled";return false;}var need=BTT_shouldRefreshAppearance(entity,meta,cfg),reason=meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh&&meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason?meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason:(!meta.appearance._did_ai_init||!meta.appearance.summary_ai?"standardized_init":Number(meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh||0)>=cfg.appearance_refresh_delta&&cfg.appearance_refresh_delta>0?"axis_delta":"turn_interval");if(need)return BTT_requestAppearanceRefresh(meta,reason);meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=false;meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=false;meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason="not_scheduled";return false;
}
function BTT_appearancePronouns(meta,cfg){
  cfg=cfg||BTT_getConfig();if(cfg.pronoun_policy==="fixed")return BTT_defaultPronouns(meta.gender);if(cfg.pronoun_policy!=="hybrid")return meta.identity.pronouns||BTT_defaultPronouns(meta.gender);var fem=Number(meta.overallFem)||0,band=Math.max(0,Number(cfg.pronoun_they_band)||0),pen=!!meta.slots.penis,vag=!!meta.slots.vagina;if(cfg.pronoun_switch_on_vagina&&vag&&!pen)return "she/her/her";if(cfg.pronoun_switch_on_penis&&pen&&!vag)return "he/him/his";if(fem>band)return "she/her/her";if(fem<-band)return "he/him/his";return "they/them/their";
}
function BTT_appearanceRefreshInstruction(entity,meta){
  if(entity!=="Player"||!meta||!meta.appearance||!meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh)return "";var ax=meta.appearance.axes,f=meta.appearance.fields,lines=["<SYSTEM>","# BTT APPEARANCE REFRESH TASK","Before continuing the story, FIRST output exactly one metadata operation in this form:","(player_appearance = `<1-4 concise sentences describing only the player's current physical appearance>`)","Then continue the story normally.","Do not explain, discuss, or quote the metadata operation. Do not place actions, dialogue, thoughts, analysis, or instructions inside it.","Use the canonical current appearance facts below as authoritative and describe only visible/current physical appearance.","Species: "+String(meta.species||meta.heritage||"human"),"Gender phenotype: "+String(meta.gender||"unknown"),"Appearance-description pronouns: "+BTT_appearancePronouns(meta,BTT_getConfig())];if(f.skin)lines.push("Skin: "+f.skin);if(f.hair_color)lines.push("Hair Color: "+f.hair_color);if(f.hair_length)lines.push("Hair Length: "+f.hair_length);if(f.eye_color)lines.push("Eye Color: "+f.eye_color);if(f.build)lines.push("Build: "+f.build);if(f.scent)lines.push("Scent: "+f.scent);lines.push("Height: "+BTT_describeHeight(ax.height),"Body Fat Axis: "+BTT_round(ax.fat,2),"Gut Size Axis: "+BTT_round(ax.gut_size,2),"Chest: "+BTT_describeChest(ax.breast_size),"Rear: "+BTT_describeRear(ax.ass_size),"Penis: "+BTT_describePenis(ax.cock_size),"Presentation: "+BTT_describeFem(ax.fem),"</SYSTEM>");return lines.join("\n");
}
function BTT_normalizeAppearanceRefreshPayload(payload){
  /* The prompt requests backticks, but model output may substitute ordinary or
     smart quotation marks or omit outer quotes entirely. Only a balanced outer
     quote pair is removed; punctuation inside the prose is preserved. */
  var s=String(payload||"").trim();if(!s)return "";
  var first=s.charAt(0),last=s.charAt(s.length-1),close={"`":"`","'":"'","\"":"\"","‘":"’","“":"”"}[first];
  if(close&&last===close)s=s.slice(1,-1).trim();
  return BTT_cleanSpace(s);
}
function BTT_findAppearanceRefreshOperation(raw,fromIndex){
  /* Locate one exact `(player_appearance = ...)` operation without depending on
     one quote style. Quoted payloads are closed only when the matching outer
     quote is followed by the operation's closing parenthesis, so apostrophes or
     quotations inside the appearance prose do not truncate capture. Unquoted
     payloads use balanced-parenthesis scanning to tolerate parenthetical prose. */
  var t=String(raw||""),re=/\(\s*player_appearance\s*=/ig;re.lastIndex=Math.max(0,Number(fromIndex)||0);var m=re.exec(t);if(!m)return null;
  var start=m.index,payloadStart=re.lastIndex;while(payloadStart<t.length&&/\s/.test(t.charAt(payloadStart)))payloadStart++;
  var opener=t.charAt(payloadStart),pairs={"`":"`","'":"'","\"":"\"","‘":"’","“":"”"},closer=pairs[opener]||"",end=-1,payloadEnd=-1;
  if(closer){
    for(var qi=payloadStart+1;qi<t.length;qi++)if(t.charAt(qi)===closer){var j=qi+1;while(j<t.length&&/\s/.test(t.charAt(j)))j++;if(t.charAt(j)===")"){payloadEnd=qi+1;end=j+1;break;}}
  }
  if(end<0){
    var depth=0;for(var i=payloadStart;i<t.length;i++){var ch=t.charAt(i);if(ch==="("){depth++;continue;}if(ch!==")")continue;if(depth>0){depth--;continue;}payloadEnd=i;end=i+1;break;}
  }
  if(end<0||payloadEnd<payloadStart)return null;
  return {start:start,end:end,payload:t.slice(payloadStart,payloadEnd)};
}
function BTT_extractAppearanceRefresh(raw){
  /* Strip every valid metadata operation from visible output while capturing the
     first non-empty payload. This prevents repeated model metadata from leaking
     into story prose without allowing arbitrary parenthetical text to match. */
  var t=String(raw||""),cursor=0,parts=[],prose="",tagged=false,op;
  while((op=BTT_findAppearanceRefreshOperation(t,cursor))){
    parts.push(t.slice(cursor,op.start));tagged=true;
    var candidate=BTT_normalizeAppearanceRefreshPayload(op.payload);if(!prose&&candidate)prose=candidate;
    cursor=op.end;
  }
  if(!tagged)return {cleaned:t,prose:"",tagged:false};
  parts.push(t.slice(cursor));
  return {cleaned:parts.join("").replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,"").replace(/\n{3,}/g,"\n\n"),prose:prose,tagged:true};
}
function BTT_captureAppearanceRefresh(raw,entity){
  entity=entity||"Player";var x=BTT_extractAppearanceRefresh(raw);if(entity!=="Player")return x.cleaned;var meta=BTT_getEntity("Player"),turn=BTT_getTurn(),expected=String(meta.appearance._refresh_request_id||"");if(x.prose){meta.appearance.summary_ai=x.prose;meta.appearance._did_ai_init=true;meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh=false;meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=false;meta.appearance._delta_since_refresh=0;meta.appearance._last_refresh_turn=turn;meta.appearance._refresh_captured_turn=turn;meta.appearance._refresh_flag_reason="captured";meta.appearance._refresh_signature=BTT_refreshSignature(meta);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"REFRESH CAPTURED | turn="+turn+" | request_id="+(expected||"none")+" | transport=player_appearance | len="+x.prose.length);}else if(meta.appearance._pendingRefresh){BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"REFRESH CAPTURE MISS | turn="+turn+" | request_id="+(expected||"none")+" | player_appearance=NO");}return x.cleaned;
}
function BTT_injectAppearanceRefreshTask(ctx,meta){
  var task=BTT_appearanceRefreshInstruction("Player",meta);if(!task)return String(ctx||"");var text0=String(ctx||""),joined=text0.trimEnd()+"\n\n"+task,limit=0;try{limit=Math.max(0,Number(info&&info.maxChars)||0);}catch(_e){}if(limit>0&&joined.length>limit){var needle="Recent Story:",idx=joined.indexOf(needle),taskStart=joined.lastIndexOf("<SYSTEM>\n# BTT APPEARANCE REFRESH TASK"),overflow=joined.length-limit;if(idx>=0&&taskStart>idx&&overflow>0){var storyStart=idx+needle.length,story=joined.slice(storyStart,taskStart),cut=Math.min(Math.max(0,story.length-1200),overflow+64);if(cut>0)joined=joined.slice(0,storyStart)+"\n[older recent story trimmed for BTT appearance task]\n"+story.slice(cut)+joined.slice(taskStart);}if(joined.length>limit)joined=joined.slice(Math.max(0,joined.length-limit));}return joined;
}
function BTT_contextExcerpt(meta,cfg){
  var lines=["[BTT PLAYER CANON]","Identity: "+BTT_entityName("Player",meta)+" | Gender="+meta.gender+" | Pronouns="+meta.identity.pronouns+" | Heritage="+meta.heritage];if(cfg.context_mode==="full"){for(var i=0;i<BTT_RENDER_SLOTS.length;i++){var s=BTT_RENDER_SLOTS[i],tr=BTT_slotRenderTraits(meta,s);lines.push(s.toUpperCase()+": "+(tr.length?tr.join("; "):"none"));}}else{var keep=["head","breasts","gut","ass","tail","wings"];if(cfg.excerpt_always_include_genitals)keep=keep.concat(["penis","vagina"]);for(var k=0;k<keep.length;k++){var sl=keep[k],traits=BTT_slotRenderTraits(meta,sl);if(traits.length||cfg.excerpt_always_include_genitals&&(sl==="penis"||sl==="vagina"))lines.push(sl.toUpperCase()+": "+(traits.length?traits.join("; "):"none"));}}
  if(meta.conditions.length)lines.push("Conditions: "+meta.conditions.map(function(c){return BTT_conditionTrait(c.type,c.count);}).join(", "));if(meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.active&&meta.developableRisks.active.length)lines.push("Developable Risks: "+meta.developableRisks.active.map(function(r){return r.name;}).join(", "));if(meta.developableRisks&&meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers&&meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers.length)lines.push("Persistent Triggers: "+meta.developableRisks.conditionedTriggers.map(function(r){return r.id+"="+r.description;}).join("; "));var f=meta.appearance.fields,ax=meta.appearance.axes,facts=["Species="+String(meta.species||meta.heritage||"human"),"Height="+BTT_round(ax.height,2),"Fat="+BTT_round(ax.fat,2),"Gut Size="+BTT_round(ax.gut_size,2),"Breast Size="+BTT_round(ax.breast_size,2),"Ass Size="+BTT_round(ax.ass_size,2),"Cock Size="+BTT_round(ax.cock_size,2),"Fertility="+BTT_round(ax.fertility,2),"Virility="+BTT_round(ax.virility,2),"Fem="+BTT_round(ax.fem,2)];if(f.skin)facts.push("Skin="+f.skin);if(f.hair_color)facts.push("Hair Color="+f.hair_color);if(f.hair_length)facts.push("Hair Length="+f.hair_length);if(f.eye_color)facts.push("Eye Color="+f.eye_color);if(f.build)facts.push("Build="+f.build);lines.push("Appearance Facts: "+facts.join("; "));if(String(meta.appearance.summary_ai||"").trim())lines.push("Appearance Description: "+String(meta.appearance.summary_ai).trim());lines.push("[/BTT PLAYER CANON]");return lines.join("\n")+"\n\n";
}
function BTT_sanitizeProfileCardTriggers(){if(!Array.isArray(storyCards))return;for(var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++){var c=storyCards[i];if(BTT_profileKind(c)||BTT_isDevRiskCard(c)||String(c&&c.title||"")==="Configure BTT"||String(c&&c.title||"")==="BTT NPC Targets"||String(c&&c.title||"")==="BTT AI Dev Risk Generation Debug")c.keys="";}}

/* ============================================================================
   18. CANONICAL TRANSACTION COORDINATOR AND PUBLIC ROUTER
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   These are the only active public hook bindings. Input/output each perform one
   transaction and call BTT_parseNarrative exactly once. Context is read-only
   with respect to gameplay interpretation.
============================================================================ */

function BTT_causalIndexFor(events,entity){var idx=null;for(var i=0;i<events.length;i++)if(events[i].type==="administration"&&events[i].recipient===entity){var n=events[i].fragment.index;if(idx===null||n<idx)idx=n;}return idx;}
function BTT_transactionEntities(work){var out=[];for(var e in work)if(work.hasOwnProperty(e))out.push(e);return out;}
function BTT_processNarrative(raw,phase){
  BTT_ensureState();var cfg=BTT_getConfig();if(!cfg.enabled)return String(raw||"");
  /* Preflight is the only card->runtime authority boundary. No later gameplay
     function reads cards into live state. */
  BTT_preflightImportAll();BTT_sanitizeProfileCardTriggers();BTT_prunePhysicalGraph();
  var visible=String(raw||"");if(phase==="output")visible=BTT_captureAppearanceRefresh(visible,"Player");
  BTT_advanceWorldDay(visible);if(phase==="output")BTT_ensureState().turnCounter=(Number(BTT_ensureState().turnCounter)||0)+1;
  var parse=BTT_parseNarrative(visible,phase);
  /* parse.shared now contains the complete explicit owner→slot lexical cache.
     No downstream gameplay domain is permitted to rebuild that evidence. */
  BTT_ensureState().currentTransaction={phase:phase,parse:parse,startedTurn:BTT_getTurn(),action:BTT_nowAction()};
  var work=BTT_materializeEntities(parse);
  /* NPC genesis is resolved before causal mutation so a first transformation
     snapshots the best available pre-change identity/body rather than a neutral
     placeholder created moments earlier. */
  BTT_prepareGenesis(work,parse);
  /* Ownership routing is complete before domain accumulation. Diagnostic logging
     happens here so wrong-owner evidence is visible without ever entering that
     owner's Anatomy/Appearance histories. */
  BTT_logOwnerBoundObservationRouting(parse,work);
  var events=BTT_resolveProductEvents(parse);BTT_recordProductEventObservations(parse,events);BTT_processInstantEvents(events);
  var ids=BTT_transactionEntities(work);
  for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){var entity=ids[i],meta=BTT_getEntity(entity),ci=BTT_causalIndexFor(events,entity);work[entity]=meta;BTT_processIdentity(meta,entity,parse,ci);BTT_processAnatomy(meta,entity,parse,ci);BTT_processAppearance(meta,entity,parse,ci);}
  BTT_processConditions(work,parse);
  /* Developable Risks are acquired/progressed from ordinary world evidence and
     canonical Conditions. They remain independent of Instant-Change lifecycle. */
  BTT_processDevelopableRisks(work,parse);
  BTT_considerDevRiskDiscovery(parse);
  /* Risks resolve while reversible snapshots still exist. Condition-triggered
     permanence and other destructive lifecycle consequences are deferred until
     after all same-event destination writes have completed. */
  BTT_processRisks(work,parse);
  for(var j=0;j<ids.length;j++){var m=work[ids[j]];BTT_applyLifecycleText(m,parse);BTT_conditionPermanence(m);BTT_applyExpirations(m);BTT_reconcileEntity(m);}
  if(phase==="output"){var pm=BTT_getEntity("Player");BTT_updateAppearanceRefresh("Player",pm,cfg);}
  for(var w=0;w<ids.length;w++)BTT_writeEntity(ids[w],work[ids[w]]);
  BTT_writeDevRiskGenerationDebugCard();
  /* If an OUTPUT transaction supplied the qualifying event, the maintenance prompt
     cannot be injected retroactively into that same model call. Emit the kickoff
     notice immediately so the next user action is an intentional Continue. */
  var gen=BTT_ensureDevRiskGenerationState();if(phase==="output"&&gen.pending&&!gen.pending.kickoffNoticeEmitted){gen.pending.kickoffNoticeEmitted=true;gen.lastMaintenanceNotice=BTT_devKickoffNotice(gen.pending);visible=String(visible||"").trimEnd()+"\n\n"+gen.lastMaintenanceNotice;BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING KICKOFF NOTICE | candidate="+gen.pending.candidateId+" | story_turn="+BTT_getTurn()+" | next_action=CONTINUE | fields="+BTT_devRequestedFields(gen.pending).join(","));BTT_writeDevRiskGenerationDebugCard();}
  BTT_ensureState().currentTransaction=null;return visible;
}
function onInput_BTT(text){try{if(BTT_devRiskAuthoringPending()){var preserved=String(text||"");BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("MAINTENANCE INPUT BYPASS | action="+BTT_nowAction()+" | story_turn="+BTT_getTurn()+" | narrative_processed=NO | input_preserved="+(preserved?"YES":"EMPTY_CONTINUE"));BTT_writeDevRiskGenerationDebugCard();return preserved;}return BTT_processNarrative(String(text||""),"input");}catch(err){try{state.message="BTT ERROR (input): "+String(err&&err.message||err);}catch(_e){}return String(text||"");}}
function onOutput_BTT(text){try{if(BTT_devRiskAuthoringPending())return BTT_processDevRiskMaintenanceOutput(String(text||""));return BTT_processNarrative(String(text||""),"output");}catch(err){try{state.message="BTT ERROR (output): "+String(err&&err.message||err);}catch(_e){}return String(text||"");}}
function onContext_BTT(text){
  try{
    BTT_ensureState();BTT_preflightImportAll();var cfg=BTT_getConfig();if(!cfg.enabled)return String(text||"");
    BTT_sanitizeProfileCardTriggers();var meta=BTT_getEntity("Player"),gen=BTT_ensureState().devRiskGeneration,baseContext=BTT_stripDevRiskMaintenanceContext(String(text||""));if(!meta.initialized&&!gen.pending)return baseContext;
    if(meta.initialized)BTT_updateAppearanceRefresh("Player",meta,cfg);var ctx=(meta.initialized?BTT_contextExcerpt(meta,cfg):"")+baseContext;
    /* Semantic-field maintenance authoring has exclusive output authority while
       pending. Every unresolved semantic dimension is requested in one batch; BTT
       retains partial outcomes and reserves context space for the complete task. */
    if(cfg.dev_risk_ai_generation_enabled&&gen&&gen.pending){BTT_devInitializeAuthoringPlan(gen.pending,cfg);var reqFields=BTT_devRequestedFields(gen.pending);BTT_touchDevRiskGenerationDebug("AUTHORING PROMPT INJECTED | requested_fields="+(reqFields.join(",")||"whole-card validation")+" | responses="+String(gen.pending.authoringResponses||0)+" | continues_used="+String(gen.pending.maintenanceContinues||0)+"/"+String(cfg.dev_risk_ai_max_authoring_continues)+" | story_frozen=YES");ctx=BTT_injectDevRiskAuthoringTask(ctx,gen.pending);return ctx;}
    if(meta.appearance._need_ai_refresh){
      if(!meta.appearance._pendingRefresh){meta.appearance._pendingRefresh=true;if(meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn<0)meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn=BTT_getTurn();if(!meta.appearance._refresh_signature)meta.appearance._refresh_signature=BTT_refreshSignature(meta);if(!meta.appearance._refresh_request_id)meta.appearance._refresh_request_id=BTT_refreshRequestId(meta,meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn);BTT_touchAppearanceDebug(meta,"REFRESH INJECTED | turn="+BTT_getTurn()+" | requested="+meta.appearance._refresh_requested_turn+" | request_id="+meta.appearance._refresh_request_id+" | transport=player_appearance");}
      ctx=BTT_injectAppearanceRefreshTask(ctx,meta);
    }
    return ctx;
  }catch(err){try{state.message="BTT ERROR (context): "+String(err&&err.message||err);}catch(_e){}return String(text||"");}
}

/* Public router used by the AI Dungeon Input / Context / Output tabs. */
function BTT(phase){try{phase=BTT_lower(phase);if(phase==="input")text=onInput_BTT(text);else if(phase==="context")text=onContext_BTT(text);else if(phase==="output")text=onOutput_BTT(text);}catch(err){try{state.message="BTT ERROR ("+String(phase||"unknown")+"): "+String(err&&err.message||err);}catch(_e){}}}

/* Initialize only the clean state container and control cards. Narrative state is
   intentionally not inferred at library load time. */
try{BTT_ensureState();BTT_ensureConfigCard();BTT_ensureTargetCard();BTT_ensureEntityCardSet("Player",BTT_getEntity("Player"));BTT_sanitizeProfileCardTriggers();BTT_writeDevRiskGenerationDebugCard();}catch(_e){}

// ============================================================================
// END BODY TRANSFORMATION TRACKER
